These results reveal both the potential and current limitations of Geospatial Foundation Models (GeoFMs) when applied to fine-grained MDA understanding in real-world scenarios.
  Additionally, a new maritime remote sensing dataset is released, pairing about 2,000 Sentinel-1 images with temporally aligned AIS signals from commercial vessels. Through manual inspection, each candidate sample was verified for bounding-box quality and for consistency between the AIS-reported vessel length and the observed vessel extent, cross-checking vessel identity and location against publicly available vessel records and, where available, photographs. The dataset was made ready for use with the NeuCo-Bench neural compression benchmark framework, publicly available on HuggingFace through https://huggingface.co/datasets/embed2scale/S1-single-ship-downstream .

</details>


### [35] [SemProxy: Semantic 3D Proxies for Controllable Agentic 3D Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34414)
*Shuyuan Zhang,Changjian Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SemProxy——一种以带标签3D包围盒实现的轻量中间表示，连接直观人类交互与智能体3D建模，并开发 ProxyStudio 界面，实现可控的人机协同3D建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体已能生成3D模型，但用户难以有效控制建模过程；仅靠文本提示与参考图像难以表达精确空间意图，需反复迭代修正。

Method: 设计 SemProxy 语义3D代理：以带标签3D包围盒让用户直接指定物体部件与空间布局，几何细节交由编码智能体完成；代理可序列化为结构化文本并渲染为图像，无需额外训练或改动即可被现有编码智能体读取。进一步构建 ProxyStudio 用户界面，整合文本、图像参考与交互式3D代理编辑。

Result: 在物体级与场景级建模任务中，结合多种编码智能体验证了 SemProxy 的通用性；并通过用户研究评估其在支持可控人机协同3D建模方面的有效性。

Conclusion: SemProxy 作为一种轻量、免训练的中间表示，能有效弥合直观交互与智能体建模之间的鸿沟，提升3D建模的可控性与协作效率。

Abstract: Coding agents are increasingly capable of generating 3D models, yet enabling users to effectively control the modeling process remains challenging. Existing workflows rely primarily on text prompts and reference images, which often struggle to convey precise spatial intentions and require iterative corrections. We introduce SemProxy, a lightweight intermediate representation that bridges intuitive human interaction and agentic 3D modeling. Instantiated as labeled 3D bounding boxes, semantic 3D proxies allow users to directly specify object parts and spatial arrangements while leaving geometric details to coding agents. These proxies can be serialized into structured text and rendered as images, making them directly accessible to existing coding agents without additional training or modifications. We further develop ProxyStudio, a user interface that integrates text, image references, and interactive 3D proxy editing into agentic modeling workflows. We demonstrate the versatility of SemProxy across object- and scene-level modeling tasks with different coding agents, and evaluate its effectiveness in supporting controllable human-agent collaborative 3D modeling through a user study.

</details>


### [36] [Lost in the Grid: Where Multimodal LLMs Fail Between Seeing and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34152)
*Luoxi Zhang,Tianyi Tan,Jiawei Liu,Xingyu Zeng,Min Yang,Shiwen Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GridProbe 是一个面向多模态大模型空间规划能力的诊断式基准：在 Sokoban 上同时评测端到端求解与四项探针（识别、定位、单步预测、状态跟踪），并将每个棋盘以图像与等价文本网格双形式给出，从而定位失败发生在视觉感知还是规划阶段，并通过针对性微调验证诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间规划基准多只报告端到端成功率，无法区分失败源于视觉感知、规则应用还是多步规划，导致对模型瓶颈的认识模糊，也难以指导训练。

Method: 以网格游戏（Sokoban 与迷宫）为测试床：端到端求解配合四项诊断探针（识别、定位、单步预测、状态跟踪）；每个棋盘同时提供图像与等价文本网格以分离视觉因素；在七个 MLLM 上评测，并通过仅微调视觉模块、以及在求解器解上训练等对照实验来验证诊断结论。

Result: 相似成功率背后瓶颈各异：闭源模型受限于多步规划，开源模型受限于规则应用或视觉网格索引（行列计数）；规划难度降低时，同一模型的视觉代价从 7.6 分升至 37.7 分，说明规划失败会掩盖感知失败。仅用棋盘读取数据微调感知受限模型的视觉模块，使其交互式 Sokoban 成功率提升 3.5 倍（5.8%→20.4%），并零样本迁移到未见迷宫（单轮成功率 13.8%→67.1%）；同样数据对规则受限模型无效，在求解器解上训练亦无增益。

Conclusion: 将端到端指标拆解为可诊断的探针并配合文本等价对照，能够精确定位 MLLM 空间规划的失败阶段；诊断结论可由针对性微调验证，且"感知—规则—规划"瓶颈分类可直接指导数据与模块级训练决策。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) excel at static visual understanding and are increasingly applied to spatial planning, where a model interprets a visual layout and outputs an action sequence to reach a goal. Grid games are a common testbed for this ability because their rules are explicit and a solver can verify every move. Yet existing benchmarks mostly report end-to-end success rates, which hide whether a failure stems from visual perception, rule application, or multi-step planning. We introduce GridProbe, a diagnostic benchmark that 1) locates the failing stage between visual perception and planning and 2) verifies the diagnosis by targeted fine-tuning. On Sokoban, GridProbe pairs end-to-end solving with four probes (recognition, localization, single-step prediction, and state tracking) and gives every board both as an image and as an equivalent text grid. Evaluating seven MLLMs on Sokoban and a maze game reveals distinct bottlenecks behind similar success rates: proprietary models are limited by multi-step planning, whereas open-source models are limited by rule application or by visual grid indexing, i.e., counting rows and columns. Planning failures also hide perception failures: when planning is easier, the visual cost of the same model grows from 7.6 to 37.7 points. The diagnosis identifies what to train: fine-tuning only the vision module of the perception-limited model on board reading raises its interactive Sokoban success 3.5-fold (5.8% to 20.4%) and transfers zero-shot to the unseen maze game (single-turn success 13.8% to 67.1%), whereas the same data does not help a rule-limited model and training on solver solutions brings no gain. We will release the benchmark and code at https://github.com/suat-llm/GridProbe.

</details>


### [37] [Out of Sight, Not Out of Clues: Can Video LLMs Guess from Partial Evidence?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34126)
*Luoxi Zhang,Jiawei Liu,Yanlin Luo,Peiyang He,Junlin Zhu,Chun To Choi,Yang Xiong,Jiang Wu,Xingyu Zeng,Min Yang,Shiwen Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出诊断型基准ClueProbe，将"感知证据"与"判断证据充分性"分离，发现Video LLM的失败源于充分性判断而非感知，且可通过监督"猜测"等级修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实场景（第一视角录制、视觉无障碍、监控回看）中视觉证据往往只被部分捕获，理想模型应随证据充分程度分别回答、基于可见线索猜测或拒答。然而现有基准仅做"可答/不可答"二元划分，部分证据情形未被测量，也无法区分失败究竟来自缺失证据还是对充分性的误判。

Method: 构建ClueProbe基准：含598个自然视频、1,721个人工标注问题，标注为Answerable、Guessable、Unanswerable三级，并配有改写措辞或改变视觉输入的受控扰动；在该基准上评测10个开源与商用Video LLM；并通过针对性微调（监督guess等级 vs. 常规拒答微调）验证诊断结论。

Result: 模型能感知到证据，但缺乏分级充分性意识，倾向于直接回答或直接拒答，很少从部分线索中猜测；监督guess等级可将恰当猜测率从1.6%提升至40.4%且不增加误拒答，而常规拒答微调会拒绝92%的可猜问题。

Conclusion: Video LLM的缺陷在于缺少分级充分性判断的监督信号，而非模型容量不足；引入三级证据标签可有效提升证据感知下的行为对齐。

Abstract: In real-world applications like egocentric recording, visual accessibility, and surveillance review, visual evidence for Video Large Language Models (Video LLMs) is often only partially captured. A reliable model should match its response to the evidence: answering when sufficient, guessing from visible clues when partial, and abstaining when absent. Yet existing benchmarks enforce a binary answerable-or-unanswerable split, leaving partial evidence unmeasured and obscuring whether failures arise from missing evidence or misjudging sufficiency. We introduce ClueProbe, a diagnostic benchmark to (1) separate perceiving evidence from judging sufficiency, and (2) validate our diagnosis via targeted fine-tuning. ClueProbe comprises 1,721 human-annotated questions across 598 natural videos, labeled Answerable, Guessable, or Unanswerable, alongside controlled perturbations altering wording or visual input. Evaluating 10 open-source and proprietary Video LLMs reveals that failures stem from judgment rather than perception: models perceive the evidence yet lack graded sufficiency awareness, answering or refusing outright while rarely guessing from partial clues. Targeted fine-tuning shows that this reflects missing supervision rather than insufficient capacity: supervising the guess level lifts appropriate guessing from 1.6% to 40.4% without extra false refusals, whereas conventional refusal tuning rejects 92% of guessable questions. Data and code will be released at https://github.com/suat-llm/ClueProbe.

</details>


### [38] [Solve Vision with Code: Coding Agents Are Stronger Visual Reasoners Than Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34813)
*Dezhi Luo,Fengyuan Yu,Haotian Zhang,Zehong Zhao,Ran Ji,Siyuan An,Huayi Qin,Qingying Gao,Zhongang Cai,Lei Yang,Ziqi Huang,Ruisi Wang,Maijunxian Wang,Hokin Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 首个严格受控对比表明：在自首帧+提示即可计算解答的视觉推理任务上，程序合成（编码智能体写渲染程序）比视频生成更可靠，闭源智能体得分比最强视频模型高 0.22 以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型被视为"视觉原生推理"路径，其优势（推理直接发生在像素帧中而非语言或代码）此前从未在与符号路径同条件对照下被检验；本文旨在检验这一原生路线是否真的更可靠。

Method: 在同一批任务、相同输入、相同输出规范与相同评估器下比较两种媒介：一侧由视频模型去噪生成像素；另一侧由编码智能体编写渲染答案视频的程序，由未修改的规则评估器打分。测试集为 VBVR-Pro-Bench（500 个实例、五个认知类别、带符号真值），智能体仅获首帧与提示，无真值、元数据与生成模型访问权限，每实例一次尝试。比较 3 个闭源智能体与 23 个可运行开源权重模型；并区分"描绘式程序"（通过导航图像恢复可迁移方案）与"命题式程序"（操作硬编码数值）。

Result: 闭源智能体：Codex(gpt-6-astra) 0.923、Claude Code(Fable 5.1) 0.894、Gemini CLI(Gemini 3.1 Pro) 0.893，均比最强视频模型高 0.22 以上。开源权重模型中最佳为 GLM-5（0.572），高于除 RL 训练视频模型外的所有视频模型。

Conclusion: 对于解可由首帧与提示直接计算的任务，程序合成当前是比视频生成显著更可靠的视觉推理路径；描绘式与命题式程序的区分揭示了视觉推理在智能体所写程序中的展开方式及其对推理本质的启示。

Abstract: Video generation models are increasingly evaluated as visual reasoners: given a first frame and a natural-language prompt, can the model produce the video in which the specified task is carried out? The paradigm is considered advantageous because the reasoning is native to the visual medium, unfolding directly in the frames rather than through language or code. Whether this native route is in fact the more reliable path to visual reasoning has not been tested against a symbolic alternative under the same conditions. We therefore pose the same tasks, with identical inputs, output specification and evaluator, to both media: rather than denoising pixels, a coding agent writes a program that renders the answer video, which an unmodified rule-based evaluator scores. On VBVR-Pro-Bench (Xu et al., 2026), a 500-instance suite spanning five cognitive categories with symbolic ground truth, the agent receives the same first frame and prompt as the video model, with no access to ground truth, metadata or generation models, and one attempt per instance. Three closed-model agents (Codex with gpt-6-astra, Claude Code with Fable 5.1 and Gemini CLI with Gemini 3.1 Pro) score 0.923, 0.894 and 0.893, each more than 0.22 above the strongest video model. Among 23 runnable open-weight models, the best, GLM-5, reaches 0.572, above all video models except the RL-trained one. Together, we show that for tasks whose solutions can be computed from a first frame and a prompt, program synthesis is currently a substantially more reliable route to visual reasoning than video generation. We further distinguish depictive programs, which navigate the image to recover a transferable scheme, from propositional programs, which operate on hard-coded values, revealing how visual reasoning unfolds in agent-written programs and what it implies for its nature of reasoning in general.

</details>


### [39] [BOP-Refer: Benchmark for Monocular 3D and 2D Object Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34862)
*Tomas Hodan,Vineet Bhat,Martin Sundermeyer,Jonathan Tremblay,Yash Patel,Médéric Fourmy,Stephen Tyree,Junwen Huang,Varun Burde,Martin Cífka,Eric Brachmann,Vincent Lepetit,Stan Birchfield,Jiri Matas,Carsten Rother*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BOP-Refer，首个面向日常与工业物体的单目2D/3D指代定位基准，源自9个BOP数据集；对17个前沿VLM的系统评测表明，2D定位接近可用，而3D定位因度量尺度估计困难而性能极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在2D指代定位上进展显著，但缺乏统一、高质量的基准来评估其在真实场景下的单目3D指代定位能力；已有3D数据集多缺少指代表达或精确的6DoF位姿与模型级真值，难以支撑系统评测。

Method: 构建BOP-Refer基准：从9个BOP数据集中选取图像，配以VLM生成并经人工校验的指代表达，并利用物体3D模型与6DoF位姿标注推导出精确的3D与2D边界框真值；随后对Gemini、GPT、Claude、Qwen、Gemma等主要闭源与开源家族的17个前沿VLM进行系统评测，并按2D/3D IoU阈值下的AP指标度量，进一步对误差来源做几何分析。

Result: 最优模型Gemini 4 Carbon在2D取得72.6% AP_IoU2D，但3D仅12.4% AP_IoU3D，尽管3D的IoU阈值更为宽松；预测的3D框通常能对准目标物体的图像中心，但深度方向偏差常超过物体自身尺寸。

Conclusion: 单目3D指代定位的瓶颈不在语义定位，而在度量尺度估计——其依赖可靠的物体尺寸先验；BOP-Refer为后续研究提供了标准化评测平台，并指出引入尺寸先验、尺度感知训练是提升3D定位的关键方向。

Abstract: We introduce BOP-Refer, the first benchmark for monocular 3D and 2D grounding of everyday and industrial objects sourced from 9 diverse BOP datasets. We pair images from these datasets with VLM-generated and human-verified referring expressions, and derive accurate ground-truth 3D and 2D bounding boxes of the referred objects from their 3D models and 6DoF pose annotations. Our systematic evaluation of 17 frontier VLMs across the major proprietary and open-weight families (Gemini, GPT, Claude, Qwen, Gemma) shows that the best model, Gemini 4 Carbon, reaches 72.6% AP_IoU2D in 2D but only 12.4% AP_IoU3D in 3D, despite the more lenient IoU thresholds in 3D. The predicted 3D boxes are usually centered on the target objects in the image but often off in depth by more than the object's size, suggesting that metric scale estimation, which needs reliable object size priors, is currently by far the main source of error.

</details>


### [40] [Same Tokenizer, Better Tokens: Choosing Image Tokens by MAP Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33261)
*Piotr Kubaty,Jacek Tabor,Łukasz Struski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 RecaP（Reconstruction and Predictability），在冻结的 VQ tokenizer 上联合优化重建保真度与序列可预测性来选取离散视觉 token，使自回归图像生成器训练目标更易学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQ tokenizer 独立地为每个编码器特征分配最近码字，但解码器是联合使用整条码序列的，因此"逐点最近"的码序列未必能最优重建图像；同时存在多种码序列可达到相似重建质量，为偏好更可预测（更符合自回归先验）的序列留下空间。

Method: 将重建损失局部建模为以解码器相关的目标与度量定义的最近向量问题，从而在冻结 tokenizer 上做依赖解码器的重建搜索；进一步引入 RecaP，在重建保真度与预训练自回归先验下序列似然之间加权平衡，选取码序列。

Result: 仅用重建搜索即可在多个冻结 tokenizer 上提升 PSNR。在 ImageNet 上，用 RecaP token 训练的 LlamaGen GPT-B 在早期 checkpoint 阶段对训练图像的 FID 低于最近邻 token 训练的 GPT-B，但对留出图像的差距较小；在 ImageNet 子集上，先验在相近重建 FID 下改善了生成质量。

Conclusion: token 选取应面向下游自回归建模，而非仅逐点最近邻；联合重建与可预测性可提升生成模型早期训练效率，但对泛化到留出数据的增益有限，需进一步验证。

Abstract: Discrete visual tokens encode images for reconstruction and serve as training targets for autoregressive image generators. Vector-quantized tokenizers typically assign each encoder feature its nearest code independently. The decoder uses these codes jointly, so a sequence of individually nearest codes can reconstruct an image less faithfully than an alternative assignment. A local model of the reconstruction loss yields a closest-vector problem with a decoder-dependent target and metric. Reconstruction-only search improves PSNR across the frozen tokenizers we test. Different code sequences can achieve similar reconstruction quality, leaving room to favour more predictable ones.
  We introduce RecaP (Reconstruction and Predictability), which selects codes for a frozen tokenizer by balancing how faithfully they reconstruct the image with how likely their sequence is under a pretrained autoregressive prior. On ImageNet, LlamaGen GPT-B trained on RecaP tokens achieves lower early-checkpoint FID against training images than GPT-B trained on nearest-neighbour tokens. The difference is smaller against held-out images. On an ImageNet subset, the prior improves generation at comparable reconstruction FID. Our code is available at: https://github.com/gmum/recap

</details>


### [41] [SEEL: Towards End-to-End 3D Sign Generation with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33588)
*Ronglai Zuo,Fei Ni,Junpeng Jing,Rolandos Alexandros Potamias,Jiankang Deng,Stefanos Zafeiriou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SEEL提出端到端框架，通过"重建感知生成"与"生成感知分词"两个互补机制，使运动分词器与生成器协同进化，实现文本到3D手语动作生成的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两阶段流程中分词器与生成器目标不匹配：分词器孤立地最小化重建误差，生成器则依赖僵硬的一热参考token学习；直接端到端扩展虽用STE连接离散选择，但存在梯度偏差与码本崩溃问题。

Method: SEEL包含两个互补机制：(1) 重建感知生成——在上下文中解码候选token，将其3D运动误差转化为软目标，以物理运动质量引导生成器，替代僵硬的一热监督；(2) 生成感知分词——将连续码本期望通过冻结的生成器前向传播，绕开STE，把生成运动梯度回传至分词器，防止码本崩溃。

Result: 在CSL-Daily、How2Sign与Phoenix-2014T三个数据集上取得state-of-the-art性能。

Conclusion: 融合两种机制使分词器与生成器协同进化，验证了以运动质量对齐分词与生成的端到端范式有效性；代码与模型将开源以促进后续研究。

Abstract: 3D sign language generation aims to produce signing motion from written text. Motivated by the linguistic nature of sign language, recent methods combine a motion tokenizer with a language model to predict motion tokens from text. However, both components are optimized through a two-stage pipeline with mismatched objectives: the tokenizer minimizes reconstruction error in isolation, while the generator learns from rigid one-hot reference tokens. A straightforward end-to-end extension bridges discrete token selection with straight-through estimators (STE), suffering from biased gradients and codebook collapse. To address these limitations, we propose SEEL, an end-to-end framework that aligns tokenization and generation with motion quality through two complementary mechanisms. First, to overcome rigid one-hot supervision, reconstruction-aware generation decodes candidate tokens in context and converts their 3D motion errors into soft targets, guiding the generator with physical motion quality. Second, to propagate generation feedback without STE, generation-aware tokenization passes continuous codebook expectations through the frozen generator, routing generated-motion gradients to the tokenizer to prevent codebook collapse. Combining both mechanisms enables the tokenizer and generator to co-evolve, achieving state-of-the-art performance on CSL-Daily, How2Sign, and Phoenix-2014T. Code and models will be released to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [42] [Neural Rendering Compiler: Generative Video Models as Programmable Renderers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34680)
*Yuxia Zhu,Xinglong Xu,Hao Liang,Linzhuang Sun,Caijun Jia,Qifeng Cai,Jincen Luo,Lei Li,Alden Cai,Asher Qin,Wentao Zhang,Cheng Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Neural Rendering Compiler (NeurCo)，通过自动探测冻结的视频生成模型构建“能力手册”，将模型无关的中间表示编译为模型特定执行策略，并发布VComBench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式视频模型缺乏类似HTML/CSS或Blender那样可预测的控制接口，其能力隐式地来自训练，难以判断哪些语义需求能被可靠执行。

Method: 以对象、约束、事件、相机为要素构建模型无关的中间表示；自动探测冻结视频模型，形成模型特定能力手册；据此编译用户意图，对可靠需求保持不变，对不可靠需求进行选择性重写或编译为替代执行策略；同时构建VComBench（380个组合式多需求任务，覆盖T2V、I2V、reference-to-video）。

Result: 在Wan上将需求满足率从46.8%提升至52.3%，在Minimax H3上从66.5%提升至69.3%；与提示增强器结合可获进一步提升（包括Seedance 2.5）。分析揭示各模型控制能力的差异以及可控性与真实感之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 能力引导的编译是通向可编程生成式视频渲染的有前景路径。

Abstract: Generative video models are becoming powerful renderers, yet lack the predictable control interfaces of conventional systems such as HTML/CSS engines or Blender. Their capabilities emerge implicitly from training, making it difficult to determine what they can reliably execute. We present Neural Rendering Compiler (NeurCo), which automatically probes frozen video models to construct model-specific capability manuals describing which semantic requirements they can reliably satisfy. NeurCo represents user intent in a model-agnostic intermediate representation of objects, constraints, events, and cameras, then compiles it into model-specific execution strategies. Reliable requirements remain unchanged, while unreliable ones are selectively rewritten or compiled into alternative execution strategies. We introduce VComBench, a benchmark of 380 compositional, multi-requirement tasks spanning text-to-video, image-to-video, and reference-to-video generation. NeurCo improves requirement satisfaction from 46.8% to 52.3% on Wan and from 66.5% to 69.3% on Minimax H3, and achieves further gains when combined with prompt enhancers, including on Seedance 2.5. Our analysis reveals model-specific differences in control capabilities and trade-offs between controllability and photorealism, highlighting capability-guided compilation as a promising approach to programmable generative video rendering.

</details>


### [43] [DocSEAM: Bidirectional Cross-Page Encoding for Multi-Page Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34420)
*Hoang-Nhat Nguyen,Chen Change Loy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DocSEAM 模块，在多页文档 VLM 的视觉编码器与语言模型之间注入跨页局部上下文偏置，视觉 token 数不变，在五个多页文档 VQA 基准上于 ≤10B 开源模型中达到 SOTA，并降低对分页边界的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨页文档视觉语言模型逐页独立编码，页边界处存在特征层面的不连续：视觉 token 在缺少相邻页内容的情况下形成，语言模型只能基于这些孤立页表示进行推理。作者希望以轻量方式引入跨页局部上下文的归纳偏置。

Method: DocSEAM（Document Selective Enrichment via Anchored Masking）插入在预训练视觉编码器与语言模型之间。对每页与其前后两页构成局部三元组，采用中心锚定的掩码自注意力并配合方向性显著性门控，融合邻页信息，仅返回增强后的中心页 token，保持视觉 token 数量不变。训练流程为有监督跨页预热，再进行多页微调且 DocSEAM 冻结。

Result: 9B 参数模型在被评估的至多 10B 参数开源模型中，于五个多页文档 VQA 基准上取得 SOTA。在受控重新分页实验中，DocSEAM 降低了对边界划分位置的敏感性，并提升了跨页片段定位能力。

Conclusion: 通过在视觉表示层面显式注入跨页邻域上下文，可在不增加视觉 token 数与不改变主干架构的前提下显著提升多页文档理解性能，并缓解逐页独立编码带来的边界不连续问题。

Abstract: Multi-page document vision-language models encode each page independently, creating a feature-level discontinuity at page boundaries: visual tokens are formed without access to content on adjacent pages, and the language model must reason over these page representations formed in isolation. We introduce DocSEAM (Document Selective Enrichment via Anchored Masking), a lightweight module inserted between a pretrained vision encoder and the language model that injects the inductive bias of local cross-page context into visual representations. For each page, DocSEAM forms a local triplet with its two neighbors, applies center-anchored masked self-attention with directional salience gates to fuse information from neighbor pages, and returns only the enriched center-page tokens, leaving the visual token count unchanged. After a supervised cross-page warm-up and multi-page fine-tuning with DocSEAM frozen, our 9B-parameter model achieves state-of-the-art results among the evaluated open-weight models of up to 10B parameters on five multi-page document VQA benchmarks. We further show that under controlled re-pagination, DocSEAM reduces sensitivity to boundary placement and improves cross-page span localization

</details>


### [44] [A Semantics-First Approach to Multimodal Video Captioning with Frozen Encoders and Stabilized Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33409)
*Albert Chiwara,Erhan Guven*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AVQFormer 用 2.7B LLM 加固定长度 K=16 语义对齐模块和 ASCST 训练，发现全局音频表征与视频帧时序冲突（"时序对齐摩擦"），导致 CIDEr 等视觉偏向指标偏低，进而质疑 MSR-VTT 上 CIDEr 的评价效度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型常依赖全局音频表征来弥补低效的逐帧视觉编码器；同时沿用 MSR-VTT 等数据集上的 CIDEr 等传统指标做评测，但该指标是否真实反映多模态语义质量存疑。

Method: 构建 AVQFormer：基于 2.7B 参数 LLM，加入固定长度 K=16 的语义对齐模块，并用自适应自批判序列训练（ASCST）配合熵正则稳定优化；通过 Phase 1 提示式评测（BERTScore F1）、Video-Only 消融（去除音频）与更大模型的对比实验进行分析。

Result: Phase 1 多模态基模型取得 0.889 的 BERTScore F1，表明深度语义对齐良好；去除音频的 Video-Only 消融在 CIDEr 上达 63.0，优于完整多模态设置，但仍低于 Qwen2.5-VL 的 67.4 CIDEr，构成 sub-3B LLM 类模型的强基线。

Conclusion: 作者将性能差距解释为"时序对齐摩擦"：全局音频表征打乱视觉帧的时间顺序，从而在视觉导向指标上受罚；这说明 CIDEr 在 MSR-VTT 上存在视觉语法偏向，无法充分刻画多模态语义丰富度，评测体系需要改进。

Abstract: We propose the Audio-Visual Query Transformer (AVQFormer), a highly efficient architecture built upon a 2.7B parameter LLM featuring a fixed-length $K=16$ semantic alignment module, stabilized via Adaptive Self-Critical Sequence Training (ASCST) with Entropy Regularization. Multimodal Large Language Models (LLMs) often rely on global audio representations to compensate for inefficient, frame-based visual encoders. While our Phase 1 prompted evaluations demonstrate that the Multimodal Base Model achieves strong deep semantic alignment (scoring 0.889 on BERTScore F1), optimizing this architecture for legacy metrics reveals a performance gap. We explore this gap as a hypothesis we term temporal alignment friction: a conflict where global audio representations disrupt the chronological sequence of visual frames, leading to penalties under visually-focused evaluation metrics. Consequently, by stripping away the audio, our Video-Only ablation conforms more closely to this rigid syntax, achieving 63.0 CIDEr. While trailing larger models such as Qwen2.5-VL (67.4 CIDEr), this establishes a strong baseline for sub-3B parameter LLM-based models. This indicates that CIDEr, as applied to datasets such as MSR-VTT, may exhibit a bias toward visually grounded syntax, limiting its ability to fully capture multimodal semantic richness.

</details>


### [45] [Temporal Weight Surgery: Repairing Video Grounding with LLM Editors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33339)
*Ali Asgarov,Chris Thomas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Temporal Weight Surgery (TWS)：用通用LLM在无梯度条件下读取视频语言模型的权重与激活，定位并修复时间戳输出错误，核心贡献是对"何处、为何出错"的机制性诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频定位中，视频-语言模型常能找对长视频里的正确时刻，却把时间写错。作者借鉴"外科医生先诊断再只修病灶"的思路，希望不靠梯度、只做最小必要编辑来纠正这类错误。

Method: TWS 由通用 LLM 作为"外科医生"读取目标模型的权重与激活，定位错误产生的具体层/位置并原地修复，无需梯度。多个独立会话复现同一诊断，用于验证因果定位。

Result: 三个 GPT-6 Astra 会话将 TimeLens-8B 的丢数字错误追溯到单个 MLP 层，最大编辑在 1,215 个未见问题上提升 7.9 mIoU，在 ≥30 分钟视频上提升 18.3；两个 Claude Opus 5.5 会话将 Qwen3-VL-8B 的"分钟写成秒"错误定位到分隔符并分别提升 2.9 与 1.3，仅修分隔符即把 mIoU 从 13.1 提升到 19.2。所有有效编辑均优于同规模随机编辑，且模型推理速度不变。

Conclusion: 视频定位的时间错误源于可精确定位的局部机制（单层、单token/分隔符），针对性无梯度权重编辑可显著修复且不损速度；跨会话一致的诊断本身构成主要贡献。

Abstract: A surgeon first finds what is wrong, then fixes only that part. We use this idea for video grounding, where video-language models often find the right moment in a long video but write its time wrong. In Temporal Weight Surgery (TWS), a general-purpose LLM reads the model's weights and activations, finds where the error arises and repairs it there, without gradients. Separate sessions find the same cause, and this diagnosis of where and why the model fails is our main contribution. Three GPT-6 Astra sessions trace a dropped digit in TimeLens-8B to one MLP layer, and the largest edit gains 7.9 mIoU on 1,215 unseen questions and 18.3 on videos of 30 minutes or more. Two Claude Opus 5.5 sessions trace minutes written as seconds in Qwen3-VL-8B to the separator and gain 2.9 and 1.3. Before any edit, its answers often have the right minute and the wrong separator, and fixing only the separator lifts their mIoU from 13.1 to 19.2. Every improving edit beats a random edit of its size, and the edited models run as fast as before.

</details>


### [46] [Measuring and Predicting Image-Token Repair Boundaries in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33388)
*Vishal Bhutani,Prasang Gupta,Sumanth Chundru,Kevin Paul*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究：降质图像导致视觉语言模型回答错误后，哪些"干净图像状态"能在视觉编码器到语言模型的链路上修复回答，并量化其稀疏性、可修复深度边界与冗余性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降质图像（模糊、噪声、低光）会使VLM回答出错，但"干净视觉状态在何处、以何种稀疏程度修复这些错误回答"缺乏系统刻画，尤其是编码器与语言模型之间的归因与可预测性尚不清晰。

Method: 构造合成的配对（clean/degraded）模糊、噪声、低光数据；沿视觉编码器至语言模型逐层注入干净状态进行修复追踪；比较排序token子集与随机顺序的修复效率；定位全块修复的失效交叉点；分析反向干预（与文本token恢复代数等价）所定义的冗余带；用干净图像灰度消融交叉点作为独立预测器预测留出集修复效果；并比较不同注意力机制（MMBench vs GQA）与Restormer非oracle一致性检验。

Result: 四个编码器模型中，前12–23个排序token即可恢复全patch效果的50%，随机顺序需4–15倍数量；无编码器的Gemma为22.2 vs 53.1。全块修复在模型特定的语言模型深度交叉点失效。反向方向干预与文本token恢复代数等价，其与图像恢复的差距反映冗余带而非独立预测量。干净图像灰度消融交叉点以1.53层误差预测留出修复，优于共享深度的3.65层。Gemma MMBench存在唯一较大的注意力—修复点间差距，Gemma GQA则无。Restormer在两模型上提供非oracle一致性验证。

Conclusion: 干净视觉状态对错误回答的修复高度稀疏且可由少量排序token近似；修复存在模型特定的深度上限；反向干预的"预测性"实为冗余带伪影；灰度消融交叉点是一个更准确的独立预测指标；注意力分配与修复效果并非普遍耦合。

Abstract: Degraded images make vision-language models answer wrongly. Using synthetic paired blur, noise, and low light, we trace which clean image states can still repair those answers from the vision encoder through the language model. In four encoder models, 12 to 23 ranked tokens recover half of the full-patch effect, whereas random orders require four to fifteen times as many; encoder-free Gemma needs 22.2 versus 53.1. Full-block repair expires at a model-specific language-model crossing. The matched reverse-direction intervention is algebraically identical to text-token restoration, so its gap from image restoration measures a redundancy band rather than an independent predictor. A clean-image gray-ablation crossing independently predicts held-out repair with 1.53-layer error, against 3.65 for shared depth. Gemma MMBench has the only large attention-to-repair point gap; Gemma GQA does not. Restormer provides a non-oracle consistency check on two models.

</details>


### [47] [The Missing Root: Geometric Imagination Without Images in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33841)
*Loïc Magnan,Tomasz Kozlowski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"连续物体token + 词token"混合的单一Transformer序列范式，让语言模型直接以连续空间表示（位姿+符号距离形状嵌入）进行空间推理，在Qwen3-1.7B上附加+4.4%参数的并行空间流即可实现二维多物体场景生成、形状迁移与基于工作区的因果空间问答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准语言模型只在离散词（事物的次级表示）上运算，"用语言猜测几何"；而多数多模态方法把空间grounding等同于相机图像/视频帧，但空间结构本身不是图片——它由坐标系、实体边界、物体在连续空间中的运动与相互关系构成。因此需要一个让语言与几何在同一序列中直接交互的范式。

Method: 在单一不间断的transformer序列中，将"连续物体token"（位姿：位置、朝向、尺度 + 有符号距离场形状嵌入）与词token并列输入，使语言与几何可相互注意力；用控制token在自然语言词输出与"对持久物体工作区的连续状态更新"之间自动切换；生成答案时用注意力掩码屏蔽先前散文，迫使回答直接从已确定的几何中读取。实现上在未冻结的Qwen3-1.7B层旁挂一个窄并行空间流（+4.4%权重）。

Result: 二维测试中，模型能：由英文描述生成并变换多物体场景；将形状迁移到未见过的名词（held-out nouns）；并以因果方式依赖该工作区回答空间问题。

Conclusion: 以连续物体token与词token共享同一序列、配合控制token与注意力掩码，可为LLM赋予"读自几何"而非"口头猜测"的空间理解；轻量并行空间流（+4.4%参数）在二维验证了该范式的可行性与因果有效性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a new paradigm and method for giving large language models spatial understanding. Standard language models operate exclusively over discrete words (secondary representations of things rather than continuous space itself), forcing them to guess geometry verbally. Whereas most multimodal approaches equate spatial grounding with camera images or video frames, spatial structure itself is not a picture: it consists of coordinate frames, solid boundaries, and how objects move and relate to each other in continuous space. Motivated by this distinction, we place continuous object tokens combining pose (position, orientation, and scale) and signed-distance shape embeddings alongside word tokens inside a single uninterrupted transformer sequence, allowing language and geometry to attend to each other as object scenes are built and updated step by step. Control tokens switch output automatically between natural-language words and continuous object state updates to a persistent object workspace, while an attention mask blocks earlier prose when generating the answer, so responses must be read directly from settled geometry. To prove this method works, we attach a narrow parallel spatial stream (+4.4% weights) alongside unfrozen Qwen3-1.7B layers. In our two-dimensional tests, the model spawns and transforms multi-object scenes from English descriptions, transfers shapes to held-out nouns, and relies causally on the workspace to answer spatial questions.

</details>


### [48] [A 3D Simulator for Passive SPAD Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33498)
*Elia Mergaert,Rong Zou,Roberto Pellerito,Simone Nascivera,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一套面向被动式SPAD相机的3D仿真管线，可同步生成含光子噪声的真实感光子探测数据与相机运动、语义标签，实现零样本仿真到真实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPAD相机适合低光短曝光感知，但为其采集任务标注困难、标注数据集稀缺，制约了感知模型的训练。

Method: 构建可控场景几何与运动的3D仿真管线，并耦合传感器观测模型，显式建模光子散粒噪声、暗计数与空间串扰；同时自动生成相机运动标签与稠密语义标签。

Result: 经标定后的光子计数对比验证仿真保真度；在三项下游任务上实现零样本sim-to-real：仅用仿真数据训练的分类器在真实低于2 lux低光录制上取得高准确率；旋转估计在0.5与5.0 lux、20°与30°真实扫掠上平均绝对误差小于1°；语义分割模型经稠密仿真监督可直接推理真实数据。

Conclusion: 该仿真器为真实SPAD低光感知提供了可实用的带标注训练数据来源，验证了合成数据零样本迁移的可行性。

Abstract: Passive single-photon avalanche diode (SPAD) cameras provide short-exposure observations for low-light perception, but collecting task annotations for these sensors remains difficult, and annotated datasets are scarce. We present a 3D simulation pipeline that generates realistic photon detections together with camera-motion and semantic labels, enabling perception models to be trained on synthetic SPAD observations and applied directly to real-world data. The pipeline couples controllable scene geometry and motion with a sensor-informed observation model that accounts for photon shot noise, dark counts, and spatial crosstalk. We evaluate the pipeline through calibrated photon-count comparisons and zero-shot sim-to-real transfer across three downstream perception tasks. We show that classifiers trained only on synthetic SPAD data achieve high accuracy on real low-light recordings at illumination levels less than 2 lux, without real-data fine-tuning. Similarly, we apply models trained entirely on simulated camera sweeps to real-world camera-rotation estimation and achieve mean absolute rotation errors of less than 1 degree on real 20-degree and 30-degree sweeps at 0.5 and 5.0 lux, when deployed zero-shot. We additionally train semantic segmentation models on synthetic data with automatically generated dense labels and perform direct inference on real-world low-light recordings; the results further demonstrate the usefulness of synthetic dense supervision for real low-light perception. Our simulator thus provides a practical source of annotated training data for real SPAD perception.

</details>


### [49] [EvOrderBench: When do sampled frames establish event order?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33492)
*Xiaoqin Feng,Yuqi Li,Xinyuan Li,Neil Rodrigues,Xianglong Wang,Kuiye Ding,Yingli Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EvOrderBench真实视频基准（579个事件家族），每个家族固定情节与问题、只改变采样帧，用于检验视觉语言模型是否只在帧足以确立两个动作先后顺序时才给出顺序、否则拒绝作答；实验发现开源模型在可答题上排序接近闭源参考，但在帧不足以确立顺序时最多仅83%正确拒答（参考≥90%），说明排序准确率不能代表对证据充分性的判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序基准多为强制二选一，把所有输入当作可回答，奖励运气猜测；而现有的"可回答性"基准又没有区分"帧缺失某动作"与"帧显示了两动作但未显示先后"这两种证据不足的情形。因此需要一种只改变采样帧、并对每一输入标注其帧是否确立各动作及其顺序的基准，以单独测量模型的"拒答/保留判断"能力。

Method: 构建EvOrderBench：579个事件家族的真实视频，每家族固定同一片段与同一问题，仅变换输入帧。每家族含两个4帧输入（仅其中一个能确立顺序）和两个6帧输入（在另一4帧基础上分别补充能解决顺序的帧或无关帧）。每个输入都标注其帧是否确立了每个动作及其先后顺序。评测16个开源权重配置与3个闭源参考模型，比较可答时的排序准确率与不可答时的正确拒答率，并对比强制选择准确率、思考模式开启前后的差异。

Result: 当帧确实确立顺序时，最佳开源模型排序准确率接近闭源参考；但当帧不足以确立顺序时，开源模型正确拒答率最高仅83%，而所有参考模型均≥90%，该差距在强制选择准确率中完全不可见。开源模型主要在"两个动作都未出现"时才拒答，一旦帧显示一个或两个动作，多数模型便强行给出顺序。开启思考模式可缩小差距（即使在同一checkpoint内切换），其作用主要是区分证据充分与不充分，而非总体上更谨慎。

Conclusion: 准确的排序能力并不意味着模型知道自己何时证据不足；应把"在证据不足时保留判断"与"正确排序"并列作为基准的奖励目标，强制选择式的评测会掩盖这一关键缺陷。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are often asked which of two actions in a video happened first, given only a few sampled frames. Such frames can show that both actions occurred without showing which came first, so a reliable model should state an order only when the frames establish it. Forced-choice temporal benchmarks treat every input as answerable and reward lucky guesses, while answerability benchmarks do not separate frames that miss an action from frames that show both actions but not their order. We introduce EvOrderBench, a real-video benchmark of 579 event families. Each family fixes an episode and a question and varies only the frames: of two four-frame inputs, only one establishes the order, and two six-frame inputs extend the other one with either the frames that resolve the order or frames that do not. Every input is labeled for whether its frames establish each action and their order. Across sixteen open-weight configurations and three closed-source references, the best open models order nearly as accurately as the references when the frames establish the order, but withhold correctly on at most 83% of the inputs where they do not, against at least 90% for every reference; forced-choice accuracy cannot reveal this gap. Open models withhold mainly when the frames show neither action; once the frames show one or both, most of them commit to an order. Thinking narrows the gap, even when it is switched on within a single checkpoint, mainly by separating sufficient from insufficient evidence rather than by making models more cautious overall. Accurate ordering thus does not imply knowing when the frames fall short, and benchmarks should reward withholding alongside ordering.

</details>


### [50] [ArtKit: Motion-Driven Re-Segmentation and Link Re-Generation for Articulated Objects from RGB-D Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33403)
*Sylvia Yuan,Dan Wang,Vivian Yu,Ravi Ramamoorthi,Xinrui Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ArtKit从单视角RGB-D视频重建带完整部件、运动学树和类型化关节的铰接模型，核心是"生成隐藏几何并用相机观测验证"，在Articulate-100与新基准HArt上大幅超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单视角RGB-D拍摄物体运动时只能看到一侧及其部分运动，被遮挡的几何与部件结构未知，难以直接恢复完整的铰接（articulated）模型。

Method: 1) 基于首帧的生成式部件先验提出完整、部件分解的几何；2) 将点轨迹（point tracks）按刚性分组，用于拆分/合并生成部件为links；3) 拟合旋转（revolute）与移动（prismatic）关节，采用可见性感知能量使模型解释全视频的深度观测；4) 用规则在同步分组拟合与逐帧链式拟合间选择，统一处理家居物体与机械手；5) 将选定拟合的每个link单体重生成并保留与观测更一致者。

Result: 在Articulate-100及新基准HArt（60个PartNet-Mobility物体+8个机械手/夹爪的RGB-D视频，含真值部件与关节）上，物体上的轴误差与部件运动误差较最强基线降低一半以上，机械手上的轴误差亦降低；并可在真实录像与生成视频上运行。

Conclusion: 以"生成+观测验证"驱动的流水线可从单视角RGB-D视频恢复结构化铰接模型，具备跨类别（物体与机械手）的通用性。

Abstract: An RGB-D camera that films an object being moved, often from one viewpoint, sees one side of it and how its parts move. We present ArtKit, which takes such a video as input to create an articulated model with complete parts, a kinematic tree, and typed joints. Our central idea is generating the hidden geometry and verifying it against what the camera sees. A generative part prior proposes complete, part-decomposed geometry based on the first frame. Point tracks, grouped into rigid trajectories, split and merge the generated parts into links. We then fit revolute and prismatic joints, with a visibility-aware energy ensuring that the articulated model explains the depth observed across the video. A rule checks against the video to choose between synchronous group fits and per-frame chain fits, so that one pipeline serves a diverse range of articulated objects, such as household items and robotic hands. Finally, we regenerate each link of the chosen fit as one part and keep it wherever it agrees better with the observations. We evaluate on Articulate-100 and on HArt, a new benchmark of 60 PartNet-Mobility objects and 8 robotic hands and grippers rendered as RGB-D videos with ground-truth parts and joints. ArtKit reduces the axis and part motion errors of the strongest baseline by more than half on the objects, as well as the axis error on the hands. ArtKit also runs on real recordings and generated videos.

</details>


### [51] [BreathSplat: Grounding One Smooth Motion in the Breathing Phase for 4D Cone-Beam CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33392)
*Sujong Shin,Sang-Il Choi,Kwang Moo Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BreathSplat 用呼吸相位驱动的 B 样条动态高斯表示 4D-CBCT，跨呼吸周期共享同相位投影，在仿真与真实病人扫描上重建与运动精度优于现有动态方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D-CBCT 每个呼吸相位仅有少量角度投影，重建严重欠定；呼吸是平滑且周期重复的运动并具有相位，但现有方法要么按相位分箱重建解剖、要么在时间上估计平滑动态运动，均未将平滑运动锚定于呼吸相位，因而无法充分利用不同呼吸周期中同相位投影的互补信息。

Method: 将 4D 体数据表示为动态高斯，其运动在空间与时间上均由 B 样条参数化；把运动状态锚定到从成熟常规替代信号（surrogate）中读取的呼吸相位，使不同呼吸周期中相同相位的投影约束同一解剖与同一运动状态，从而实现跨相位投影共享。

Result: 在仿真肺部扫描上，BreathSplat 的重建精度与运动估计精度均优于近期动态方法，包括被提供真值相位的方法；在两个真实病人扫描上的定性结果表明其重建比当前最优方法更清晰。

Conclusion: 以呼吸相位为锚、用时空 B 样条驱动的动态高斯表示可有效共享跨周期同相位投影，为稀疏视角 4D-CBCT 提供高保真重建与运动估计；作者以 BreathSplat 命名该方法，并展示其对真实临床数据的适用性。

Abstract: Four-dimensional cone-beam computed tomography (4D-CBCT) images the anatomy of a breathing patient, but sees each breathing phase from only a few angles. Breathing, however, is a smooth motion that repeats; it has a phase. Existing methods exploit this either by reconstructing the anatomy as a function of the breathing phase, often through bins, or by estimating smooth dynamic motions in time. Yet none of these grounds one smooth motion in the phase. We thus propose to share projections across breathing phases with splines. Specifically, we represent the 4D volume with dynamic Gaussians whose motion uses B-splines in space and time. We ground the motion state in the respiratory phase, which we read from well-studied conventional surrogates. Projections at the same phase in different breathing cycles then constrain the same anatomy and motion. On simulated lung scans, our method, BreathSplat, outperforms recent dynamic methods in reconstruction and motion accuracy, including those given the ground-truth phase. We further show qualitatively, on two real patient scans, that our method provides clearer reconstructions than the state of the art.

</details>


### [52] [VersionGS: Compact and Motion-Robust Dynamic Gaussian Splatting via Versioned Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33374)
*Shihang Wei,Yichi Zhang,Fengqing Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VersionGS 直接编码逐帧高斯状态，用运动估计仅作引导，通过版本化字典与选择性顺序构建实现高动态场景高效重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态高斯溅射方法将时间变化编码为时间平滑函数或帧间约束更新，在快速运动（如舞蹈序列）下重建质量显著下降，高运动帧 PSNR 比低运动帧低 1.1–4.0 dB。

Method: 直接编码每帧高斯状态而非拟合运动模型。每帧组内，未变化高斯在共享字典中保留当前版本，变化高斯追加为新版本；提出选择性顺序构建，依据光流、光度差异或渲染误差筛选需精修的高斯，其余保持不变以最大化版本复用；字典经剪枝、模拟量化微调后编码为图像存储；调整剪枝率可在不重建字典的前提下权衡存储与质量。

Result: 在 DanceNet3D 快速舞蹈序列上前景 PSNR 较最佳基线提升 2.7 dB，最高运动帧 PSNR 下降不足 0.3 dB；在 N3DV 与 Technicolor 上，较最强基线 TAD-GS 存储分别减少 6.0x 与 5.7x，且 PSNR 更高；播放无需运动模型或神经网络，仅需 3DGS 光栅化器。

Conclusion: 以版本化显式高斯状态替代时间函数建模，可有效缓解快速运动下的质量退化，并在存储效率与渲染速度上取得优势。

Abstract: Recent dynamic Gaussian splatting methods encode temporal change, either as smooth functions of time or as constrained updates between frames. We observe that their reconstruction quality degrades significantly under fast motion. In challenging dance sequences, their PSNR drops by 1.1-4.0 dB on high-motion frames compared to low-motion frames. To overcome this limitation, we propose VersionGS, a method that directly encodes per-frame Gaussian states and uses motion estimation only to guide reconstruction. Within each group of frames, unchanged Gaussians keep their current versions in a shared dictionary, while modified Gaussians are appended as new versions. To maximize version reuse, we introduce selective sequential construction, which refines the Gaussians selected by optical flow, photograph differences or rendering errors and leaves the others unchanged. The dictionary is then pruned, fine-tuned with simulated quantization and encoded as images. Adjusting the pruning ratio trades off storage and quality without rebuilding the dictionary. On the fast dance sequences of DanceNet3D, VersionGS improves foreground PSNR by 2.7 dB over the best baseline, and its PSNR decreases by less than 0.3 dB on the highest-motion frames. On N3DV and Technicolor, it requires 6.0x and 5.7x less storage than TAD-GS, the strongest baseline overall, while achieving even higher PSNR. Finally, playback is fast and requires no motion model or neural network, only a 3DGS rasterizer.

</details>


### [53] [LGCount: Layout-Guided Diffusion for Zero-Shot Object Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33344)
*Adriano D'Alessandro,Ali Mahdavi-Amiri,Ghassan Hamarneh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出以结构化文本布局作为计数规格，并用单步扩散计数器将其转换为密度图，通过合成反事实训练使模型必须利用布局信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类别无关计数通常仅依赖类别名或少量标注框，规格信息贫弱，难以区分目标、所在区域与干扰物；同时现有计数数据集每图仅对应唯一密度图，导致模型学会忽略文本布局。

Method: 由prompt upsampler基于图像与目标类别生成结构化布局（目标、所在区域、需忽略的干扰物），再用图像编辑扩散模型改造为单步计数器输出密度图；并在合成反事实数据上训练（同一图像对应多个有效密度图），迫使模型依赖布局。

Result: 在FSC-147上超越此前零样本SOTA，在LOOKALIKES上取得已报告最佳结果，且对每张图像提供的先验信息最少。

Conclusion: 无需重训练即可按类别、区域或粒度对单张图像计数，验证了布局条件化与单步扩散计数的有效性。

Abstract: Class-agnostic counting requires the user to specify what to count, usually with a class name or a few annotated boxes. We propose a richer text-based specification: a structured layout that names the target, the region that holds it, the distractors to ignore. A prompt upsampler writes this layout from the image and the requested category alone, and an image-editing diffusion model, adapted into a single-step counter, turns it into a density map. Because counting datasets pair each image with a single valid density map, a counter trained on it learns to ignore the layout. We therefore train on synthetic counterfactuals in which one image admits several valid density maps, so that the layout cannot be ignored. On FSC-147 we improve on the previous zero-shot state of the art and we set the best reported result on LOOKALIKES while being told the least about each image. A single photograph can be counted by category, by region, or by granularity without retraining.

</details>


### [54] [Do Not Let the Prior Heal the Anomaly: Selective Anchoring for Detection from Incomplete Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33331)
*Nicolas Tricard,Stefan Brandstätter,Jason Chen,Zituo Chen,Linzheng Wang,Georg Langs,Sili Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出\"选择性锚定\"（selective anchoring），保护测量中可靠测得的图像分量不被生成先验改写，仅让先验重建未解析结构，从而在保留病灶对比度的同时获得可校准的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式先验能从稀疏/欠采样医学测量中恢复解剖细节，但可能\"抹去\"恰恰需要被检出的病灶——即使测量数据本身含有病灶证据。需要一种机制在利用生成先验表达力的同时避免病灶被\"修复掉\"。

Method: 对可靠测得的分量施加锚定约束，禁止先验驱动修改；其采集不确定性以两种方式传播：确定性方式直接加入下游检验，或随机扰动读数由集成样本承载。实现为预训练扩散/流采样器的包装器，以及快速约束流模型 AnchoredFM。在线性-高斯测量模型下给出理论分析。

Result: 线性-高斯模型下，受保护系数期望无偏且达到名义区间覆盖率，且与学习到的先验无关。在稀疏视角CT、加速脑MRI、光声断层成像上，病灶对比度保持与不确定性可靠性均提升，病灶内重建误差可降低。加速MRI肿瘤检测（以仅由测量估计的健康参考）：确定性锚定使8个采样器平均AUROC从78.4%升至84.6%，每层0.25假阳性标记处病灶敏感度从38.8%升至47.5%，四个预设肿瘤大小组均提升；在健康验证数据上冻结阈值时，平均敏感度从20.2%升至44.1%（随机锚定为37.6%）。

Conclusion: 表达力强的生成先验可以在不\"治愈\"测量所支持的异常的前提下重建未解析解剖结构；选择性锚定是兼顾重建质量、病灶保留与不确定性可靠性的通用且高效方案。

Abstract: Generative priors can recover anatomical detail from incomplete medical measurements, but may erase the very lesions that imaging aims to detect, even when the measurements contain evidence of them. We introduce selective anchoring that protects reliably measured image components from prior-driven changes, while allowing expressive generative models to reconstruct unresolved structure. Acquisition uncertainty in the protected components is either added deterministically to the downstream test or carried by the ensemble through stochastic perturbations of the readings. We implement this principle as a wrapper for pretrained diffusion and flow samplers and as AnchoredFM, a fast constrained flow model. Under a linear-Gaussian measurement model, the protected coefficients are unbiased in expectation and achieve nominal interval coverage across repeated acquisitions, independently of the learned prior. Across sparse-view CT, accelerated brain MRI, and photoacoustic tomography, selective anchoring improves lesion contrast preservation and uncertainty reliability, and can reduce reconstruction error within lesions. For tumor detection in accelerated MRI, with a healthy reference estimated from the measurements alone, deterministic anchoring raises mean AUROC across eight samplers from 78.4% to 84.6% and lesion sensitivity at 0.25 false-positive marks per slice from 38.8% to 47.5%, with gains in all four predefined tumor-size groups. At thresholds frozen on healthy validation data, mean sensitivity rises from 20.2% to 44.1% (37.6% with stochastic anchoring). These findings show how expressive generative priors can reconstruct unresolved anatomy without healing away the abnormalities that the measurements support.

</details>


### [55] [ScaleCurve: From Constant Scale to Consistent Motion for World-Space Human Mesh Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34005)
*Sanghyeon Lee,Jong Taek Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ScaleCurve：利用人体-地面几何估计随时间变化的度量校正，解决世界坐标系人体网格恢复中的局部尺度畸变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界坐标人体网格恢复即便相机坐标下的人体估计准确，也可能失去空间一致性。单一序列全局尺度只能解决全局尺度歧义，无法修正重建相机轨迹上的局部尺度畸变。

Method: 通过比较预测的足部几何与局部拟合的地面平面构造尺度观测；联合时序优化对不一致观测降权并耦合相邻尺度估计，使标定沿相机路径自适应而不追随测量噪声；随后缩放相机平移增量并积分得到标定轨迹；固定相机后利用2D图像证据与预训练3D运动先验精化提升后的人体运动，同时限制全局轨迹重缩放。

Result: 在EMDB-2与SLOPER4D上多数指标达到SOTA；在三种HMR骨干上均取得一致增益，无需重训练即可兼容不同人体估计。

Conclusion: 基于人体-地面几何的时序尺度校正可有效缓解世界空间局部尺度畸变，且具备良好的骨干通用性。

Abstract: World-space human mesh recovery can lose spatial consistency even when camera-space body estimates are accurate. A single sequence-wide scale resolves global scale ambiguity but cannot correct local scale distortion along a reconstructed camera path. We present ScaleCurve, a framework that estimates time-varying metric corrections from human-ground geometry. We construct scale observations by comparing predicted foot geometry with locally fitted ground planes. Joint temporal optimization downweights inconsistent observations and couples neighboring scale estimates, allowing calibration to adapt along the camera path without freely following measurement noise. We scale camera translation increments and integrate them into a calibrated trajectory. With this camera fixed, we refine the lifted body motion using 2D image evidence and pretrained 3D dynamics, while restricting global trajectory rescaling. ScaleCurve achieves state-of-the-art performance on most evaluated metrics on both EMDB-2 and SLOPER4D. Consistent gains across three HMR backbones demonstrate its compatibility with diverse body estimates without retraining. Code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [56] [A Computational Model of Continuous Recognition Memory for Natural Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33947)
*Sheng Zhao,Yijin Wang,Bei Xiao,Yuhao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个连续再认记忆（CRM）的计算模型，统一刻画图像编码、记忆衰退与决策三要素，仅需极少参数即可逐试次预测人类再认行为并准确估计图像可记忆性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视觉记忆中的连续再认是基本功能；理解CRM既有助于机器视觉系统预测并生成更易被记住的图像，也可用于记忆障碍诊断与记忆干预开发。但此前缺乏针对CRM的计算模型。

Method: 模型包含三个组件：图像编码、记忆衰退、决策。每张图像被编码为感知空间中的一个独立随机变量；随记忆衰退，该随机变量的分布在该空间内发生漂移；判断当前图像是否见过时，观察者从先前编码图像的分布中采样并与当前图像比较。模型参数极简：4个共享参数＋每位观察者2个参数。

Result: 模型能在人类被试间观察到的变异范围内逐试次预测CRM任务反应；对图像可记忆性的预测精度不劣于需数百万参数的既有方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为首个CRM计算模型，以极高参数效率同时实现行为级逐试次预测与图像级可记忆性预测，为记忆机制建模、机器视觉可记忆性估计及记忆相关临床应用提供统一框架。

Abstract: Recognizing an image we have seen before is a basic function of human visual memory. Understanding human continuous recognition memory (CRM) can help machine vision systems predict which images people are likely to remember and generate more memorable images. It can also inform the diagnosis of memory impairments and the development of interventions to improve memory. This paper proposes the first computational model for CRM. The model captures three key components of continuous recognition memory (CRM): image encoding, memory decay, and decision making. It encodes each image as a distinct random variable in a perceptual space. As the memory of an image fades, the distribution of its random variable drifts within that space. To decide whether the current image has been seen before, the observer retrieves samples from the distributions associated with previously encoded images and compares them with the current image. Our model predicts human observers' trial-by-trial responses in CRM tasks within the variability observed across human participants. Our model is parameter efficient, requiring only four shared parameters and two parameters per observer. It can accurately predict the memorability of an image no worse than prior methods that require millions of parameters.

</details>


### [57] [Event-WAM: Learning to Act with Event-Derived Futures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33917)
*Jongseong Bae,Hyunwoo Kim,Yeongin Lee,Eubin So,Ha Young Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Event-WAM，将事件视觉流导出的视觉变化作为 RGB 之外的辅助未来监督，用于世界动作模型的动作学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）多以 RGB 未来预测作为动作学习目标，但仅依赖外观重建不足以刻画与动作相关的动态变化；作者希望引入"变化导向"的未来监督，并避免事件相机数据采集与推理时生成未来的额外代价。

Method: 从 RGB 演示中构造时间结构化的事件表示，并用冻结的预训练 RGB 视频 VAE 编码（不训练事件专用 VAE）；采用 RGB–Event 联合隐空间流匹配（joint latent flow matching），同时提供外观导向与变化导向的监督；推理阶段不需事件观测，也不做显式未来生成。

Result: 仿真基准上标准条件下性能与基线相当；扰动条件下的鲁棒性提升依赖基准，其中在 LIBERO-Plus 上有改进。真实世界实验中，标准与未见扰动条件下操作成功率均提升。分析显示策略改进并不总是伴随更高的未来预测保真度。

Conclusion: 变化导向的监督（事件导出的视觉变化）可作为有效的辅助目标提升操作策略鲁棒性，且其收益不依赖未来预测精度的提升，提示该监督对动作学习具有独立价值。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) leverage future visual modeling for robotic action learning, with recent studies exploring alternative or complementary objectives beyond conventional RGB prediction. We introduce Event-WAM, which incorporates event-derived visual changes as auxiliary future supervision alongside RGB. Event-WAM constructs temporally structured event representations from RGB demonstrations and encodes them using a frozen pretrained RGB video VAE, without training an event-specific VAE. Joint RGB--Event latent flow matching provides appearance- and change-oriented supervision without requiring event observations or explicit future generation at inference. Across simulated benchmarks, Event-WAM maintains comparable performance under standard conditions, while robustness gains under perturbations are benchmark-dependent, including improvements on LIBERO-Plus. Real-world evaluations further demonstrate improved manipulation success under both standard and unseen perturbation conditions. Further analyses show that policy improvements are not consistently accompanied by higher future prediction fidelity, suggesting the potential of change-oriented supervision for action learning.

</details>


### [58] [X-Matte: VAE-Free Pixel-Space Flow Matching for Single-Step Image Matting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33874)
*Zanyi Wang,Jiaang Li,Dengyang Jiang,Ziru Wang,Chengzu Li,Davit Abrahamyan,Harry Yang,Serge Belongie,Mengmeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: X-Matte：无VAE的像素空间flow matching单步抠图框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练生成模型为抠图提供丰富视觉先验，但适配时依赖VAE，其空间压缩会损害精细alpha细节并带来额外推理开销。

Method: 在像素空间直接建模RGB到alpha的连续流，去除潜空间压缩；采用轨迹级x-prediction与转移感知alpha监督，从中间状态学习并单次前向预测目标alpha。

Result: P3M基准上与SOTA无trimap抠图方法竞争力相当，零样本泛化到RWP-636与AIM-500表现良好；高分辨率下较构造的单步潜空间对照最高9.4倍推理加速。

Conclusion: 无VAE的像素空间建模可实现准确且高效的图像抠图。

Abstract: Pre-trained generative models offer rich visual priors for image matting, but their adaptation often relies on variational autoencoders (VAEs), whose spatial compression can degrade fine alpha details and introduce additional inference overhead. To address these limitations, we propose X-Matte, a VAE-free pixel-space flow matching framework for single-step image matting. By eliminating latent compression, X-Matte formulates the RGB-to-alpha transition as a continuous flow directly in pixel space. We adopt trajectory-wide x-prediction with transition-aware alpha supervision, enabling the model to learn from intermediate states while directly predicting the target alpha matte in a single forward pass. This design avoids both VAE encoding and decoding and iterative sampling. Extensive experiments demonstrate performance competitive with state-of-the-art trimap-free matting methods on P3M benchmarks, together with promising zero-shot generalization to RWP-636 and AIM-500. Moreover, X-Matte achieves up to a 9.4x inference speedup over a constructed single-step latent counterpart at high resolution, demonstrating the effectiveness of VAE-free pixel-space modeling for accurate and efficient image matting.

</details>


### [59] [OmniMeld: Enhancing Audio-Visual Understanding via Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33861)
*Daili Hua,Yifan Dai,Bohan Zeng,Yuran Wang,Zihao Wu,Chengzhuo Tong,Guoqing Jiang,Liwei Chen,Zhiheng Qin,Xuanping Li,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniMeld：基于模态-响应引导分布匹配的多教师on-policy蒸馏框架，用最优传输对齐学生与教师在完整音视频输入下的预测，提升音视频理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音视频理解需感知接地、时序理解与语义推理三类能力；多教师蒸馏可整合这些专长，但直接概率匹配无法显式刻画教师预测在音视频条件变化下的动态差异。

Method: 训练四个分别覆盖接地与对应关系、时序动态与事件结构、因果推理、上下文整体理解的教师模型；以学生生成的答案前缀为条件，将样本路由到对应教师监督；基于教师预测在共享候选token上随音视频条件变化的差异构造传输代价，用最优传输将学生预测对齐到完整音视频输入下教师的预测分布。同时构建OmniMeld-20K数据集（四类能力域，响应分为证据/推理/答案三段，经一致性检查与媒体复核）。

Result: 教师在同类规模开源模型中表现具竞争力；学生在所有评测基准上优于其基座模型，并在若干基准上超过教师的基座模型。

Conclusion: 以模态响应变化为代价信号的最优传输式on-policy多教师蒸馏，可有效融合多域专长并超越基座模型，为音视频理解提供可扩展的能力整合路径。

Abstract: Audio-visual understanding requires perceptual grounding, temporal understanding, and semantic inference. Multi-teacher distillation can consolidate these capabilities, but direct probability matching does not explicitly capture teacher prediction changes across audio-visual conditions. We introduce OmniMeld, a multi-teacher on-policy distillation method that integrates domain expertise through modality-response-guided distribution matching. We train four teachers covering grounding and correspondence, temporal dynamics and event structure, causal and inferential reasoning, and contextual and holistic understanding. Each sample is routed to its corresponding teacher for supervision conditioned on student-generated answer prefixes. We derive transport costs from teacher prediction changes over shared candidate tokens across audio-visual conditions, using optimal transport to match student predictions to those of the teacher under complete audio-visual input. We also construct OmniMeld-20K for domain-specialized teacher training. The dataset spans four capability domains, with responses structured into evidence, reasoning, and answer sections and verified through consistency checks and media-based review. Experiments on audio-visual and general video understanding benchmarks demonstrate competitive teacher performance among similarly sized open-source models. The student improves over its base model across all evaluated benchmarks and surpasses the teachers' base model on several benchmarks.

</details>


### [60] [Structured Noise Diffusion for Echocardiography Haze Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33860)
*Zike Luo,Hao Li,Mengxiao Wang,Qingya Li,Ching-Hui Sia,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出结构感知扩散框架，将超声雾霾建模为受扇形几何约束的局部高斯成分累积，用双头网络预测锚点概率图与高斯尺度参数实现渐进去雾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声心动图图像受空间结构化的雾霾伪影影响，遮蔽解剖边界、阻碍临床分析；传统扩散模型的像素独立高斯噪声无法刻画局部化的超声雾霾。

Method: 以前向过程采样高斯成分的位置与尺度，生成空间相关的退化以反映真实回声伪影分布；反向恢复阶段采用双头网络分别预测锚点概率图和高斯尺度参数，逐步去除结构化伪影，并以概率图替代直接坐标回归以缓解中心偏置。

Result: 在公开基准DehazingEcho2025及两个私有临床数据集上（私有集均未重训练或微调）评测，三数据集平均广义对比噪声比（GCNR）均为最高，公开基准达0.715±0.090，且能保留组织边缘。

Conclusion: 结构感知的雾霾建模与双头概率化预测可有效去除超声结构化伪影，并具备跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Echocardiographic images are frequently degraded by spatially structured haze artifacts that obscure anatomical boundaries and hinder clinical analysis. Conventional diffusion models typically employ pixel-independent Gaussian noise, limiting their ability to represent localized ultrasound haze. In this study, we propose a structure-informed diffusion framework that explicitly models ultrasound haze as an accumulation of localized Gaussian components constrained by the ultrasound fan geometry. The forward process generates spatially correlated corruption by sampling component locations and scales, providing a formulation that better reflects the spatial characteristics of real echo artifacts. During reverse restoration, a dual-head network predicts an anchor probability map and Gaussian scale parameters to progressively remove structured artifacts while reducing the center bias associated with direct coordinate regression. We evaluated the framework on the public DehazingEcho2025 benchmark and two private clinical datasets, without retraining or fine-tuning on either private cohort. Our method achieved the highest mean generalized contrast-to-noise ratio across all three datasets, reaching $0.715 \pm 0.090$ on the public benchmark while preserving tissue edges.

</details>


### [61] [MMotion: Collaborative Cross-Scale Memory for Text-to-Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33819)
*Zhaoxin Fan,Qingchen Yu,Kejian Wu,Jian Yang,Zeming Liu,wenjun wu,Lili Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MMotion——协作式多尺度记忆框架，将尺度条件语义检索、潜动力学建模与因果工作记忆统一起来，以决定生成何种动作、如何演化并保持历史一致，架构无关，可嵌入扩散与自回归模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到动作生成方法或依赖动作样例检索，或保留长程上下文，但很少利用记忆联合推理语义、动力学与生成历史，导致三者割裂。

Method: 设计三类记忆：语义记忆在不同生成尺度提供动作先验；动力学记忆建模潜状态转移；工作记忆保存已生成动作的因果上下文；并通过协作融合机制整合互补记忆。框架与具体架构无关，可接入扩散模型和下一尺度自回归模型。

Result: 在标准基准与多种骨干网络上进行大量实验，相较强基线取得一致性提升。

Conclusion: 多尺度记忆的协同利用能有效提升文本到动作生成的语义对齐、动态合理性与历史一致性，且具备良好的通用性与可移植性。

Abstract: Text-to-motion generation aims to synthesize realistic human motions from natural-language descriptions, enabling applications in animation, virtual avatars, and embodied agents. Despite recent progress, existing methods either retrieve motion exemplars or retain long-range context, but rarely exploit memory to jointly reason about semantics, dynamics, and generation history. In this paper, we propose MMotion, a collaborative multi-scale memory framework that unifies scale-conditioned semantic retrieval, latent dynamics modeling, and causal working memory. Specifically, semantic memory provides motion priors at different generation scales, dynamics memory captures latent state transitions, and working memory preserves causal context from previously generated motions. A collaborative fusion mechanism integrates these complementary memories to determine what motion to generate, how it should evolve, and how it should remain consistent with its history. MMotion is architecture-agnostic and can be readily integrated into both diffusion-based and next scale autoregressive models. Extensive experiments across standard benchmarks and diverse backbones demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines.

</details>


### [62] [Spectral Colorimetry Based on GMoE for Temperature Measurement in Explosion Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33750)
*Jie Fang,Wenhao Li,Jianning Yang,Nan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出门控混合专家(GMoE)框架，将物理先验与深度学习分治策略结合，用于爆炸场光谱测温。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 爆炸场光谱测温可避免接触式方法的干扰并解决高温测量难题，但物理模型驱动方法仅回归单一辐射比，映射随温度变化导致宽温域适应性差；数据驱动方法受限于训练数据稀缺。

Method: 每个专家用物理标定的多项式函数建模特定谱段对下灰度比与辐射比的关系，再由含相应参数的物理模型估计温度；门控网络自适应加权各专家输出生成最终温度。

Result: 基于Transformer门控网络表现最佳，平均RMSE 12.70±1.32 K，MAE 7.96±0.86 K；较传统光谱比色法RMSE和MAE分别降低63.4%和70.8%。

Conclusion: 物理先验+分治式混合专家可有效缓解宽温域适应性与数据稀缺问题，显著优于传统光谱比色法。

Abstract: Spectroscopic thermometry in explosion fields has attracted considerable attention in related engineering domains, primarily because it can mitigate the interference of traditional contact methods with temperature distributions and address the limitations of high-temperature measurement scenarios. However, existing physics-model-driven methods typically rely on regressing a single radiance-ratio pair, which limits their adaptability to wide-range temperature estimation. This limitation arises from notable variations in the mapping between grayscale ratios and radiance ratios at different temperatures. In contrast, existing data-driven methods often suffer from limited performance due to the scarcity of training data, a challenge exacerbated by the sensitivity of explosion fields. In this work, we propose a gated mixture of experts (GMoE) framework that integrates a divide-and-conquer strategy in deep learning with physical prior knowledge for temperature estimation, thereby decomposing the complex task into multiple expert-specific subtasks. Specifically, each expert is developed using physically calibrated polynomial functions to model the relationship between grayscale ratios and radiance ratios for a given pair of spectral bands, with temperature then estimated using a physical model incorporating the corresponding parameters. Additionally, a gating network is designed to adaptively weight the outputs of different experts and generate the final temperature estimate. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves the best performance when implemented with the Transformer-based gating network, yielding a mean RMSE of $12.70 \pm 1.32$ K and a mean MAE of $7.96 \pm 0.86$ K. Compared with conventional spectral colorimetry, the proposed framework reduces the mean RMSE and MAE by 63.4\% and 70.8\%, respectively.

</details>


### [63] [VTK-Bench: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Distinguishing Language Priors and Text Modal Bias in Visual Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33725)
*Yao Lu,Yuhang Zheng,Yaxin Gao,Zhe Tang,Zhaowei Zhu,Jiaheng Wei,Yunhao Wang,Shanqing Yu,Qi Xuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VTK-Bench基准，通过显式分离视觉(V)、文本(T)、先验知识(K)三种信息源并设置互斥答案，实现多模态幻觉失败模式的细粒度归因，并引入离散归因与软归因指标刻画模型的信息源偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型幻觉基准多聚焦于识别幻觉及其表现形式，忽视其成因。多模态幻觉可源于两类不同失效模式：文本模态偏见（TMB，过度依赖误导性文本输入）与语言先验（LP，依赖已学知识），二者缺乏可区分的评测手段。

Method: 构建VTK-Bench，显式分离视觉信息(V)、文本信息(T)与先验知识(K)三类信息源，为V/T/K设置互斥答案，从而对模型回答进行细粒度归因分析；同时提出离散归因指标与软归因指标以刻画信息源偏好。

Result: 在11个代表性开源VLM上的实验表明：多数模型中LP比TMB更普遍；TMB随模型规模扩大显著下降，而LP改善有限并始终是主导失效模式。

Conclusion: 区分信息源偏好对深入理解多模态幻觉至关重要，且缓解语言先验导致的幻觉仍是关键挑战。

Abstract: Visual language models (VLMs) have demonstrated exceptional capabilities across various multimodal tasks, but they remain susceptible to hallucinations, undermining their reliability. Existing hallucination benchmarks primarily focus on identifying hallucinations and describing their manifestations, often neglecting their underlying causes. Specifically, multimodal hallucinations can stem from two distinct failure modes: text modality bias (TMB), reflecting an over-reliance on misleading text input; and language prior (LP), reflecting a reliance on previously learned knowledge. To differentiate these failure modes, we propose VTK-Bench, a benchmark that explicitly separates three information sources: visual information (V), textual information (T), and prior knowledge (K). By establishing mutually exclusive answers corresponding to V, T, and K, VTK-Bench enables fine-grained attribution analysis of model responses. Furthermore, we introduce discrete attribution and soft attribution metrics to characterize information source preferences. Extensive experiments on 11 representative open-source VLMs show that LP is more prevalent than TMB in most evaluated models. Notably, TMB decreases substantially with model scaling, whereas LP exhibits relatively limited improvement and remains the dominant failure mode. These findings highlight the importance of different information source preferences for a deeper understanding of multimodal hallucinations.

</details>


### [64] [Efficient Scene-Distribution-Aware Second-Order Optimization for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33698)
*Hyunseung Son,Young Sun Choi,Youngsik Yun,Kibeom Hong,Youngjung Uh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向3DGS的二阶优化加速方法：通过考虑视图重叠的视图选择策略降低冗余，并用多视图采样像素联合处理与透射率/累积颜色复用两项实现策略减少开销，在三数据集上以不到一半时间超过采样式二阶基线的PSNR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS逐视图更新需数万次迭代；多视图批处理可降低对单一视图依赖，二阶优化可用更大结构化步长减少更新次数。但更新次数越少，修正"仅拟合本次所用视图"的偏差的机会越少，单次更新的场景覆盖度更关键。而多视图二阶更新代价高，像素采样虽可降本，仍存在两个问题：视图选择未考虑重叠导致冗余；现有方法对每个视图的采样像素分趟处理并从头重算alpha合成。

Method: 1) 视图选择策略：在选视图时考虑视图间重叠，减少冗余视图；2) 两项实现策略：将多视图采样像素在联合遍历（joint pass）中一并处理，并复用渲染过程中已缓存的透射率（transmittance）与累积颜色，避免从头重算alpha合成。

Result: 在三个数据集上，相比采样式二阶基线，方法在不到一半优化时间内达到更高PSNR，SSIM与LPIPS具竞争力。

Conclusion: 多视图二阶优化结合重叠感知视图选择与像素处理/缓存复用，可在显著缩短优化时间的同时提升重建质量，验证了"联合处理+中间量复用"对降低二阶3DGS训练开销的有效性。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) optimization updates the Gaussian parameters one training view at a time, tens of thousands of times. Unlike such single-view updates, multi-view batches reduce dependence on individual training views, and second-order optimization reduces the number of updates with larger, structured parameter changes. Because fewer updates leave fewer chances to correct a change that fits only the views used in one update, scene coverage becomes more important for each second-order update. Multi-view second-order updates, however, are expensive. Pixel sampling reduces this cost, but two issues remain. First, selecting views without considering overlap leaves some views redundant. To address this, we propose a view selection strategy that reduces this redundancy. Second, existing work processes the sampled pixels of each view in a separate pass and recomputes alpha compositing for these pixels from scratch. We introduce two implementation strategies that reduce both overheads by processing the sampled pixels of multiple views in joint passes and reusing the transmittance and accumulated color cached during rendering. On three datasets, our method reaches higher PSNR than the sampled second-order baseline in less than half its optimization time, with competitive SSIM and LPIPS.

</details>


### [65] [CariesBench: A Hierarchical Multi-view Benchmark for Dental Caries Screening and Diagnostic Reasoning from 3D Intraoral Scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33643)
*Xuefen Liu,Yanxiong,Xinquan Yang,Fangqian Zhang,Qinyi Lu,Yuning Zhang,Xianxu Hou,Shinan Zhang,Wen Ma,Linlin Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CariesBench——基于5,932例3D口内扫描构建的层次化龋齿评估基准，包含85,455张标准化多视角渲染图与133,994条专家质控VQA实例，覆盖牙弓级筛查、牙位级诊断推理与结构化临床决策三类任务，并据此微调Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct得到IntraoralGPT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 龋齿评估是结构化临床流程，需病灶定位、患牙与牙面识别及严重程度分级，而非图像级识别；3D IOS提供几何与表面形态信息并支持多视角渲染，因而要求解剖学基础的多视角推理。现有MLLM与牙科视觉语言基准均未同时评估"牙弓—牙位—牙面"层次推理、跨视角证据整合与牙面级龋损评估。

Method: 构建CariesBench基准：5,932例3D IOS生成85,455张标准化多视角渲染图，经专家质控标注133,994条临床相关VQA实例；任务分三类——牙弓级临床筛查(ACS)、牙位级诊断推理(TDR)、结构化临床决策(SCD)。使用其训练集微调Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct，得到IntraoralGPT。

Result: 对24个代表性基线MLLM的评测显示，牙弓级筛查与牙位级诊断推理间存在显著性能落差，ACS准确率高于TDR。CariesBench专用监督使Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct基线的19任务宏平均临床准确率由52.7%提升至69.5%，TDR Overall由34.4%提升至44.7%。

Conclusion: 现有MLLM在细粒度牙位级诊断推理上明显弱于粗粒度筛查，层次化、跨视角的基准监督可有效提升其临床评估能力，但仍存在较大提升空间。

Abstract: Dental caries assessment is a structured clinical process requiring lesion localization, identification of the affected tooth and surface, and severity grading rather than simple image-level recognition. 3D intraoral scans (IOS) preserve detailed dental geometry and surface morphology and support standardized rendering from complementary viewpoints, enabling fine-grained visual assessment while introducing a distinct need for anatomically grounded multi-view reasoning. Despite recent advances in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and dental vision-language benchmarks, existing evaluations do not jointly assess hierarchical arch-to-tooth-to-surface reasoning, cross-view evidence integration, and surface-level caries assessment. To address this gap, we introduce CariesBench, a hierarchical benchmark constructed from 5,932 3D IOS, comprising 85,455 standardized multi-view renderings and 133,994 clinically grounded visual question answering instances with expert quality control. CariesBench comprises three complementary task families: Arch-level Clinical Screening (ACS), Tooth-level Diagnostic Reasoning (TDR), and Structured Clinical Decision-Making (SCD). Using the CariesBench training set, we further fine-tune Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct to obtain IntraoralGPT. Extensive evaluation of 24 representative baseline MLLMs reveals a substantial benchmark-level performance disparity between arch-level screening and tooth-level diagnostic reasoning, with higher accuracy on ACS than on TDR. CariesBench-specific supervision substantially improves the Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct baseline, increasing the 19-task macro-average clinical accuracy from 52.7% to 69.5% and TDR Overall from 34.4% to 44.7%.

</details>


### [66] [Separating Environment and Sensor Shift from Acquisition Protocol in Physical Vision Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33507)
*Yuyang Yuan,Jason Wu,Mohammadali Khodabandehlou,Jianpeng Yao,Hang Qiu,Mani Srivastava*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文检验物理基准 ImageNet-ES-Diverse 中采集协议（印刷图像尺度、相机变焦）对自适应曝光控制策略 Lens 评估结论的影响，发现采集选择显著改变基准难度，且 Lens 相对自动曝光的精度优势在多套采集流程与真实物体预实验中均不复现，而 oracle 曝光选择仍有显著收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实部署中视觉系统需应对光照变化等环境条件，已有工作提出按环境和下游任务自适应选择相机参数的策略（如 Lens 的自适应曝光控制）。评估此类策略依赖可靠的物理基准，但基准的采集协议本身是否影响结论尚未被系统考察，因此需要研究采集选择对基准难度与策略收益评估的影响。

Method: 以物理基准 ImageNet-ES-Diverse 为对象，在不同光照条件下重新拍摄印刷的 ImageNet 图像。构建四条受控采集流程，系统改变印刷图像尺度与相机变焦；并开展针对直接拍摄真实物体的预实验进行对照。在多个下游基线模型上比较分类精度，比较 Lens、自动曝光（auto exposure）与 oracle 曝光选择的表现。

Result: 采集选择在所有下游基线上均显著改变基准难度。原基准上 Lens 相对 auto exposure 的平均分类精度优势，在自建的各采集流程及真实物体预实验中均未能保持。相比之下，oracle 曝光选择仍保留显著收益，说明传感器控制仍有潜力。

Conclusion: 采集协议是物理基准评估中的重要因素。评估自适应感知收益时应明确报告采集协议，并在多种采集设置下进行评测，以避免结论对特定采集配置的过度依赖。

Abstract: In real-world deployments, vision systems must operate under varying environmental conditions, such as illumination changes. Recent work has explored adaptive sensing strategies that select camera parameters according to the current environment and downstream task to improve task performance. Reliable physical benchmarks are therefore essential for evaluating such strategies. We study ImageNet-ES-Diverse, a physical benchmark that evaluates the adaptive exposure control strategy Lens by recapturing printed ImageNet images under varying illumination conditions. We construct four controlled acquisition pipelines that vary printed-image scale and camera zoom, and conduct a pilot study with directly captured physical objects. We find that the acquisition choices substantially change benchmark difficulty on all downstream baselines. Moreover, the average classification-accuracy advantage of Lens over auto exposure observed on the original benchmark does not persist in either our collected pipelines or real-object pilot study. Oracle exposure selection nevertheless retains substantial gains, indicating remaining potential for sensor control. These findings highlight acquisition protocol as an important factor in physical benchmark evaluation and motivate explicit protocol reporting and evaluation across multiple acquisition setups when assessing adaptive-sensing benefits.

</details>


### [67] [Bridging Transferability and Discriminability via Positive-Unlabeled Domain Adaptation for Building Damage Mapping Across Disaster Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33790)
*Qirui Wang,Hengwei Zhao,Xiaoshu Yang,Jingtao Li,Bo Li,Sorin Popescu,Rusty Feagin,Wenzhe Jiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出正样本-未标注域自适应（PUDA）范式与PANDA方法，用少量目标域损坏建筑标注增强目标域判别性，实现跨灾害事件的建筑损毁制图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨事件的分布偏移（灾害类型、地理环境、建筑特征、成像条件）导致深度学习模型及地理空间基础模型在新灾害事件上泛化受限；现有无监督域自适应在“损坏比例偏移”与“损坏外观偏移”共存交织时难以奏效，作者认为关键在于提升目标域判别性。

Method: 借鉴正样本-未标注（PU）学习，引入PU弱监督（仅需目标域少量标注的损毁建筑）构建PUDA范式；据此设计PANDA，包含两个互补互促组件：Pairwise Alignment with Intra-class Robustness（PAIR）用于判别性表征学习，Refine Gate and Verifier（RAV）用于监督信号精炼。

Result: 在全球尺度多灾害基准上总体优于代表性域自适应与PU学习方法；在2025年洛杉矶野火场景中，仅用少量目标域损毁标注即取得有效结果；进一步实验显示与地理空间基础模型结合具有跨事件自适应潜力。

Conclusion: PANDA为快速、可靠、跨事件的建筑损毁制图提供了可行路径，验证了PU弱监督在缓解复合分布偏移中的实用价值。

Abstract: Mapping the damage to buildings following natural and human-caused disasters is essential for disaster response and humanitarian assistance. However, differences in hazard types, geographic environments, building characteristics, and imaging conditions cause cross-event distribution shifts that limit the generalization of deep learning models to new disaster events, even for geospatial foundation models (GFMs) pretrained on extensive Earth observation data. Unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) can mitigate these shifts, but adaptation remains challenging when damage-proportion and damage-appearance shifts coexist and interact. We identify enhancing target-domain discriminability as key to addressing this challenge. To this end, inspired by positive-unlabeled (PU) learning, we introduce positive-unlabeled domain adaptation, a paradigm that incorporates PU weak supervision to enhance target-domain discriminability, requiring only a few labeled damaged buildings in the target domain. Building upon this straightforward intuition, we develop Positive-ANchored Domain Adaptation (PANDA), comprising two complementary and mutually reinforcing components: Pairwise Alignment with Intra-class Robustness (PAIR) for discriminative representation learning and Refine Gate and Verifier (RAV) for supervision refinement. On a global-scale multi-disaster benchmark, PANDA generally outperforms representative domain adaptation and PU learning methods. Results on the 2025 Los Angeles wildfires show its effectiveness in new disaster scenarios with few labeled damaged buildings. Further experiments demonstrate the potential of integrating PANDA with GFMs for cross-event adaptation. These findings highlight PANDA's practical potential for rapid, reliable, and cross-event building damage mapping.

</details>


### [68] [CriticalScenes: Scalable Photorealistic Critical-Scenario Generation for Closed-Loop Driving Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33255)
*Alexandros Theocharous,Holger Caesar,Ziqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CriticalScenes 重建框架，将普通行车场景转为逼真安全关键交互并生成避撞监督，有效降低端到端规划器碰撞率，并揭示开环指标与闭环安全收益的不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实驾驶数据中安全关键交互稀少，端到端规划器缺乏规避行为的监督信号，易产生不安全行为甚至碰撞。

Method: 基于重建的 CriticalScenes 框架：将普通录制场景改造为可交互的逼真安全关键场景，并自动生成相应避撞监督信号；在 53 个源场景上进行可行性验证，并用于训练两种结构不同的端到端规划器。

Result: 1,060 次交互尝试中 979 次（92.4%）生成可行威胁轨迹；插入的威胁行为体在 5–35 m 非遮挡观测中检出率超 95%。SparseDrive 在独立逼真闭环基准上碰撞率相对下降 46.5%，UniAD 开环提升显著但闭环改善有限。

Conclusion: 生成的安全关键监督可提升关键场景规划能力，但闭环收益随规划器架构而异；开环轨迹预测提升不等同于安全增益，凸显闭环评测的必要性。

Abstract: Safety-critical interactions are rare in recorded driving data, leaving end-to-end driving planners with limited exposure to evasive behaviour during training. This lack of critical supervision can lead to unsafe behaviour and, in rare instances, collisions. To address this gap, we introduce CriticalScenes, a reconstruction-based framework that transforms ordinary recorded driving scenes into photorealistic safety-critical interactions with corresponding collision-avoidance supervision. Across 53 reconstructed source scenes, 979 of 1,060 attempted interactions yield feasible threat trajectories (92.4\%), while the inserted threat actors remain detectable in more than 95\% of non-occluded observations within 5--35\,m. Experiments with two structurally different end-to-end planners show that the generated supervision improves critical-scenario planning, while closed-loop transfer varies across planner architectures. SparseDrive achieves a 46.5\% relative reduction in collision rate on an independent photorealistic closed-loop benchmark, whereas UniAD shows only a modest closed-loop improvement despite substantial open-loop gains. The results further highlight the importance of closed-loop evaluation, as improvements in open-loop trajectory prediction do not necessarily translate into equivalent safety gains.

</details>


### [69] [Manga Page Parser: Comprehensive Visual Parsing through Local Story Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34006)
*Yingxuan Li,Jiafeng Mao,Arata Furukawa,Mayu Otani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MangaParser：无训练的多模态智能体阅读框架，在统一会话中联合完成角色聚类、说话人日志和说话人-角色绑定，并借助结构化故事记忆与主动区域检查实现可复用的双页版面故事结构化解析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解漫画需将"谁出现、谁在说话"与跨分镜的事件、角色状态及关系联系起来；现有漫画专用模型通常孤立处理子任务，缺乏可复用、可组织的局部故事理解表示。

Method: 以单个双页跨页为独立分析单元，构建智能体式阅读框架，在同一多模态对话中同时进行角色聚类、说话人分离（diarization）与说话人-角色绑定，无需额外训练或微调；利用结构化故事记忆指导角色与说话人识别，在不确定时检索相关图像区域，并随证据累积共同迭代精化预测与故事记录。

Result: 定量评估显示该解析器在多个骨干模型上显著优于漫画专用模型；解析示例表明统一表示能将角色与说话人预测同叙事内容及支撑证据关联；以先于问题生成输出的故事问答测试验证了输出的可复用性。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了漫画局部故事理解的可复用结构化表示范式，为漫画翻译、内容检索与问答等下游应用提供基础。

Abstract: Understanding manga requires connecting who appears and who speaks with the events, character states, and relationships that unfold across panels. We study manga visual parsing through local story understanding: analyzing each double-page spread independently to capture and organize its story information in a reusable structured representation. We propose MangaParser, an agentic reading framework that jointly performs character clustering, speaker diarization, and speaker-to-character binding within a shared multimodal conversation, without additional training or fine-tuning. The agent uses structured story memory to guide character and speaker identification, inspecting relevant image regions when uncertain and refining its predictions and story records together as evidence accumulates. Quantitative evaluations show that our parser substantially outperforms manga-specific models across multiple backbones. Parsing examples illustrate how a unified representation links the character and speaker predictions with narrative content and supporting evidence. We further test reusability through story question answering using outputs generated before the questions are provided. The results confirm output reusability, offering a foundation for manga translation, content retrieval, and question answering.

</details>


### [70] [FasterGRN: Reducing the Refinement Redundancy for Accelerating Generative Refinement Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33982)
*Ziqian Zhang,Pengcheng Wang,Yulong Chen,Yaxing Wang,Senmao Li,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 FasterGRN——一种无需训练的生成精炼网络（GRN）加速方法，通过自适应保留扩展（ARE）与选择性 token 冻结（STF）缩减精炼步数与 token 计算，在 GenEval/DPG-Bench 上实现 2.03× 采样加速且基本保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRN 通过迭代精炼实现高质量图像生成，但对完整视觉表征的重复处理带来巨大计算开销；且其二元表征与全局精炼机制使扩散/自回归模型上的加速策略无法直接迁移。作者通过分析整个生成过程，发现阶段特性、双分支相似性以及关键的"精炼冗余"现象，据此设计专用加速方案。

Method: 训练无关的加速方法：先依据语义建立程度将 GRN 划分为语义建立阶段与细节精炼阶段；针对主要源于细节精炼阶段的精炼冗余，提出 Adaptive Retention Expansion（ARE）收缩精炼步数，以及 Selective Token Freezing（STF）减少 token 计算量。
