Result: 在 GenEval 与 DPG-Bench 上取得 2.03× 采样加速，同时较大程度保留图像生成性能；在 COCO 数据集上的评测显示，FasterGRN 在图像保真度上甚至优于原始 GRN。

Conclusion: GRN 的精炼过程存在可利用的阶段结构与冗余特性，基于此的训练无关加速（ARE+STF）能有效提速且不损害、甚至略提升保真度，说明面向特定生成范式的定制化加速策略具有可行性。

Abstract: Departing from the paradigm of diffusion and autoregressive models, Generative Refinement Networks (GRN) enables high-quality image generation through iterative refinements. However, this repeated processing over the entire visual representations results in substantial computation overhead. Furthermore, the binary representations and global refinement mechanism in GRN disable the direct application of acceleration strategies developed for other generative models. To accelerate GRN model, we first observe several key properties by analyzing the entire generative process. That includes the stage characteristic, dual branch similarity and more importantly, the $\textbf{refinement redundancy}$. Motivated by the observations, we present $\textbf{FasterGRN}$, a training-free acceleration method for the Generative Refinement Network. $\textbf{FasterGRN}$ first divides the GRN into the semantic establishment stage and detail refinement stage according to its semantic establishment. For alleviating the refinement redundancy mainly resulting from the detail refinement stage, we propose Adaptive Retention Expansion ($\textbf{ARE}$) and Selective Token Freezing ($\textbf{STF}$) to shrink refinement steps and reduce token computations, respectively. In the experiments on GenEval and DPG-Bench benchmarks, our method $\textbf{FasterGRN}$ achieves a $2.03\times$ sampling speedup while largely preserving image generation performance. Our evaluation on COCO datasets additionally demonstrates that $\textbf{FasterGRN}$ even outperforms the original GRN in image fidelity.

</details>


### [71] [Where Does Free-Text 3D Grounding Fail? An Error Analysis of Report-Sentence Segmentation in Chest CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33958)
*Abdullah Arif Sheikh,Kamalaker Dadi,Raju S. Bapi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了VoxTell模型在CT自由文本3D定位任务上的全部失败案例，指出失败主要源于范围估计过大与小病灶检测不足，而非解剖位置或病变识别错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由文本3D定位能在规模化层面将放射报告与影像关联，但现有模型具体在何处失效尚不清楚，需明确瓶颈以指导后续改进。

Method: 在ReXGroundingCT验证集全部381条findings上评估VoxTell，并分析错误类型；进行去除位置词的消融实验与基于句子推导的解剖规则后处理实验，并在隐藏的300例测试集上复核。

Result: VoxTell定位成功率为53.5%；错误极少涉及侧别或病变混淆（无跨侧漏检）；主要表现为预测范围过大、落在同侧相邻病灶、小病灶漏检，弥漫性病变几乎无法定位。去除位置词使性能减半至仅标签提示水平，解剖规则后处理无增益；隐藏测试集表现一致。

Conclusion: 未来进展依赖范围估计与小病灶敏感性，而非解剖后处理。

Abstract: Free-text 3D grounding, segmenting in a CT volume the region described by one sentence of the radiology report, could link reports to images at scale, but it is unclear where current models fail. We analyse the publicly released VoxTell model on all 381 findings of the 200-scan ReXGroundingCT validation set, where it grounds 53.5% of findings. Its failures are rarely about which lesion or side is meant. No miss lies on the side opposite to the one named, and findings are almost never confused with one another. Instead, predictions are too large, land on a neighbouring lesion on the correct side, or are missing for small findings, and diffuse findings are rarely grounded. Removing location words halves performance, to the level of label-only prompting, and anatomical rules derived from the sentence never improve the full-sentence output. The same pattern holds on the hidden 300-scan test set. Further progress therefore depends on extent estimation and small-lesion sensitivity rather than on anatomical post-processing.

</details>


### [72] [UPP-Gauss: Ultrasound Propagation Physics-informed Gaussian Splatting for B-mode Novel-View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33920)
*Guil Jung,Seok-Hwan Oh,Myeong-Gee Kim,Young-Min Kim,Hyeon-Jik Lee,Sang-Yun Kim,Hyeok-Jun Kwon,Hyuk-Sool Kwon,Hyeon-Min Bae*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UPP-Gauss将超声声学传播过程显式融入3D高斯表示，通过探头位姿与波束方向采样三个高斯场构建声学图并合成B-mode图像，实现高效且高精度的新视角合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B-mode超声新视角合成的难点在于需建模声波在组织中的传播以及探头位姿变化引起的成像外观差异，现有方法缺乏对声学传播的显式建模。

Method: 构建显式高斯表示并引入超声传播物理约束：依据探头位姿与声束传播方向对三个3D高斯场采样，生成对应声学图，再按超声传播机理将各声学图组合以合成B-mode图像。

Result: 在三个声学特性各异的数据集上取得最佳重建质量，渲染高效，PSNR较各数据集最佳基线平均提升1.43 dB。

Conclusion: 通过对超声传播的显式高斯建模，可实现准确且高效的B-mode新视角合成。

Abstract: Accurate novel-view synthesis of B-mode ultrasound images requires modeling acoustic propagation through tissue and the resulting appearance variations across probe poses. We propose Ultrasound Propagation Physics-informed Gaussian Splatting (UPP-Gauss), which integrates these acoustic interactions into an explicit Gaussian representation. UPP-Gauss constructs acoustic maps by sampling three 3D Gaussian fields using the probe pose and acoustic beam propagation direction, then combines these maps according to ultrasound propagation to synthesize B-mode images. Across three acoustically diverse datasets, UPP-Gauss achieves the best reconstruction quality with efficient rendering, improving PSNR over the best baseline on each dataset by 1.43 dB on average. These results demonstrate accurate and efficient B-mode novel-view synthesis through explicit Gaussian modeling of ultrasound propagation.

</details>


### [73] [MotionODE: Enhancing Autoregressive Text-to-Motion Generation with Continuous-Depth Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33826)
*Zhaoxin Fan,Jian Yang,Kejian Wu,Zeming Liu,Wenjun Wu,Lili Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MotionODE：将预训练自回归生成器中的部分Transformer残差分支视为局部向量场，用中点Runge–Kutta（RK2）积分替换原有更新，并以混合一步/两步RK2后训练精化隐状态演化，从而在不重训、不改表征与生成机制的前提下提升文本-动作对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归文本到动作生成要求局部预测同时兼顾跨时序的文本约束与已有动作上下文。现有工作多改进动作表征或生成顺序，而对"条件动作表征的内部形成过程"探索不足。

Method: 提出连续深度后训练框架MotionODE：把选定的Transformer残差分支重解释为局部向量场，以RK2中点积分替代原更新，保留动作表征与外围生成机制；针对预训练更新路径与新引入中间状态的不匹配，采用动作token监督下的混合一步/两步RK2后训练，并可选加入蒸馏与更新映射约束以保持预训练行为。方法微创，支持next-token与next-scale两类生成，无需从头训练。

Result: 在HumanML3D上，于MoScale、BAMM、LMR三个基线上均取得文本-动作对齐的一致提升。

Conclusion: 内部表征更新（隐状态演化）是改进自回归动作生成的一个互补方向，可与表征/顺序层面的改进叠加。

Abstract: Autoregressive text-to-motion generation requires local motion predictions to reconcile cross-temporal textual requirements with the available motion context. While existing methods primarily improve motion representations and generation order, the internal formation of conditioned motion representations remains less explored. We introduce MotionODE, a continuous-depth post-training framework that refines hidden-state evolution in pretrained autoregressive generators. MotionODE reinterprets selected Transformer residual branches as local vector fields and replaces their original updates with midpoint Runge--Kutta (RK2) integration, while preserving the motion representation and outer generation mechanism. To address the mismatch between pretrained update paths and newly introduced intermediate states, we propose mixed one-step/two-step RK2 post-training under motion-token supervision, with optional distillation and update-map constraints to preserve pretrained behavior. This minimally invasive framework supports both next-token and next-scale generation without training the generator from scratch. Extensive experiments on HumanML3D demonstrate consistent improvements in text--motion alignment across MoScale, BAMM and LMR, highlighting internal representation updates as a complementary direction for improving autoregressive motion generation.

</details>


### [74] [FlipVLA: Backdoor Attacks on Vision-Language-Action(VLA) Models Only Through Bit Flips](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33810)
*Zexi Zhao,Zizhen Liu,Jing Justin Ye,Huawei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FlipVLA，通过少量固定参数位翻转（无需重训练）在VLA机器人模型中植入物体触发后门：选择器借GELU在特定通道构造触发相关响应，放大器修改下游权重将其转为动作级扰动，并辅以通道筛选与离散位搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型直接输出真实世界机器人动作，恶意篡改会威胁物理安全。作者探究能否在不重训练的前提下，仅用少量固定的参数位翻转植入物体触发后门。核心难点在于：持久的权重修改需在触发输入上破坏行为，同时在非触发输入上保持性能。

Method: 两阶段"选择器-放大器"方法，利用FC-GELU-FC结构中已有的视觉特征：选择器在选定通道构造触发相关响应，借助GELU抑制背景激活；放大器修改相连的下游权重，将该响应转化为与动作相关的扰动。在有限修改预算下，通过通道筛选与离散位搜索构造两个组件。

Result: 在OpenVLA-OFT与GR00T N1.7上、跨四个LIBERO套件，攻击成功率90.22%–100%，干净场景任务成功率下降不超过1.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 物体触发的后门可通过极少量位翻转、无需重训练地植入VLA模型，在保持干净性能的同时实现高攻击成功率，揭示VLA模型面对参数级篡改时的物理安全风险。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models generate robot actions from visual observations and language instructions in real-world environments, making malicious model tampering a potential threat to physical safety. We study whether object-triggered backdoors can be implanted through a small, fixed set of parameter bit flips without retraining. The central challenge is to make persistent weight modifications disrupt trigger-present behavior while preserving performance on non-trigger inputs. We propose FlipVLA, a two-stage selector-amplifier method that exploits existing visual features within an FC-GELU-FC structure. The selector constructs a trigger-dependent response in a selected channel, using GELU to attenuate background activity. The amplifier modifies a connected downstream weight to convert this response into an action-relevant perturbation. Channel screening and discrete bit search construct both components under a limited modification budget. Experiments on OpenVLA-OFT and GR00T N1.7 across four LIBERO suites achieve attack success rates of 90.22% to 100%, with task success decreasing by at most 1.5 percentage points on clean scenes.

</details>


### [75] [Anatomy-Aware Pseudo-Labels and Homotopy-Annealed Hausdorff Supervision for Semi-Supervised Pancreas Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33425)
*Alireza Kheyrkhah,Amir Hossein Saleknia,Reza Azad,Sina Houshmand,Dorit Merhof,Ulas Bagci,Alaa Sulaiman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出解剖矫正、边界移植与同伦退火Hausdorff监督三项无需额外参数的方法，解决胰腺CT中伪标签在位置、边界与损失惩罚三层面的失败，并在NIH、MSD Task07及新构建的PanTS-SSL协议上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有置信度门控协同训练假设伪标签误差不确定且集中在器官边界；作者认为胰腺CT中目标器官为单一连通结构，分离碎片在解剖上不合理，主要失败来自三个层次：器官位置（何处）、边界形态、以及正确边界如何被惩罚。

Method: 1) Anatomical Rectification：将教师预测中不合理解的分离碎片重标为高置信背景，不增加损失项或参数；2) Boundary Grafting：将带标注的表面区域连同真值移植到无标注体数据中，以真值替代不可靠的边界伪标签；3) Homotopy-Annealed Hausdorff (HAH)：从以标注为锚、对预测呈凸性的目标，退火至Hausdorff代理，仅当预测表面可靠后才引入依赖预测的距离项。

Result: NIH胰腺CT标准半监督划分下达84.60% Dice与4.97体素95HD，四项指标全面优于对比方法；MSD Task07上78.90% Dice、4.19体素95HD。PanTS-SSL协议上，100标签时超越协同训练基线，250标签达85.29% Dice，配合HAH在100标签达85.50% Dice。

Conclusion: 通过解剖先验矫正、边界真值移植与退火式距离监督，可从定位、边界形状与损失惩罚三个层面系统性消除伪标签失效；审计表明矫正拒绝的几乎均为脱离主体的幻觉，薄尾状切片未被侵蚀。同时贡献PanTS-SSL：含1,015体积主池、50/100/250标签分层、80例肿瘤赛道、采集与解剖漂移探针及固定901体积测试集的半监督评测协议。

Abstract: Confidence-gated co-training assumes that pseudo-label errors are uncertain and concentrated at organ boundaries. We argue that for pancreas CT, where the target organ is a single connected structure and detached fragments are anatomically implausible, the dominant failures arise at three levels: where the organ is, what its boundary should look like, and how a correct boundary is penalized. Anatomical Rectification relabels implausible fragments of a teacher prediction as confident background, without adding any loss or parameter; an audit confirms that its rejections are overwhelmingly detached hallucinations and that thin, tail-like slices are not eroded. Boundary Grafting transplants labelled surface regions with their annotation into unlabeled volumes, replacing unreliable boundary pseudo-labels with ground truth. The residual error is coherent surface displacement, weakly penalized by permutation-invariant Dice and cross-entropy losses. Homotopy-Annealed Hausdorff (HAH) supervision anneals a distance-weighted penalty from an annotation-anchored objective that is convex in the prediction to a Hausdorff surrogate, admitting the prediction-dependent distance term only after the predicted surface becomes reliable. On NIH pancreas CT, under the standard semi-supervised split, the full method reaches 84.60% Dice and 4.97 voxels 95HD, improving all four metrics over compared methods; on MSD Task07, 78.90% Dice and 4.19 voxels 95HD. We further contribute PanTS-SSL, a semi-supervised evaluation protocol derived from the public 9,901-case PanTS corpus, with a curated 1,015-volume main pool, labelled tiers of 50, 100 and 250 volumes, an 80-case tumor track, shift probes for acquisition and anatomy change, and a fixed 901-volume test set. On it, the method exceeds the co-training baseline at 100 labels, scales to 85.29% Dice at 250 labels, and reaches 85.50% Dice at 100 labels with HAH.

</details>


### [76] [CoSMo: A Compact Spectral Space of Body Motion in Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33296)
*Maham Tanveer,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoSMo 将视频中的身体运动编码为基于 DCT 时序基的紧凑谱空间，每个运动为 384 维单一编码，可免训练比较、编辑、迁移、插值、组合与强度缩放，并可由冻结的图像到视频模型渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动表示（如逐帧表示 DisMo）冗余、易混杂外观/场景信息，且难以在不需逐运动训练的情况下直接操作（插值、组合、迁移到新主体）。作者希望获得一个时间对齐、粗细有序、可加可混、与主体解耦的紧凑运动编码空间。

Method: 用固定时序基（离散余弦变换 DCT）表达片段 DINOv2 特征随时间的变化，并压缩为 384 维编码；各频带在所有编码中为相同余弦模式，故编码在时间上对齐、由粗到细排序，可无需帧匹配直接混合或相加；片内恒定成分相互抵消，保留较少频率即去除更多主体与场景信息。编码器在真实视频与多角色合成运动上训练，并用轻量适配器使冻结的图像到视频模型以任意主体渲染任意编码。

Result: 在未见视频上，同一运动在不同身体上的排序优于逐帧表示 DisMo；最近邻中出现同一表演者的比例约低 40%，而编码紧凑约 7 倍。生成方面无需逐运动训练即比 FlexiAct 和文本提示基线迁移更忠实，自动指标上至少不逊于 DisMo，在人形身体间明显更优，且混合保留的两种源运动信息远多于 DisMo。用户研究中运动迁移偏好 59% vs 30%（DisMo），94% 进入前二，插值比较中 76% 偏好 CoSMo。

Conclusion: 固定 DCT 谱基可得到时间对齐、层级化、主体无关的紧凑运动编码，实现免训练的通用运动编辑与忠实迁移，在自动指标与人类评估上均优于现有基线。

Abstract: We introduce CoSMo, a compact spectral space of body motion in video, in which each motion is a single code that can be compared and edited without training, transferred to a new subject, interpolated, composed, scaled in intensity and detail, or sampled anew. Our encoder expresses how a clip's DINOv2 features change over time in a fixed temporal basis, the discrete cosine transform (DCT), and compresses this change into 384 numbers. Because each frequency band is the same cosine pattern in every code, codes are aligned in time, ordered from coarse to fine, and can be blended or added without matching frames. What stays constant over a clip cancels, and keeping fewer frequencies leaves less of the performer and scene. The encoder is trained on real videos and on synthetic motions of several characters, and lightweight adapters let a frozen image-to-video model render any code with any subject. On unseen videos, our code ranks the same motion on other bodies higher than DisMo, a per-frame representation, while its nearest neighbors show the same performer about 40% less often with a seven times more compact code. In generation, without per-motion training, CoSMo transfers motion more faithfully than FlexiAct and a text-prompt baseline, at least as faithfully as DisMo on automatic metrics and clearly better between humanoid bodies, and its blends keep much more of both source motions than DisMo's. Users rank CoSMo first nearly twice as often as DisMo for motion transfer (59% vs. 30%), place it top-two 94% of the time, and prefer it in 76% of the interpolation comparisons.

</details>


### [77] [DoseSteer: Steering diffusion-based dose prediction models towards clinical targets via Dual-Response Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33577)
*Shupeng Cheng,Jing Jia,Jincheng Wu,Zhangyu Wang,Gao Wang,Maohe Jiang,Chi Xu,Feiyang Wang,Zhilei Liu Shen,Guanyang Wang,Ruishan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DoseSteer：一种免训练的推理时引导框架，将冻结的扩散式剂量预测模型导向指定临床靶区剂量目标，核心为双响应引导（DRG）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI剂量预测模型虽能生成患者特异性3D剂量分布，但在结构级别的临床剂量目标上仍可能偏离；部署后缺乏直接纠正机制，只能把目标满足推给下游计划优化。

Method: 提出推理时框架DoseSteer，在不重训练的前提下引导冻结的扩散剂量预测模型；并提出以结构为中心的双响应引导DRG：对各结构噪声剂量与上下文的扰动探测模型响应，联立求解每个结构的引导强度。

Result: 在覆盖三种扩散架构与三个数据集的五个数据集-模型配置上，DRG在相同参考衍生的目标代理下优于12个免训练基线，消除至少97%的目标误差，官方剂量误差平均降低7.8%，并改善被引导结构之外的剂量保真度。

Conclusion: DoseSteer在测试阶段协调学习到的解剖剂量先验与显式结构级目标，为计划优化前精修AI剂量预测提供了有原则的免训练机制。

Abstract: AI dose-prediction models generate patient-specific 3D dose distributions from patient anatomy. Yet these predictions can still miss structure-level clinical dose targets. Once deployed, most existing predictors offer no direct mechanism to correct such deviations, deferring target enforcement to downstream plan optimization. We introduce DoseSteer, an inference-time framework that steers frozen diffusion-based dose prediction models towards specified clinical targets without retraining. To meet clinical targets while preserving overall dose prediction quality, we propose Dual-Response Guidance (DRG), a structure-centric algorithm that probes the model's responses to perturbations in each structure's noisy dose and context and jointly solves for per-structure steering strengths. Across five dataset-model configurations spanning three diffusion architectures and three datasets, DRG outperforms 12 training-free baselines under identical reference-derived target proxies, removing at least 97% of the target error, reducing the official dose error by 7.8% on average, and improving dose fidelity outside the steered structures. DoseSteer reconciles learned anatomical dose priors with explicit structure-level targets at test time, establishing a principled, training-free mechanism to refine AI dose predictions prior to plan optimization. Code is available at https://github.com/csp2002/DoseSteer.

</details>


### [78] [Frame geometry, fascicle extrapolation and leaderboard overfitting in muscle ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33539)
*Soheil Kooklan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究在UMUD Challenge一个提交条目的框架下量化了B型超声羽状角、肌束长度与肌肉厚度自动测量的几何依赖性与可复现性，发现帧几何与肌束延伸规则的影响可与模型选择相当，且在约100帧公开榜上做的小幅调参不能迁移到私有榜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动超声测量的读数依赖于存储帧的几何参数（比例、像素纵横比）以及离开图像边界的肌束如何外延，而挑战赛参赛条目往往基于小型公开榜单挑选设置，缺乏对这些决策稳健性的系统评估。作者希望在一次参赛中同时测量三项读数，并逐项量化各设计决策在公开/私有划分上的作用。

Method: 针对UMUD Challenge一个提交条目，处理309帧、6种屏幕布局（公开榜103帧、私有榜206帧）。流程按布局取尺度与像素纵横比、重采样为方形像素，用U-Nets分割腱膜与肌束片段，再几何计算三项读数；提交条目将角度与长度与公开notebook结果混合。采用加性评分设计，从13个基准条目构造65个单变量改动对照，在两个划分上分别衡量每个决策的效果；另对UMUD专家基准做数据泄漏审计（与DL_Track_US及挑战训练图像的组织匹配），并在9帧无可检匹配样本上与专家和DL_Track_US比较。

Result: 主选条目私有榜得分0.27608，690支队伍中报第69位。全部帧按方形像素读取的代价为公开榜0.029、私有榜0.037。20项后期修正中仅9项在私有榜上保持效应符号。审计发现专家基准35帧中26帧的组织与DL_Track_US及挑战训练图像重合；在9帧无可检匹配样本上，本方案得分0.213，两名专家对其余两名专家0.218，DL_Track_US 0.263（要求参考对双方均评分时分别为0.209、0.196、0.259），未建立显著差异。

Conclusion: 帧几何与肌束延伸规则对读数的影响与模型选择相当；在约100帧公开榜上调出的微小修正无法迁移到私有榜，提示此类排行榜驱动的调参存在过拟合风险。同时现有专家基准存在明显训练集重叠，其泛化评估能力存疑，需谨慎解读与专家"无差异"的结论。

Abstract: Automated readings of pennation angle, fascicle length and muscle thickness from B-mode ultrasound depend on the geometry of the stored frame and on how fascicles that leave the image are extended, and challenge entries add settings chosen on a small public leaderboard. This study measures all three in one entry of the UMUD Challenge, which scored 309 frames from six screen layouts, 103 on a public and 206 on a private leaderboard. The pipeline applies layout-specific values of scale and pixel aspect, resamples each frame to square pixels, segments aponeuroses and fascicle fragments with U-Nets and measures the three readings geometrically; the submitted entry blended its angle and length with public notebooks. Because the score is additive, 65 single-change contrasts from 13 base entries measured each decision on both splits. The primary selected entry scored 0.27608 on the private split, the 69th reported position of 690 teams. Reading every frame with square pixels cost 0.029 public and 0.037 private. Of 20 late-stage corrections, 9 kept the sign of their effect on the private split. An audit of the UMUD expert benchmark found that 26 of its 35 frames share their tissue with training images of both DL_Track_US and the challenge. On the 9 frames with no detected match in either set or orientation, the recipe scored 0.213, two experts against two others 0.218 and DL_Track_US 0.263 (0.209, 0.196 and 0.259 when both raters of a reference pair are required), with no difference established. Frame geometry and the extension rule moved readings as much as the choice of model, and small corrections tuned on about 100 public frames did not carry over.

</details>


### [79] [VAPOR: Improving Multimodal Reasoning with Sparse Visual Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33751)
*Mingyu Su,Wei Wu,Erle Zhu,Chengxing Xie,Deyao Hong,Hongning Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VAPOR：无需训练，跨解码步复用视觉注意力输出并按需刷新，在八个图像、四个视频推理基准上普遍优于稠密注意力，同时获得1.11–1.21×解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在长视觉上下文自回归解码时，每个生成token都重复计算视觉注意力，即便相邻token依赖相似的视觉证据，造成显著冗余与计算开销。

Method: 训练无关框架：（1）缓存视觉注意力输出、每步仍更新文本注意力，以抑制视觉 grounding 漂移；（2）用低秩近似估计视觉注意力质量（attention mass）判断复用时机；（3）结合 prefill 阶段注意力一致性与缓存年龄，对每个注意力头独立决策 Persist 或 Refresh；（4）保留完整视觉 KV cache，需要新证据时全部视觉 token 仍可用。

Result: 在两类MLLM家族的8个图像与4个视频推理基准上，几乎全部基准准确率超过稠密注意力，解码加速1.11–1.21×；相比各设置下最强的SOTA加速基线，准确率再提升0.35–2.15个百分点，且解码延迟相当或更低。

Conclusion: 通过细粒度（逐头）的视觉注意力复用与按需刷新，可在不训练、不牺牲视觉信息完整性的前提下同时提升MLLM长视觉推理的精度与解码效率。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made substantial progress in visual reasoning, but extended generation over large visual contexts remains computationally expensive. During autoregressive decoding, visual attention is recomputed for every generated token, even when consecutive tokens rely on similar visual evidence. We introduce VAPOR (Visual Attention Persistence with On-demand Refresh), a training-free framework that reduces this redundancy by reusing visual attention outputs across decoding steps and refreshing them when needed. VAPOR caches the visual attention output while continuing to update textual attention at every step, helping reduce visual grounding drift during extended reasoning. To decide when to reuse cached outputs, VAPOR estimates visual attention mass using a low-rank approximation. It also uses prefill-based attention consistency and cache age to make independent Persist and Refresh decisions for each attention head. VAPOR retains the full visual key-value (KV) cache, so all visual tokens remain available when new evidence is needed. Experiments on eight image and four video reasoning benchmarks across two MLLM families show that VAPOR improves accuracy over dense attention on nearly all benchmarks while achieving 1.11-1.21x decoding speedups. Compared with the strongest state-of-the-art acceleration baseline in each setting, VAPOR further improves accuracy by 0.35-2.15 percentage points while achieving comparable or lower decoding latency. Our code is available at https://github.com/Su-my/VAPOR.

</details>


### [80] [Nepali Currency Denomination Classification Using Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and MobileNetV2: An Experimental Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33713)
*Sakar Lamichhane,Sandesh Bhatta,Aayush Adhikari,Aaron Sapkota*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究构建16,249张图像的尼泊尔卢比数据集，比较传统CNN、MobileNetV2迁移学习与超参数调优三种配置，并用Flask+TensorFlow Lite部署带语音输出的Web原型；最佳测试准确率为89%（纯迁移学习）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动货币面额识别在现金处理、金融科技与辅助系统中具有应用价值，需要评估轻量级深度学习方案在尼泊尔卢比七种面额上的可行性与部署方式。

Method: 自建16,249张图像数据集（训练10,500/验证3,500/独立测试2,249），统一缩放至128×128像素并归一化、增强（朝向、尺度、光照、外观）。对比三种配置：常规CNN、MobileNetV2迁移学习、MobileNetV2超参数调优；评价指标为准确率、精确率、召回率、F1及PR/ROC分析；并以TensorFlow Lite+Flask实现支持图片上传与实时摄像头识别、含视觉与语音输出的Web原型。

Result: 超参数调优的MobileNetV2验证准确率最高（95%，精确率0.93、召回率0.91、F1 0.92），但独立测试集仅84%；未调优的迁移学习MobileNetV2测试准确率为89%，常规CNN为85%。调优带来的验证集增益未转化为测试集增益。

Conclusion: 在当前条件下迁移学习是具竞争力的方案，轻量级深度学习结合语音辅助Web界面对尼泊尔货币识别具有可行性；但需更丰富多样的数据集、更强的不受控成像鲁棒性及更广泛的真实场景评估。

Abstract: Automated currency denomination recognition is a computer vision task with applications in cash handling, financial technology, and assistive systems. This study classifies seven Nepali currency denominations (Rs. 5, 10, 20, 50, 100, 500, and 1,000) using three configurations: a conventional convolutional neural network (CNN), MobileNetV2 with transfer learning, and MobileNetV2 with hyperparameter tuning. A compiled dataset of 16,249 images was used, comprising 10,500 training, 3,500 validation, and 2,249 independent test images. Images were resized to 128 x 128 pixels, normalized, and augmented to vary orientation, scale, illumination, and appearance. Models were evaluated using accuracy, precision, recall, F1-score, and precision-recall and ROC analysis. The hyperparameter-tuned MobileNetV2 achieved the highest validation accuracy (95\%), with precision, recall, and F1-score of 0.93, 0.91, and 0.92, but reached only 84\% accuracy on the held-out test set. MobileNetV2 with transfer learning but no additional tuning achieved 89\% test accuracy, and the conventional CNN achieved 85\%. Transfer learning therefore provided a competitive approach under the evaluated conditions, while higher validation accuracy from tuning did not yield higher test accuracy. To demonstrate practical deployment, a Flask-based web prototype using TensorFlow Lite was developed, supporting image upload and live webcam recognition with both visual and voice output. The study shows the feasibility of combining lightweight deep learning with a voice-assisted web interface for Nepali currency recognition, and highlights the need for more diverse datasets, greater robustness under uncontrolled imaging conditions, and broader real-world evaluation.

</details>


### [81] [Recall4D: Forgetting, Not Misalignment, Limits Long-Horizon Feed-Forward 4D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33655)
*Tianle Lyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架Recall4D，用持久世界状态与稀疏运动锚点解决前馈4D跟踪器的"遗忘"问题，在长时序基准上同时提升精度并加速7.6-9.0倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈4D跟踪器（联合恢复相机运动、深度与点轨迹）在短视频片段上表现良好，但把窗口逐个串联到长视频时精度显著下降。作者发现瓶颈并非窗口间的对齐（替换为真值变换后误差几乎不变），而是"遗忘"：对已不再被当前窗口观测到的静态点反复进行预测，会破坏此前已恢复的几何。

Method: 先做诊断实验：仅保留未观测点的最后已知位置（不改动跟踪器）即可在PointOdyssey上降低26%误差。据此提出Recall4D——训练无关框架，在冻结的跟踪器外挂持久世界状态与稀疏运动锚点：静态点在相机视野外保持其世界坐标，在视野内时通过稀疏关键帧上的观测做平均精化；动态物体方面，跟踪器只解码稀疏代表性锚点，再由几何蒙皮（skinning）将其位移传播到稠密查询点。

Result: 在两个长时序基准上，Recall4D提升了其骨干网络的精度，同时运行速度快7.6-9.0倍；精度增益可迁移到另外五个骨干网络及三类其他长序列任务。

Conclusion: 长视频4D跟踪的主要退化源是跨窗口的"遗忘"而非对齐误差；以持久世界状态+稀疏锚点为核心的免训练外挂方案可即插即用地提升多种骨干的精度与效率，具备良好通用性。

Abstract: Feed-forward 4D trackers recover camera motion, depth, and point trajectories from short video clips, but chaining windows over long videos degrades their accuracy. We find that replacing inter-window alignment with ground-truth transforms leaves tracking error essentially unchanged. Static-point error instead grows with time since last observation: repeatedly predicting a static point from windows that no longer observe it corrupts previously recovered geometry. We call this failure forgetting. Merely retaining the last observed positions of unseen points reduces error by 26% on PointOdyssey without modifying the tracker. We introduce Recall4D, a training-free framework that augments a frozen tracker with a persistent world state and sparse motion anchors. Static points retain their world-space positions outside the camera view and are refined by averaging observations decoded at sparse keyframes while in view. For dynamic objects, the tracker decodes sparse representative anchors, while geometric skinning propagates their displacements to dense queries. Across two long-horizon benchmarks, Recall4D improves the accuracy of its backbone while running 7.6-9.0$\times$ faster, and its accuracy gains carry over to five further backbones and three other long-sequence tasks, demonstrating its generality.

</details>


### [82] [GlassGround: GUI Grounding Beyond Native Screenshots with Egocentric Wearable Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33760)
*Yun Wang,Siyue Sun,Jiaying Du,Xinyi Wei,Xiaoming Zhai,Zhenlin An,Ninghao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GlassGround基准，固定GUI状态、指令与目标，对比原生截图与可穿戴相机观测下的GUI元素定位，发现模型精度从94.8%骤降至48.5%，并提出PairDistill跨视图对齐基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴助手在无法直接获取截图时需从相机图像中定位界面元素，但现有GUI grounding benchmark主要以原生截图评测，缺乏对真实可穿戴相机观测的系统评估。

Method: 构建GlassGround：3250个任务，覆盖Android与iOS，使用4种可穿戴设备、4种观看条件采集，每个任务配对截图与相机视图且保持UI状态、指令、目标一致；评测10个开源模型；分析目标尺度、屏幕几何与背景变化的影响；对比推理时的局部放大与屏幕校正，以及训练时提出的PairDistill（在坐标监督之外跨视图对齐目标表征，基于独立数据训练、推理不用截图）。

Result: 平均grounding精度由截图上的94.8%降至相机图像上的48.5%；尺度、几何、背景因素无法完全解释下降。推理时局部放大与校正缩小部分差距但仍不足。PairDistill在两个骨干上分别提升9.4与12.2个点，并可迁移至未见设备与操作系统；结合局部放大达69.6%与64.4%，但差距仍未闭合。

Conclusion: 现有GUI grounding模型在可穿戴相机观测下存在显著鲁棒性缺口，配对视图数据与跨视图表征对齐是有效方向，但仍未解决问题，为后续研究提供了基准与基线。

Abstract: Wearable assistants must localize interface elements from camera observations when direct screenshot access is unavailable, yet existing GUI grounding benchmarks primarily evaluate native screenshots. We introduce GlassGround, a new benchmark that pairs native screenshots with wearable camera observations while holding GUI states, instructions, and targets fixed. GlassGround contains 3,250 tasks across Android and iOS, captured using four wearable devices under four viewing conditions. Across ten open-source models, mean grounding accuracy drops from 94.8% on screenshots to 48.5% on camera images. Further analysis reveals that changes in target scale, screen geometry, and background cannot fully explain this performance degradation. We then study two ways to narrow this gap. At inference time, local zooming and screen rectification help but leave most of the gap. For training-time adaptation, we build on GlassGround's paired-view design and introduce PairDistill, a baseline that aligns target representations across views alongside coordinate supervision. Trained on independently collected data and using no screenshots at inference, it improves camera-image accuracy by 9.4 and 12.2 points on two backbones and transfers to unseen devices and operating systems. Combined with local zooming, it reaches 69.6% and 64.4%, but does not close the gap. The GlassGround dataset and code are available at https://github.com/yunnw/GlassGround.

</details>


### [83] [Show Me the Change You Want: Visual Example-Guided Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33756)
*Yuan Qian,Jiaqi Cui,Jie Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出视觉示例引导的变化检测（VECD），用一对无标注的前后图像作为视觉示例，在另一对图像中定位同类变化，无需文本、示例掩码或测试时微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指代式变化检测依赖预定义类别、文本描述或标注掩码，灵活性受限且需额外标注；用户实际只想找某一类特定变化。

Method: 框架从单张图像学习可比较的属性状态，并引入示例条件读出（example-conditioned readout），在不同物体与场景间匹配变化；示例本身即构成请求。

Result: 在EditCLEVR、MagicBrush、HumanEdit、MaskEdit上实现无需微调的跨数据集泛化，单一固定示例可跨多场景引导检测；能在多重变化场景中选出匹配变化，请求变化缺失时返回空掩码；对训练中未配对出现的属性族仍达74.54%–93.38% mIoU。

Conclusion: 以视觉示例替代文本或掩码指令，是一种更灵活、无需额外标注的变化检测范式，具备强泛化能力与空缺判别能力。

Abstract: Change detection identifies where images differ, but in practice users often seek only a particular kind of change. Existing referring change detection methods rely on predefined categories, textual descriptions, or annotated masks, limiting flexibility or requiring additional annotation. In this paper, we propose a new way to perform change detection, where an unannotated before-and-after image pair serves as a visual example to guide change localization in another pair. Our framework, Visual Example-Guided Change Detection (VECD), learns comparable attribute states from single images and introduces an example-conditioned readout to match changes across different objects and scenes. The example itself defines the request, without text, example masks, or test-time fine-tuning. We evaluate VECD on four datasets spanning rendered scenes and natural image edits: EditCLEVR, MagicBrush, HumanEdit, and MaskEdit. Experiments demonstrate cross-dataset generalization without fine-tuning: a single fixed example pair guides change detection across multiple scenes, even when examples and queries originate from different datasets. VECD selects matching changes in scenes with multiple simultaneous changes and returns an empty mask when the requested change is absent. It further generalizes to attribute families withheld from paired training, achieving 74.54\% to 93.38\% mIoU across four holdout settings. Code is available at https://github.com/qianyuancs/VECD

</details>


### [84] [SPiXSeg: Rethinking MLLM-based Image Segmentation as Superpixel Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33842)
*Piyush Tiwary,Sohini Dutta,Prathosh A. P*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPiXSeg 将多模态大模型的分割输出从"逐patch"改为"逐superpixel"标签分配：用现成模型生成任务无关的superpixel划分，将patch级视觉特征聚合成superpixel token，并通过 Spix-to-Mask 模块在单次前向中比较MLLM隐表示完成打分，无需自回归生成、独立评分网络、可训练解码头或后处理精修。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MLLM的图像分割方法存在权衡困境：使用额外解码器，或采用patch-based方案——粗网格会将前景与背景像素混在一起，牺牲边界精度；细网格则需大量预测；自回归生成mask还带来串行解码开销。作者希望找到一种兼顾边界精度与效率的输出粒度。

Method: 提出SPiXSeg：(1) 利用现成模型将每张图像划分为任务无关的superpixel，划分只计算一次并跨任务复用；(2) 聚合patch级视觉特征形成superpixel token；(3) Spix-to-Mask (S2M) 模块在单次MLLM前向中，直接比较MLLM隐表示对每个superpixel token打分，无需自回归生成或单独评分网络；(4) 被选中的superpixel直接映射回像素，不做精修，也无trainable decoder head。

Result: 在RefCOCO、RefCOCO+、RefCOCOg上，平均cIoU达80.5（LLaVA-1.5-7B）与81.9（InternVL2.5-8B）；仅在COCO-Stuff上训练即可扩展到开放词汇语义分割，在ADE20K、PASCAL Context、PASCAL VOC上mIoU分别为29.0、59.5、95.4，优于多个SoTA MLLM基线。

Conclusion: 固定、与查询无关的superpixel划分足以支撑准确的语言引导分割；将粒度从patch转向superpixel可在不引入解码器与精修的前提下同时获得精度与效率。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are widely used for image segmentation. However, existing methods either use a decoder or use patch-based approach which trades boundary precision against the number of decisions as coarse grid cells mix foreground and background pixels, while finer grids require many more predictions, and autoregressive mask generation adds sequential decoding overhead. We propose SPiXSeg, which shifts the output from per-patch to per-superpixel label assignment, using superpixel tokens formed by aggregating patch-level visual features. An off-the-shelf model partitions each image into task-agnostic superpixels, so the partition is computed once and reused across tasks. A Spix-to-Mask (S2M) module scores each superpixel token in a single MLLM forward pass by directly comparing the MLLM's hidden representations, without autoregressive generation or a separate scoring network, and the selected superpixels are mapped directly to pixels without refinement. Without any trainable decoder head or post-hoc refinement, SPiXSeg achieves an average cIoU of 80.5 with LLaVA-1.5-7B and 81.9 with InternVL2.5-8B on RefCOCO, RefCOCO+ and RefCOCOg and, when trained only on COCO-Stuff, extends to open-vocabulary semantic segmentation with mIoU of 29.0, 59.5 and 95.4 on ADE20K, PASCAL Context and PASCAL VOC, outperforming several SoTA MLLM baselines. Our results suggest that a fixed, query-agnostic superpixel partition is enough for accurate language-guided segmentation.

</details>


### [85] [Continuous Foundation-Guided Pseudo-Label Weighting for Breast Ultrasound Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33843)
*Xinyu Fan,Xinyi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于冻结超声分割基础模型（AutoSAMUS）的连续伪标签加权，用于乳腺超声无监督域适应；EMA教师生成目标域伪标签，以有界权重[0.5,1]监督所有目标像素，测试阶段无需基础模型推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺超声分割模型在跨设备、跨机构和跨人群时泛化性差；此前的选择性BiRP规则只监督部分像素，且依赖测试时的基础模型推理，因此需要更简洁、覆盖全像素的加权方案并系统评估其有效性。

Method: EMA教师模型生成目标域伪标签；冻结的AutoSAMUS作为先验，将先验置信度与教师预测的概率一致性组合为有界连续权重（[0.5,1]），对所有目标像素加权监督。在5个公开乳腺超声数据集、3个源域、11个源-目标方向上进行评估，分别采用TransUNet与U-Net两种流程。

Result: TransUNet协议下：BUSI为源时4个目标均优于全像素EMA与原始BiRP；BUS-BRA为源时4个目标取得最高平均Dice与最低HD95；BrEaST为源时结果混合（BUSI最佳、OASBUD与原始BiRP相当、BUS-UCLM上所有域适应方法均不及仅源域训练）。U-Net流程中仅一致性加权与完整公式相当，先验置信度的增量价值未确定。

Conclusion: 连续基础模型引导的伪标签加权在多数源-目标方向上有效且无需测试时基础模型推理；但增益依赖源域与架构，先验置信度的独立贡献尚未证实；因加权规则在看部分目标测试结果后确定，结论应视为探索性。

Abstract: Breast ultrasound segmentation models often generalize poorly across scanners, institutions, and patient populations. We investigate continuous foundation-guided pseudo-label weighting for unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA). An exponential-moving-average (EMA) teacher generates target-domain pseudo-labels, while a frozen ultrasound segmentation model (AutoSAMUS) modulates their contribution using a bounded weight that combines the prior's certainty with its probabilistic agreement with the teacher. In contrast to our earlier selective BiRP rule, the proposed formulation supervises every target pixel with a weight in [0.5, 1] and requires no foundation-model inference at test time. We evaluate the method on five public breast ultrasound datasets, spanning three source domains and eleven source-target directions. Under the TransUNet protocol, continuous weighting outperforms both all-pixel EMA and original BiRP on all four BUSI-source targets, and achieves the highest mean Dice and lowest HD95 among the compared methods on all four BUS-BRA-source targets. Results with BrEaST as the source are mixed: continuous weighting performs best on BUSI and comparably to original BiRP on OASBUD, whereas none of the adaptation methods surpasses source-only training on BUS-UCLM. In a separate U-Net pipeline, agreement-only weighting performs comparably to the full formulation, leaving the incremental value of prior certainty unresolved. Because the weighting rule was developed after some target-test results had been examined, we treat the study as exploratory and report adverse as well as favorable outcomes.

</details>


### [86] [GeoTex: Geometry-as-Texture Encoding and History-Grounded 4D Occupancy Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33693)
*Shijun Guo,Yaming Yang,Haoran Xu,Wei Zhao,Ziyu Guan,Cai Xu,Peixi Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GeoTex占用世界模型，用"几何即纹理"编码联合建模列内高度模式与列间关系，并以历史锚定的预测机制保留观测证据，在参数量从3.47M降至2.33M的同时显著提升4D占用预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D占用预测需刻画3D场景演化以支撑自动驾驶世界模型的未来预测与场景生成。语义占用将颜色纹理抽象为离散标签，局部形状判别高度依赖3D体素排布；而现有方法沿用图像编码框架，通过高度通道化或平面分解获得紧凑隐表示，在压缩前未能充分联合建模列内高度模式与列间关系，削弱了这些几何线索。同时，预测阶段对互补历史的利用不足，且难以在自回归展开中保持可靠的观测证据。

Method: GeoTex包含两部分：(1) GeoTex Representation：将列内高度模式与列间关系统一编码为紧凑连续隐表示，并通过内容相关的高度展开与几何交互恢复3D结构；(2) GeoTex Prediction：以固定跨观测关系记忆在整个rollout中保留初始证据，并通过目标参照的残差更新引入互补的近期历史。预测训练完成后，采用decoder-only适配提升对预测状态的语义解码能力。

Result: 相对GenieDrive，平均预测mIoU提升9.79%、IoU提升11.91%，参数量由3.47M降至2.33M，且增益随预测时域延长而增大。

Conclusion: GeoTex表明，在压缩前显式联合建模占用几何结构、并在预测中锚定历史观测证据，可在更小模型规模下获得更强且长时域更稳健的4D占用预测性能，为占用世界模型提供了几何感知的编码与历史感知的预测范式。

Abstract: 4D occupancy forecasting models 3D scene evolution to support future prediction and scenario generation in autonomous-driving world models. Semantic occupancy abstracts image color and texture into discrete labels, leaving 3D voxel arrangements as the primary cues for distinguishing local shapes within each class. Existing methods borrow image-encoding frameworks to obtain compact latent representations through height channelization or plane factorization, but do not sufficiently model within-column height patterns and cross-column relations jointly before compression, weakening these geometric cues. Forecasting also underutilizes complementary history and struggles to retain reliable observation evidence. We therefore propose GeoTex, an occupancy world model combining geometry-as-texture encoding with history-grounded forecasting. GeoTex Representation jointly encodes within-column height patterns and cross-column relations into compact continuous latent representations and restores 3D structure through content-dependent height unfolding and geometric interaction. GeoTex Prediction retains initial evidence throughout rollout in fixed cross-observation relational memory and incorporates complementary recent history through target-referenced residual updates. After prediction training, decoder-only adaptation improves semantic decoding of predicted states. GeoTex improves average forecasting mIoU and IoU by 9.79% and 11.91% relative to GenieDrive while reducing parameters from 3.47M to 2.33M, with gains increasing at longer horizons. Code is included in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [87] [Look at What Matters: Learning Lesion Prototypes from Severity Labels for Domain-Generalized Diabetic Retinopathy Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33671)
*Ziyang Zhang,Juming Xiong,Yanfan Zhu,Yuechen Yang,Junchao Zhu,Zhengyi Lu,Chongyu Qu,Yiming Zhong,Yalin Zheng,Yuankai Huo,He Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种仅用图像严重度标签即可学习病灶证据的DR分级框架，通过多尺度病灶/背景原型竞争学习+加性序数评分，提升跨域泛化（LODO下QWK 0.761，领先最强基线0.036）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DR深度学习分级模型在未见成像域泛化差，因其预测可能依赖源数据集的特定相关性（捷径/伪相关）而非真实视网膜病理特征。

Method: ①以临床DR分级含义构造多示例约束，在多个尺度上竞争学习病灶与背景原型；②用加性序数评分模型，仅基于单个病灶原型响应预测严重度，取消全局特征分类通路，显式连接证据与决策；③引入教师引导的退化保持（teacher guided preservation）与抑制固定坐标偏好的空间正则化，提升证据可靠性。

Result: 在六个公开DR数据集、留一域外（LODO）协议下，平均二次加权kappa达0.761，超最强对比方法0.036；定位分析与原型移除实验显示学习响应与视网膜病灶对齐并对分级有贡献；在五种其他原型架构及图像退化条件下验证了通用性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于病灶原型证据的可解释加性评分框架能有效抑制数据集特异捷径、提升跨域泛化与鲁棒性，且与架构无关、具备通用性。

Abstract: Deep learning models for diabetic retinopathy (DR) grading often generalize poorly to unseen imaging domains, in part because predictions may exploit correlations specific to source datasets rather than retinal pathology. We address this challenge by constructing grading decisions from lesion evidence learned using only image severity labels. Clinical implications of DR grades provide multiple instance constraints that guide competitive learning of lesion and background prototypes at multiple scales. An additive ordinal scoring model then predicts severity exclusively from individual lesion prototype responses, explicitly connecting learned evidence with grading decisions without a global feature classification pathway. To improve the reliability of this evidence, we introduce teacher guided preservation under image degradations and spatial regularization against persistent coordinate preferences. Across six public DR datasets under a leave one domain out protocol, our method attains a mean quadratic weighted kappa of 0.761, outperforming the strongest competing method by 0.036. Localization and prototype removal analyses show that the learned responses align with retinal lesions and contribute to grading decisions. Experiments across five other prototype architectures and under image degradations further demonstrate the generality and robustness of the approach.

</details>


### [88] [Linear-Time Online Visual Geometry Transformer with Hybrid Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33650)
*Haoyu Kang,Pingzhi Li,Ruichen Zhang,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LinearVGGT 是一种从 StreamVGGT 蒸馏得到的混合架构，用 Mamba2 层承担大部分序列建模、少量寄存器引导稀疏注意力补偿长程记忆，实现恒定成本、每帧约 27 ms、对 1024 帧近常数延迟的在线 3D 重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Transformer 类多视图重建模型（VGGT、π³）依赖全局注意力，StreamVGGT 虽支持在线视频但缓存全部历史 token，总计算量随流长度二次增长；CUT3R、TTT3R 等循环模型虽保持固定状态、成本恒定，却会逐步遗忘早期几何信息。作者希望同时获得恒定成本与长程几何记忆。

Method: 提出 LinearVGGT：在每四个全局层中的三层用 Mamba2 做高效序列建模，剩余层采用寄存器引导的稀疏注意力，仅检索少量相关历史帧以弥补 Mamba2 精确长程召回能力的不足；并以蒸馏方式从 StreamVGGT 迁移知识（StreamVGGT 本身由 VGGT 蒸馏而来），显著降低训练开销。

Result: 蒸馏仅需 StreamVGGT 从 VGGT 学习所用 GPU 小时的 9%、训练帧数的 31%。在 1024 帧序列上保持近乎恒定的每帧 27 ms 延迟，平均比 StreamVGGT 快 12×；精度上超过其教师 StreamVGGT，并与当前最优流式模型 LingBot-Map 相当，同时速度快 2.5×、内存少 2.7×。

Conclusion: 通过 Mamba2 与稀疏检索注意力的混合设计并结合蒸馏，LinearVGGT 在在线 3D 重建中同时实现了恒定计算/延迟、有效长程几何保持与高精度，验证了线性序列模型加少量精确检索是替代全缓存因果注意力的高效方案。

Abstract: Transformer-based 3D reconstruction models such as VGGT and $π^3$ predict cameras, depth and point maps directly from many input views. Causal attention models such as StreamVGGT extend them to online video but cache all past tokens, so their total compute grows quadratically with the stream length. Recurrent models such as CUT3R and TTT3R keep a fixed-size state at constant cost, but they progressively forget early geometry. We present LinearVGGT, a hybrid model distilled from StreamVGGT, which uses Mamba2 layers for efficient sequence modeling in three of every four global layers. The remaining layers use register-guided sparse attention to retrieve a few relevant past frames, compensating for the limited exact long-range recall of Mamba2. Our distillation needs only 9\% of the GPU hours and 31\% of the training frames that StreamVGGT uses to learn from VGGT. LinearVGGT keeps an almost constant latency of 27 ms per frame over 1,024 frames and is on average $12 \times$ faster than StreamVGGT. It outperforms its teacher and is competitive with the state-of-the-art streaming model LingBot-Map, while running $2.5\times$ faster and using $2.7\times$ less memory.

</details>


### [89] [EgoCARBS: Egocentric Video Meets Active Acoustics for Kitchen Action Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33565)
*Saif Mahmud,Vineet Parikh,Bharath Hariharan,Cheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 EgoCARBS：一个将主动声学传感（智能手表扬声器发出人耳不可闻的 chirp、麦克风接收回波）与第一人称视频、第三人称视频同步的多模态数据集与基准，含 15 名参与者在自家厨房无脚本烹饪的 11.4 小时数据与 4,019 条动宾动作标注，并研究可穿戴声学传感对第一人称视频的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称视频只记录"人看什么"，手部出画、手臂运动不可见；第三人称相机可补足该上下文但需在活动场所预先部署。主动声学传感可由可穿戴设备自身从回波中恢复手、臂及附近物体运动，然而此前没有公开数据集将其与第一人称视频配对，因此无法量化其价值。

Method: 构建 EgoCARBS：(1) 15 名参与者佩戴摄像眼镜与现成智能手表，在各自厨房无脚本烹饪，同步采集第一人称视频、第三人称视频、手表音频与 IMU，主动声学由设备扬声器发射不可闻 chirp 并由麦克风接收回波；共 11.4 小时、4,019 条动宾动作标注，并提供采集与标注工具及预训练模型。(2) 以动词识别为切入，对比可穿戴传感与第三人称相机对第一人称视频的补充效果，并做消融分析（仅用主动声学、按手部是否出画分层）。

Result: 动词识别上，对训练集中出现过的参与者，可穿戴传感带来的增益至少与第三人称相机相当；对未见过的参与者增益更大，而第三人称相机对后者无效。该增益主要来自主动声学本身，且在手部不在画面中时帮助最大。裁剪后的动作识别、检测与预测仍为开放问题。

Conclusion: 可穿戴主动声学传感是一种自包含、无需环境部署的补充模态，能有效弥补第一人称视频中手与手臂运动的缺失，尤其利于跨参与者泛化与手部出画场景；作者将公开数据、采集标注工具与预训练模型以推动后续研究。

Abstract: Egocentric video records what a person looks at, but often misses what their body does: the hands leave the frame, and arm motion goes unseen. An exocentric camera recovers this context, but it must be installed wherever the activity happens. Active acoustic sensing can recover it from the wearables themselves: a device's speaker emits inaudible chirps, and its microphone senses the motion of the hands, arms, and nearby objects from their echoes. Yet no public dataset pairs it with egocentric video. We present EgoCARBS, with two deliverables. The first is a multimodal dataset and benchmark: 15 participants cook unscripted meals in their own kitchens wearing camera glasses and an off-the-shelf smartwatch that both sense with active acoustics, synchronized with egocentric and exocentric video and the watch's audio and IMU, with 4,019 verb-noun action annotations in 11.4 hours. The second is a study of what the wearables add to egocentric video. On verb recognition, they add at least as much as an exocentric camera for participants seen in training, and even more for new participants, for whom the exocentric camera does not help. Active acoustics alone provides most of this gain, and helps most where the camera does not see the hands. Trimmed action recognition, detection, and anticipation remain open. We will release the data, our data collection and annotation tools, and pre-trained models.

</details>


### [90] [ArtLang-4D: Articulation-Aware Dynamic Language Fields from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33511)
*Sylvia Yuan,Dan Wang,Ravi Ramamoorthi,Xinrui Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ArtLang-4D 从单目 RGB-D 视频重建铰接物体，并用"关节状态时间 t"（而非视频时间）索引动态语言场，实现状态敏感的语言查询与命令执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 语言场每点仅附一个特征，无法区分"抽屉拉到最外""门开一半"等状态性描述；4D 语言场以视频时间条件化，缺乏运动学含义且用户无法设定。

Method: 几何为规范三角面场，三角面随带类型的螺旋关节运动，关节无需初始化、从视频中发现；语言场由带 t 标签的比较性状态短语字幕库监督，文本空间为白化开放词表；自由查询通过在部件与状态上联合最大化部件相关性回答，同时返回部件及关节状态，命令则仅沿该部件恢复的关节运动执行。

Result: 在 Articulate-100 的 100 个物体上，关节与部件几何精度优于重建与铰接基线；状态检索可靠性显著优于状态无关语言场；语言命令执行的运动误差更低。

Conclusion: 以关节状态时间替代视频时间作为语言场索引，可有效解耦部件识别与状态描述，为铰接物体的语言驱动交互提供统一表示。

Abstract: Language that describes an articulated object is often about its state: the drawer pulled out the furthest, the door opened halfway, push the drawer all the way in. Existing 3D language fields attach one feature to each point and cannot tell these descriptions apart, and 4D language fields condition on video time, which has no kinematic meaning and cannot be set by a user. We present ArtLang-4D, which reconstructs an articulated object from a single monocular RGB-D video and equips it with a dynamic language field indexed by articulation time t, the normalized state of its joints, instead of video time. The geometry is a canonical triangle field whose triangles follow typed screw joints, discovered from the video without any joint initialization. The language field is supervised by a t-tagged caption bank that describes each part with comparative state phrases in a whitened open-vocabulary text space. A free-form query is answered by maximizing part relevance jointly over parts and states, which returns both the part and the articulation state it describes; a command is then executed by moving only that part along its recovered joint. On the 100 objects of Articulate-100, ArtLang-4D recovers more accurate joints and part geometry than reconstruction and articulation baselines, retrieves the queried state far more reliably than a state-agnostic language field, and executes language commands with lower motion error.

</details>


### [91] [Memory as the Backbone: A Dual-Store Associative Memory Architecture for Visual Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33475)
*Ruizhi Wang,Xiangtao Wang,Bayar Menzat,Jianfeng Wang,Thomas Lukasiewicz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DuSAM 提出以显式双存储（工作记忆 + 持久语义记忆）替代自注意力/状态空间模块，作为分层视觉骨干的组织原则，在分类、分割、检测任务上性能相当，且在高分辨率下吞吐更高、显存更省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究显式记忆能否作为通用视觉骨干的组织原则；受人类认知中工作记忆与长时记忆交互机制启发，希望将"上下文计算"与"可寻址语义记忆"统一设计。

Method: 构建双存储联想记忆骨干 DuSAM：(1) 图像局部工作记忆（WM）将局部视觉证据组织为紧凑、空间锚定的状态，跨阶段更新与传递，并回读以精化稠密特征；(2) 固定容量的持久语义记忆（PM）将训练图像与标注区域的语义证据固化为原型，检索所得内容用于增强同一工作状态。以这两种记忆操作取代自注意力或状态空间块。

Result: 在 ImageNet-1K 分类、ADE20K 语义分割、COCO 检测与实例分割上与同规模代表性骨干性能相当；消融显示两个存储均有贡献，PM 对标注像素较少的类别增益尤为明显；2048×2048 下完整 UPerNet 分割器吞吐为 DeiT-S/16 的 2.36 倍、显存减少 59.5%，为线性复杂度 VMamba-T 的 1.24 倍。记忆分析揭示 WM 的空间选择性访问及原型与训练区域的语义关联；类别定向移除与恢复实验表明可在不改参数的情况下通过状态更新补充类别特定经验。

Conclusion: 显式记忆可作为视觉骨干的可行组织原则，为上下文计算与可寻址语义记忆的联合设计奠定基础。

Abstract: We investigate whether explicit memory can serve as the organizing principle of a general-purpose visual backbone. Inspired by the interaction between working and long-term memory in human cognition, we introduce DuSAM (Dual-Store Associative Memory), a hierarchical memory-native visual backbone coupling image-local working memory (WM) with a fixed-capacity persistent semantic memory (PM). WM organizes local visual evidence into compact, spatially anchored states that are updated and transported across stages, then read back to refine dense features. PM consolidates semantic evidence from training images and annotated regions into prototypes, whose retrieved content enriches the same working states. With these memory operations in place of self-attention or state-space blocks, DuSAM achieves competitive performance relative to representative, similarly sized backbones on ImageNet-1K classification, ADE20K semantic segmentation, and COCO object detection and instance segmentation. Ablations show contributions from both stores, with PM particularly benefiting class groups with fewer annotated training pixels. At $2048\times2048$, the complete DuSAM-based UPerNet segmentor achieves $2.36\times$ the throughput of the evaluated DeiT-S/16 counterpart while using $59.5\%$ less GPU memory, and $1.24\times$ the throughput of the linear-complexity VMamba-T. Memory analyses reveal spatially selective WM access and semantic associations between retrieved prototypes and training regions. Class-targeted removal and restoration further demonstrate that category-specific experience can be replenished through state updates without changing network parameters. These results establish explicit memory as a viable organizing principle for visual backbones and provide a foundation for jointly designing contextual computation and addressable semantic memory.

</details>


### [92] [OrdiHand-GS: Ordinal-Guided Tracking and Hand-Refined Gaussian Avatars for Digital Signers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33465)
*Mingxuan Ma,Ronglai Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OrdiHand-GS，分阶段解决手语数字人双手交叠时的深度序歧义与神经渲染手部模糊问题，腕序准确率从32.9%提升至77.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字手语人（signing avatar）的视觉保真度与语言学可懂度主要取决于手部。当双手交叠时存在两类主导失败模式：一是单目表达性人体跟踪的序深度歧义，两只手均可满足同一组2D关键点，基线跟踪器在365帧标注交叠数据上腕部前后顺序仅达随机水平；二是即使几何被修正，神经渲染中的手部外观依然模糊。

Method: 将两类误差解耦处理。跟踪阶段：融合视频深度估计器的相对深度与手部表观尺寸投票，加入置信度加权与弃权机制，得到前后关系，并以中位手部表面深度的铰链约束形式注入表达性人体跟踪。渲染阶段：在avatar主干与渲染器冻结条件下，用源条件残差头细化手部相关UV高斯的局部位置。整体框架以GUAVA实例化。

Result: 相比匹配的无约束细化，腕序准确率由32.9%升至77.3%，代价为0.39像素的重投影误差；随机符号安慰剂实验无平衡准确率提升。2x2析因评估表明，交叠渲染改善主要归因于跟踪阶段细化，残差头带来较小的额外增益。

Conclusion: 通过深度序约束与源条件高斯残差的解耦式两阶段设计，可有效缓解双手交叠下的深度歧义与渲染模糊，且跟踪阶段细化是性能提升的主要来源。

Abstract: Digital signers, also known as signing avatars, convey sign language through animated virtual humans, where visual fidelity and linguistic intelligibility hinge primarily on the hands. When one hand crosses over the other, two failure modes dominate. First, monocular expressive-human tracking suffers from ordinal depth ambiguity: either hand can satisfy the same 2D landmarks, and the baseline tracker orders wrists at chance level on 365 annotated overlapping frames from How2Sign. Second, hand appearance remains blurred in neural rendering even after geometry is corrected. We propose OrdiHand-GS to address these errors separately. Relative depth from a video depth estimator and apparent hand size vote, with confidence weighting and abstention, for a front-back relation, which enters expressive-human tracking as a hinge on median hand-surface depth. A source-conditioned residual head then refines the local positions of hand-associated UV Gaussians with the avatar backbone and renderer frozen. We instantiate the framework with GUAVA. Against matched unconstrained refinement, wrist-order accuracy rises from 32.9% to 77.3% at a cost of 0.39 pixels of reprojection error, while a random-sign placebo gives no balanced-accuracy gain. A 2x2 factorial evaluation attributes most of the interaction-rendering improvement to tracking-stage refinement, with a smaller additional benefit from the residual.

</details>


### [93] [Beyond Random Starts: Amortized Initialization for Adversarial Attacks on Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33414)
*Haz Sameen Shahgir,Ali Asif Khan,K. M. Mehemud Azad,Dongwei Lyu,Zhipeng Wei,Rifat Shahriyar,Yufei Li,Xian Chen,Xiaohan Wei,Yunchen Pu,Fei Tian,Chonglin Sun,Frank Shyu,Sandeep Pandey,Luke Simon,Xi Liu,N. Benjamin Erichson,Yue Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPARK: 一个轻量级循环式扰动生成器，以源图和目标图为条件，通过摊销式学习为新输入快速生成对抗扰动，可作为梯度攻击的高效热启动，也可独立使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于梯度的可迁移对抗攻击对黑盒视觉-语言模型（VLM）成功率很高，但每对图像需数百次昂贵优化步。作者认为原因之一是攻击从随机扰动出发，不含源图或目标图信息，因此探索摊销式对抗生成能否提供更好的初始化。

Method: 提出SPARK：轻量级循环（looped）扰动生成器，同时以源图和目标图为条件；同一网络被反复应用以迭代精化扰动，仅需数次前向传播即可生成攻击，推理时无需对代理视觉编码器求梯度。

Result: 生成的扰动作为现有梯度攻击的有效热启动，显著提升攻击成功率并减少所需优化步数；改进在多种攻击方法、不同VLM及未见图像类别上均可泛化；SPARK 亦可作为独立攻击，计算成本仅为梯度优化的一小部分。

Conclusion: 摊销式扰动生成可为黑盒VLM对抗攻击提供高效初始化与独立攻击方案，兼顾成功率与计算效率，并具备跨方法、跨模型、跨类别的泛化性。代码与权重已开源。

Abstract: Gradient-based transferable adversarial attacks achieve state-of-the-art attack success rates against black-box vision-language models (VLMs). However, they require hundreds of costly optimization steps for each image pair. One possible reason is that these attacks typically start from random perturbations that contain no information about the source or target image. In this work, we study whether amortized adversarial generation can provide better initializations for gradient-based attacks. By learning across many image pairs, amortization allows a generator to produce adversarial perturbations for new inputs without optimizing each perturbation from scratch. We introduce SPARK, a lightweight looped perturbation generator conditioned on both the source and target images. SPARK repeatedly applies the same network to refine a perturbation, producing effective adversarial attacks in a few forward passes without requiring gradients through surrogate vision encoders at inference time. We show that these perturbations provide effective warm starts for existing gradient-based attacks, substantially improving attack success rates while reducing the number of optimization steps required. These improvements generalize across different attack methods, VLMs, and unseen image categories. SPARK also produces effective standalone attacks at a fraction of the computational cost of gradient-based optimization. Code and model weights are available at https://github.com/Patchwork53/SPARK.

</details>


### [94] [Synchronizing Cameras via 3D Object Relationships](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33387)
*Sujong Shin,Sang-Il Choi,Kwang Moo Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于物体间关系（3D距离等）的多视频时间同步方法，仅依赖各视角物体身份，配合VLM识别、开放词表分割跟踪与深度估计获得3D线索，辅以点轨迹2D运动，先成对后联合鲁棒优化求解时间偏移，在四个基准上超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有同步方法（面向动态重建与新视角合成）依赖像素级证据，如跨视角点对应或重建场景的光度损失；但动态场景中快速运动与遮挡使承载大量时序信息的运动区域难以跨视角匹配，导致同步不可靠。

Method: 回归"物体是相机全局推理可靠线索"的传统思路：以3D度量的物体间关系（如两人间距离）作为跨视角在尺度意义下稳定的对齐线索。仅需各视角中物体身份即可比较关系线索——用视觉语言模型看每个相机少量帧并提示其识别场景物体，再用开放词表分割器分割并逐视频跟踪；用预训练深度估计器获取物体3D位置；在深度或分割失败处，用点轨迹的2D运动补充3D线索；最后通过寻找使不同相机线索最一致的时间偏移完成同步，先逐相机对、再以鲁棒优化联合求解全部相机。

Result: 在四个标准基准上评测，性能优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 以关系型3D物体线索替代像素级证据进行多视频同步是有效且鲁棒的方案；物体身份即可支撑跨视角对齐，结合2D运动作为补充可提升在分割/深度失效情形下的稳健性。

Abstract: In this paper, we synchronize multiple videos of the same dynamic event through object relationships. Driven by dynamic reconstruction and novel view synthesis, many recent synchronization methods rely on pixel-level evidence, such as point correspondences across views or the photometric losses of a reconstructed scene. In dynamic scenes, however, fast motion and occlusions make the moving regions that carry much of the timing information hard to match across views. We thus propose to bring back the traditional wisdom that objects are reliable cues for global reasoning about cameras. Measured in 3D, the relationships between objects, such as the distance between two people, are stable across all views up to scale; they can serve as cues for alignment. To compare these relationships across cameras, we only need the identity of each object in every view, as the cues are relational. We obtain these identities by giving a vision-language model a few frames of every camera and prompting it to identify the objects in the scene. We then segment and track each identified object through every video with an open-vocabulary segmentor. To obtain the 3D locations of objects, we use a pretrained depth estimator. Where depth or segmentation fails, we complement these 3D cues with 2D motion from point tracks. Finally, we synchronize the cameras by finding the temporal offsets at which the cues of different cameras agree best, first for each camera pair and then for all cameras jointly with robust optimization. We evaluate our method on four standard benchmarks, outperforming the state of the art.

</details>


### [95] [Searching Ultra-Long Videos with Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33368)
*Ce Zhang,Baiqi Li,Seongsu Ha,Gedas Bertasius*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DeLoVi框架，用预训练决策模型替代LLM进行超长视频证据检索：DeLoVi-S以决策模型对片段字幕打相关性分数，取得三个超长视频基准SOTA；DeLoVi-E通过自动策略演化减少线性成本，平均减少34倍输入token，并在EgoLifeQA上比最佳先前方法快3倍以上且精度更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超长视频理解需从海量视频历史中检索稀疏的、与查询相关的证据。现有相似度检索独立编码问题与视频，缺乏对二者的联合推理，易漏检；生成式LLM虽可联合推理，但需多轮搜索并生成文本，速度慢、成本高。

Method: 提出DeLoVi框架，利用预训练决策模型同时读取问题与候选证据并直接输出相关性分数（不生成文本）。DeLoVi-S：为短视频片段生成字幕，用决策模型按问题相关性排序字幕，再由生成式LLM基于Top-ranked字幕作答。DeLoVi-E：引入自动化策略演化框架，围绕固定的预训练决策模型迭代优化记忆构建与检索策略，以降低随视频长度线性增长的成本。

Result: DeLoVi-S在EgoLifeQA、EgoMemReason、MM-Lifelong三个超长视频理解基准上达到SOTA，并有效泛化到VideoMME-L与LVBench。DeLoVi-E在保持相当性能的同时，平均减少34倍输入token；在EgoLifeQA上运行速度超过最佳先前方法3倍以上且精度更高。

Conclusion: 决策模型可作为超长视频证据检索的高效高性能替代方案，兼具LLM式的联合推理能力与无生成的效率优势；配合策略演化可进一步大幅压缩成本，兼顾精度与效率。

Abstract: Understanding ultra-long videos requires retrieving sparse, query-relevant evidence from extensive video histories. Existing similarity-based retrieval methods are efficient, but they encode the question and the video content independently and thus often miss evidence whose relevance requires reasoning over both. Generative large language models (LLMs) can perform such reasoning, but they typically generate text over multiple rounds of search, which makes each query slow and costly. In this work, we introduce DeLoVi (Decision Models for Long Video), a framework that leverages pretrained decision models to retrieve evidence from ultra-long videos. Like an LLM, a decision model reads the question together with the candidate evidence, so it can reason over both. Unlike an LLM, it directly outputs a relevance score without generating text, which makes it fast and inexpensive. We first present DeLoVi-S, a simple method that generates captions for short video clips and uses a decision model to rank those captions by their relevance to a question. A generative LLM then answers the question using the top-ranked captions. Despite its simplicity, DeLoVi-S achieves state-of-the-art performance on three ultra-long video understanding benchmarks, EgoLifeQA, EgoMemReason, and MM-Lifelong, and generalizes effectively to long-video understanding benchmarks, VideoMME-L and LVBench. However, DeLoVi-S scores every caption for every question, so its cost grows linearly with video length. To reduce this cost, we propose an automated policy evolution framework that iteratively refines the memory construction and retrieval policies around a fixed pretrained decision model. The resulting method, DeLoVi-E, maintains comparable performance while reducing input tokens by 34x on average compared with DeLoVi-S. It also runs over 3x faster than the best prior method on EgoLifeQA while achieving higher accuracy.

</details>


### [96] [What Does RL Post-Training Teach Text-to-Image Models? A Skill-Wise Analysis of Flow-GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33316)
*Cheng Zhang,Jiesong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文追问 Flow-GRPO 带来的 GenEval 提升中有多少本就存在于基座模型的样本中。作者在共享噪声种子上按技能分别测 pass@k，发现四种技能上是"锐化"（32 个基座样本已近乎全解，一个后训练样本约等于 4–10 个基座样本，pass@32 几乎不变），空间关系上是"解锁"（pass@32 从 0.69 升至 1.00，128 个基座样本也抵不上一个后训练样本，从未解出的提示 93% 被解出）。基准均值（31:1）无法描述任何单一技能。作者提出统一解释：prompt 内 group-relative 更新只能重加权已有结果且 flow 策略无法拓宽产生这些结果的噪声；prompt 间共享参数使部分提示的成功可修复多提示共有的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL 后训练（如 Flow-GRPO 将 SD3.5-M 的 GenEval 从 0.63 提升到 0.95）常被直接解读为"模型学会了组合生成"，但这一解读混淆了两件事：模型真的获得了新能力，还是仅仅把基座模型采样分布中本已存在的正确样本更高效地挑了出来。仅看单一指标的均值无法回答这一问题，需要按技能拆解增益的来源。

Method: 在训练中未使用的提示上，固定共享噪声种子，按 GenEval 六项技能逐一、训练前后分别测量 pass@k，以"一个后训练样本等价于多少基座样本"为度量。进一步做三类对照与检验：(1) 用奖励背后的 judge 在 8 个基座样本中做选择，考察其能回收多少增益；(2) 用无 KL 约束的 checkpoint 检验预测；(3) 检验 prompt 内多样性与受控训练（保持某些物体类别不参与训练），观察锐化与解锁的出现与迁移顺序。同时把同一分析迁移到文本渲染 checkpoint 上验证普适性。

Result: 六项技能中有四项（32 个基座样本已近乎解出全部提示）表现为锐化：1 个后训练样本约等于 4–10 个基座样本，pass@32 几乎不变。空间关系表现为解锁：pass@32 由 0.69 升至 1.00，128 个基座样本仍不及 1 个后训练样本，基座模型 32 次采样从未解出的提示被解出率达 93%，与曾解出的提示相当。基准均值 31:1 不描述任何单一技能。关系类技能贡献 35% 的增益；其余 65% 中，用奖励背后的 judge 在 8 个基座样本中择优即可回收约九成。无 KL 的 checkpoint、prompt 内多样性以及留出物体类别的受控训练均支持该解释，且锐化先出现并发生迁移，解锁随后出现，先在练过的物体上、后推广到未见物体。相同解剖结构也适用于文本渲染 checkpoint。

Conclusion: RL 后训练的基准提升并非单一机制：多数技能上是"锐化"——对基座分布中已有的正确结果重新加权；少数技能上是"解锁"——真正扩展了可达输出的支撑集。机制解释为：prompt 内 group-relative 更新只能重加权其样本所包含的结果，且 flow 策略无法拓宽产生这些结果的噪声；prompt 间共享参数则允许部分提示上的成功修复多提示共有的缺陷。因此应以按技能拆解的 pass@k 而非单一基准均值来评估 RL 后训练，因为均值会掩盖增益来源的异质性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training has become a standard way to improve text-to-image flow models: Flow-GRPO lifts the GenEval score of SD3.5-M from 0.63 to 0.95, a gain read as the model having learned compositional generation. We ask how much of that gain the base model's own samples already contained, by measuring pass@k over shared noise seeds, one skill at a time, before and after RL, on prompts never used in training. The answer differs by skill. On four of GenEval's six skills, where 32 base samples already solve almost every prompt, the gain is sharpening: one post-trained sample is worth 4 to 10 base samples, and pass@32 barely moves. On spatial relations it is unlocking: pass@32 rises from 0.69 to 1.00, 128 base samples do not match one post-trained sample, and prompts the base model never solved in 32 samples are solved 93% of the time, as often as those it solved. The benchmark average, 31 base samples per post-trained sample, describes no skill. Relations carry 35% of the gain; of the other 65%, selecting among 8 base samples with the judge behind the reward recovers nine tenths. We propose an account of when each occurs: within a prompt, a group-relative update re-weights only the outcomes its samples contain, and a flow policy cannot widen the noise that produces them; across prompts, shared parameters let successes on some prompts repair a deficit common to many. The same anatomy applies to a text-rendering checkpoint. The account's predictions are tested with a checkpoint trained without KL, within-prompt diversity, and controlled training that holds object classes out, where sharpening comes first and transfers, and unlocking follows, on practised objects before unseen ones.

</details>


### [97] [JetDiffusion: Pushing the Speed Limits of Video Generation with NVFP4 Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33268)
*Yulun Wu,Zihan Min,Minshen Zhang,Kaiqin Kong,Ji Xie,Will Lin,Junda Chen,Wenxuan Tan,Aryan Kumar,Loay Rashid,Qingyu Yin,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JetDiffusion 是一个基于可训练视频稀疏量化注意力（VSQA）的视频生成系统，结合 NVFP4 计算与算子融合的自定义推理内核，并通过分阶段训练配方实现稀疏、量化与少步蒸馏三者协同，在 Wan2.1-14B 上于 RTX 5090 取得 273 倍端到端加速且画质相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列注意力是视频生成的主要计算瓶颈。稀疏化与低比特量化理论上可缓解，但单独使用各技术尚可保持生成质量，三者联合适配（尤其在激进的 NVFP4 量化下）十分困难，缺乏稳定的训练方案。

Method: 提出可训练的 Video Sparse Quantized Attention（VSQA），并配套自定义推理内核，融合原生 NVFP4 计算与 kernel fusion；将 VSQA 与 NVFP4 线性量化、步蒸馏（step distillation）结合；通过实证研究设计分阶段（staged）训练配方，使模型稳定适配量化、稀疏与少步去噪。

Result: 在 90% 稀疏度下，VSQA 相较 FlashAttention 在不同硬件上实现 8–29 倍注意力加速；在 Wan2.1-14B 上，最终模型于 RTX 5090 获得 273 倍端到端视频生成加速，并保持可比拟的视频质量。

Conclusion: 稀疏注意力、NVFP4 量化与步蒸馏可通过精心设计的分阶段训练稳定联合，实现数量级的视频生成加速而不明显损失质量，验证了系统级协同优化（算法+内核）的可行性。

Abstract: Attention over long sequences is a major computational bottleneck in video generation, which can be alleviated through sparsity and low-bit quantization. We propose JetDiffusion, a video generation system built on trainable Video Sparse Quantized Attention (VSQA), with custom inference kernels that combine native NVFP4 computation and kernel fusion. At 90% sparsity, VSQA accelerates attention by 8-29x compared with FlashAttention on different hardwares. To further accelerate video generation, we combine VSQA with NVFP4 linear quantization and step distillation. Although each technique can individually preserve generation quality, jointly adapting to all three remains challenging, particularly under aggressive NVFP4 quantization. Through empirical studies, we develop an effective staged training recipe that enables stable adaptation to quantization, sparsity, and few-step denoising. On Wan2.1-14B, the resulting model achieves 273x end-to-end video generation speedup on the RTX 5090 while maintaining comparable video quality.

</details>


### [98] [DriftWAM: Faster World-Action Models via Action-Aware Video Drifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33797)
*Minyang Li,Siyu Xu,Yunke Wang,Ying-Cong Chen,Chang Xu,Daochang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DriftWAM：一种少步世界-动作模型（WAM）蒸馏框架，将预测视频所诱导的动作引入视频学习，通过动作感知的视频 drifting 与动作引导的视频正则化实现分布级对齐，在少步设定下优于现有一致性蒸馏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM 统一了未来视频预测与动作生成，但依赖迭代去噪导致推理成本高；蒸馏为少步生成器是高效部署的可行路径，然而在激进降步下保持任务性能仍困难。现有方法采用逐点轨迹一致性蒸馏，只追求视觉保真度，未在视频生成优化中利用下游动作信息，且缺乏显式的分布级对齐。

Method: 提出 DriftWAM，包含两个组件：（1）动作感知视频 drifting：通过联合视觉-动作相似度核进行视频分布匹配，在所诱导动作的引导下驱动样本演化；（2）动作引导视频正则化：利用联合核识别出的兼容教师-学生匹配，将动作级梯度直接反传至视频生成器。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 与 LIBERO 基准以及真实世界机器人操作任务上，DriftWAM 在相同推理速度的少步设定中一致优于先前的一致性蒸馏方法。

Conclusion: 将下游动作信息纳入视频蒸馏优化并结合分布级对齐，可在少步生成条件下维持甚至提升任务性能，为 WAM 的高效部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) unify future video prediction and action generation for robotic control, but their reliance on iterative denoising incurs substantial inference costs. Distilling these models into few-step generators offers a promising route to efficient deployment, yet maintaining task performance under aggressive step reduction remains challenging. Existing methods rely on point-wise trajectory consistency distillation, which pursues high visual fidelity while leaving downstream action information unexploited in optimizing video generation, and also lacks explicit distribution-level alignment. To address this, we propose DriftWAM, a few-step WAM distillation framework that incorporates the actions induced by predicted videos into video learning. Specifically, DriftWAM integrates action-aware video drifting, which performs video distribution matching through a joint visual--action similarity kernel to steer sample evolution under the guidance of these induced actions, with action-guided video regularization, which leverages the compatible teacher--student matches identified by the joint kernel to backpropagate action-level gradients directly into the video generator. Evaluations on the RoboTwin 2.0 and LIBERO benchmarks, as well as in real-world robotic manipulation tasks, show that DriftWAM consistently outperforms prior consistency distillation methods across few-step settings at the same inference speed.

</details>


### [99] [MonoQPM: Splitting Features into Concepts for Inherent Interpretability and Predictive Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33252)
*Thomas Norrenbrock,Robert Zimmermann,Timo Kaiser,Sovan Biswas,Neslihan Kose,Bodo Rosenhahn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出以干扰诱导激活误差下降衡量多义性的效用化指标，并据此刻画解耦后的MonoQPM作为更高效共形预测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多义性与叠加性通常耦合，且缺乏公认的多义性度量方法；QPM可在最终特征上天然避免叠加却仍表现多义性，为分离研究二者提供了理想载体。

Method: 以干扰引起的激活误差下降量构建效用导向的多义性度量，并以共形预测（CP）预测集大小的收紧程度体现其实用价值；将QPM的多义特征显式解耦为单义概念得到MonoQPM，并构建带共享概念真值标注的CUBCars数据集用于评测。

Result: 在所有测试架构上QPM均出现多义性，MonoQPM可有效缓解；在ImageNet上采用自适应预测集时，MonoQPM仅用冻结QPM 66%的预测集大小即保证88%覆盖率。

Conclusion: 多义性可独立于叠加性存在，解耦多义特征能显著提升共形预测效率，所提度量与新数据集为多义性研究提供了可量化、可验证的框架。

Abstract: Alongside superposition, polysemanticity is one of the primary obstacles to achieving interpretable deep neural networks. While these phenomena are typically intrinsically linked, the Quadratic Programming Enhanced Model (QPM) naturally decouples them. By representing classes with a sparse binary assignment of very few features, QPM prevents superposition by design on its final features but still exhibits polysemanticity. However, measuring polysemanticity is an open problem. This work proposes a utility-focused approach to measuring polysemanticity by quantifying the decrease in interference-induced activation errors, which yields the practical utility of tighter prediction sets using Conformal Prediction (CP). We explicitly disentangle the polysemantic features of QPM into monosemantic concepts to create the Monosemantic QPM (MonoQPM). Because its features are disentangled, MonoQPM acts as a significantly more efficient Conformal Predictor. Additionally, we introduce CUBCars, a dataset providing ground truth information about shared concepts. Using this and other datasets, we demonstrate that polysemanticity emerges in QPM across all tested architectures, but is effectively alleviated by MonoQPM. For instance, MonoQPM guarantees 88% coverage using Adaptive Prediction Sets on ImageNet with just 66% of the frozen QPM's set size.

</details>


### [100] [Where to Approximate: Understanding and Accelerating Looped Multi-View Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33948)
*Siyuan Zhang,Yuhan Chen,Yangyang Ning,Nan Wang,Jun Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文通过配对干预实验分析循环多视图重建中近似误差的注入、传播与输出影响，据此提出降低空间分辨率、早期池化KV、交错注意力输出复用并结合自蒸馏的加速方法，在五个基准上实现1.63×加速且平均pose AUC@3°仅降1.86点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环式多视图重建通过反复应用共享transformer块细化场景特征，计算开销大；要降低单步更新成本，必须理解某一步的近似如何影响后续细化过程，而现有工作缺乏对近似误差注入、传播与输出影响的分层分析。

Method: 在Déjà View与堆叠重建transformer上设计配对干预实验，分离近似注入的误差、误差传播方式及其对输出的影响；采用尺度调整的状态误差度量比较近似误差与匹配随机扰动，并用局部Jacobian分解区分通道门控贡献与对空间误差结构的响应；基于发现设计方法：细化早期降低空间分辨率并池化key/value，交错使用注意力输出复用与完整注意力，并通过轻量自蒸馏进行适配。

Result: Déjà View中近似误差在尺度调整度量下比匹配随机扰动衰减更强；空间近似若发生在早期可被后续细化受益，而跨步复用效果取决于缓存量与复用步间隔；在相同最终范数下，近似误差对预测旋转的改变是随机误差的1.55倍。五基准上平衡配置比官方16步Déjà View快1.63×，平均pose AUC@3°仅降1.86点；同等延迟下较使用完整细化块的训练基线插值曲线高4.73点AUC@3°，几何指标亦有提升。

Conclusion: 对循环重建中近似误差的机理分析可直接指导加速设计：早期空间近似配合KV池化、注意力输出复用与自蒸馏，可在较小精度损失下显著提速，并在等延迟条件下优于全块细化基线。

Abstract: Looped multi-view reconstruction refines scene features by repeatedly applying a shared transformer block. Making these updates cheaper requires knowing how an approximation at one step affects the rest of the refinement. We study this with paired interventions in Déjà View and stacked reconstruction transformers, separating the error an approximation injects, how that error propagates, and how it affects the output. In Déjà View, approximation errors are attenuated more strongly than matched random perturbations under a scale-adjusted state-error measure, and a local Jacobian decomposition separates the contribution of channel gating from the response to spatial error structure. Spatial approximations made early benefit from subsequent refinement, whereas cross-step reuse depends on which quantity is cached and how reuse steps are spaced. At equal final norm, approximation errors change the predicted rotations 1.55 times as much as random errors. These findings guide our proposed method, which reduces spatial resolution and pools keys and values early in refinement, then interleaves attention-output reuse with full attention, and is adapted through lightweight self-distillation. Across five reconstruction benchmarks, the balanced configuration runs $1.63\times$ faster than the official 16-step Déjà View model with a 1.86-point reduction in average pose AUC@$3^\circ$. At matched latency, it exceeds the interpolated curve of a trained baseline using full refinement blocks by 4.73 points in AUC@$3^\circ$ and improves the corresponding geometry metrics.

</details>


### [101] [Concept Generalization in Visual Brain Decoding via Encoder-Generated fMRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33870)
*Peter Yongho Kim,Juhyeon Park,Dongsheng Li,Yansen Wang,Dongqi Han,Mingqing Xiao,Taesup Moon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究fMRI-to-image解码器的概念泛化问题：现有解码器在未见语义概念上性能显著下降，而直接映射预训练视觉表征到体素的brain encoder更鲁棒；作者用encoder生成合成fMRI数据增强解码器训练，可恢复大部分泛化损失且无需额外采集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉脑解码器通常在相似语义分布上训练与评估，对未见概念的泛化能力缺乏理解；论文希望系统研究该"概念泛化"问题，并寻找无需额外耗时fMRI采集的改进途径。

Method: 从目标被试训练数据中剔除特定语义概念以构造语义留出实验；比较三种fMRI-to-image解码器与直接将预训练视觉表征映射到体素的brain encoder在留出概念上的表现；用该encoder为新图像生成合成fMRI响应，构建图像-fMRI对并加入解码器训练；在三种解码器、四名被试、十二个语义概念上评估，并分析encoder训练条件、encoder设计与合成数据控制条件。

Result: 三种解码器在留出概念上性能大幅下降，即使该概念在多被试模型中仍可用；brain encoder对语义留出明显更鲁棒。合成数据在三种解码器、四名被试、十二个概念上恢复了未见概念上相当一部分性能损失，并在较宽的数据预算范围内提升整体解码表现。控制实验表明增益依赖encoder泛化能力与合成监督的语义内容，而非单纯增加训练数据量。

Conclusion: encoder生成的fMRI可将语义泛化能力迁移给解码器，为提升脑解码器对未见概念的泛化提供了一种不依赖额外fMRI采集的有效方案。

Abstract: Visual brain decoders are typically trained and evaluated on stimuli drawn from similar semantic distributions, leaving their ability to reconstruct unseen concepts poorly understood. We study this concept generalization problem by withholding semantic concepts from a target subject's training data. We find that three recent fMRI-to-image decoders degrade substantially for the held-out concepts, even when the concept remains available for multi-subject models. In contrast, brain encoders that map a pretrained visual representation directly to voxels are markedly more robust to the same semantic holdout. Motivated by this asymmetry, we use such an encoder to generate synthetic fMRI responses for new images and add the resulting image-fMRI pairs to decoder training. Across three decoders, four subjects, and twelve semantic concepts, synthetic data recovers a substantial fraction of the performance lost on unseen concepts without requiring additional, time-consuming fMRI acquisition. We further show that the same approach improves general decoding performance across a wide range of data budgets. Analyses of encoder training conditions, encoder designs, and synthetic-data controls show that the gains depend on both encoder generalization and the semantic content of the synthetic supervision, rather than merely increasing the amount of training data. Together, our results show that encoder-generated fMRI can transfer semantic generalization to decoders.

</details>


### [102] [3D CoCa v3: Contrastive Learners with Scene Graphs for Urban Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33835)
*Nannan Zhang,Ting Huang,Zeyu Zhang,Haoyu Chen,Yanjie Zhu,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出3D CoCa v3，一个场景图增强的对比+生成式框架用于城市3D场景理解，结合金字塔视觉编码器、问题引导的caption检索与视觉交叉注意力，并引入基于场景图的关系统描述以增强3D-语言对齐，同时构建City3D-9.6K多视角城市视觉-语言数据集，在City-3DQA上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模3D城市场景理解需要同时刻画多尺度几何结构与复杂空间关系。现有3D视觉-语言方法主要聚焦全局语义对齐或物体级表示，在统一的对比与生成框架内对结构化关系建模探索不足。

Method: 提出3D CoCa v3：(1) 金字塔视觉编码器进行多尺度几何表示学习；(2) 基于语言模型的解码器，含问题引导的caption检索与视觉交叉注意力；(3) 引入基于场景图（MSG）的关系描述，以结构化空间关系丰富对比式3D-语言对齐；(4) 构建City3D-9.6K多视角城市视觉-语言数据集，提供对齐的视觉-几何观测、场景caption与场景图监督。

Result: 在City-3DQA上，句级（sentence-wise）与城市级（city-wise）划分的总体top-1准确率分别为88.35%和64.79%，较3D CoCa v2分别提升20.88和3.57个百分点；在GeoEval3D上取得最佳性能，体现其在城市空间推理与几何理解上的有效性。

Conclusion: 将场景图结构化的关系描述融入统一的对比-生成框架，可有效提升城市3D场景的多尺度几何与空间关系理解能力，配合多视角数据集监督在城市VQA与空间推理任务上均取得领先表现。

Abstract: Understanding large-scale 3D urban scenes requires representations that capture both multi-scale geometric structures and complex spatial relationships. However, existing 3D vision-language methods primarily emphasize global semantic alignment or object-level representations, leaving structured relational modeling underexplored within unified contrastive and generative frameworks. In this paper, we present 3D CoCa v3, a scene-graph-enhanced contrastive and generative framework for urban 3D scene understanding. Our framework features a Pyramid Vision Encoder for multi-scale geometric representation learning and a language-model-based decoder with question-guided caption retrieval and visual cross-attention. We further introduce MSG-based relational descriptions to enrich contrastive 3D-language alignment with structured spatial relationships. To support semantic and relational supervision, we construct City3D-9.6K, a multi-view urban vision-language dataset with aligned visual-geometric observations, scene captions, and scene graphs. Extensive experiments on City-3DQA demonstrate that 3D CoCa v3 achieves 88.35% and 64.79% overall top-1 accuracy on the sentence-wise and city-wise splits, surpassing 3D CoCa v2 by 20.88 and 3.57 percentage points, respectively. Furthermore, 3D CoCa v3 achieves the best performance on GeoEval3D, demonstrating its effectiveness in urban spatial reasoning and geometric understanding. Code is available at https://github.com/AIGeeksGroup/3DCoCav3.

</details>


### [103] [AVERT: Anomalous Value Erasure with Response Testing against Visual Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33532)
*Newt Hue-Nam K. Nguyen,Jui-Yu Ho,Wei-Han Chen,Pin-Yen Ting,Yangqiu Song,Shang-Tse Chen,Jun-Cheng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AVERT：仅在模型答案依赖异常特征方向时才移除该方向，从而在不牺牲良性效用的前提下防御视觉提示注入攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM易受视觉提示注入攻击：对抗扰动图像可迫使模型输出攻击者指定内容（恶意工具调用、链接、广告），甚至无视用户问题。现有防御（扩散净化、图像token掩码）在良性输入上造成显著效用损失，难以兼顾安全与可用性。

Method: 核心思路是"按需移除对抗性特征方向"，包含两步：(1) 识别异常值子空间——将每个输入的注意力头输出统计量与干净图像比较，提取偏离最大的方向；(2) 检测答案依赖性——由于相同方向也承载良性视觉信息，仅对依赖这些方向的答案执行投影移除，判据为：移除后答案似然坍塌，或该答案在无关问题下与用户问题下似然相当。

Result: 在4个VLM（Qwen2-VL、Qwen2.5-VL、LLaVA、InternVL2.5）与4个注入基准上进行白盒攻击测试，平均攻击成功率从86–90%降至最高0.6%，同时在13个基准上平均保留至少96%的无防御效用；防御-效用F分数F_DU为98.0–99.1，在所有模型的全部对比防御中均最高。

Conclusion: AVERT通过"仅在答案依赖时移除对抗方向"的选择性干预，实现了近乎完全的攻击阻断与高良性效用保留，显著优于现有防御，并在多模型、多基准上表现一致。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) are vulnerable to visual prompt injection, in which an adversarially perturbed image forces the model to respond with the attacker's chosen content, such as malicious tool calls, website links, or advertisements, sometimes regardless of the user's question. Existing defenses, such as diffusion-based purification and image-token masking, mitigate these attacks at a substantial cost to the model's utility on benign inputs.
