To address this challenge, we propose AVERT, which removes adversarial feature directions only when the model's answer depends on them. Our key procedures involve identifying anomalous value subspaces and detecting answer dependence. To identify the anomalous value subspace, we compare the statistics of each input's attention-head outputs with those of clean images and extract the directions along which the input deviates the most. We then project out these directions to remove the injected content. Because the same directions also carry benign visual information, we apply the removal only to answers that depend on them, namely answers whose likelihood collapses once the directions are removed or that are as likely under unrelated questions as under the user's question. Against white-box attacks on four VLMs (Qwen2-VL, Qwen2.5-VL, LLaVA, InternVL2.5) and four injection benchmarks, AVERT lowers average attack success from 86-90% to at most 0.6% while retaining at least 96% of undefended utility averaged over thirteen benchmarks. Its defense-utility F-score ($F_{DU}$), the harmonic mean of attacks stopped and utility retained, is 98.0-99.1 and the highest among all compared defenses on every model.

</details>


### [104] [geo-eval-harness: An Evaluation Harness for Geospatial Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33990)
*Nils Lehmann,Caleb Robinson,Isaac Corley,Jennifer Marcus,Inbal Becker-Reshef,Juan M. Lavista Ferres,Hannah Kerner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出开源评测框架 geo-eval-harness，在固定设置下对比 100 个地理空间基础模型（GFM）与通用视觉模型，揭示受控评测下结论差异、结果不确定性大、模型规模与性能弱相关，并以部署算力量化精度差异的实际代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GFM 研究普遍存在评测设置不一致（预处理、探针方式不同）的问题，导致难以区分性能提升究竟来自模型本身还是评测选择，缺乏可比性。

Method: 构建开放式受控评测框架 geo-eval-harness；在 6 个分类与 14 个分割数据集上，对 100 个 GFM 与通用视觉模型进行评测，采用多种探针（冻结编码器或低秩适配 LoRA）；统计前十里任两模型在单数据集上的 95% 置信区间重叠比例；分析 inference FLOPs 与分割性能的相关性；将推理算力折算为大范围制图工作负载成本。

Result: 受控评测下，通用视觉骨干在 RGB 分类与高分辨率分割上具竞争力，GFM 在多光谱任务上占优；前十分类模型中任两者 95% 置信区间重叠率达 73%；模型更大并非更好，FLOPs 与分割性能弱相关；在 SpaceNet2 冻结编码器设定下，0.9 个点 mIoU 差异对应 5.9 倍算力与额外数天的 GPU 时间。

Conclusion: 强调需在显式固定设置下比较模型，并在报告精度同时给出不确定性与部署算力；geo-eval-harness 使受控实验简便、快速且完全可复现，代码、配置与结果已开源。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation model (GFM) research papers are plagued by inconsistent evaluation settings (e.g., different pre-processing and probes), making it difficult to separate performance from evaluation choices. We propose geo-eval-harness}, an open-source framework for controlled model comparisons. We use this harness to evaluate 100 GFMs and general vision models on 6 classification and 14 segmentation datasets, using multiple probes on frozen or low-rank-adapted encoders. We show that under controlled evaluation, general vision backbones are competitive on RGB classification and high-resolution segmentation, but GFMs shine on multispectral tasks. When we compare any two of the top-ten classification models on a single dataset, their 95% confidence intervals overlap 73% of the time. We show that bigger models are not always better: inference FLOPs correlate weakly with segmentation performance. Moreover, translating inference compute into large-area mapping workloads shows how small accuracy differences have outsized deployment cost differences: with frozen encoders on SpaceNet2, a 0.9-point mIoU difference corresponds to 5.9x the compute and days of additional projected GPU time. Our findings stress the importance of comparing models under explicit and fixed settings, and reporting uncertainty and deployment compute alongside accuracy. Our geo-eval-harness makes controlled experiments easy, fast, and fully reproducible. We release the code, configurations, and results at https://github.com/torchgeo/geo-eval-harness.

</details>


### [105] [Streaming Tax: Can Streaming VLMs Replace Their Offline Parents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33474)
*Zhi Yang,Junzhou Chen,Jindong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Streaming Tax 框架系统评估流式 VLM 在离线任务上对离线父模型的替代性，发现理解保持与实用可替代性之间存在显著差距，并提出免训练插件 Stream++ 改进流式 VLM 的离线表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式 VLM 部署成本更低、效率更高，若能替代离线父模型执行离线任务可降低开销；但现有工作多关注流式交互能力，缺乏对"流式版本能否真正替代离线版本"的系统性验证。

Method: 提出 Streaming Tax 评估体系，从理解、可信性、泛化三个核心维度在 15 个基准上对比流式 VLM 与其离线父模型；并分析性能损失的成因（历史视觉证据访问受限、无法给出可用响应等）。

Result: 发现离线能力保持与实际可替代性之间存在关键差距，且出现在两个阶段：流式适配即使在大体保留离线理解能力时也会改变可信行为，原生流式协议进一步带来性能损失；安全方面，流式模型往往对有害与合法请求均不作答，而非真正拒答不安全请求。

Conclusion: 流式 VLM 尚不能简单替代离线父模型；提出免训练的 Stream++ 插件以提升流式 VLM 在离线任务上的表现。

Abstract: Most vision-language models (VLMs) specialize in offline video understanding; in contrast, their post-trained versions, streaming VLMs, can perform live interactions. We investigate whether streaming VLMs can replace their offline parent models on offline tasks. Such replacement could reduce deployment costs and improve efficiency. We propose Streaming Tax to systematically evaluate streaming VLMs on offline tasks. Our exploration spans three core dimensions: understanding, trustworthiness, and generalization across 15 benchmarks. Our findings reveal a critical gap between offline capability preservation and practical replaceability. This gap emerges at two stages: streaming adaptation can alter trustworthy behavior even when offline understanding is largely preserved, while the native streaming protocol can introduce further performance losses. Our analyses show that some of these losses arise from restricted access to historical visual evidence and failures to deliver usable responses. Importantly, regarding safety, streaming models often leave both harmful and legitimate requests unanswered rather than genuinely refusing unsafe ones. We then propose Stream++, a training-free plugin to improve streaming VLMs on offline tasks.

</details>


### [106] [SplatWAM: Splatting World Action Models for Real-Time 3D Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33467)
*Frank Tsu-Fang Lu,Karthik Sundar Senthilkumar,Christopher Thirgood,Oscar Mendez,Simon Hadfield*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SplatWAM 是一种直接在紧凑 3D Gaussian 基元上学习动作条件动力学的生成式世界模型，通过基于高斯 token 的分词器与动力学模型实现显式 3D 状态预测，并以可微渲染监督，在渲染速度、新视角质量与策略性能上均优于像素/潜空间方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型通常在像素或潜空间中预测未来观测，难以直接表征真实三维场景的演化过程，缺乏统一的显式 3D 表示，导致跨视角一致性与渲染效率受限。

Method: 提出 SplatWAM 框架：SplatWAM-T 分词器将数量可变的无位姿视图编码为固定预算的高斯 token；SplatWAM-D 动力学模型在单次前向中对每个动作预测未来高斯状态，采用 log-Euclidean 协方差坐标并以可微渲染作为监督；预测状态可从任意视角光栅化，天然保证跨视角共享 3D 表示。另提出 SplatWAM-P，一个与动力学模型联合训练的轻量 flow-matching 策略头。

Result: 在 OGBench Scene 上，新视角渲染速度约为视频世界模型的 12 倍（RTX 5090 上约 80 FPS），PSNR 最高提升 2.0 dB；在 8 个任务套件上，无模型的高斯 token 策略较像素策略最高提升 24.8 个百分点；SplatWAM-P 较 LPWM 提升 12 个百分点，并在无学习价值函数情况下与 HIQL 持平；在 LIBERO 上，约 1B 参数的模型优于 WorldVLA（约 7B）和机器人预训练的 OpenVLA（约 7B），在均不使用大规模机器人预训练时与 OpenVLA-OFT 相当。

Conclusion: 在显式 3D Gaussian 空间中建模动作条件动力学，可兼顾高效渲染、跨视角一致的新视角合成与强策略学习能力，为参数规模更小却性能具竞争力的具身世界模型提供了可行路径，代码与权重将开源。

Abstract: World models typically predict future observations in pixel or latent space, limiting their ability to directly represent true 3D scene evolution. We present SplatWAM (Splatting World Action Model), a generative world model that learns action-conditioned dynamics directly over compact 3D Gaussian primitives. A tokenizer SplatWAM-T first encodes a variable number of unposed views into a fixed budget of Gaussian tokens. A dynamics model SplatWAM-D then predicts future Gaussian states in a single forward pass per action, using log-Euclidean covariance coordinates and differentiable rendering supervision to facilitate learning in an explicit 3D space. Each predicted state can be rasterized from arbitrary viewpoints, enforcing a shared 3D representation across views by construction. On OGBench Scene, SplatWAM renders novel views 12x faster than video world models (~80 FPS on an RTX 5090) with up to 2.0 dB higher PSNR. We further introduce SplatWAM-P, a lightweight flow-matching policy head jointly trained with the dynamics model. Across 8 task suites, model-free Gaussian-token policies outperform pixel-based counterparts by up to 24.8 percentage points. SplatWAM-P improves over Latent Particle World Model (LPWM) by 12 percentage points and matches HIQL without a learned value function. On LIBERO, our ~1B model outperforms WorldVLA (~7B) and the robot-pretrained OpenVLA (~7B), while matching OpenVLA-OFT when both are trained without large-scale robot pretraining. Code and checkpoints will be released.

</details>


### [107] [FoamSurf: A Voronoi-Surfel Representation for Differentiable Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33462)
*Grzegorz Wilczyński,Julia Farganus,Krzysztof Byrski,Rafał Tobiasz,Mikołaj Zieliński,Dominik Belter,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FoamSurf：用平面高斯splat表示表面、Voronoi元胞表示介质，单一可微光线追踪沿任意光线合成，实现表面与介质的准确分离及独立编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散射介质（水、雾、烟）场景中，高斯方法在纯表面渲染中残留介质痕迹；神经场虽分离更准确但训练慢、难编辑；且两者仅沿相机光线求值介质，未建模反射/折射光线的介质效应。这阻碍了可靠的表面恢复、表面与介质的独立编辑以及插入物体的一致渲染。

Method: 提出混合表示：表面用平面高斯surfel，介质用空间填充的Voronoi元胞；用单一可微光线追踪器沿任意光线合成两者；借助训练先验在无分割掩码情况下保持两者分离。

Result: 在真实水下场景、合成场景和浓雾中，图像质量与SOTA相当，表面-介质分离精度在对比方法中最高；组件可独立移除、编辑或风格化，同一重建支持插入网格物体并在直接与反射/折射光线上正确表现衰减与散射。

Conclusion: 在单一重建模型中统一实现场景恢复、编辑与合成，克服了仅沿相机光线建模介质的局限。

Abstract: Scenes inside a scattering medium contain solid surfaces, such as the seabed or rocks, and a semi-transparent medium, such as water, fog, or smoke. Recent Gaussian-based methods produce realistic images of scenes in scattering media, but traces of the medium can remain visible in surface-only renders. Neural fields separate surfaces and medium more accurately, but they are slow to train and difficult to edit. Both approaches evaluate the medium only along camera rays, so its effects along reflected and refracted rays are not modeled. These limitations prevent reliable surface recovery, independent editing of surfaces and the medium, and consistent rendering of inserted objects. We present FoamSurf, a hybrid representation that models surfaces with planar Gaussian surfels and the medium with space-filling Voronoi cells. A single differentiable ray tracer composites both along arbitrary rays, while training priors keep them separate without segmentation masks. Across real underwater scenes, synthetic scenes, and dense fog, FoamSurf achieves image quality on par with the state of the art and the most accurate surface-medium separation among the compared methods. The recovered components can be removed, edited, or stylized independently, and the same reconstruction supports inserted mesh objects with attenuation and scattering along both direct and reflected or refracted rays. Together, these capabilities enable scene restoration, editing, and composition within a single reconstructed model.
  Project page: https://grzegorzwilczynski.com/foamsurf/

</details>


### [108] [ArtTet: Watertight Part Meshes of Articulated Objects from Rigid-Body Rules and Partitioned Marching Tetrahedra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33382)
*Sylvia Yuan,Dan Wang,Ravi Ramamoorthi,Xinrui Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ArtTet 从两个关节状态的多视角观测重建铰接物体，将物体划分为刚性部件并输出各部件的水密网格及连接关节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 mesh splatting 方法虽能给出显式三角形网格，但把物体当作单一场景处理，仅提取全局表面，导致不同部件三角形无法分离，导出网格出现层叠、自相交和孔洞。

Method: 分而治之策略：先用动态与几何约束的铰接及归属推断把物体划分为刚性部件，通过两状态的多体配准恢复部件及其螺旋关节，并利用附着、接触与扫掠运动的刚体规则在无部件标注下修正关节类型与部件标签；再将空间划分为部件胞元（铰接四面体分割），每个胞元在共享四面体网格上拥有各自的可微符号距离场，分区行进四面体中场天然互斥，从而提取闭合且不重叠的部件网格；所有部件网格经由恢复的关节在两状态图像上联合优化。

Result: 在 Articulate-100 上，相比所有不使用部件掩码的方法，ArtTet 在轴方向、部件运动和部件几何精度上最优，且部件网格几乎没有开放边界边；同时在 SplArt 数据集的真实采集数据上展示了重建结果。

Conclusion: 通过显式部件划分与互斥SDF场构造，ArtTet 解决了单体网格方法部件混杂、网格破损的问题，实现了无需部件标注的高质量铰接物体水密重建。

Abstract: We present ArtTet, a method that reconstructs an articulated object from multi-view observations of two articulation states and outputs one watertight mesh per rigid part together with the joints that move them. Mesh splatting gives explicit triangles, but it treats the object as one scene. It extracts a single global surface and cannot keep the triangles of different parts apart; the mesh it exports is layered, self-intersecting, and full of holes. ArtTet follows a divide-and-conquer strategy instead. We first divide the object into rigid parts with dynamic- and geometric-constrained articulation and ownership inference. A multibody registration of the two states recovers the parts and their screw joints. Rigid-body rules on attachment, contact, and swept motion then correct joint types and part labels without any part annotation. We then divide space into part cells with an articulated tetrahedral partition, and conquer each cell with its own differentiable signed distance field on a shared tetrahedral grid. In these partitioned marching tetrahedra the fields are mutually exclusive by construction, so the extracted part meshes are closed and never overlap. All part meshes are optimized jointly from the images of both states through the recovered joints. On Articulate-100, ArtTet gives the most accurate axis directions, part motions, and part geometry over all objects among methods that use no part masks, and its part meshes have virtually no open boundary edges. We also show reconstructions of real captures from the SplArt dataset.

</details>


### [109] [MotifFlow: Training-Free, Self-Contained Style Transfer from Color to Motif in Rectified Flow Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33371)
*Chun Xie,Itaru Kitahara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MotifFlow：一种免训练、自包含的 FLUX 风格迁移框架，将风格注入与风格对齐引导结合，用以在保留内容结构的同时复现调色板、笔触与标志性母题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力注入在 U-Net 扩散模型中有效，但迁移到 FLUX 时往往只转移颜色而丢失风格特有的笔触。作者通过跨主干分析，将这一差距归因于注入特征在相邻空间位置上的融合方式：注入只提供风格特征，却未显式促使模型原生注意力输出与之对齐。

Method: 以原生注意力输出与注入注意力输出之间的差异作为引导信号，驱动潜空间更新（style-alignment guidance），使生成图像逐步长出风格的笔触与母题；风格编码、引导与合成都复用同一冻结主干，无需辅助图像编码器或训练适配器；仅用一个强度参数控制从颜色迁移到显著笔触/母题的过渡，并支持区域风格化与风格混合。

Result: 在 1,000 组内容–风格对上，MotifFlow 相比基线方法更贴合风格图像的局部纹理，且风格化一致性优于单纯注入；该设计同样可扩展至 FLUX.2。

Conclusion: 在 FLUX 上，仅靠注入不足以复现笔触，关键在原生注意力与注入注意力的对齐；以二者差异做引导是一种免训练、轻量且可控的风格迁移方案。

Abstract: Expressive style transfer must reproduce a painting's palette, brushwork, and characteristic motifs while retaining the content image's structure. Attention injection is effective in U-Net-based diffusion models, but adapting it to FLUX often transfers color without capturing the style's distinctive brushwork. Our analysis across backbones links this gap to how injected features are integrated across neighboring spatial locations. We introduce MotifFlow, a training-free, self-contained framework that combines style injection with style-alignment guidance in FLUX. Injection supplies style features during generation but does not explicitly encourage the model's native attention output to align with them. We therefore use the discrepancy between native and injected attention outputs to guide latent-space updates, encouraging the evolving image to develop the style's distinctive brushwork and motifs. Style encoding, guidance, and synthesis all use the same frozen backbone, without auxiliary image encoders or trained adapters. A single strength parameter controls the transition from color transfer to pronounced brushwork and motifs, with additional support for regional stylization and style mixing. Experiments on 1,000 content-style pairs show that MotifFlow better matches the local textures of the style image than baseline methods and produces more consistent stylization than injection alone. The same design extends to FLUX.2.

</details>


### [110] [StyleID++: Position-Decoupled and Structure-Coherent Style Retrieval across Diffusion Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33323)
*Chun Xie,Itaru Kitahara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文指出基于注意力注入的免训练风格迁移存在跨图位置泄漏这一被忽视的失效模式，并提出 StyleID++ 通过位置解耦检索与亲和一致性精修两个模块联合解决位置泄漏与风格分配碎片化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力注入式风格迁移虽简单有效，但在一张图像内有用的位置信号会在两张空间无关的图像间形成虚假共享坐标系，导致内容 token 偏向检索相同坐标处的风格 token；此外解除位置捷径后又暴露出无约束全局检索使相关内容 token 选到不兼容风格区域、产生外观碎片化的问题。

Method: 提出免训练框架 StyleID++：Position-Decoupled Retrieval 在 U-Net 中采用循环填充、在 DiT 中仅移除注入部分的 RoPE，以削弱坐标依赖匹配同时保留其余原生位置建模；Affinity-Coherent Refinement 利用内容自注意力图正则化风格分配，使结构相关的 token 检索兼容的风格证据。

Result: 在卷积 U-Net 与 DiT 两类骨干上均观察到跨图位置泄漏；通过坐标—质心相关性与风格参考上的置换检验量化泄漏，两类骨干的置换误差均可降至零，从而确认位置机制是泄漏载体；StyleID++ 在所比较方法中取得最优 ArtFID。

Conclusion: 跨图位置泄漏是注意力注入式风格迁移的关键失效模式，且根源于位置机制本身；位置解耦检索与亲和一致性精修可协同消除泄漏与碎片化，实现免训练的高质量风格迁移。

Abstract: Attention injection offers a remarkably simple route to training-free style transfer: content queries retrieve style keys and values inside a pretrained diffusion model. We show that this operation also creates an overlooked failure mode. Position signals that are useful within one image become a spurious shared coordinate system across two spatially unrelated images, causing content tokens to favor style tokens at similar coordinates. This cross-image positional leakage appears in both convolutional U-Nets and diffusion transformers (DiTs), despite their different position mechanisms. Removing the positional shortcut exposes a second problem: unconstrained global retrieval lets related content tokens select incompatible style regions, producing fragmented appearance. We present StyleID++, a training-free framework that addresses the two problems jointly. Position-Decoupled Retrieval reduces coordinate-dependent matching through circular padding in U-Nets and injection-only RoPE removal in DiTs, while preserving native position modeling elsewhere. Affinity-Coherent Refinement then regularizes style assignments with the content self-attention graph, encouraging structurally related tokens to retrieve compatible style evidence. We quantify the leakage with a coordinate--centroid correlation and a permutation test on the style reference. The permutation error can be driven to zero in both backbones, which identifies the position mechanism as the carrier of the leakage, and StyleID++ obtains the best ArtFID of all methods we compare.

</details>


### [111] [Large-scale Model Merging via Recovery-aware Task Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33315)
*Kaiyang Li,Shaobo Han,Boming Chen,Martin Renqiang Min,Shihao Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出Recovery-aware Task Weighting (RaTW)，通过按专家模型相对初始合并模型的"可恢复样本比例"分配任务权重并重新合并，缓解大规模模型合并中的任务能力失衡；并构建100任务基准CLIPMerge-100，给出ReForge++框架，在全部设置下同时提升平均精度与尾部任务保留度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并将多个任务专家整合为单一模型，但各任务的能力保留并不均衡；在大规模（多任务）场景下该失衡尤为严重，即使平均精度仍高，个别任务也可能出现严重的能力损失。因此需要针对"保留差"的任务进行补偿。

Method: 1) RaTW：以任务验证集上"专家预测正确而初始合并模型预测错误"的样本占比量化恢复机会，作为任务权重，据此重新合并专家；2) 构建CLIPMerge-100基准：100个任务、任务专属专家，基于CLIP ViT-L/14与ViT-B/32；3) ReForge++：将RaTW与迭代式模型精炼结合。

Result: 在11种合并方法、20/50/100任务、两种CLIP骨干共66个设置中，RaTW在全部33个ViT-L/14设置与33个ViT-B/32中的32个设置上，同时提升平均精度与tail20（最差保留的20%任务的平均专家归一化精度）；ReForge++在全部设置上于两项指标均优于所有基线，包括等预算对照。

Conclusion: 以恢复机会为导向的任务加权可有效缓解合并中的任务能力失衡，兼顾平均性能与尾部任务表现；结合迭代精炼的ReForge++在大规模多任务合并下取得一致、稳健的增益。

Abstract: Model merging combines multiple task-specific expert models into a single model. However, merging preserves expert capabilities unevenly across tasks. This imbalance is particularly pronounced in large-scale settings, where some tasks can suffer severe capability losses even when average accuracy remains high. To address this imbalance, we introduce Recovery-aware Task Weighting (RaTW), a model merging framework that assigns larger weights to tasks whose expert capabilities are poorly retained by an initial merged model. We quantify these recovery opportunities as the fraction of a task's labeled validation samples that its expert predicts correctly but the initial merged model gets wrong, and use the resulting task weights to re-merge the experts. To evaluate merging at large scale, we construct CLIPMerge-100, a 100-task benchmark with task-specific experts on CLIP ViT-L/14 and ViT-B/32. Across eleven merging methods with 20, 50, and 100 tasks on two CLIP backbones, RaTW improves both mean accuracy and tail20 (the mean expert-normalized accuracy of the 20% worst-retained tasks) in all 33 ViT-L/14 settings and 32 of 33 ViT-B/32 settings. We further introduce ReForge++, which combines RaTW with iterative model refinement and outperforms all evaluated baselines, including a matched-budget control, in both mean accuracy and tail20 across all settings.

</details>


### [112] [WorldUAV: Scaling UAV Data Synthesis via World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33682)
*Xuewen Liu,Mengjuan Chen,Jing Zhang,Mengzhe Shen,Zekun Xu,Zibo Chen,Zuoxiu Zhang,Yue Dong,Zhikai Li,Qingyi Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WorldUAV：基于世界模型与多模态大模型智能体流程的全自动框架，将UAV合成到真实视频中以构建高质量、低成本的UAV检测与跟踪数据集（6,878段视频、205,778张图像）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAV检测与跟踪受限于高质量训练数据稀缺：游戏引擎合成数据存在显著的合成-真实域差距，而人工采集真实UAV数据成本高、规模受限。

Method: 将UAV直接嵌入现成真实视频，保留真实背景、光照与场景动态；利用世界模型的时空先验生成具有合理运动、姿态变化与跨帧一致性的UAV视频；并构建由多模态大语言模型编排的智能体流程（源获取、轨迹规划、调用合成、视频校验），实现全自动数据集构建。

Result: 构建WorldUAV数据集（6,878段UAV视频、205,778张UAV图像）；实验中显著提升UAV检测器性能；每张高质量UAV图像仅需约15秒与0.0005美元API成本，大幅降低构建开销。

Conclusion: 工作同时提供高质量数据集与可扩展的数据生成范式，为UAV检测与跟踪提供低成本、可规模化的数据解决方案，有效缩小合成-真实域差距。

Abstract: UAV detection and tracking remain constrained by the scarcity of high-quality training data. Existing game-engine-based synthetic UAV data suffer from a substantial synthetic-to-real gap, while the high cost of manually collected real-world UAV data limits dataset scale. Therefore, we introduce WorldUAV, a high-quality UAV dataset constructed through an efficient framework powered by world models. Specifically, WorldUAV directly integrates UAVs into readily available real-world videos, naturally preserving realistic backgrounds, illumination, and scene dynamics. Leveraging the spatiotemporal priors of world models, it generates UAV videos with plausible motion, pose variations, and cross-frame consistency, effectively narrowing the gap between synthetic and real data. We further develop an agentic pipeline orchestrated by a multimodal large language model that acquires sources, plans UAV trajectories, invokes synthesis, and verifies generated videos, enabling fully automated dataset construction. WorldUAV comprises 6,878 UAV videos and 205,778 UAV images. Experiments show that WorldUAV significantly improves the performance of UAV detectors. Moreover, it substantially reduces dataset construction costs, requiring only 15 seconds and USD 0.0005 API cost per high-quality UAV image. Our work offers both a high-quality dataset and a scalable data generation paradigm for UAV detection and tracking.

</details>


### [113] [VivaWorld: Long-Horizon Video World Model With Compositional 3D Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33591)
*Ying Jiang,Donglai Xiang,Anita Hu,Masha Shugrina*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VivaWorld：基于混合3D表示与混合相机嵌入，从单张图像生成几何与视角一致的长时序视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序视频生成对交互式世界建模、物理智能与空间推理至关重要，但缺乏3D grounding时，难以保持几何一致性与精确相机控制。

Method: 构建组合式混合3D表示：重建点云保留已观测场景几何与外观，生成式纹理网格补全360°新视角下的完整物体几何；引入连通区域过滤去除不一致的漂浮组件以稳健跨视角重建；提出融合全局与物体锚定Plücker射线的混合相机嵌入，捕捉场景级相机运动与相机-物体关系，并与视觉引导一同经控制适配器注入视频生成模型。

Result: 大量实验表明在视觉质量与相机控制上达到SOTA，并支持可控新视角合成、长时序场景探索及物理AI应用。

Conclusion: 通过混合3D表示与混合相机嵌入实现几何/视角一致的长视频生成，为世界建模与物理AI提供可行框架。

Abstract: Long-horizon video generation is key to interactive world modeling, physical intelligence, and spatial reasoning, yet maintaining geometric consistency and accurate camera control remains challenging without 3D grounding. We introduce \textbf{VivaWorld}, a framework for generating geometry- and view-consistent long videos from an image. We first construct a compositional hybrid 3D representation that combines reconstructed point clouds, which preserve observed scene geometry and appearance, with generated textured meshes, which provide complete object geometry across $360^\circ$ novel views. We also introduce a connected-region filter removing inconsistent floating components for robust cross-view reconstruction and propose a hybrid camera embedding that combines global and object-anchored Plücker rays to capture both scene-level camera motion and camera-object relationships, and inject it together with the visual guidance into a video generation model via a control adapter. Extensive experiments show VivaWorld achieves state-of-the-art visual quality and camera control, while supporting controllable novel-view synthesis, long-horizon scene exploration, and physical AI. Project page is available at: https://research.nvidia.com/labs/sil/projects/vivaworld/

</details>


### [114] [LLMVC: Large Language Model-Guided Generative Video Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33580)
*Lebin Zhou,Zhenghao Chen,Nam Ling,Wei Wang,Wei Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 LLMVC，利用冻结的视频大语言模型生成紧凑语义 token 作为文本侧通道，引导视频压缩的编码、解码与熵建模，在 UVG、MCL-JCV、HEVC 上取得更优的 LPIPS/DISTS 感知压缩性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式视频压缩将高层语义与稠密视觉细节压缩进统一隐空间，导致熵编码与解码复杂化，并在激进压缩下产生语义漂移。

Method: 用冻结 Video-LLM 的摘要能力生成与时间隐切片对齐的紧凑语义 token；设计 Hybrid Bidirectional Bimodal Attention（HBBA）实现编解码中视觉与语义特征的双向交互；设计 Semantic-Conditioned Cross-Attention（SCCA）将语义特征注入熵编码的概率建模；语义特征经文本侧通道传输。

Result: 在 UVG、MCL-JCV、HEVC 数据集上，LPIPS 与 DISTS 指标一致优于传统及近期神经视频压缩方法，保真度也优于近期感知编解码器。

Conclusion: 以高层语义作为显式引导信号可提升表示质量与语义一致性，为语义—视觉解耦的感知视频压缩提供可行路径。

Abstract: Recent generative video methods achieve strong perceptual compression by encoding videos into compact latent representations and reconstructing them with powerful generative decoders. However, visual encoders must compress both high-level semantics and dense visual details into a unified latent space, which complicates entropy coding and decoding and may cause semantic drift under aggressive compression. In this work, we propose Large Language Model-Guided Video Compression (LLMVC), which leverages the summarization capability of frozen video large language models (Video-LLMs) to generate compact semantic tokens aligned with temporal latent slices for guiding video compression. Specifically, Hybrid Bidirectional Bimodal Attention (HBBA) enables bidirectional interactions between visual and semantic features during encoding and decoding. Semantic-Conditioned Cross-Attention (SCCA) incorporates semantic features into probability modeling during entropy coding. These semantic features provide high-level guidance throughout the pipeline, improving representation quality and encouraging semantic consistency through a transmitted text side channel. Experiments on UVG, MCL-JCV, and HEVC show that LLMVC consistently improves compression performance over traditional and recent neural video compression methods in terms of LPIPS and DISTS, while also achieving better fidelity than recent perceptual codecs.

</details>


### [115] [ProPolar: Progressive Polar Decomposition for Implicit Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33567)
*Pureum Kim,Younggeon Ryu,Dongyoon Lee,Hae Beom Lee,Kyong Hwan Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对INR由粗到细的学习特性，提出渐进增长秩调度与秩感知学习率调度器，改进低秩子空间动量优化器，在图像拟合、SISR与NeRF上提升收敛与重建质量，增益集中于高频细化阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: INR训练先捕获全局低频结构、后细化局部高频细节，但标准优化器对此粗到细行为无感知：其动量缓冲累积完整梯度矩阵，早期阶段保留与主导低频方向不对齐的分量，造成干扰。

Method: 采用子空间动量优化框架（动量矩阵仅在低秩子空间内更新），并引入两个组件：(1) 渐进增长秩调度器，在训练中逐步扩展子空间秩；(2) 秩感知学习率调度器，在秩增长期间维持峰值学习率，使新增子空间能有效贡献于细节恢复。

Result: 在图像拟合、单图像超分辨率（SISR）与神经辐射场优化实验中，收敛速度与峰值重建质量优于竞争性优化器；最大增益出现在高频细化阶段，与秩调度所针对的粗到细进程一致；NeRF上的超参数优化（HPO）研究进一步表明，在匹配搜索预算下增益依然保持。

Conclusion: 将优化器的子空间秩演化与INR的粗到细频谱学习规律对齐，是一种有效且通用的加速与提质手段；该机制在高频细化阶段收益最大，并在等预算HPO条件下稳健。

Abstract: Implicit neural representations (INRs) capture global low-frequency structure during the early stage of training and then refine localized high-frequency details, afterwards. However, standard optimizers are agnostic to the coarse-to-fine learning behavior of INRs. Such optimizers accumulate a gradient matrix in momentum buffer and retain components misaligned with the dominant low-frequency directions in the early stage. We propose a progressively growing rank scheduler and a rank-aware learning-rate scheduler for enhancing a subspace-based momentum optimization, where a momentum matrix is updated within a low-rank subspace. The rank schedule expands the subspace during training, and the scheduler holds the peak learning rate through rank growth so that the added subspace contributes to fine-detail recovery. Experiments on image fitting, single-image super-resolution (SISR), and neural radiance field optimization show that the proposed method improves convergence and peak reconstruction quality over competitive optimizers. The largest gains appear in the high-frequency refinement stage, mirroring the coarse-to-fine progression that the rank schedule targets. A significant study on hyperparameter optimization (HPO) on NeRF further confirms that these gains persist under matched search budgets.

</details>


### [116] [SprintGS: Eliminating Redundancy for Fast 3D Gaussian Splatting Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33796)
*Ryosuke Watanabe,Tomoaki Konno*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SprintGS 是基于 FastGS 的训练框架，通过调度式 tile 剔除、收敛感知剪枝、shuffle 高效反向传播与融合损失，在保持画质的条件下实现比 FastGS 快 2.15x（高质量设置 1.81x）的训练加速，并可推广到其他快速训练方法与动态、稀疏视角、表面重建任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D Gaussian Splatting 虽能实时渲染逼真新视角，但逐场景优化训练仍很慢。FastGS 等快速训练方法通过减少高斯数量把训练缩短到数分钟，但剖析发现即使模型已紧凑，仍有两项开销：一是光栅化仍是最大开销且存在冗余计算；二是损失计算量不随高斯数量缩减，因此在紧凑模型中占比不断上升。

Method: 在 FastGS 基础上构建，保持模型紧凑并同时降低两类开销。光栅化方面：(1) 调度式 tile 剔除（scheduled tile culling），用粗粒度 footprint 将每个高斯限制在真正重要的 tile 上，并在训练结束前退火至精确 footprint；(2) 收敛感知剪枝（convergence-aware pruning），移除已收敛且近乎透明的高斯；(3) shuffle 高效反向传播，减少 GPU 线程间的数据交换。损失方面：融合损失在正向传播中直接计算损失及其图像梯度，无需构建 loss map，从而消除大部分损失开销。

Result: 在可比画质下训练速度比 FastGS 快 2.15x，高质量设置下快 1.81x；同时可加速其他快速训练方法，并适用于动态场景、稀疏视角与表面重建任务。

Conclusion: 紧凑模型下的剩余瓶颈在于光栅化冗余与不可随高斯数缩减的损失计算；针对性地消除这两类开销可在不牺牲画质的前提下显著缩短 3DGS 训练时间，且该框架具有良好的可迁移性。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) renders photorealistic novel views in real time, but its per-scene training (optimization) remains slow. Recent fast training methods such as FastGS shorten training to a few minutes by reducing the number of Gaussians. Profiling FastGS, we find two costs that remain even in its compact model. First, rasterization is still the largest cost, and redundant work remains in it. Second, the loss computation does not shrink with the number of Gaussians, so its share of the training time grows as the model becomes compact. We present SprintGS, a training framework built on FastGS that keeps the model compact and reduces both costs. For rasterization, scheduled tile culling limits each Gaussian to the tiles where it matters, using a coarse footprint that is annealed to the exact one before training ends; convergence-aware pruning removes converged, nearly transparent Gaussians; and a shuffle-efficient backward pass reduces the data exchanged between GPU threads. For the loss, a fused loss computes the loss and its image gradient in the forward pass without a loss map, removing most of its cost. SprintGS trains 2.15x faster than FastGS at comparable quality, and 1.81x faster in its high-quality setting. It also accelerates other fast training methods and dynamic, sparse-view, and surface reconstruction.

</details>


### [117] [Spatial Transcriptomics Prediction from Histology via Topology-Informed Graph Attention and Gene-Semantics Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33792)
*Yuqi Cheng,Boshuai Zhang,Ziqi Zhang,Xiuwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TIGEM，一种拓扑感知、基因条件化的组织学图像到空间转录组推断框架，并引入图谱相关性指标GSC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组成本高，需从H&E图像推断表达。现有方法存在三点不足：(i)单patch嵌入不足——形态相似区域因空间上下文表达不同，而朴素邻域聚合会过度平滑边界；(ii)固定基因索引输出头将基因独立处理，无法支持未见基因的严格零样本预测；(iii)常用指标低估空间模式保真度与边界分辨率。

Method: TIGEM以组织级拓扑与形态线索正则化图注意力，学习拓扑感知的spot表示以避免跨区室平滑；用基因语义调制替代基因索引头，实现未见基因零样本预测；并提出Graph Spectral Correlation(GSC)指标，作为PCC/MSE的补充以评估空间模式保真度与边界分辨率。

Result: 在三个大规模ST数据集上取得整体SOTA性能，边界预测分辨率显著提升，并验证了对训练中未见基因表达模式的预测能力。

Conclusion: TIGEM通过拓扑感知空间传播与基于PLM的基因条件化，拓展了既往零样本组织学→ST工作，提升了性能与边界分辨率，并支持未见基因预测。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics (ST) profiles gene expression with spatial context but remains costly, motivating models that infer expression from H&E images. Existing approaches face three key limitations: (i) a single patch embedding is often insufficient: morphologically similar regions can exhibit distinct expression due to spatial context, while naive neighborhood aggregation can over-smooth boundaries; (ii) conventional fixed, gene-indexed output heads treat genes independently and cannot directly support strict zero-shot prediction for unseen genes; and (iii) common metrics under-measure spatial pattern fidelity and boundary resolution. To address these challenges, we propose TIGEM, a topology-informed, gene-conditioned framework for histology-to-ST inference. TIGEM learns topology-aware spot representations by regularizing graph attention with tissue-level topology and morphology cues to avoid cross-compartment smoothing, and uses gene semantic modulation to replace gene-indexed heads, enabling zero-shot prediction for unseen genes. We also introduce Graph Spectral Correlation (GSC) to evaluate spatial pattern fidelity and boundary resolution complement to PCC/MSE. Across three large-scale ST datasets, TIGEM achieves overall state-of-the-art performance and significantly improved boundary prediction resolution, and further demonstrates the ability to predict expression patterns for genes unseen during training, extending prior zero-shot histology-to-ST efforts with topology-aware spatial propagation and PLM-based gene conditioning.

</details>


### [118] [DuoWM:Integrating Dual 6-DoF Visual Flows into Action-Conditioned Drone World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33779)
*Zixuan Li,Kangyao Huang,Meng Xu,Wan Tian,Xiaoyu Tang,Jinge Sun,Yingliang Yao,He Chen,Lingzhong Meng,Yunzhi Xue,Jingyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DuoWM 是一种第一人称无人机视频世界模型，通过"动作流"与"物体流"双视觉条件，将相机轨迹与场景 6-DoF 变换显式对齐，并配合变换感知预训练，在视频质量、3D 一致性与轨迹跟随上全面优于 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作条件视频世界模型虽能由初始帧与相机轨迹生成未来观测，但常出现相机指令与视觉动态不对齐的问题，且难以表示完整的 6-DoF 场景变换。

Method: 提出 DuoWM，采用两种互补视觉条件：1) Action flow：将未来相机位置与朝向投影到每个目标帧；2) Object flow：前向扭曲初始观测并叠加局部 6-DoF 变换，以 grounding 诱导的场景运动。此外引入 transform-aware 预训练，在标准 RGB 视频训练前，将物体外观与显式变换轴配对。

Result: 在 Sekai-drone 数据集上，视频质量、3D 一致性、轨迹跟随均取得最佳。相较 SOTA 方法 Captain Safari，真实域 FVD 降低 17.2%、LPIPS 降低 21.6%；游戏域 AUC@30 从 0.5003 提升至 0.5719，AUC@15 从 0.3088 提升至 0.3747。

Conclusion: 显式的动作流与物体流双条件建模结合变换感知预训练，可有效实现相机控制与 6-DoF 场景动态的对齐，显著提升世界模型的多维生成质量。

Abstract: Action-conditioned video world models generate future observations from an initial frame and a camera trajectory, but often fail to align camera commands with visual dynamics or represent full 6-DoF scene transformations. We present DuoWM, a first-person drone video world model with two complementary visual conditions. Action flow projects future camera positions and orientations into each target frame, while Object flow forward-warps the initial observation and overlays local 6-DoF transformations to ground the induced scene motion. We further introduce transform-aware pretraining, which pairs object appearance with explicit transformation axes before standard RGB video training. On Sekai-drone dataset, DuoWM achieves the best results across video quality, 3D consistency, and trajectory following. Compared with Captain Safari, the state-of-the-art method, our approach reduces real-domain FVD and LPIPS by 17.2\% and 21.6\%, respectively, while improving game-domain AUC@30 from 0.5003 to 0.5719 and AUC@15 from 0.3088 to 0.3747.

</details>


### [119] [A comprehensive survey of deep face verification systems adversarial attacks and defense strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33231)
*Sohair Kilany,Ahmed Mahfouz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理了深度人脸验证（FV）的算法设计、数据集、协议与应用场景，并深入考察针对FV的对抗样本生成算法与防御机制，旨在为安全敏感部署提供参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸验证因深度神经网络而性能卓越并广泛用于身份核验、移动支付等场景，但深度模型对对抗样本的脆弱性引发了对安全关键场景部署风险的担忧，亟需对该领域的攻击与防御研究进行系统整理。

Method: 文献综述方法：概述深度人脸验证的算法设计、数据库使用、评测协议与应用场景；在此基础上系统分类与深入分析前沿的对抗样本生成算法及相应的防御机制。

Result: 给出了人脸验证技术全景式的知识框架（算法、数据、协议、应用），并归纳总结了对抗攻击生成方法与防御策略的现状与分类。

Conclusion: 该综述为人脸验证的对抗鲁棒性研究提供了统一视角与参考，有助于推动安全可信的深度人脸验证系统部署及后续攻击/防御研究。

Abstract: Face Verification (FV) systems have exhibited remarkable performance in verification tasks and have consequently garnered extensive adoption across various applications, from identity duplication to authentication in mobile payments. However, the surge in popularity of face verification has raised concerns about potential vulnerabilities in the face of adversarial attacks. These concerns originate from the fact that advanced FV systems, which rely on deep neural networks, have recently demonstrated susceptibility to crafted input samples known as adversarial examples. Although imperceptible to human observers, adversarial examples can deceive deep neural networks during the testing and deployment phases. These vulnerabilities raised significant concerns about the deployment of deep neural networks in safety-critical contexts, prompting extensive investigations into adversarial attacks and corresponding defense strategies. This comprehensive survey provides a comprehensive overview of recent advances in deep face verification, encompassing a broad spectrum of topics such as algorithmic designs, database utilization, protocols, and application scenarios. Furthermore, we conduct an in-depth examination of state-of-the-art algorithms to generate adversarial examples and the defense mechanisms devised to mitigate such adversarial threats.

</details>


### [120] [Confidence-Based Adaptive Retrieval Fails Where It Matters Most: A Paradox in Biomedical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33210)
*Chengying She,Siyu Deng,Haiyan Ge,Lizhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文揭示置信度自适应RAG在生物医学问答中的"悖论"：在检索收益小的标准考题上置信度校准良好（AUROC 0.78–0.89），但在检索收益最大的专家级难题上几乎随机（AUROC 0.506），且五种不确定性信号与五种后校准方法均无法修复，故在高风险场景应默认始终检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM用于生物医学问答存在"看似合理但错误"的安全风险，RAG可 grounding 回答，而置信度自适应RAG（仅在模型不确定时检索）被视为节省成本且有效的路由策略。但该策略的前提——置信度能否在需要检索时可靠地触发检索——在临床高风险场景下尚未被系统检验。

Method: 在三个难度递增的生物医学QA基准上评测置信度路由策略，覆盖广泛参数规模的模型。比较五类不确定性信号（首token概率、margin、熵、一致性采样、言语化置信度）与五种事后校准修正策略（含密度比重加权、保秩变换），并进行跨模型规模的对比分析。

Result: 标准医学考题上置信度校准良好（AUROC 0.78–0.89），但检索增益仅+2.4%至-1.4%；专家级问题上检索增益达+11.2%，置信度却近乎随机（AUROC 0.506）。五类不确定性信号呈现相同失效模式；五种事后校准策略均无法恢复有效路由信号（最佳AUROC 0.507）。更大参数规模与更高基础准确率并不能保证在专家级难度迁移下获得可靠的置信度路由。

Conclusion: 置信度自适应RAG的前提在生物医学场景中不成立：当目标域校准未知时，在高风险临床应用中"始终检索"比置信度路由更安全、更准确。该结论为RAG系统设计提供了"宁滥勿缺"的实用建议，并指出域迁移下的置信度校准是核心瓶颈。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for biomedical question answering, but plausible incorrect answers remain a safety risk in clinical settings. Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) can ground responses in medical literature, while confidence-based adaptive RAG retrieves only when the model appears uncertain. We evaluate this routing strategy on three biomedical QA benchmarks of increasing difficulty and compare models across a broad range of parameter scales. The results reveal a paradox: confidence is well calibrated (AUROC 0.78--0.89) on standard medical exam questions where retrieval adds little value (+2.4\% to -1.4\%), yet becomes \emph{near-random} (AUROC 0.506) on specialist-level questions where retrieval helps most (+11.2\%). Five uncertainty signals---first-token probability, margin, entropy, consistency-based sampling, and verbalized confidence---show the same failure pattern. Five post-hoc calibration correction strategies, including density-ratio reweighting and rank-preserving transformation, do not recover a useful routing signal on hard questions (best AUROC: 0.507). Cross-model comparison shows that larger parameter scale and higher base accuracy do not guarantee reliable confidence-based routing under specialist-domain difficulty shift. These findings challenge the premise of confidence-based adaptive RAG: in high-stakes biomedical settings, defaulting to always-retrieve is safer and more accurate than confidence-based routing when calibration on the target domain is unknown.

</details>


### [121] [MoSk: A Unified Motion-Skeleton Foundation Model for Human Motion Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34022)
*Yun Chen,Munchurl Kim,Jeonghyeok Do*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoSk是首个融合大规模运动(motion)与骨架(skeleton)数据的运动基础模型，通过自监督掩码运动建模学习共享表征，并借助关节名称嵌入统一不同骨架格式，在动作识别与运动生成任务上均取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在视觉理解与生成中已展现语义丰富表征的价值，但在人体运动领域尚缺乏探索。大规模运动数据集提供多样动作但缺少动作标签，难以系统评估表征的理解能力；骨架数据集有成熟的动作识别评测协议，却规模较小且局限于受控环境下的预定义动作。因此需要将两者优势结合。

Method: 构建MoSk模型：以自监督掩码运动建模学习共享表征；引入关节名称嵌入(joint-name embeddings)为不同骨架格式提供统一的解剖参考；采用交叉注意力解码器，从编码器特征重建被掩码的运动，并由关节名称嵌入指定目标关节点。

Result: 在多种设置下（线性探针评估与微调）均表现出强动作识别性能；通过表征对齐显著改善多种骨干网络的运动生成，在MotionMillion上无条件生成的FID最多降低9.75，文本到运动生成FID最多降低3.83。

Conclusion: MoSk验证了将大规模运动的规模优势与骨架数据的评测/结构优势相结合是可行且有效的，可作为统一的人体运动基础模型提升理解与生成两方面性能。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) have advanced visual understanding and generation by providing semantically rich representations. However, this role of FMs remains underexplored in human motion. Large motion datasets offer diverse movements at scale, but often lack action labels for systematically evaluating learned representations on understanding tasks. Skeleton datasets enable such evaluation through established recognition protocols, yet are typically smaller and focus on predefined actions captured in controlled environments. To combine these strengths, we present MoSk, to the best of our knowledge, the first Motion-Skeleton foundation model to integrate motion and skeleton datasets. MoSk learns shared representations through self-supervised masked motion modeling. We incorporate joint-name embeddings to provide a common anatomical reference across various skeleton formats. A cross-attention decoder reconstructs masked motion from the encoder's features, with joint-name embeddings specifying the target joints. Experiments demonstrate strong action-recognition performance across diverse settings, including linear evaluation and fine-tuning. MoSk also substantially improves motion generation across multiple backbones through representation alignment, reducing FID on MotionMillion by up to 9.75 for unconditional generation and 3.83 for text-to-motion generation.

</details>


### [122] [Rethinking Unsupervised Cross-Modal Homography Estimation:Spatially Aware Representations and Geometric Consistency Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34021)
*Zhuoran Yang,Junchen Yu,Runmin Zhang,Linshan Li,Chenghao Zhang,Hui-liang Shen,Si-Yuan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GeoCo框架，通过RVHomo估计器融合冻结VFM与CNN特征，并施加双重几何一致性约束，实现无监督跨模态单应性估计；再经两阶段渐进蒸馏得到轻量GeoCo-D，精度优于基线且参数/算力大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督跨模态单应性估计受制于模态间外观差异，外观差异掩盖了共享几何结构；将模态转换与单应性估计分离可缓解该问题，但精确对齐仍需要有效的几何表征与两个子任务之间的一致性约束，且缺乏跨模态转换标签。

Method: 1) 单应性估计器RVHomo：适配冻结视觉基础模型（VFM）的层次化特征，并与局部CNN特征融合，兼顾结构上下文与空间细节；2) 两个几何一致性约束：约束模态转换保持几何结构，约束单应性预测在合成几何变换下保持一致；3) 两阶段渐进蒸馏：中间模型先学习无模态转换的跨模态匹配，再将预测与特征知识迁移给学生模型RVHomo-D，从而免去在线VFM特征提取。

Result: 在GoogleMap、OPT-SAR、FSD、VIS-IR四个数据集上，GeoCo相对各数据集最佳基线将平均角点误差（mean average corner error）降低17.4-46.7%；GeoCo-D优于所有对比基线，同时相比完整GeoCo教师减少98.5%参数与70.4% FLOPs。

Conclusion: 几何感知特征表征配合一致性约束可有效支撑无监督跨模态单应性估计；渐进蒸馏能在不依赖在线VFM提取的前提下获得轻量且性能更优的学生模型，兼顾精度与效率。

Abstract: Unsupervised cross-modal homography estimation is challenging because appearance differences obscure shared geometric structures. Separating modality transfer from homography estimation helps reduce these differences, but accurate alignment still requires effective geometric representations and consistency constraints on the two subtasks. We propose GeoCo (Geometry-Aware and Consistency-Constrained Homography Estimation), a framework that addresses these two requirements without cross-modal transformation labels. Its homography estimator, RVHomo, adapts hierarchical features from a frozen vision foundation model (VFM) and fuses them with local CNN features to capture structural context and spatial detail. Two geometric consistency constraints encourage modality transfer to preserve geometry and homography predictions to remain consistent under synthetic geometric transformations. Through two-stage progressive distillation, we further obtain GeoCo-D, which uses the lightweight RVHomo-D estimator. An intermediate model first learns to perform cross-modal matching without modality transfer, then transfers prediction and feature knowledge to the student, eliminating online VFM extraction. Experiments on GoogleMap, OPT-SAR, FSD, and VIS-IR show that GeoCo reduces mean average corner error by 17.4-46.7% relative to the best-performing baseline on each dataset. GeoCo-D also outperforms all compared baselines while using 98.5% fewer parameters and 70.4% fewer FLOPs than the complete GeoCo teacher.

</details>


### [123] [BayesPath: Reliability-Aware Bayesian Reasoning for Whole-Slide Pathology Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33935)
*Bryan Wong,Xun Xu,Jongwoo Kim,Huazhu Fu,Nancy F. Chen,Mun Yong Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BayesPath，一种免训练的全切片图像（WSI）智能体，将病理学家的"扫描-观察-权衡-决策"过程建模为可靠性感知的贝叶斯推理，在MultiPathQA五个任务上平均准确率达58.6%，显著优于次优方法（34.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练WSI智能体缺乏显式的假设驱动推理框架：通常由LLM聚合MLLM的区域描述，既没有对候选诊断的显式信念分布，也不考虑描述本身的可靠性，导致模型重复出错时会被误当作独立证据，强化错误诊断。

Method: BayesPath以显式信念分布表示候选诊断，每次观测通过可靠性感知似然函数进行贝叶斯更新；该似然对模型答案偏差进行校正、对相关观测降权，并在无标签条件下估计诊断混淆；信念指导下一步的观察位置与停止时机，更新过程形成可解释的证据链。

Result: 在覆盖切片分类与切片VQA的五个MultiPathQA任务上，BayesPath在免训练智能体中平均准确率最高（58.6% vs 次优34.1%），切片分类提升尤为显著；在相同观测下，贝叶斯更新优于LLM聚合（即使后者使用更大LLM）；其信念校准优于朴素贝叶斯，可将不确定样本转交病理学家。

Conclusion: 显式、可靠性感知的贝叶斯信念更新是构建高性能、可解释、可校准的免训练WSI诊断智能体的有效框架，兼具准确率与临床可用性优势。

Abstract: Diagnosing whole-slide images (WSIs) requires hypothesis-driven reasoning: pathologists form a differential diagnosis, seek discriminative evidence, weigh its reliability, and stop once it suffices. However, existing training-free WSI agents lack an explicit framework for such reasoning. They typically use a large language model (LLM) to aggregate the region descriptions of a multimodal LLM (MLLM), without a belief over the candidates or regard for the descriptions' reliability. Consequently, a repeated model error counts as independent evidence for an incorrect diagnosis. We introduce BayesPath, a training-free WSI agent that casts the pathologist's Scan-Look-Weigh-Decide process as reliability-aware Bayesian reasoning. An explicit belief over the candidates is updated with each observation through a reliability-aware likelihood and guides where to look next and when to stop. This likelihood corrects for the models' answer bias, discounts correlated observations, and captures diagnostic confusions estimated without labels. The updates form an interpretable evidence trail of how each observation shapes the belief. On five MultiPathQA tasks spanning slide classification and slide VQA, BayesPath achieves the highest average accuracy among training-free agents, 58.6% against 34.1% for the next best, with large gains on slide classification. On identical observations, Bayesian updating outperforms LLM aggregation on average, even with a larger LLM. BayesPath's belief is also better calibrated than naive Bayes, so uncertain cases can be deferred to pathologists. Code is available at https://github.com/bryanwong17/BayesPath

</details>


### [124] [Tortho-Gaussian++: Suppressing Geometric Drift and Floaters for Robust Gravity-Aligned TDOM Generation in Large-Scale Aerial Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33905)
*Xiang Wang,Xin Wang,Zongqian Zhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Tortho-Gaussian++，一种抑制几何漂移与漂浮物、自动裁剪弱几何支撑边缘区域，并具备鲁棒重力对齐渲染的 3DGS 训练框架，用于高质量 TDOM 生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS 在航拍场景重建中表现出色，但直接扩展到真正射影像（TDOM）生成时存在几何漂移、漂浮物（floaters）以及重力方向不准确导致的立面倾斜伪影等问题，且边缘区域几何支撑薄弱。

Method: 1）基于视线的几何可观测性分析，定量刻画每个高斯的几何支撑可靠性，并裁剪支撑极弱的边缘区域；2）在致密化过程中改进新建高斯的优化器状态初始化，无外部先验地内在抑制漂移与漂浮物；3）融合图像重力线索、相机分布先验与天底视角的鲁棒重力方向估计，实现重力对齐的正交投影参考用于 TDOM 渲染。

Result: 在多组大规模无人机数据集上，平均 PSNR 较原始 3DGS 提升 0.5–3.5 dB，性能与先验引导的 3DGS 方法相当或更优；训练时间、显存与高斯数量分别降低 45.5%、47.2%、58.6%。定性评估确认有效抑制漂移与漂浮物，并减少倾斜引起的立面伪影。

Conclusion: Tortho-Gaussian++ 通过几何可观测性驱动的裁剪、优化器状态初始化改进与鲁棒重力对齐渲染，在提升 TDOM 重建质量的同时显著降低计算与存储开销，是一种高效且实用的 3DGS 航拍 TDOM 生成方案。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has demonstrated remarkable performance in aerial scene reconstruction, motivating its recent extension to true digital orthophoto maps (TDOMs) generation. We propose Tortho-Gaussian++, a novel 3DGS training framework that suppresses geometric drift and floaters and automatically prunes unreliable peripheral regions, coupled with a robust gravity-aligned TDOM rendering solution. First, we explore geometric support via viewing-ray-based geometric observability analysis, quantitatively characterizing the reliability for each Gaussian and subsequently pruning peripheral regions with particularly weak geometric support. Second, the optimizer state initialization is refined for newly created Gaussians during densification, intrinsically suppressing drift and floaters without external priors. Lastly, a robust gravity direction estimation solution is designed, combining image-derived gravity cues, camera-distribution priors and nadir views, to achieve a gravity-aligned orthogonal projection reference for TDOM rendering. Extensive experiments on multiple large-scale UAV (Unmanned Aerial Vehicle) datasets demonstrate that our Tortho-Gaussian++ outperforms vanilla 3DGS by 0.5-3.5 dB in average PSNR and yields comparable or superior performance to prior-guided 3DGS methods. Notably, it reduces the training time, VRAM and Gaussian number by 45.5%, 47.2%, and 58.6%, respectively. Qualitative evaluations confirm that our method effectively suppresses drift and floaters, while robust gravity direction estimation reduces tilt-induced facade artifacts in TDOMs.

</details>


### [125] [TWIST: Teacher-Weighted Initial-Noise Spherical Transform for Quality-Preserving Diversity in Distilled Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33895)
*Shayan Baghayi-Nejad,Jeongsol Kim,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TWIST：一种轻量网络，通过对初始噪声低频分量做球面变换，将噪声优化摊销进单次前向推理，以极低推理成本显著提升步数蒸馏扩散模型的多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 步数蒸馏扩散模型（如SDXL-Turbo、SDXL-Lightning）能在一到几步内生成高质量图像，但模式覆盖不足、多样性下降。现有方法在推理时优化初始噪声以提升多样性，需对去噪器反向传播，代价高昂；且其多样性目标仅让样本相互排斥，未考虑教师模型实际的输出分布。

Method: 提出TWIST（Teacher-Weighted Initial-noise Spherical Transform）轻量网络，仅变换初始噪声的低频分量，无需推理时优化。采用教师引导的行列式点过程（DPP）目标，使学生样本的特征空间多样性与教师样本的多样性对齐。通过将噪声优化摊销为单网络前向，消除推理时的反向传播，同时保留少步生成效率。

Result: 在SDXL-Turbo上，TWIST以低于MoNO约25%的推理成本达到其多样性水平，并在同等计算预算下取得更高召回率及更优的PickScore、ImageReward与CLIPScore。在SDXL-Lightning上，以更低成本获得相当召回率，且未出现MoNO那样的ImageReward下降。

Conclusion: 将推理期噪声优化摊销为一次轻量前向变换，并以教师分布加权的DPP目标对齐多样性，是兼顾少步生成效率与样本多样性的有效方案。

Abstract: Timestep-distilled diffusion models generate high-quality images in one or a few steps, but often suffer from reduced mode coverage and limited diversity. Recent methods improve diversity by optimizing the initial noise at inference time, requiring costly backpropagation through the denoiser. Moreover, their diversity objectives typically repel generated samples without accounting for how the corresponding teacher model distributes its outputs. We introduce Teacher-Weighted Initial-noise Spherical Transform (TWIST), a lightweight network that transforms the low-frequency components of initial noise to improve diversity without inference-time optimization. TWIST uses a teacher-guided determinantal point process (DPP) objective to align the feature-space diversity of student samples with that of the teacher. By amortizing noise optimization into a single network, TWIST eliminates inference-time backpropagation while preserving the efficiency of few-step generation. On SDXL-Turbo, TWIST matches the diversity of MoNO at less than 25% of its inference cost, while achieving higher recall and better PickScore, ImageReward, and CLIPScore under matched compute budgets. On SDXL-Lightning, it achieves comparable recall at lower cost without the ImageReward degradation observed with MoNO.

</details>


### [126] [SIA: Selective Image Analogy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33894)
*Amirhossein Alimohammadi,Kian Izadpanah,Bardiya Kariminia,Yiorgos Chrysanthou,Ali Mahdavi-Amiri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出选择性图像类比(SIA)新设定：从单一演示对中编码多种编辑，推理时由用户选择性地将部分编辑迁移到查询图像B，并用数据集与掩码对齐抑制机制(门控LoRA+概念抑制向量)实现无需为每种编辑组合提供新演示对的选择性控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像类比方法假设演示对(A, A')中的所有变化都必须被完整迁移，当用户只想应用其中一部分编辑时缺乏灵活性，且为每种编辑组合重新提供演示对代价高昂。

Method: 1) 构建多编辑图像类比数据集，包含完整编辑与部分编辑目标；2) 先训练模型学习迁移完整演示变换，再通过掩码对齐抑制机制获得选择性控制：token门控LoRA在去噪过程中将适配器更新限制在被抑制的编辑token上，概念抑制向量在去噪前弱化文本嵌入中选定的token，从而抑制A'中仍可见但用户不需的编辑，同时保留所选编辑。

Result: 大量定量与定性实验及用户研究表明，该方法能有效实现选择性编辑迁移，同时保留用户选定编辑并抑制不想要的编辑。

Conclusion: SIA为图像类比提供了灵活的选择性编辑迁移能力，单次演示即可支持多种编辑组合，无需为每种组合重新提供演示对。

Abstract: Image analogy transfers a demonstrated transformation from a before/after pair (A, A') to a new query image B. Existing methods assume all changes in (A, A') should be fully transferred, limiting flexibility when users want only a subset of the demonstrated edits. We introduce Selective Image Analogy (SIA), a new setting where a single demonstration pair encodes multiple edits, and the user selects at inference time which edits to apply to B. To support this, we curate a multi-edit analogy dataset with full-edit and partial-edit targets. We then train a model that first learns to transfer the complete demonstrated transformation, and then gains selective control through a mask-aligned suppression mechanism: a token-gated LoRA that restricts adapter updates to suppressed edit tokens during denoising, paired with a concept suppression vector that weakens the selected tokens in the text embeddings before denoising. Together, these components allow SIA to suppress unwanted edits that remain visible in A', while preserving the user-selected ones, all without requiring a new demonstration pair for each edit combination. We perform extensive quantitative and qualitative evaluations along with user studies to demonstrate the efficacy of our approach.

</details>


### [127] [DeltaRAE: Derivative Tokenization for Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33886)
*Mingzhuo Yu,Shengcao Cao,Chengzhi Mao,Yong Cheng,Di Jiang,Lijun Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeltaRAE 是一种面向视频的因果表示自编码器，通过"导数式分词"降低 token 消耗，使生成器能够生成长达一分钟的视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜空间视频生成器仅在数秒级片段上训练，瓶颈在于分词器：静态帧与运动帧消耗同样多的 token，且生成时需要不存在的未来帧，无法因果化。

Method: 提出 DeltaRAE：周期性锚帧完整编码，其余帧各用少量 token 编码（导数式分词）；因果编码器与解码器读取冻结的预训练图像编码器；分词器可在一分钟视频上端到端训练。

Result: 在 UCF-101 与 Kinetics-600 上，基于 DeltaRAE 潜变量的标准扩散 Transformer 与 SOTA 视频分词器相当；16 帧片段上分词器 GFLOPs 减半，一分钟视频 token 数减少 18.8 倍；视频预测可生成完整一分钟视频；同等 token 预算但无预训练编码器时，生成器在多数视频中 15 秒内崩溃。

Conclusion: 因果化的稀疏帧表示结合冻结预训练图像编码器，是突破短视频长度限制、实现长时视频生成的关键设计。

Abstract: Latent video generators are trained on clips of a few seconds, and much of this limit lies in their tokenizers, which spend as many tokens on a static frame as on a moving one and need future frames that do not exist during generation. We introduce DeltaRAE, a causal representation autoencoder for video. Its derivative tokenization encodes periodic anchor frames in full and all remaining frames with a few tokens each, and its causal encoder and decoder read a frozen pretrained image encoder. On UCF-101 and Kinetics-600, a standard diffusion transformer trained on DeltaRAE latents is competitive with state-of-the-art video tokenizers. DeltaRAE halves the tokenizer GFLOPs on 16-frame clips relative to keeping the same number of patch tokens for every frame, and it represents a minute of video with 18.8x fewer tokens. The tokenizer trains end to end on one-minute videos, and in video prediction a generator on its latents produces a full minute of video. The same generator collapses within 15 seconds in most videos when the tokenizer has the same token budget but no pretrained encoder.

</details>


### [128] [Off the Driven Path: Transferable Road Understanding in Driving VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33878)
*Lena Wild,Christian Helten,Katie Z Luo,Jakob Thumm,Rafael Valencia,Patric Jensfelt,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Road-KSU框架将HD地图与标注转为道路中心场景图，用以探测端到端驾驶策略内化的道路知识；在NAVSIM上发现多数测试样本与训练样本地理邻近，驾驶分数随训练经过该地次数陡增，表明存在路线记忆；地理不相交划分下动作性能在最不熟悉的转弯处退化最严重，语言问答则在直道弯道均下降而在动态场景保持稳定；数据扩展呈现双重分离，地理多样性才是可迁移道路知识的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端驾驶策略在推理阶段越来越不依赖显式HD地图，因此"模型究竟内化了多少道路知识"缺乏可观测的检验手段；现有评测分数无法区分真正的道路理解与对训练地点几何的记忆。

Method: 1) Road-KSU：自动将HD地图与交通参与者标注转换为道路中心场景图，并由此生成道路导向的问答与推理任务，辅以基于地图的轨迹合规性指标；2) 构造地理不相交（disjoint）数据划分，与输出模态（语言 vs. 动作）× 地点熟悉度（见过 vs. 未见道路）构成2×2评测矩阵；3) 在NAVSIM上分析13个已发表规划器性能随测试地点训练经过次数的关系；4) 在地理不相交划分上重训练并做Road-KSU探测；5) 数据规模扩展实验对比"同地更多趟次"与"固定预算铺更多地点"两种策略。

Result: NAVSIM中95%测试样本距某训练样本在10m以内；13个规划器在转弯处的得分随训练经过该测试地点的次数急剧上升，指向道路几何记忆。地理不相交重训练后，动作空间性能在最不熟悉的转弯场景下降最多，失败以几何合规性为主；语言侧，道路中心VQA在直道与弯道上均下降，但在动态场景内容上保持稳定。数据扩展显示双重分离：增加同地点行驶趟次改善未见道路上的驾驶表现但道路问答不变；将固定数据预算分散到更多地点则提升道路问答而驾驶性能变化很小。

Conclusion: 驾驶分数的提升并不必然意味着对未见道路具备更广的道路理解；地理多样性是获得可迁移道路知识的关键，道路中心探针评测可作为动作指标之外的必要补充。

Abstract: End-to-end driving policies increasingly operate without explicit HD maps at inference, raising the question of what road knowledge they actually internalize. To probe this knowledge through model outputs, we introduce Road-KSU, a framework that automatically converts HD maps and actor annotations into a road-centric scene graph. From this, we derive road-focused question answering and reasoning targets, complemented by map-based trajectory compliance metrics. Pairing Road-KSU with geographically disjoint splits yields a $2 \times 2$ evaluation crossing output modality (language vs. action) with place familiarity (seen vs. unseen roads). On NAVSIM, 95% of test samples lie within 10m of a training sample, and scores of 13 published planners on turns rise steeply with the number of training drives through the test location, pointing to memorized road geometry. Retraining on geographically disjoint splits and probing with Road-KSU confirm that action-space performance degrades most on unfamiliar turns, with failures dominated by geometric compliance. Language accuracy, however, drops on road-centric VQA on straight and curved roads alike, and remains stable on dynamic scene content. Scaling the training data further reveals a double dissociation: more drives through the same places improve driving on unseen roads but leave road question answering unchanged, whereas spreading a fixed data budget over more places increases road question answering with little change in driving performance. These findings show that improved driving scores need not imply broader road understanding on unseen roads, and identify geographic diversity as key to transferable road knowledge.

</details>


### [129] [MAFNet-UR$^3$: Mask-Aware Fusion Network for Unified Raindrop and Reflection Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33866)
*Yeongjin Jeong,Hyunjin Cho,Minsang Kim,Jiwon Lee,Jae Woong Soh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出三阶段框架MAFNet-UR³，利用多视角观测实现场景一致性的雨滴与反射统一去除（UR³），在RDRF-testing上超越DiffUR³ 2.33 dB PSNR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雨滴与反射在挡风玻璃上频繁共现并共同遮挡场景内容，使统一去除（UR³）成为高度病态问题。现有基于扩散模型的方法虽能生成视觉上合理的修复结果，但在严重受损区域可能生成与场景不一致的结构。

Method: 提出MAFNet-UR³，采用三阶段框架充分利用多观测信息：Stage I 抑制雨滴同时保留反射层；Stage II 进行先验引导的反射去除，随后做图像空间精修；Stage III 从中间修复结果估计退化掩码以提供退化线索，并在多观测间执行掩码感知的多尺度融合，以保持场景结构与细节。

Result: 在RDRF-testing基准上取得SOTA，PSNR较DiffUR³提升2.33 dB，挑战赛分数提升2.7634；在LoViF 2026 UR³ Challenge中排名第三，最终得分41.1416。

Conclusion: 通过多阶段分解与多观测掩码感知融合，MAFNet-UR³缓解了扩散类方法的结构不一致问题，实现了场景一致的高质量统一雨滴与反射去除。

Abstract: Raindrops and reflections frequently co-occur on windshields and jointly obscure scene content, making their unified removal, referred to as UR$^3$, a highly ill-posed problem. Existing approaches are mainly based on diffusion models and thus can produce visually plausible restorations. However, they may generate scene-inconsistent structures in severely corrupted regions. To address this limitation, we propose MAFNet-UR$^3$, which adopts a three-stage framework to exploit multiple observations for scene-consistent unified raindrop and reflection removal. Specifically, Stage I suppresses raindrops while preserving the reflection layer, and Stage II performs prior-guided reflection removal followed by image-space refinement. Stage III estimates degradation masks from intermediate restoration results to provide degradation cues and performs mask-aware multi-scale fusion across observations to preserve scene structure and fine details. As a result, our proposed MAFNet-UR$^3$-L achieves state-of-the-art performance on the RDRF-testing benchmark, outperforming DiffUR$^3$ by 2.33 dB in PSNR and 2.7634 in the challenge score. Our method ranked third in the LoViF 2026 UR$^3$ Challenge, achieving a final score of 41.1416.

</details>


### [130] [Recovering What Matters, Transferring Only What Is Reliable: Toward Precise Reference-Based Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33455)
*Sihan Ji,Wenxin Chen,Yue Shi,Jiezhang Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PreciseRef：提出非对称孪生注意力（ATA）与双尺度参考门控（DRG）的参考感知生成框架，实现忠实且可靠的参考图像超分，并建立VeriRef基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RefSR存在两大根本问题：(1) 感知合理性不等于内容忠实性，方法常生成视觉可信但未忠实恢复参考特有信息（文字、编码、身份线索）的细节；(2) 忠实读取参考不等于可靠信息迁移，提高参考利用率会把误导性内容引入复原过程，因全局语义相关并不保证局部对应一致。

Method: 提出PreciseRef框架。核心为Asymmetric Twin-Attention (ATA)：目标与参考图像共享统一注意力空间但参考保持自身形状，通过联合注意力让图像token直接交互，无需显式对齐即可实现细粒度参考信息检索。并引入Dual-Scale Reference Gating (DRG)，含Semantic Relevance Gating (SRG)过滤全局无关参考上下文，以及Bidirectional Consistency Gating (BCG)抑制不可靠的局部对应。同时建立VeriRef基准，评测精确参考依赖内容恢复与对失配参考的鲁棒性。

Result: 字符准确率85.6%、精确编码解码76.3%，现有方法分别为17.4%与0%；有效抑制错误参考迁移；在CUFED5与WR-SR上取得SOTA感知质量。

Conclusion: PreciseRef兼顾参考内容忠实恢复与可靠信息迁移，并通过VeriRef基准为RefSR的忠实性评测提供新标准。

Abstract: Reference-based super-resolution (RefSR) aims to recover missing details from high-quality references. However, existing methods face two fundamental challenges. First, perceptual plausibility does not guarantee content fidelity. Existing methods often produce visually convincing details without faithfully recovering reference-specific information, such as text, codes, and identity cues. Second, faithful reference reading does not guarantee reliable information transfer. Increasing reference utilization also exposes the restoration process to misleading content, as global semantic relevance does not necessarily imply local correspondence consistency. To address these challenges, we propose PreciseRef, a reference-aware generative framework for faithful and reliable RefSR. At its core, Asymmetric Twin-Attention (ATA) allows target and reference images to participate in a unified attention space while the reference keeps its own shape. By letting their image tokens interact directly through joint attention, ATA facilitates fine-grained reference information retrieval without explicit image alignment. To ensure reliable information transfer, we introduce Dual-Scale Reference Gating (DRG), which combines Semantic Relevance Gating (SRG) to filter globally irrelevant reference context and Bidirectional Consistency Gating (BCG) to suppress unreliable local correspondences. Furthermore, we establish VeriRef, a benchmark evaluating exact reference-dependent content recovery and robustness to mismatched references. Extensive experiments show that our method achieves 85.6% character accuracy and 76.3% exact code decoding, compared with 17.4% and 0% for existing methods, while effectively suppressing erroneous reference transfer. It also achieves state-of-the-art perceptual quality on CUFED5 and WR-SR.

</details>


### [131] [DualCast: Dual-Image Conditioned Diffusion Transformers for Digital Human E-Commerce Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33734)
*Lifan Jiang,Yifan Huang,Jixuan Zhang,Peifeng Wang,Yuying Ma,Dehai Zhao,Deng Cai,Boxi Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GameWeaver：统一扩散框架，用 tipxel 表示联合生成视频-相机轨迹数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型训练需要大规模视频与同步相机轨迹配对数据，但现有采集依赖游戏专用管线，难以扩展。

Method: 提出 tipxel（像素射线端点在参考坐标系下的归一化3D坐标），三通道 tipxel 场与 RGB 帧共享预训练视频模型 VAE；采用全可训练双流 Transformer，通过双向门控注意力交换外观与几何特征并以共享文本特征双向条件化；最后用确定性拟合从生成的 tipxel 场恢复相机内参与归一化轨迹。统一覆盖三项任务：视频→相机位姿估计、给定轨迹的视频生成、参考图+文本的联合视频-相机生成。

Result: 相机控制任务归一化平移 MSE 为 0.0029；联合生成指令遵循得分 85.48/100，优于最佳级联方案的 64.71。

Conclusion: 验证了以 tipxel 场统一建模外观与几何、自动化生成配对视频-相机数据的可行性。

Abstract: Recent advances in world models for game interaction and control have increased the demand for large-scale gameplay videos paired with camera trajectories. However, capturing synchronized camera trajectories often requires game-specific pipelines, limiting scalability. We therefore propose GameWeaver, a unified diffusion framework that automatically produces paired video--camera data through three complementary tasks: camera pose estimation from observed video, video generation along a prescribed camera trajectory, and joint video--camera generation from a reference image and text. First, each tipxel stores the normalized 3D coordinates of a pixel ray's endpoint in a reference coordinate frame. The resulting three-channel tipxel fields and RGB frames share the pretrained video model's VAE. Next, a fully trainable dual-stream Transformer exchanges appearance and geometry features through bidirectional gated attention and conditions both streams on shared text features. Finally, deterministic fitting recovers camera intrinsics and normalized trajectories from generated tipxel fields. In our evaluation tasks, GameWeaver achieves a normalized translation MSE of 0.0029 for camera control; for joint generation, it scores 85.48 out of 100 on instruction adherence versus 64.71 for the best cascade on that metric, validating the feasibility of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [132] [MOVR-SAR: A Multi-resolution SAR Registration Dataset under Orbit-crossing and View-variant Imaging Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33673)
*Yuchi Pu,Bosong Qi,Pengcheng Shi,Jiayuan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文发布MOVR-SAR——一个包含45万余对配准瓦片的多分辨率几何感知SAR影像配准数据集与基准，并证明在其实数据上微调可大幅提升跨平台、跨几何构型配准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同成像几何（轨道方向、视向、入射角）会改变后向散射、引起地形畸变并影响特征可见性，使SAR-SAR配准困难；而大规模、几何感知的公开基准数据集仍然稀缺。

Method: 构建MOVR-SAR：从Capella、Umbra、Sentinel-1、NISAR的2774对场景派生450,845对已配准且质量筛选的瓦片对，覆盖X/C/L波段、0.25–20 m地面采样距离及多样区域与地物类型；建立统一框架，依据轨道方向与视向定义四类成像几何对，并为Capella、Umbra标注视角分离度，支持同向/反向照明分层评测；对代表性匹配方法进行系统评测与微调实验。

Result: 官方权重与跨模态预训练权重在高分辨率X波段上表现很差；在MOVR-SAR上微调后精度显著提升并缩小平台、几何对类别与视角分离度间的差异：FlexGlue AUC@5由3.06%升至89.79%（Capella）、1.79%升至89.03%（Umbra），在Sentinel-1与NISAR上达93.41%与94.77%；微调显著削弱官方权重的“同轨道同视向”优势，并在Capella上反转三方法平均类别排序，说明该偏置源于训练数据覆盖而非跨几何匹配的固有局限；地物类别影响仍随平台与方法而异。

Conclusion: MOVR-SAR为鲁棒SAR-SAR配准提供了训练资源与几何感知基准，数据集与模型权重将于GitHub公开。

Abstract: Synthetic aperture radar (SAR) image registration under varying acquisition geometries is challenging because orbit direction, look side, and incidence angle affect backscatter, terrain-induced distortion, and feature visibility. However, large-scale geometry-aware benchmarks remain scarce. This paper presents MOVR-SAR, a multi-resolution dataset of 450,845 registered and quality-screened tile pairs derived from 2774 scene pairs from Capella, Umbra, Sentinel-1, and NISAR. It covers X, C, and L bands, ground sampling distances of 0.25-20 m, and diverse regions and land-cover types. A unified framework defines four imaging-geometry pair categories from orbit direction and look side, plus viewing-angle-separation labels for Capella and Umbra, enabling stratified evaluation under same- and opposite-direction illumination. Systematic evaluation of representative matching methods shows that official and cross-modal pretrained weights perform poorly on high-resolution X-band imagery. Fine-tuning on MOVR-SAR improves accuracy and reduces disparities across platforms, imaging-geometry pair categories, and viewing-angle separations. For example, fine-tuning increases FlexGlue AUC@5 from 3.06% to 89.79% on Capella and from 1.79% to 89.03% on Umbra, reaching 93.41% and 94.77% on Sentinel-1 and NISAR, respectively. Fine-tuning substantially reduces the official-weight same-orbit, same-look-side advantage and reverses the three-method average category ranking on Capella, suggesting that this bias reflects training-data coverage rather than an invariant limitation of cross-geometry matching. Land-cover effects remain platform- and method-dependent. MOVR-SAR provides a training resource and geometry-aware benchmark for robust SAR-SAR registration. The dataset and model weights will be released at https://github.com/fdhzhz5870/MOVR-SAR.

</details>


### [133] [Fine-Grained Ego-Vehicle Action Recognition from Driving Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33654)
*Zhipeng Bao,Wenjie Zhao,Qianwen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出统一的大规模自车动作识别基准EgoManeuver（16类横向+6类纵向动作，融合7个公开驾驶数据集，86.6K片段、190.8小时、覆盖26国），并提出视频基线DenseEgo，横向/纵向mAP分别达82.7/83.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度自车动作识别可将大规模驾驶视频转化为结构化、可检索的驾驶场景，服务于关键机动发现与自动驾驶模型的开发评估。但现有驾驶视频普遍缺乏显式自车动作标签，各数据集在动作定义、标注粒度与可用监督上不一致，缺乏统一基准。

Method: 构建多源标注系统：利用各源数据集已有的车辆信号、自车位姿、HD地图拓扑与文本描述等互补证据，在统一的细粒度动作分类体系下赋予标签；跨数据集标准化时间动作边界；通过运动学一致性校验与定向人工审核验证标签质量；提出DenseEgo基线，结合空间感知表征与逐步稠密动作监督。

Result: EgoManeuver含86.6K片段、190.8小时、覆盖26国，并提供长尾感知评测协议。DenseEgo在横向与纵向动作上分别取得82.7与83.5 mAP，较最强现有视频模型分别提升1.5与5.0个点。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了统一、可复现的细粒度自车动作识别基准与强基线，并演示了DenseEgo与语言模型智能体结合以检索符合用户指定动作与场景的驾驶视频；代码、数据与模型权重将公开。

Abstract: Fine-grained ego-vehicle action recognition turns large-scale driving video into structured, searchable driving scenarios, providing a foundation for discovering critical maneuvers and systematically developing and evaluating autonomous driving models. However, large-scale driving videos often lack explicit ego-action labels, and existing datasets vary in action definitions, annotation granularity, and available supervision. We introduce EgoManeuver, a unified benchmark of 16 lateral and 6 longitudinal actions constructed from seven public driving datasets. Our multi-source labeling system leverages existing annotations and complementary evidence available in each source dataset, including vehicle signals, ego poses, HD-map topology, and captions, to assign labels under a unified fine-grained action taxonomy. We further standardize temporal action boundaries across datasets and validate the resulting labels through kinematic consistency checks and targeted human review. The resulting benchmark contains 86.6K segments spanning 190.8 h across 26 countries and provides a long-tail-aware evaluation protocol. We also introduce DenseEgo, a video-based baseline that combines spatially aware representations with dense per-step action supervision. On the EgoManeuver, DenseEgo achieves 82.7 lateral and 83.5 longitudinal mAP, outperforming the strongest existing video model by 1.5 and 5.0 points, respectively. Finally, we demonstrate one way to integrate DenseEgo with a language-model agent for retrieving driving videos that match user-specified actions and scenarios. All code, datasets, and model weights will be publicly released.

</details>


### [134] [Dynamic Forcing: Learning Action-Grounded Sparse Dynamic Representations with Dense Future Guidance for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33799)
*Ziqiang Li,Shisheng Chen,Yunnan Wang,Wenyao Zhang,Zili Lin,Yuyang Zhang,Xudong Yang,Fangzhuo Jin,Chenyu Zhou,Wenjun Zeng,Xin Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CompactWAM 提出稀疏-稠密联合预测框架，用少量动态 token 表示动作相关的未来变化、以当前观测为静态锚点，并通过 Dynamic Forcing 训练，实现推理时无需稠密未来建模的高效世界动作模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）依赖稠密未来视觉预测来支持机器人控制，但稠密建模会反复编码当前观测中已有的静态上下文，而真正与动作相关的动态变化在空间上往往稀疏，造成计算冗余与表示低效。

Method: 提出 CompactWAM：在视频监督的共享骨干内进行稀疏表示学习，保留当前观测作上下文锚点，用少量紧凑动态 token 表示预期变化，稀疏通路不依赖重建目标。引入 Dynamic Forcing：以直接动作监督结合特权稠密未来引导，包含“动作功能引导”（使稀疏与稠密分支对动作专家的贡献一致）与“动态感知分布匹配”（以静态参考对齐时序关系变化），从而促使 token 兼具动作相关性与动态保真度；推理时单次视觉前向即可生成动作。

Result: LIBERO 上成功率 98.4%，LIBERO-Plus 上 77.9%；仅用 9.2% 的稠密未来 token 预算时仍保持 98.3% 与 76.6%。在 RoboTwin 2.0 与真实世界操作任务上进一步验证了所学稀疏预测表示的有效性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，动作相关的未来信息可由极少量紧凑动态 token 有效承载，配合静态锚点与 Dynamic Forcing 监督即可在显著压缩未来建模开销的同时保持甚至接近稠密建模的控制性能，为高效 WAM 提供可行路径。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) support robot control through visual prediction, but dense future modeling repeatedly encodes static context already available in the current observation, while action-relevant changes are often spatially sparse. We study sparse dynamic representation learning, retaining the current observation as a contextual anchor and using a small set of compact dynamic tokens to represent anticipated changes. We develop CompactWAM, a sparse-dense joint predictive framework that enables sparse representation learning within a video-supervised shared backbone, without requiring a reconstruction objective for the sparse pathway. Since a limited token budget alone does not specify which information to retain, we introduce Dynamic Forcing, which pairs direct action supervision with privileged dense future guidance to promote action relevance and dynamic fidelity. While action prediction gradients directly drive compact tokens to capture control-relevant cues, dense future guidance further shapes them via action functional guidance that matches sparse and dense contributions to the action expert, and dynamics-aware distribution matching, which aligns temporal relational changes relative to a static reference. At inference, a single visual forward pass encodes current observations and compact dynamic tokens for action generation, eliminating dense future modeling. CompactWAM achieves 98.4\% success on LIBERO and 77.9\% on LIBERO-Plus, retaining respective success rates of 98.3\% and 76.6\% with only 9.2\% of the dense future token budget. Evaluations on RoboTwin~2.0 and real-world manipulation further demonstrate the effectiveness of the learned sparse predictive representations.

</details>


### [135] [Agentic Reinforcement Learning with Immediate and Prospective Credit Assignment for Medical Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33777)
*Yuqi Cheng,Rui Meng,Yijin Li,Chandra Sekhar Reddy Edula,Xiuwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedPACT是一个智能体强化学习框架，通过即时答案支持和前瞻性引导学习选择性观察策略，在六个医学VQA基准上提升准确率、泛化性并降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学VQA需要智能体获取有用证据并决定下一步观察位置，但仅依赖最终答案的奖励无法揭示哪些观察支撑了回答，也无法引导后续检查。

Method: 提出内容受控估计器，在共享的真实观察历史下比较真实观察与匹配的替代观察所诱导的下一动作，从而隔离观察内容对后续决策的贡献；结合即时答案支持与前瞻引导两种信用，在GRPO中将观察信用分配给对应的工具调用token，并配合轨迹级结果监督；仅用单一视觉语言模型与图像-问题-答案监督，信用估计仅在训练阶段使用。

Result: 在4B与8B骨干、六个医学VQA基准上取得更高准确率、更强的分布外泛化能力，并显著降低推理成本；案例表明模型能避免重复观察并利用已获证据指导后续检查。

Conclusion: MedPACT通过细粒度观察信用分配实现高效、可泛化的选择性观察策略，具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Medical visual question answering (VQA) requires acquiring useful evidence and deciding where to look next. Yet final-answer rewards do not reveal which observations support answering or guide subsequent inspection. We introduce MedPACT, an agentic reinforcement learning framework that learns selective observation policies through immediate answer support and prospective guidance. Our content-controlled estimator compares next actions induced by real and matched alternative observations under a shared real-observation history, isolating how observed content improves subsequent decisions. We combine both forms of credit within GRPO, assigning observation credit to the corresponding tool-call tokens alongside trajectory-level outcome supervision. MedPACT uses a single vision-language model and image-question-answer supervision, with credit estimation confined to training. Experiments with 4B and 8B backbones on six medical VQA benchmarks demonstrate improved accuracy, strong out-of-distribution generalization, and substantially lower inference costs. Case studies further illustrate how MedPACT avoids repeated observations and uses acquired evidence to guide subsequent inspection, demonstrating real-world practice potential.

</details>


### [136] [Playfulness as Feedback for Agentic Game Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33457)
*Weijia Mao,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Zhen Fang,Danze Chen,Dongxing Mao,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Play2Evolve 递归游戏开发框架（初始化-精炼-演化三阶段），以"可玩性"为反馈信号驱动智能体试玩诊断、修复与迭代，并结合视频生成模型增强素材，在 GameCraft-Bench 上取得 60.05 的 SOTA 分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码智能体可从自然语言生成可运行游戏，但"可运行"不等于"好玩"。作者认为高质量可玩游戏需具备三点：可靠的核心功能、具有吸引力的视觉设计与素材、支持探索与成长的丰富玩法。现有工作对智能体如何提升这些可玩性维度缺乏探索。

Method: 提出递归式游戏开发框架 Play2Evolve，包含三阶段：Initialization（初始化）、Refinement（精炼）、Evolution（演化）。以可玩性作为反馈，智能体通过试玩诊断功能、视觉呈现与玩法三方面的问题，反馈用于缺陷修复与素材改进，并进一步提出新的玩法与美学目标以持续演化游戏。为丰富素材，将视频生成模型与代码结合，生成静态与动态素材并集成到交互式玩法中。

Result: 在所评估方法中达到 SOTA：GameCraft-Bench 得分 60.05，对比仅经 Game Initialization 的 50.81 与 Play2Code 的 52.46；人类整体质量评分 3.67，对比分别为 2.38 与 2.43。代码将开源。

Conclusion: 以可玩性为反馈的三阶段递归开发范式，配合视频生成模型补强的多模态素材生成，可显著提升智能体生成游戏的玩法质量与视觉表现，优于单轮初始化与已有基线方法。

Abstract: Coding agents can generate runnable games from natural-language descriptions. However, a runnable game does not guarantee an excellent gameplay experience for players. In our view, a playful game should provide 1) reliable core functionality, 2) appealing visual design and assets, and 3) rich gameplay that supports exploration and progression. How agents can improve these aspects of playfulness remains underexplored. In our work, we propose Play2Evolve, a recursive game development framework with three stages: Initialization, Refinement, and Evolution. Using playfulness as feedback, agents playtest the game and diagnose issues in its functionality, visual presentation, and gameplay. Their feedback guides bug fixes and asset improvements. It also helps agents propose new gameplay and aesthetic goals for continued game evolution. To enrich game materials, we combine a video generation model with code to create appealing static and dynamic assets and integrate them into interactive gameplay. Finally, Play2Evolve achieves state-of-the-art performance among the evaluated methods, scoring 60.05 on GameCraft-Bench compared with 50.81 after Game Initialization and 52.46 for Play2Code. Human overall-quality ratings reach 3.67, compared with 2.38 and 2.43, respectively. Our code will be released at https://github.com/showlab/Play2Evolve.

</details>


### [137] [ViCARD: Visual Credit Assignment with Reward-Gated Distillation for Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33437)
*Zijian Huang,Jiasi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViCARD 将逐 token 的视觉自蒸馏监督与可验证正确性奖励结合，解决结果导向 RL 在视觉推理中的“全失败无信号”和“优势均匀广播”两大问题，在细粒度视觉推理上优于 RL 与蒸馏基线且保持通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结果导向 RL 在视觉推理中存在两个关键缺陷：(1) 当感知错误导致整组采样回复全部失败时，无任何学习信号；(2) 优势值均匀广播到回复中每个 token，缺乏细粒度信用分配。而视觉自蒸馏虽能借助以证据为中心的图像裁剪提供密集监督，但学生仅从 token 分布学习往往无法得到正确最终答案。作者通过审计训练 rollout 揭示这两种失效模式互补，因而希望融合二者。

Method: 提出 ViCARD（Visual Credit Assignment with Reward-Gated Distillation）：对每个 rollout 组，使用输入图像被裁剪到相关视觉区域的教师模型，向学生提供逐 token 监督，同时以正确性为价值/门控；若整组采样全部失败，则额外采样一个裁剪教师答案，仅当该答案正确时才激活 on-policy 蒸馏，从而为无信息组提供学习信号。

Result: 跨模型规模的广泛对比实验表明，ViCARD 在细粒度视觉推理性能上优于 RL 与蒸馏基线，同时保留了广泛的视觉推理能力。

Conclusion: 细粒度视觉信用分配（逐 token 蒸馏）与可验证正确性奖励的融合，可在有信息奖励组内提供密集视觉信用、在全失败组内恢复学习信号，是提升视觉推理 RL 训练效果的有效方案。

Abstract: Visual reasoning requires models to identify relevant visual evidence and use it to derive correct answers. Outcomebased reinforcement learning (RL) faces two key challenges in this regime: it yields no learning signal when perceptual errors cause all sampled responses to fail, and it broadcasts a uniform advantage across every token within a response. Conversely, while visual self-distillation offers dense guidance from evidence-centered image crops, student learning from token distributions alone often fails to elicit correct final answers. Motivated by an audit of training rollouts that exposes these complementary failure modes, we introduce ViCARD (Visual Credit Assignment with RewardGated Distillation), which integrates per-token visual supervision with verified correctness rewards.
