For each rollout group, we leverage a teacher with its input image cropped to the relevant visual area, and use this to provide per-token supervision to the student while also valuing correctness. If all sampled rollouts fail, ViCARD samples an additional crop-teacher answer and activates on-policy distillation only if that answer is correct. Together, these mechanisms provide fine-grained visual credit within informative reward groups and a learning signal for otherwise uninformative all-fail groups. Extensive comparisons across model scales demonstrate that ViCARD improves fine-grained visual reasoning performance over RL and distillation baselines, while retaining broad visual reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [138] [DreamTour: Layout Steering for Training-Free World Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33378)
*Srikumar Sastry,Meher Gitika Karumuri,Chenqi Li,Sarosij Bose,Mellon Zhang,Allen Tu,Ziyang Long,Yen-Liang Lin,Alexander Schwing,Nathan Jacobs*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DreamTour 是一个免训练框架，从单张图像生成相机轨迹视频，通过扩展视野、深度渲染近似目标视图并施加 Layout Steering 对齐早期潜变量，实现更精确的相机方向与移动距离控制，并支持自回归长轨迹生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散与世界模型能生成逼真视频，但难以可靠匹配指定的相机方向与移动距离，缺乏精确的相机运动控制。

Method: 1) 使用冻结的图像 inpainting 先验在保留原始像素的前提下将照片扩展至原始视野之外；2) 沿目标相机路径对扩展场景做基于深度的渲染，得到近似目标视图作为空间引导；3) 提出 Layout Steering，通过衰减的低频校正将早期视频潜变量与这些视图对齐，后续去噪步骤负责细化外观与动态；4) 对更长轨迹采用自回归分段生成，每段从上一段末帧重新构造引导，保持全程相机控制。

Result: 在 VBench 上提升方向准确性与移动距离标定，同时保持有竞争力的视频质量；在 WorldScore 上增强链式运动中的相机控制与场景一致性。

Conclusion: 免训练的空间引导可在预训练视频扩散与世界模型中实现更准确、更一致的相机控制。

Abstract: Video diffusion and world models generate plausible videos but do not reliably match the requested camera direction and travel distance. We present DreamTour, a training-free framework that generates camera tours from a single image with more accurate control over camera motion. DreamTour first uses a frozen image inpainting prior to extend the photograph beyond its original field of view while preserving the input pixels. Depth-based rendering of this extended scene along the requested camera path produces approximate target views that serve as spatial guidance. We introduce Layout Steering, which aligns early video latents with these views through decaying low-frequency corrections, leaving later denoising to refine appearance and dynamics. For longer tours, we autoregressively generate the video segments, repeating guidance construction from each segment's final frame to maintain camera control across the full trajectory. DreamTour improves directional accuracy and travel calibration on VBench while maintaining competitive video quality. On WorldScore, it strengthens camera control and scene consistency across chained movements. Together, these results demonstrate that training-free spatial guidance enables more accurate and consistent camera control in pretrained video diffusion and world models.

</details>


### [139] [DDxBasis: Explaining Frozen Foundation Encoder-based Medical Classification by Exact Differential Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33358)
*Luojie Huang,Gregory N. McKay,Nicholas J. Durr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DDxBasis 在冻结基础编码器上，用"概念瓶颈+极轴概念基"将医学图像分类器的解释组织为鉴别诊断（DDx）：每个轴区分两组诊断，并可读出典型表现、关键征象与反事实编辑。五个医学数据集上精度最优且接近领域专用方法；概念基可精确分解决策且不损精度，并能恢复细胞谱系、稀有亚型、与皮肤科医生结构标注一致，并暴露真实与人为植入的捷径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床鉴别诊断依靠少数能将目标诊断与替代诊断区分开的征象来解释。现有医学图像分类器的解释要么"忠实但分散"（散布于数百个概念，难以阅读），要么"紧凑但部分决策未被解释"（不完备），缺乏既紧凑又忠实、且能按鉴别诊断形式呈现的解释方式。

Method: 三步框架，全部作用于冻结的基础编码器：(1) 概念瓶颈在编码器每一层读取全部 patch token，采用门控二阶槽注意力（gated, second-order slot attention）提取概念；(2) 概念基将概念重组为"两极轴"，每轴为两组诊断之间的差异（differential）；(3) 从每个轴读出鉴别诊断：两极的典型表现、本病例的相关征象，以及对边界病例的反事实编辑。无需有序的层级或概念标签。

Result: 在五个医学数据集上，DDxBasis 是精度最高的冻结编码器模型，并接近已发表的最佳领域专用结果。由于每个概念贡献自身证据，其可复现的概念基能精确拆分每一次决策，且不带来精度损失。在缺乏有序阶段或概念标签的情况下，轴仍能恢复细胞谱系与稀有亚型，与皮肤科医生的结构标注一致，并暴露人为植入的与真实的捷径。

Conclusion: 将可解释性组织为鉴别诊断形式的概念基，可在保持精度竞争力的同时实现紧凑且精确分解的忠实解释，并具备结构发现与捷径检测能力。

Abstract: In differential diagnosis (DDx), a clinician explains a diagnosis by the few findings that separate it from the alternatives. Medical image classifiers cannot yet explain themselves this way. Existing explanations are either faithful but spread over hundreds of concepts, or compact but leave part of each decision unexplained. We present DDxBasis, which explains a classifier on a frozen foundation encoder as a differential diagnosis in three steps. First, a concept bottleneck reads all patch tokens at every encoder layer using gated, second-order slot attention. A concept basis then regroups the concepts into two-pole axes, each a differential between groups of diagnoses. Finally, we read a differential diagnosis from each axis through the typical presentation of its poles, the pertinent findings of the case, and counterfactual edits of borderline cases. Across five medical datasets, DDxBasis is the most accurate frozen-encoder model and close to the best published domain-specific results. Because every concept adds its own evidence, its reproducible concept basis splits each decision exactly, at no cost in accuracy. Without ordered stage or concept labels, the axes recover cell lineages and rare subtypes, agree with dermatologists' structure annotations, and expose planted and real shortcuts.

</details>


### [140] [TAT-Det: A Dataset for Testicular Appendage Torsion Detection and Segmentation in Ultrasound Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33940)
*Ying Jiang,Juntao Jiang,Linxuan Fan,Lingling Li,Yaqi Wang,Xiuzhen Yang,Yong Liu,Jiangning Zhang,Jingjing Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发布TAT-Det——首个面向儿童睾丸附件扭转（TAT）的专家标注超声数据集，含1,203例患者共2,600张图像，提供病灶级检测框与像素级分割掩膜，并给出检测与分割基线基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TAT是青春期前儿童急性阴囊疼痛的首要病因，但临床表现非特异，且缺乏标准化的儿童急性阴囊急症数据集，导致诊断困难、AI研究无公开资源可用。

Method: 在浙江大学医学院附属儿童医院采集2,600张超声图像（1,203例患者）；每张阳性图像标注病灶级边界框（用于检测）与像素级分割掩膜（用于精细勾画）；标注经多阶段流程生成：由执业认证的儿童超声医师独立标注、共识复核与精修。

Result: 提供了目标检测与语义分割的基线基准：代表性深度学习模型中，YOLO11m取得最佳检测性能，RWKV-UNet取得最佳分割性能。

Conclusion: TAT-Det弥补了儿童急诊超声公开资源的空白，为自动化TAT分析的人工智能方法开发与评估奠定基础。

Abstract: Background: Testicular appendage torsion (TAT) is the leading cause of acute scrotal pain in prepubertal children. Yet, its diagnosis remains challenging due to nonspecific clinical manifestations and the absence of standardized datasets for pediatric acute scrotal emergencies.
  Results: We present TAT-Det, an expert-annotated ultrasound dataset specifically designed for TAT. TAT-Det comprises 2,600 ultrasound images from 1,203 patients collected at the Children's Hospital, Zhejiang University School of Medicine. Each TAT-positive image is accompanied by lesion-level bounding boxes for detection and pixel-level segmentation masks for fine-grained delineation. Annotations were produced through a multi-stage workflow involving independent annotation by board-certified pediatric sonographers, consensus review, and refinement. Baseline benchmarks for object detection and semantic segmentation using representative deep learning models are further provided, with YOLO11m achieving the best detection performance and RWKV-UNet the best segmentation performance.
  Conclusions: TAT-Det addresses the lack of publicly available resources for pediatric emergency ultrasound and establishes a foundation for developing and evaluating artificial intelligence methods for automated TAT analysis.

</details>


### [141] [RelPRM: Grounding Visual Process Reward Models in Relational Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33903)
*Zhaoxing Zhang,Ziqiu Gui,Cheng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面向视觉关系的过程奖励建模方法RelPRM，并构建自动化数据流水线与基准，显著提升视觉关系验证与步骤评估性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉过程奖励模型（Visual PRM）能检出计算错误，却会给"误读视觉关系"的步骤打高分；而基于后续答案正确性的监督并不能直接判定某一步的关系性断言是否成立，因此需要强化视觉关系验证。

Method: (1) 构建自动化流水线，程序化验证每条断言并标注所需对象、关系与图像区域，产出RelProcessTrain与RelProcessBench（RPB），通过受控图像编辑与步骤改写检验评分是否对关系证据作出恰当响应；(2) 提出RelPRM，含两种训练机制：关系证据自蒸馏（构造保留必要对象、关系与依赖关系的视图，将可靠证据辅助判断迁移到全图）与关系证据驱动的一致性训练（关系编辑反转真值时分数应变化、无关编辑下保持稳定、不同释义下评分变化一致）；推理时仅用原图与推理上下文直接打分。

Result: RelPRM在VisualProcessBench（VPB）上达66.2%、RPB上达68.4%，较VisualPRM-8B分别提升4.2与9.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 针对视觉关系进行训练可同时改善关系验证与步骤评估能力，并可推广到更广泛的多模态推理任务。

Abstract: Visual process reward models (PRMs) can detect calculation errors yet assign high scores to steps that misread visual relations. Supervision based on subsequent answer success does not directly establish whether a step's relational claim holds. To strengthen visual-relation verification, we first build an automated pipeline that programmatically verifies each claim and identifies the required objects, relations, and image regions. It produces RelProcessTrain and RelProcessBench (RPB), which tests whether scores respond appropriately to relational evidence through controlled image edits and step rephrasings. Building on this pipeline, we further introduce RelPRM, a visual-relation-oriented method for process reward modeling with two training mechanisms. Relation-evidence self-distillation constructs views preserving necessary objects, relations, and dependencies, reducing the burden of finding decisive evidence in complex scenes and transferring reliable evidence-assisted judgments to full images. Relation-evidence-driven consistency training encourages appropriate score changes when relation edits reverse a step's truth, stability under irrelevant edits, and similar score changes across paraphrases. At inference, RelPRM directly scores the current step using only the original image and reasoning context. RelPRM achieves 66.2% on VisualProcessBench (VPB) and 68.4% on RPB, gains of 4.2 and 9.9 percentage points over VisualPRM-8B. These results show that training for visual relations improves both relational verification and step assessment across broader multimodal reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [142] [LayoutRecall: Preserving Appearance in Multi-Round 3D Layout-Controlled Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33885)
*Yankuan Chi,Xin Lin,Baihan Yang,Yuheng Liu,Jason Li,Truong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LayoutRecall: 在多轮3D布局控制图像编辑中，将物体外观存于3D框局部坐标系并在目标视图重访时检索复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮编辑序列中，物体被遮挡或移出视野后，后续编辑会丢失其外观；目标布局只给出位置，外观需从早期观测恢复。

Method: 1) Box-Surface Latent Memory：把图像latent存于盒子表面，并按目标视图射线检索；2) Geometry-Aligned Memory Conditioning：以目标视图位置、共享盒局部嵌入与object-wise attention将检索token注入FLUX.2编辑器；3) 对应性损失促使去噪时关注匹配观测。

Result: 提出LayoutLoop多轮3D布局控制编辑基准（校准布局、设计重访）；在LayoutLoop与真实场景数据上实验验证外观恢复能力。

Conclusion: 以3D框局部坐标系的latent记忆实现跨轮外观复用，可在重访视角下恢复对象外观并保持布局控制。

Abstract: 3D layout-controlled image editing gives users explicit control over object placement and camera viewpoint. In a sequence of edits, however, the latest image can lose the appearance of an object when it is occluded or leaves view. The target layout specifies where the object should reappear, while its appearance must be recovered from earlier observations. We introduce LayoutRecall, a multi-round editor that stores and reuses appearance in the local coordinate systems of 3D boxes. Box-Surface Latent Memory retains image latents on box faces and retrieves them along rays in the target view. Geometry-Aligned Memory Conditioning supplies the retrieved tokens to a FLUX.2 editor using target-view positions, shared box-local embeddings, and object-wise attention. A correspondence loss encourages the editor to attend to matching observations during denoising. We also introduce LayoutLoop, a multi-round 3D layout-controlled image editing benchmark with calibrated layouts and designed revisits. We conduct extensive experiments on LayoutLoop and real-scene data to verify the ability of our method. The project page is at https://ykchizs.github.io/layoutrecall.github.io/.

</details>


### [143] [Hybrid Reinforcement Learning for One-step Degraded Infrared and Visible Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33857)
*Hongwei Yu,Daoqing Zha,Jiawei Li,Yancheng Bai,Lei Sun,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一步式融合框架 OneHRL，将退化输入视为生成轨迹中的中间噪声状态，结合 Flow-GRPO 强化学习与粗到细混合奖励，在提升红外与可见光图像融合质量的同时保持高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外与可见光图像融合在复杂退化条件下性能受限；扩散模型虽具强建模能力但迭代推理计算代价高，而一步蒸馏虽加速却易导致结果过度平滑。

Method: 将退化输入视为生成轨迹上的中间噪声状态，使蒸馏后的一步生成器直接将其校正为干净融合结果；引入 Flow-GRPO 范式为确定性一步生成注入随机性，以恢复采样式强化学习所需的随机探索；设计融合全局语义奖励与目标级奖励的粗到细混合奖励。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法在融合质量与效率上均取得更优表现。

Conclusion: OneHRL 通过一步生成加混合强化学习有效缓解蒸馏过度平滑问题，兼顾自然性与细粒度纹理，实现高质量、高效率的 IVIF。

Abstract: Infrared and visible image fusion (IVIF) is essential for robust perception, yet existing methods remain limited under complex degradations. While diffusion models (DMs) offer powerful modeling capabilities to combat such degradations, their iterative process incurs high computational cost. One-step distillation effectively accelerates inference but typically leads to over-smoothed results. To resolve these limitations, we propose a one-step fusion framework with hybrid reinforcement learning, termed OneHRL. We treat degraded inputs as intermediate noisy states along a generative trajectory, enabling a distilled one-step generator to rectify them directly to the clean fusion results. Concurrently, to mitigate the over-smoothed results in distillation, we incorporate the Flow-GRPO paradigm to introduce stochasticity into the deterministic one-step generation. This restores the stochastic exploration necessary for sampling-based reinforcement learning. Furthermore, we design a coarse-to-fine reward that integrates a global semantic reward with an object-level reward. This hybrid reward ensures that the generator captures both naturalness and fine-grained texture. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves superior fusion quality and efficiency.

</details>


### [144] [Probabilistic Monocular Biomechanical Reconstruction via Guided Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33809)
*R. James Cotton,Fabian Sinz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出生物力学流模型（BFM），以连续时间流匹配先验结合测试时2D关键点引导，从单目视频估计带不确定性的3D关节运动学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目视频重建3D关节运动学存在深度与身高歧义、肢体遮挡及缺乏不确定性估计等病态问题。

Method: 构建覆盖受试者人体测量学、解剖关节角轨迹与虚拟标记的连续时间流匹配先验，基于565名受试者的多机位临床记录训练；测试时以单目2D关键点引导，无需配对视频训练。

Result: 与拟合同一骨架的确定性轨迹优化器对比，BFM在步行与TUG上关节角精度相当；在留出对照、假肢与神经科受试者上，步行与TUG平均绝对关节角误差分别为3.91°与4.97°，并提供可跨越时间与全肢遮挡的可信区间，遮挡处区间随误差扩大且覆盖率接近名义值；个体人体测量学校准可消除大部分单目骨盆深度误差；在分布外儿童蹲伏步态上出现过拟合并过度自信的伸展预测。

Conclusion: 流匹配生物力学先验可提供带不确定性的单目3D运动学估计，但需病理多样化的运动先验以提升泛化性。

Abstract: Reconstructing 3D joint kinematics from a single video is ill-posed due to depth and stature ambiguity, limb occlusion, and missing uncertainty estimates. We introduce the Biomechanical Flow Model (BFM), a continuous-time flow-matching prior over subject anthropometry, anatomical joint-angle trajectories, and virtual markers, trained on multi-camera clinical recordings from 565 participants and steered toward monocular 2D keypoints at test time without paired video training. In a head-to-head comparison on the same clinical trials against a state-of-the-art deterministic trajectory optimizer fitting the identical skeleton, BFM achieves comparable joint-angle accuracy across walking and timed-up-and-go (TUG). Across held-out control, prosthetic, and neurologic participants, BFM reaches 3.91 deg and 4.97 deg mean absolute joint-angle error on walking and TUG while providing credible intervals that bridge temporal and whole-limb occlusions. Where keypoints are hidden, the intervals widen with the error and keep their coverage near nominal. Supplying individual anthropometric calibration removes most monocular pelvis depth error, whereas testing on out-of-distribution pediatric crouch gait produces biased, overconfident extension predictions, highlighting the need for pathologically diverse movement priors.

</details>


### [145] [EvoVAU: Tool Evolution for Video Anomaly Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33808)
*Benfeng Wang,Chao Huang,Yudong Cheng,Chengliang Liu,Wei Wang,Wenqi Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EvoVAU 提出用外部提议器为冻结的视频求解器演化可复用工具库，通过跨版本对比反馈与采纳感知选择，在不改动求解器的情况下提升视频异常理解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频异常理解需要推理事件进程、交互与上下文条件，而现有视频智能体的预定义分析操作难以满足多样化的推理需求。

Method: 冻结视频求解器 + 外部提议器迭代精炼工具库；以任务结果与执行轨迹为引导，跨版本对比反馈结合当前错误与历史修正/回归的配对轨迹指导精炼；采纳感知选择仅在开发集整体精度提升且新/改工具调用问题上修正数多于回归数时接受候选库；测试阶段工具库固定。

Result: 在多个 VAU 基准上表现强劲，且演化后的工具库可跨异常域灵活复用。

Conclusion: 将任务经验沉淀为可复用分析操作并以外置演化机制增强冻结求解器，是提升视频异常理解的有效途径。

Abstract: Video anomaly understanding (VAU) requires reasoning about event progression, interactions, and the contextual conditions that distinguish abnormal behavior from common activity across diverse scenes. Although video agents enable multi-round analysis, the predefined analytical operations may not adequately satisfy these diverse reasoning demands. In this paper, we present EvoVAU, a framework that evolves a reusable tool library for a frozen video solver. Guided by task outcomes and execution trajectories, an external proposer identifies potential limitations in the library and refines it, retaining task experience as analytical operations that can be reused across questions. Specifically, Cross-Version Contrastive Feedback guides tool refinement by combining current errors with paired execution trajectories from historical corrections and regressions. Adoption-Aware Selection accepts a candidate library only when overall development-set accuracy improves and corrections outnumber regressions among questions on which the solver invokes new or modified tools. Using a small development set, EvoVAU alternates tool refinement with candidate evaluation and selection. At test time, the evolved library remains fixed, with no further involvement from the proposer. Experiments on several VAU benchmarks demonstrate strong performance and the flexibility of the evolved library across anomaly domains.

</details>


### [146] [Revisiting Monocular In-Hand Object Scanning: Dataset, Pose Estimation, and Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33824)
*Di Wu,Jeeeun Kim,Shu Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ToysRUs 数据集与一套手持物体扫描(IHOS)框架：子序列采样+轨迹拼接的 SfM 与遮挡感知 2D Gaussian Splatting 重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目手机视频的手持物体扫描有望让非专业用户实现3D数字化，但因缺乏真实数据集与系统基准而研究不足；核心困难在于相邻帧基线过小、大视角长程帧间特征匹配稀疏，以及动态手部遮挡。

Method: 构建 To ysRUs 数据集(4K UHD 单目视频、多样小型雕像)；从第一性原理分析 SfM，对以足够角度间隔采样的视频子序列分别做 SfM，并迭代拼接子轨迹为统一全局轨迹；提出遮挡感知 2D Gaussian Splatting，引入不透明度渲染并在手部区域省略不透明度损失以实现高保真表面重建。

Result: 定量与定性实验表明，该框架在几何精度与光度保真度上显著优于神经隐式表示与3D基础模型等现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过数据集、SfM 策略与遮挡感知2DGS的协同设计，可有效解决手持单目视频扫描的位姿估计与重建难题，推动消费级3D数字化普及。

Abstract: In-Hand Object Scanning (IHOS) from monocular video captured by a commodity smartphone holds immense potential to democratize 3D digitization for non-expert users, facilitating applications in digital archiving, augmented reality, and custom fabrication. Yet, IHOS remains largely understudied due to a lack of realistic datasets and systematic benchmarks. To bridge this gap, we introduce ToysRUs, a high-quality dataset featuring diverse figurines captured by 4K Ultra High-Definition (UHD) monocular videos. Studying IHOS from first principles, we examine structure-from-motion (SfM) for camera pose estimation, which is key to IHOS. We identify two key challenges: small baselines between consecutive frames, and sparse feature matches across large-view, long-range frames, further exacerbated by dynamic hand occlusions. Guided by these insights, we propose performing SfM on video sub-sequences sampled at sufficient angular intervals, and iteratively stitches the corresponding sub-trajectories into a unified global trajectory. Furthermore, for high-fidelity surface reconstruction, we introduce occlusion-aware 2D Gaussian Splatting, which incorporates opacity rendering and omits the opacity loss on hand regions. Quantitative and qualitative evaluations demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms existing approaches, including neural implicit representations and 3D foundation models, in both geometric accuracy and photometric fidelity.

</details>


### [147] [LiveRelight: Sub-Second Global Illumination Editing for Gaussian Interiors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34226)
*Noah Rothenberger,Dor Verbin,Serge Belongie,Bernhard Kerbl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种混合重光照方法，通过预计算几何光交换而非光照响应，实现室内重建3D场景的交互式高保真直接与间接光照编辑，无需逐次优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重建3D场景的重光照仍具挑战：光传输复杂，现有方法反馈慢或近似粗糙，限制编辑者反复微调以达到满意效果。

Method: 预计算几何光交换以保留可低成本求解的传输过程，从而适配发射与表面反射率变化。直接光照在重建的2D高斯上用单侧发射体的可见性感知采样计算；漫反射互反射通过三角代理上的稀疏辐射度传播；再用经高斯alpha合成拟合的稀疏投影把代理解迁移到重建表示。该形式还通过重算光源注入支持快速灯具变换与发射体插入。

Result: 在合成测试集的八种重光照条件下，亚秒级编辑相比先前基于路径追踪的编辑平均提升5 dB PSNR，编辑延迟最多降低三个数量级；定性结果展示了对真实采集场景的交互式真实感重光照。

Conclusion: 将预计算对象从光照响应改为几何光交换，可在无逐次优化前提下实现近实时、高保真的房间尺度重光照编辑。

Abstract: Relighting reconstructed 3D scenes remains challenging due to the complex nature of light transport: slow or approximate visual feedback restricts the editor's ability to fine-tune the results to satisfaction. We present a hybrid relighting approach that enables interactive, high-fidelity updates of direct and indirect indoor illumination without per-edit optimization. Our approach permits the editor to make a wide range of relighting edits at room-scale, with near-instant feedback. Our key insight is to precompute geometric light exchange rather than illumination responses, retaining an inexpensive transport solve that accommodates changes in emission and surface reflectance. We evaluate direct illumination on reconstructed 2D Gaussians using visibility-aware sampling of one-sided emitters, while propagating diffuse interreflection through sparse radiosity on a triangle proxy. A sparse projection fitted through Gaussian alpha compositing transfers the proxy solution to the reconstruction. Our formulation further supports fast fixture transformations and emitter insertion by recomputing light source injection. Across eight relighting conditions of our synthetic test suite, our sub-second edits improve relighting fidelity over prior path-traced editing by an average of 5\,dB PSNR, while reducing editing latency by up to three orders of magnitude. Qualitative results further demonstrate realistic, interactive relighting of real-world captures.

</details>


### [148] [EgoMyo: Physical Hand State Estimation from Egocentric Video and Wrist Electromyography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34224)
*Guanglong Sun,Kanglei Zhou,Ziteng Wang,Nana Wang,Hengkai Tan,Xiaolong Liu,Hang Su,Yi Zhong,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出从自我中心视频与腕部sEMG联合估计手部物理状态（姿态、接触、法向力）的新任务与基准EgoMyo，并给出视觉-肌电融合方法MyoBridge。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手部物理交互由构型、接触与力共同决定，但外观近乎相同的手势可能对应不同的接触状态与施加力，仅凭视觉难以区分；需要结合视觉几何与神经肌肉证据进行联合估计。

Method: 构建EgoMyo基准：575万视频帧、50名被试、34种接触构型、多次佩戴，同步自我中心视频、sEMG、3D手部姿态，以及由裸手压力测得的手表面对齐接触与法向力。开展受控分析揭示视觉与sEMG的互补性。提出MyoBridge：以手部区域为视觉-sEMG融合单元，各区域对自身sEMG份额进行门控，推理时仅将区域力幅值分配到预测的接触顶点上。

Result: 受控分析显示视觉擅长空间结构、sEMG更擅长力幅估计，sEMG增益集中于视觉最不可靠区域，接触定位精度制约力估计精度。留出试验中，MyoBridge相比最强基线将姿态与力误差分别降低39.8%与18.5%，并在未见会话与未见被试上取得最低姿态误差。

Conclusion: 视觉与sEMG在多模态层面上互补，区域级门控融合与接触约束的力分配是有效的物理手状态估计范式，EgoMyo为后续研究提供标准化评测平台。

Abstract: Physical hand interaction depends on configuration, contact, and force, yet nearly identical appearances can conceal different contact states and exerted forces. We therefore formulate physical hand state estimation from egocentric video and wrist surface electromyography (sEMG), combining visual geometry with neuromuscular evidence to jointly recover these coupled components. To support this task, we introduce EgoMyo, a benchmark spanning 5.75M video frames, 50 participants, 34 contact configurations, and repeated donnings, synchronizing these inputs with 3D hand pose and with contact and normal force registered onto the hand surface from bare-hand pressure. Controlled analysis reveals that vision resolves spatial structure while sEMG better estimates force magnitude, that sEMG's gains concentrate in regions where vision is least reliable, and that contact localization limits force accuracy. Guided by these findings, we propose MyoBridge, which makes the hand region the unit of vision-sEMG fusion, lets each region gate its own share of sEMG, and distributes regional force magnitudes only over predicted contact vertices at inference. On held-out trials, MyoBridge reduces pose and force error by 39.8% and 18.5% over the strongest baseline and achieves the lowest pose error on unseen sessions and subjects.

</details>


### [149] [Modality-Shift Contrastive Decoding for Temporally Grounded Audio-Visual Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34162)
*Minyeong Jeong,Yeonju Kim,Yong Man Ro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文通过音频-视觉同步探针实验揭示AV-LLM对音视时间对齐变化不敏感，并提出免训练的模态偏移对比解码（MSCD）来强化时间对齐信息的使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AV-LLM被设计用于表征音视频的时间结构，但其是否真正利用时间信息来关联声音与视觉事件尚不明确。探针实验显示：当把音频移位、使查询声音与另一视觉事件重叠时，模型仍偏好原始对齐的事件，说明其对音视时间对齐的敏感性有限。

Method: 提出Modality-Shift Contrastive Decoding（MSCD），一种免训练的解码方法：保持一种模态不变、对音频或视频做时间移位以破坏对齐，然后在保留对齐与破坏对齐两种条件下对比logits，从而偏向由对齐的音视频共同支持的预测。

Result: 在两个AV-LLM与三个基准上的评测表明MSCD有效提升时间落地的音视频理解；在Daily-Omni上，OmniVinci和video-SALMONN 2+分别较基座模型提升2.2和2.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 强调音视时间对齐不仅能改善时间敏感任务，还可惠及更广泛的音视频理解，且MSCD无需额外训练即可集成到现有AV-LLM解码中。

Abstract: Audio-visual large language models (AV-LLMs) are designed to represent the temporal structure of audio and video, yet it remains unclear how effectively they use this temporal information to relate sounds to visual events. To examine this, we probe an AV-LLM on an audio-visual synchronization task by shifting the audio so that the queried sound overlaps a different visual event. We observe that the model still favors the originally aligned event over the newly aligned one, indicating limited sensitivity to changes in audio-visual temporal alignment. To address this, we propose Modality-Shift Contrastive Decoding (MSCD), a training-free decoding method that emphasizes audio-visual temporal alignment in model predictions. MSCD contrasts logits under preserved and disrupted temporal alignment to favor predictions supported by aligned audio and video. We disrupt this alignment by temporally shifting either the audio or the video, while keeping the other modality fixed. Extensive evaluations with two AV-LLMs across three benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of MSCD for temporally grounded audio-visual understanding. In particular, MSCD improves accuracy over the base models on Daily-Omni by 2.2 and 2.8 percentage points for OmniVinci and video-SALMONN 2+, respectively, suggesting that emphasizing temporal alignment can also benefit broader audio-visual understanding.

</details>


### [150] [Surf-Style: Surface-Based Scene Stylization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34062)
*Wanrong Hong,Ancheng Lin,Mukesh Prasad,Jun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Surf-Style，一种基于表面重建的 3D 场景风格化方法，通过对 3D 空间定义可学习外观模型并借助重建表面实现几何一致性与局部化风格化，在跨视角一致性和可控性上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D 风格化在沉浸式可视化、AR/VR 内容创作等应用中很关键。2D 风格化方法逐视角独立应用会导致跨视角不一致；基于隐式辐射场的方法虽试图改善一致性，但像素外观与可学习场景参数之间是间接依赖，颜色更新存在歧义，优化中难以施加一致的风格约束。

Method: Surf-Style 先重建场景中物体的表面，再以这些表面为载体实现几何一致性与局部化风格化；在 3D 空间上定义可学习外观模型，并对其优化以复现参考图像的视觉风格。

Result: 与当前最优方法相比，该方法取得更优的跨视角一致性，并在可控风格化方面更灵活，包括针对特定表面区域施加风格以及组合不同风格。

Conclusion: 以表面为中心的表示可缓解隐式辐射场中颜色更新的歧义问题，为一致且细粒度可控的 3D 场景风格化提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Consistent and controllable 3D stylization is critical for visual computing applications such as immersive visualization, augmented and virtual reality content creation, and artistic scene rendering. While 2D image stylization has advanced significantly, extending these methods to 3D remains challenging due to the need for cross-view consistency, where stylized renderings must align across all viewpoints. Applying 2D stylization independently to each rendered view often results in inconsistencies across perspectives. Existing methods attempt to improve consistency by leveraging implicit radiance field representations, which model scene appearance as a function of spatial locations. However, these methods struggle with ambiguity in updating colors, as the indirect dependence between pixel appearance and learnable scene parameters makes it difficult to enforce consistent stylization during optimization. This work presents Surface-based Scene Stylization (Surf-Style), which reconstructs object surfaces in a 3D scene and uses these surfaces to achieve geometric consistency and localized stylization. Surf-Style defines a learnable appearance model over 3D space, which is modified to replicate the visual style of a reference image. Compared to state-of-the-art methods, our method achieves superior consistency and offers greater flexibility in controllable stylization, including the ability to target specific surface regions and combine different styles.

</details>


### [151] [Atunda: Building an Afrogenic Dance Movement Dataset for AI-Driven Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34649)
*Diego Carrascosa,Tristan Pank,Qudus Onikeku,Mas'ud Onikeku,Jeremy A Frusco,Osubi Craig,Angelos Barmpoutis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Atunda——首个系统采集的非洲裔舞蹈动作视频数据集，用于动作分类模型的训练与评测，基于时序卷积网络实现超过95%的分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲及非洲侨民表演性文化中的舞蹈研究严重不足，缺乏AI就绪的标准化数据资源，制约了动作分类、相似性度量与自动化归属等技术的发展。

Method: 构建Atunda数据集，第一阶段收录约400个视频、覆盖13种不同舞蹈动作，并训练与评测多种配置的时序卷积神经网络（TCN）进行动作分类。

Result: 取得超过95%的分类准确率，为后续动作分类研究提供了基线基准。

Conclusion: 该工作为该欠研究领域建立基准，并为未来开发相似性数字度量、自动化归属系统及非洲裔舞蹈文化遗产的保存与推广工具奠定基础。

Abstract: This paper introduces Atunda, the first systematically collected video dataset of Afrogenic dance moves, designed for training and evaluating deep learning models for movement classification. The first phase of the dataset includes nearly 400 videos covering 13 distinct dance moves from African and African Diaspora performative culture, making it the first AI-ready resource of its kind. Using this dataset, we train and evaluate various configurations of temporal convolutional neural networks, achieving a classification accuracy exceeding 95%. The baseline results provide a benchmark for future studies in movement classification, especially in the understudied area of Afrogenic performative culture and tradition. This work lays the foundation for developing in the future digital metrics of similarity, automated attribution systems, and tools that preserve and promote the cultural heritage of Afrogenic dances.

</details>


### [152] [Who Is Ego? Scaling Cross-View Camera Wearer Identification and Segmentation beyond Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34489)
*Yifan Jin,Zheng Zhang,Tianwen Qian,Dong Wang,Bo Zhao,Yanwei Fu,Yuqian Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FindEgo-1M大规模ego-exo配对数据集与EgoRay方法，利用ego相机在exo视图中的对极点（epipole）落在佩戴者附近的几何关系，从冻结的3D重建模型VGGT-Ω中提取几何读出，实现免训练的佩戴者识别与分割（EgoRay-ZS），并经训练得到完整模型EgoRay。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称(ego)图像只反映相机所见，不包含佩戴者身份信息；借助同步的第三人称(exo)图像可推断"谁是ego"。现有工作局限于人类、且仅在给定候选mask中选择，缺乏对多样佩戴者类型、佩戴者分割、缺失预测与双向相机定位的系统性建模与规模化基准。

Method: 1) 构建FindEgo-1M：116万真实与仿真ego-exo对，涵盖12类佩戴者、17个域、111种测试设置；2) 核心几何先验：ego相机投影到exo图像的对极点位于或邻近其佩戴者；3) EgoRay-ZS：将冻结的前馈3D重建模型VGGT-Ω转为免训练方案，按候选mask/分割提案相对ego相机的角偏移与距离排序，并由相对位姿给出互相机方向；4) EgoRay：在FindEgo-1M上做位姿监督训练，并采用候选mask约束的无标签测试时自适应。

Result: EgoRay-ZS在TF2023原始标签上达78.0%识别准确率（数据集训练的PEN为52.9%）；FindEgo-1M上EgoRay在seen/unseen设置取得机会校正准确率87.1/77.7的SOTA；匹配查询下较GPT-6 Astra分别高31.6和21.3个点，且速度快18倍。

Conclusion: 以对极点几何为桥梁、复用冻结3D基础模型，可有效将ego-exo跨视角关系转化为佩戴者识别/分割与互定位能力；免训练方案已超越专用训练基线，完整模型在规模化基准上刷新SOTA并兼顾效率，数据、代码与模型将开源。

Abstract: A first-person (ego) image reveals what a camera sees, but not who or what carries it. Given a synchronized third-person (exo) image, camera wearer identification asks who is ego: which candidate carries the ego camera? We extend this task beyond humans to robots, vehicles, animals, and drones, and broaden its spatial scope (where is ego) from selecting a provided candidate mask to wearer segmentation with absence prediction and mutual camera localization across ego-exo views. We introduce FindEgo-1M, comprising 1.16M real and simulated ego-exo pairs across 12 wearer types, 17 domains, and 111 test settings. Our approach exploits a geometric relation: the ego camera's projection into the exo image, i.e., the epipole, lies on or near its wearer. We introduce EgoRay-ZS, the first method to turn a frozen feed-forward 3D reconstruction model, VGGT-$Ω$, into a training-free solution for wearer identification and segmentation. Its geometric readout ranks candidate masks or segmentation proposals by angular offset and distance to the ego camera, while the relative pose provides mutual camera directions. We then develop the full EgoRay model through pose-supervised training on FindEgo-1M and label-free test-time adaptation constrained by candidate masks. Without task-specific training, EgoRay-ZS achieves 78.0% identification accuracy on TF2023's original labels, versus 52.9% reported for the dataset-trained PEN. On FindEgo-1M, EgoRay achieves state-of-the-art chance-corrected accuracy of 87.1 and 77.7 on seen and unseen settings. On matched queries, EgoRay also outperforms GPT-6 Astra by 31.6 and 21.3 points on seen and unseen settings, respectively, while running $18\times$ faster. Data, code, and models will be released.

</details>


### [153] [ProManO: Beyond Sim(3) for Metric Upgrade of Feed-Forward Geometry with Visual-Inertial Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34480)
*Lingxiang Hu,Zhenjun Zhao,Qi Shao,Naima Ait Oufroukh,Fabien Bonardi,Raymond Ghandour,Shenghai Yuan,Javier Civera,Baoru Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将稠密前馈几何与稀疏度量VIO锚点的对齐问题形式化为与预标定内参兼容的变换族，证明其构成十维流形（Sim(3)+3个不可见自由度），并提出ProManO概率流形优化框架显式估计无穷远面，在四个数据集上取得最高平均F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈单目几何升级到度量尺度需与稀疏VIO锚点对齐。现有方法或用Sim(3)（无法表达随深度变化的畸变），或用投影变换（破坏预标定内参），均非理想选择，缺乏对"应使用何种校正"的理论刻画。

Method: 证明与预标定内参兼容的变换集合构成十维流形，其中Sim(3)之外的3个自由度定位无穷远面，且在图像中不可观测，只能由沿视线方向的度量深度或多视图约束求解。提出ProManO：在单一EM过程中，以沿视线不确定性与内点概率对每个锚点加权，进行概率流形优化；先认证相似变换同步，仅在可观测处精化无穷远面，保证目标值不劣于最优Sim(3)对齐。

Result: 在EuRoC、CID-SIMS、Oxford Spires和KITTI-360上，ProManO获得最高平均F1，并在全部四个数据集上优于Sim(3)对齐。

Conclusion: 与内参兼容的对齐应超越Sim(3)，额外的3个无穷远面自由度是消除深度依赖残差的关键；ProManO提供了可观测性感知、理论有保证的概率优化方案，实证优于Sim(3)基线。

Abstract: Upgrading feed-forward geometry to metric scale with visual-inertial odometry (VIO) raises the question of which correction should align dense feed-forward prediction with sparse metric VIO anchors. Existing methods use either a similarity transformation, which cannot represent depth-dependent distortion, or a projective transformation, which alters pre-calibrated intrinsics. We prove that the transformations compatible with pre-calibrated intrinsics form a ten-dimensional manifold. Beyond Sim(3), this manifold contains three degrees of freedom (DoF) that locate the plane at infinity and leave residuals growing quadratically with depth range after any similarity alignment. These DoF are invisible in the image and can be resolved only by metric depth along viewing rays or by multi-view constraints. To estimate them, we propose ProManO, a probabilistic manifold optimization framework in which every anchor is weighted by its along-ray uncertainty and inlier probability within a single expectation-maximization (EM) procedure. ProManO certifies the similarity synchronization and refines the plane at infinity only where it is observable, which guarantees an objective value no worse than that of the optimal similarity alignment. Experiments on EuRoC, CID-SIMS, Oxford Spires, and KITTI-360 demonstrate that ProManO achieves the highest mean F1 and outperforms Sim(3) alignment on all four datasets. The project page is available at https://h0xg.github.io/ProManO/.

</details>


### [154] [Evidence-Aligned Benchmarking and Learning for Medical Video Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34465)
*Zhongpai Gao,Anwesa Choudhuri,Meng Zheng,Benjamin Planche,Terrence Chen,Ziyan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MedEvidU，从基准、输入编码与训练三个环节修复医学视频语言模型的视觉证据接地问题，4B模型IoU从30.4升至44.4，超过27B监督模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视频语言模型应基于问题所指的视觉证据作答，但作者发现三处失效：(1) 现有基准奖励忽略视频的答案——某数据集上片段位置可预测37%问题的手术步骤，常数答案在安全评估指标上击败所有训练模型；(2) 常用视频编码器将连续帧合并为共享token，使单帧问题由两帧混合内容作答；(3) 微调模型记住了训练问题，可学信息有限。

Method: 三项对应措施：构建MedVidBench-v2（18个手术与临床视频数据集的34个任务，提示与指标经"无视频作答"审计，并测试未见医院与整段录像盲切窗口）；提出query-slot输入，为每个被查询帧分配独立token（近零额外开销）；提出标签匹配问题重采样（LQR），在最后监督epoch与强化学习中引入同录像的新问题且保持答案分布不变。

Result: 4B模型接地IoU由30.4提升至44.4，超过27B监督模型；未见医院器械接地由33.9升至72.5；盲切窗口上事件存在准确率+5.5点，定位F1+8.1点。

Conclusion: 视觉证据接地失效源于基准设计、帧token合并与训练问题记忆三个层面，针对性修复可显著提升小模型在跨医院、盲切窗口等严苛场景下的接地与定位能力。

Abstract: A medical video language model should answer from the visual evidence its question asks about. We find three places where this breaks down and address each with an approach we call MedEvidU. First, benchmarks reward answers that ignore the video: on one dataset, the position of a clip within its recording predicts the surgical step in 37% of questions, and a constant answer beats every trained model on a standard safety-assessment metric. We build MedVidBench-v2, 34 tasks from 18 surgical and clinical video datasets, in which prompts and metrics are audited with answers given without video, and which tests unseen hospitals and windows cut blindly from whole recordings. Second, common video encoders merge consecutive frames into shared tokens, so a question about one frame is answered from a blend of two. Query-slot inputs give each queried frame its own tokens at almost no extra cost. Third, a fine-tuned model has memorized its training questions, which leaves little to learn from. Label-matched question resampling (LQR) adds unseen questions about the same recordings while keeping the answer distribution fixed, in a final supervised epoch and in reinforcement learning. Together, these raise the grounding IoU of a 4B model from 30.4 to 44.4, above a supervised 27B model, and instrument grounding at an unseen hospital from 33.9 to 72.5. On blindly cut windows, event-presence accuracy improves by 5.5 points and localization F1 by 8.1 points. Project page: https://gaozhongpai.github.io/MedEvidU/

</details>


### [155] [A Mesh for Every Moment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34939)
*Roy Or-El,Jeong Joon Park,Ira Kemelmacher-Shlizerman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MEMO 提出基于高斯过程的逐高斯运动模型（位置与旋转表示为时间高斯核组合），从多相机同步视频重建完整动态场景的几何，并在任意帧从高斯定义的场中提取网格。作者还构建了 Blender-Moments 基准（9 个动画场景、逐帧真值网格），MEMO 在几何精度上优于现有方法，新视角合成与训练时间具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态高斯抛雪方法要么只建模外观，要么仅针对前景掩码分割出的孤立物体，且均依赖 TSDF 融合或泊松重建，固定网格分辨率限制了细节捕捉能力；此外真实采集中的大幅快速运动常被忽视。

Method: 核心为由高斯过程启发的逐高斯运动模型：将每个高斯的位置与旋转在时间上表示为时间高斯核的组合，使高斯在整段序列中始终保持为有效表面元素，并隐含携带动态几何，从而可在任意时刻由高斯场导出网格；同时设计基于点轨迹的初始化方案，以闭式解将每个高斯的初始轨迹拟合到点轨迹上，以应对大幅度快速运动。

Result: 在 Blender-Moments（9 个动画场景、逐帧真值网格）及两个真实数据集上评估，MEMO 的几何重建精度优于当前最优方法，并在新视角合成与训练时间上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: MEMO 通过连续时间的高斯运动表示与轨迹初始化，实现了完整动态场景的逐帧网格重建，并配套提出带逐帧真值网格的基准，为动态场景几何重建提供了新范式。

Abstract: We present MEMO (a Mesh for Every MOment), a method that reconstructs the geometry of complete dynamic scenes, captured by multiple synchronized cameras, and extracts a mesh at every frame. Existing dynamic Gaussian splatting methods either model appearance alone or target isolated objects segmented by foreground masks, and all of them recover meshes by TSDF fusion or Poisson reconstruction, whose fixed grids limit the detail they can capture. At the core of MEMO is a per-Gaussian motion model inspired by Gaussian processes, which represents each Gaussian's position and rotation over time as a combination of temporal Gaussian kernels. The model keeps every Gaussian a valid surface element throughout the sequence and carries an implicit representation of the dynamic geometry, so a mesh can be derived at any time from a field defined by the Gaussians. Although common in real captures, large and fast motion poses a challenge that dynamic Gaussian splatting methods usually overlook. To account for it, we design a track-based initialization scheme that fits each Gaussian's initial trajectory to point tracks in closed form. We evaluate MEMO on Blender-Moments, a new benchmark of nine animated scenes with ground-truth meshes at every frame, and on two real-world datasets. MEMO reconstructs the most accurate geometry among state-of-the-art methods, and remains competitive in novel-view synthesis and training time. Project page: https://memo-4d.github.io

</details>


### [156] [DynMF2: Rigid Motion Factorization by Expectation-Maximization for Monocular 4D Reconstruction and Motion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34931)
*Agelos Kratimenos,Yile Hua,Kostas Daniilidis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 基于DynMF，将场景运动建模为少量SE(3)刚体轨迹，通过EM从3D点轨迹直接求解运动分解，无需渲染与掩码，并提出requery/merge/split三个工具自适应调整运动数量，在视角合成与3D跟踪上超越基于掩码的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单段随意视频下场景重建欠定：点沿视线方向滑动仍能匹配所有帧。因此需要利用运动结构本身作为额外约束来消除歧义。

Method: 将运动表示为一小组SE(3)刚体轨迹，每个点仅跟随其中一个（硬分配，不混合）。直接对3D点轨迹做EM：M步对分配给某运动的点闭式拟合刚体运动，E步按误差最小重分配点。背景作为相机运动本身的一个运动，无需掩码。再用requery（在跟踪缺失处补充轨迹）、merge（合并同步运动）、split（拆分不同步运动）修正分解，使运动数量随场景自适应。

Result: 无掩码情况下，在iPhone与NVIDIA数据集上视角合成明显优于基于掩码的基线，iPhone上3D跟踪也更优；能恢复掩码遗漏的细薄部件、细节及远处背景小运动物体；在未做分割训练的情况下，对MOVi-F与iTACO场景的运动分割具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 场景中"什么在运动"不由先验预先决定，而是从刚体运动本身中涌现出来。

Abstract: A single casual video leaves a moving scene under-determined: a point seen once from each place can slide toward or away from the camera and still match every frame. We constrain the reconstruction with the structure of the motion itself. Building on DynMF, we model a scene's motion as a handful of SE(3) trajectories, each rigid, and let every point follow one of them alone, never a blend. DynMF2 computes this factorization directly from 3D point trajectories, with no rendering, by Expectation-Maximization (EM). Its M-step fits, in closed form, every rigid motion to the points assigned to it, and its E-step reassigns every point to the motion that explains it with the least error. The background is simply one more motion, the camera's, so no masks are needed. Three tools then revise the factorization: requery adds trajectories where the rendering shows parts the tracker missed, merge joins motions that move together, and split separates those that do not. Thus the number of motions follows the scene. Without masks, DynMF2 clearly outperforms the mask-based baselines in view synthesis on iPhone and NVIDIA and in 3D tracking on iPhone. It recovers what masks leave out, from thin parts and details to small movers far in the background. Its motions segment the moving parts of MOVi-F and iTACO scenes competitively, although DynMF2 is never trained to segment. What moves in a scene is not decided in advance: it emerges from the rigid motions themselves.

</details>


### [157] [LiveVAE: Causal Window Attention Makes Efficient Streaming Video VAE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34912)
*Jiachen An,Sijie Zhao,Le Li,Hongyu Liu,Zhiyuan Li,Tianyu Yang,Xiaoyu Kong,Xunliang Cai,Yong Zhang,Chi-Man Pun,Xiaoming Wei,Xiaodong Cun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiveVAE 基于有效感受野分析发现视频 VAE 的编解码高度局部化，提出因果窗口注意力流式编解码器，实现 5.2-6.2x 编码与 3.0-3.3x 解码加速且质量更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频 VAE 是交互式生成与编辑流程中的主要延迟瓶颈；现有 VAE 及其加速方法依赖重卷积或全帧注意力，且多只优化解码，编码开销在流式条件生成/编辑中仍昂贵。作者质疑编解码是否真需要全局时空建模。

Method: 通过分析预训练视频 VAE 的有效感受野，发现每个 patch 的编解码几乎只依赖其时空邻域；据此设计 LiveVAE——基于因果窗口注意力的流式编码器与解码器，降低单帧计算并将时序 KV 缓存保持有界；采用编码器/解码器解耦蒸馏加联合微调以保持原潜空间，可作为原 VAE 的即插即用替代。

Result: 在 MiniMax-H3 上，编码速度提升 5.2-6.2x，解码提升 3.0-3.3x，且 PSNR 更高；配合专用推理基础设施，加速比达 13.4x，几乎无质量损失。

Conclusion: 视频 VAE 的全局时空建模并非必需，局部因果窗口注意力足以支撑高质量流式编解码，LiveVAE 为流式生成与编辑提供了高效且兼容的 VAE 替代方案。

Abstract: While video diffusion models have grown faster and increasingly streamable, the video VAE remains a primary latency bottleneck across interactive generation and editing pipelines. Existing VAEs and their acceleration methods rely mainly on heavy convolutions or full frame attention, and most focus only on decoding, leaving encoding costly for streaming conditional generation and editing. This raises a natural question: Do video VAE encoding and decoding actually require such global spatiotemporal modeling? By analyzing the effective receptive fields of pretrained video VAEs, we find that both encoding and decoding a patch rely almost entirely on its spatial and temporal neighbors. To exploit this strong locality, we introduce LiveVAE, a streaming encoder and decoder built on causal window attention, which cuts per frame compute while keeping the temporal KV cache bounded for streaming. Decoupled encoder/decoder distillation followed by joint finetuning preserves the pretrained latent space, making LiveVAE a drop-in replacement for the original VAE. On MiniMax-H3, LiveVAE achieves 5.2-6.2x faster encoding and 3.0-3.3x faster decoding than the official VAE with higher PSNR. Paired with our dedicated inference infrastructure, LiveVAE pushes the speedup to 13.4x with almost no quality loss. Code and models will be released.

</details>


### [158] [Beyond Feature Fusion: Sensor-Grounded Semantic-Spiking Learning for Robust Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34828)
*Hanwen Liang,Wenxuan Liu,Wenxin Huang,Jingling Yuan,Zhaofei Yu,Xian Zhong,Tiejun Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SPIRE——一种基于脉冲相机的语义-脉冲融合目标检测框架，通过外观视图与多前缀累积视图、ANN引导的脉冲初始化以及残差校正，在欠/过曝场景下实现光照鲁棒的检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欠曝光削弱目标细节、过曝模糊边界，传统相机在极端光照下难以检测。脉冲相机以高频二值流编码光强，长窗口计数提供稳定外观，短观测反映发放率变化，为光照鲁棒检测提供新信息源，但如何统一利用单条脉冲流中的长/短时信息并与语义特征结合尚待解决。

Method: SPIRE从单一脉冲流构建两类视图：由完整数据包经面向检测的光照自适应得到外观视图；由逐渐加长的前缀构建累积视图序列，每个前缀提供率图、边缘图与率差分以追踪脉冲累积变化。在选定尺度上，用ANN特征与脉冲派生初始化共同设定初始膜电位与发放阈值，使语义线索在时序编码中调制响应；训练时用局部脉冲计数对比监督双分支。将实测与ANN预测对比度的残差与脉冲特征结合，对融合特征金字塔产生有界校正。

Result: 在仿真VOC与COCO上，SPIRE-D在四种欠/过曝设置下比最强基线高4.00–5.50 AP50；在真实PKU-Vidar-DVS上达到67.07 AP50与40.10 AP75；相比SPFD减少50.4%的MACs。

Conclusion: SPIRE验证了以脉冲相机为传感基础、长短时脉冲信息与ANN语义协同的检测范式在极端曝光条件下的有效性与计算高效性。

Abstract: Underexposure weakens object details, and overexposure obscures boundaries. Spike cameras encode light into high-frequency binary streams. Long-window spike counts give stable appearance cues, while short observations reveal changes in firing rates from sparse measurements. We propose SPIRE, a sensor-grounded semantic-spiking framework for illumination-robust object detection from a single spike stream. SPIRE builds an appearance view from the full packet through detection-oriented illumination adaptation and constructs a sequence of cumulative views from progressively longer prefixes. Each prefix supplies a rate map, an edge map, and a rate difference that tracks changes as spikes accumulate. At selected scales, ANN features and spike-derived initialization jointly set the initial membrane potentials and firing thresholds, allowing semantic cues to shape the responses during temporal encoding. Local spike-count contrasts supervise both branches during training. The residual between measured and ANN-predicted contrasts combines with the spiking features to produce a bounded correction to the fused feature pyramid. On simulated VOC and COCO, SPIRE-D outperforms the strongest baseline by 4.00-5.50 AP50 points across the four under- and overexposure settings. On real PKU-Vidar-DVS recordings, it achieves 67.07 AP50 and 40.10 AP75. SPIRE-D also uses 50.4% fewer MACs than SPFD.

</details>


### [159] [CIRKDV2: Cross-Image Relational Knowledge Distillation with Contextual Modeling for Efficient Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34795)
*Chuanguang Yang,Yu Wang,Chengqing Yu,Xinqiang Yu,Weilun Feng,Yuqi Li,Zhulin An,Libo Huang,Boyu Diao,Fei Wang,Fuzhen Zhuang,Yongjun Xu,Yingli Tian,Tingwen Huang,Yongduan Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CIRKDV2，通过跨图像关系蒸馏（像素-像素、像素-区域、通道-通道）与上下文特征蒸馏，缩小师生语义差距，显著提升语义分割KD性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义分割KD方法仅让学生模仿单张图像内的结构化信息，忽略了跨图像间有价值的全局语义关系。

Method: CIRKDV2从数据集的全局图像中建模并迁移三类结构化关系：像素-像素、像素-区域和通道-通道关系，使学生模仿教师更具结构性的全局语境依赖；此外引入上下文特征蒸馏，通过上下文聚合增强学生像素嵌入，使其语义层级与教师对齐。

Result: 在Cityscapes、ADE20K、Pascal VOC和COCO-Stuff-164K上，CIRKDV2在多种网络架构下大幅优于当前最先进的语义分割蒸馏方法。

Conclusion: 跨图像关系蒸馏与上下文特征蒸馏相结合，可有效提升学生特征表示能力并规避传统特征蒸馏的语义差距问题，代码与预训练模型已开源。

Abstract: Existing Knowledge Distillation (KD) methods for semantic segmentation often guide the student to mimic the teacher's structured information generated from an individual image. However, they ignore the global semantic relations across various images that are valuable for KD. This paper proposes Cross-Image Relational KD V2 (CIRKDV2), which focuses on transferring structured pixel-to-pixel, pixel-to-region, and channel-to-channel relations modeled from global images in the dataset. A large teacher network often constructs a well-structured feature space in terms of global contextual dependencies. CIRKDV2 makes the student mimic the better structured semantic relations of the teacher, thus improving the feature representational capability. Beyond relational distillation, we further propose contextual feature distillation by using context aggregation to augment student pixel embeddings to match the semantic level with the teacher. This avoids the semantic gap problem between the teacher and student occurred in traditional feature distillation studies. Experimental results on Cityscapes, ADE20K, Pascal VOC, and COCO-Stuff-164K demonstrate that CIRKDV2 outperforms the state-of-the-art semantic segmentation distillation methods by large margins across various network architectures. Our code and pre-trained models are publicly available at https://github.com/winycg/CIRKD.

</details>


### [160] [SceneDesigner3D: Agentic Evolutionary Search for Principled Rearrangement of 3D Indoor Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34783)
*Chandan Yeshwanth,Angela Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneDesigner3D：面向3D室内场景重排的智能体进化搜索方法，以可编辑程序表示布局，通过VLM智能体变异+评分+存档，生成多样、美观且功能合理的重排方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D内容创作需要生成多样、美观且功能性的场景重排，但设计空间巨大、需满足多项室内设计原则，单一生成难以兼顾提示一致性与设计质量。

Method: 将重排表示为可编辑程序，进化一组候选布局；从不同设计构思出发，由VLM智能体执行三类变异（精炼优选候选、探索新策略、从头重设计）并做物理有效性验证；用VLM评审按提示遵循度与平衡/分区/强调等原则打分，并结合流通性、杂乱度等数值指标；维护结构多样化的高分候选存档以提升多样性。

Result: 在SAGE-10k与SceneSmith数据集上，相比基线提示遵循度最高提升5.1%，计算型设计原则得分最高提升15.2%。

Conclusion: 将进化搜索与VLM智能体、程序化布局表示及多维度自动评估结合，可有效提升3D场景重排的提示一致性与设计原则满足度，并保持方案多样性。

Abstract: Scene rearrangement is an essential task for 3D content creation applications, requiring generation of diverse, aesthetic and functional scene rearrangements. We present SceneDesigner3D, an agentic evolutionary search method for 3D indoor scene rearrangement that systematically explores the design space of 3D scenes and generates multiple solutions according to common interior design principles. Given an input scene and a text prompt, SceneDesigner3D represents the rearrangement as an editable program and evolves a population of candidate layouts. Starting from distinct design ideas, a VLM agent mutates the programs by refining promising candidates, exploring new rearrangement strategies or redesigning from scratch, while verifying the layouts for physical validity. Proposals are scored by a VLM judge for adherence to the input prompt and satisfaction of design principles such as balance, zoning and emphasis, as well as numerically computed principles such as circulation and clutter. An archive of top-performing, structurally varied candidates further increases diversity of rearrangements. Experiments on the SAGE-10k and SceneSmith datasets show that SceneDesigner3D improves prompt adherence and computed design principle scores over baselines by up to 5.1% and 15.2%, respectively. Code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [161] [Comparative Study of Deep Learning Architectures for Brain Tumour Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34781)
*Prisha Gupta,Pradeep Kumar Gupta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究在四个公开MRI数据集上系统比较六种深度学习架构的四分类（健康、脑膜瘤、胶质瘤、垂体瘤）性能，并构建防数据泄漏流程（MD5去重550张、感知哈希去近重复8,024张、相似图像同折分组）与留一数据集测试（LODO），揭示传统随机划分下准确率被高估的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有深度学习脑肿瘤分类研究多在小规模、单一来源数据集上训练和测试，所报告的准确率难以泛化到数据集之外，缺乏对数据泄漏的严格控制与跨数据集泛化评估。

Method: 比较六种架构：基线CNN、注意力CNN、ShuffleNet、以及带CBAM模块的EfficientNet-B0、ResNet-50、Xception。在四数据集合并语料上训练；构建泄漏感知流程，用MD5哈希去除完全重复图像（550张）、感知哈希去除近重复图像（8,024张），5折分组交叉验证中将视觉相似图像划入同一折；并采用完全留出数据集的LODO测试衡量跨数据集泛化，辅以配对t检验与Grad-CAM可视化。

Result: 常规随机划分下ShuffleNet达97.1%准确率（1.2M参数）；泄漏感知分组K折下ShuffleNetV2（98.41%）与EfficientNet-B0（98.23%）无统计显著差异（p=0.19），且显著优于自建CNN。留出数据集上所有模型准确率均下降：EfficientNet-B0泛化最佳（78.4%），ShuffleNetV2降至66.2%。主要误差模式为胶质瘤与脑膜瘤混淆；Grad-CAM显示预测主要依赖肿瘤区域。

Conclusion: 仅报告分布内准确率会高估真实世界性能；应同时报告泄漏感知评估与跨数据集（LODO）评估结果，以更可靠地反映模型的临床可用性。

Abstract: Accurate and early classification of brain tumours from magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is essential for treatment planning, yet many published deep learning studies are trained and tested on small, single-source datasets, reporting accuracies that may not hold outside these datasets. This study compares six deep learning architectures for four-class classification (healthy, meningioma, glioma and pituitary tumour): a baseline convolutional neural network (CNN), an attention-based CNN, ShuffleNet, and EfficientNet-B0, ResNet-50 and Xception augmented with the Convolutional Block Attention Module (CBAM). The models were trained on a corpus assembled from four publicly available MRI datasets. Because these datasets carry no patient identifiers, a leakage-aware pipeline was developed that removes exact duplicates using MD5 hashing (550 images) and near-duplicates using perceptual hashing (8,024 images), and assigns visually similar images to the same fold during 5-fold group cross-validation. A leave-one-dataset-out (LODO) test on a fully held-out dataset was used to measure cross-dataset generalisation. Under a conventional random split, ShuffleNet achieved 97.1% accuracy with 1.2M parameters. Under leakage-aware group K-fold cross-validation, ShuffleNetV2 (98.41%) and EfficientNet-B0 (98.23%) were statistically indistinguishable (paired t-test, p = 0.19), and both significantly outperformed the custom CNNs. On the held-out dataset, accuracy dropped for every model: EfficientNet-B0 generalised best (78.4%), while ShuffleNetV2 fell to 66.2%. Glioma-meningioma confusion was the dominant error mode, and Grad-CAM maps showed that predictions were driven by tumour regions. These results indicate that in-distribution accuracy alone overstates real-world performance and that leakage-aware and cross-dataset evaluation should be reported alongside it.

</details>


### [162] [A Simple Yet Effective Agent for Open-World Aerial Object Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34115)
*Jitai Hao,Liangxing Yang,Zhenyang Li,Fan Liu,Qiang Huang,Jun Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SimpleUAV：一个基于冻结的现成视觉语言模型（VLM）、无需训练的空中目标搜索智能体。它将当前视图的目标判别与基于历史的动作选择解耦，结合局部几何与深度检测闭环执行搜索，在UAV-ON上达41.61% SR，在AerialDojo-200K上达11.98%，显著超越最强基线，并可在少量适配下迁移至空中VLN任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放世界空中目标搜索方法依赖持久空间地图、学习到的世界模型或任务特定策略训练，系统复杂且代价高。作者希望探究一个极简、无需训练的设计能达到何种性能上限，并为判断"额外架构复杂度是否必要"建立强基线。

Method: 核心为冻结的现成VLM智能体。系统解耦两个环节：当前视图目标评估与基于历史的动作选择，将新鲜候选证据与轻量搜索上下文结合。局部几何模块把视觉选择转换为有界直线运动，基于深度的检查中止受阻路段，实测执行反馈用于指导后续决策，形成无需持久空间地图与任务特定训练的闭环搜索。

Result: UAV-ON上SR 41.61%（最强基线23.80%），AerialDojo-200K上11.98%（最强基线3.20%）。经有限基准特定适配后，同一核心智能体可迁移至TravelUAV上的空中VLN，在L1辅助下三个划分的Full SR均至少提升19.66%。

Conclusion: 一个训练无关、极简的VLM闭环智能体即可在空中目标搜索与VLN中大幅超越既有方法，为评估"何时需要额外架构复杂度"提供了强基线，并表明复杂化设计的边际收益需被审慎检验。

Abstract: Open-world aerial object search methods often rely on persistent spatial maps, learned world models, or task-specific policy training. To examine how far a minimal, training-free design can go, we introduce SimpleUAV, an agent built around a frozen, off-the-shelf vision-language model (VLM). SimpleUAV separates current-view target assessment from history-conditioned action selection, combining fresh candidate evidence with lightweight search context. Local geometry converts visual selections into bounded straight-line movements, depth-based checks halt blocked segments, and measured execution feedback guides subsequent decisions. This closed loop sustains search without persistent spatial maps or task-specific training. SimpleUAV achieves 41.61% Success Rate (SR) on UAV-ON and 11.98% on AerialDojo-200K, compared with 23.80% and 3.20% for the strongest prior baselines, respectively. With limited benchmark-specific adaptation, the same core agent transfers to aerial Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) on TravelUAV, improving Full SR by at least 19.66% across all three splits under L1 assistance. These results establish a strong baseline for evaluating when additional architectural complexity benefits aerial search.

</details>


### [163] [High-Fidelity Image Matting with Pixel-Space Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34929)
*Baihan Yang,Xin Lin,Yufan Wei,Baiang Li,Yuheng Liu,Juanxi Tian,Xiaohui Xie,Truong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PixelMatte将预训练像素空间扩散Transformer（DiT）适配到高保真图像抠图，复用原生patch embedding把图像、trimap与噪声alpha作为空间对齐token联合处理，无需额外条件编码器，并配合LayerShift与High-Pass Refiner两个轻量校准模块，单次前向即可预测alpha。在四个基准、两个像素扩散骨干上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的抠图方法多依赖潜空间扩散模型，其压缩表示与VAE重建与抠图所需的高频精度不匹配，导致细节与边界失真。

Method: 提出PixelMatte：把纯文本到图像预训练的像素空间扩散Transformer直接迁移到抠图任务，无需成对图像到图像预训练；复用骨干原生patch embedding，将输入图像、trimap和噪声alpha matte编码为空间对齐token，实现像素空间直接预测，免去额外条件编码器或适配器；采用像素级精确合成监督加真实图像微调；引入两个轻量校准模块——LayerShift（改善区域不透明度精度）与High-Pass Refiner（提升边界保真度）；推理为单次前向。

Result: 在四个基准和两种像素扩散骨干上验证：相较RenderMatte，在P3M-500-NP与AM-2K上SAD与梯度误差降低24-44%；在AIM-500与RenderMatte-2K上取得有竞争力表现。

Conclusion: 像素空间生成先验对精度敏感的图像抠图有效：无需潜空间压缩与额外条件适配器即可获得高保真alpha，验证了纯T2I预训练骨干在密集预测任务上的可迁移性。

Abstract: Diffusion models have shown strong potential for image matting by transferring generative priors learned from large-scale pretraining. However, existing approaches largely rely on latent diffusion models, whose compressed representations and VAE-based reconstruction introduce a mismatch with the high-frequency precision required for matting. We present PixelMatte, a framework that adapts pretrained pixel-space diffusion transformers for high-fidelity image matting. We show that strong image-conditioned matting capabilities can be acquired even from a pure text-to-image model without paired image-to-image pretraining. Our approach reuses the backbone's native patch embedding to jointly process the input image, trimap, and noisy alpha matte as spatially aligned tokens, enabling direct pixel-space prediction without additional condition encoders or adapters. With pixel-accurate synthetic supervision and real-image fine-tuning, PixelMatte predicts alpha mattes in a single forward pass. Two lightweight calibration modules, LayerShift and High-Pass Refiner, further improve regional opacity accuracy and boundary fidelity. Experiments across four benchmarks and two distinct pixel diffusion backbones demonstrate substantial improvements over existing methods, reducing SAD and gradient errors by 24-44% relative to RenderMatte on P3M-500-NP and AM-2K, while achieving competitive performance on AIM-500 and RenderMatte-2K. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of pixel-space generative priors for precision-sensitive image matting. Project page: https://white-yc.github.io/pixelmatte/.

</details>


### [164] [Non-Linear Motion from Two Frames: Cubic Hermite Trajectories for Video Frame Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34143)
*Md Rifat Ur Rahman,Md Abtahi Majeed Chowdhury,Md Sakib Hossain Shovon,Jaesik Choi,Mohammad Ali Moni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HermiteVFI用逐像素的显式连续轨迹（三次Hermite曲线）替代query时刻独立的光流重采样，端点位移固定轨迹两端，两个隐式端点导数决定内部路径；系数每帧对预测一次、可任意t闭式求值，多时刻监督解决秩亏问题，在非均匀运动与大插值倍数上提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两帧视频插帧通常按查询时间重采样端点光流，这等价于强制选择匀速直线轨迹，而同一位移其实对应无数条中间轨迹，因此对非均匀运动建模能力受限。

Method: 为每个像素构建一条显式连续轨迹：端点位移固定轨迹的两个位置量，由帧对学习两个隐式端点导数控制内部路径；采用三次Hermite曲线（能独立满足两个位置+两个导数边界条件的最低次多项式）。使用残差形式，精确包含线性运动、结构上保证端点位移不变（与网络输出无关），系数每帧对预测一次并在任意t闭式求值。理论上指出单个内部查询时刻对两个自由系数只构成秩亏约束，任意两个不同内部时刻在表示层面满秩，从而引入多时刻监督；并利用外观导出的方向性线索推断端点位移无法确定的隐式导数。轨迹系数预测被摊销，后续反转、细化与合成仍依赖query。

Result: 在X4K1000FPS、SNU-FILM、Vimeo90K-Septuplet上验证，最大增益出现在困难的非均匀运动场景与高插值倍数条件下。

Conclusion: 以显式连续轨迹参数化替代逐query独立运动预测，可在保持端点一致性的同时刻画非匀速中间路径，并通过多时刻监督有效约束轨迹自由度。

Abstract: Two-frame video frame interpolation commonly resamples endpoint optical flow by the query time, which selects a constant-velocity straight-line path even though the same endpoint displacement is compatible with many intermediate trajectories. We present HermiteVFI, which replaces independent query-time motion prediction with one explicit continuous trajectory per pixel. The measured endpoint displacement fixes the two trajectory positions, while two latent endpoint derivatives learned from the frame pair control the interior path. A cubic Hermite curve is the minimum-degree polynomial that can independently satisfy these two position and two derivative boundary quantities. Its residual form contains linear motion exactly, structurally preserves the measured displacement endpoints for any network output, and has coefficients that are predicted once per frame pair and evaluated in closed form at arbitrary t. We further show that one interior query time gives a rank-deficient constraint on the two free trajectory coefficients, whereas any two distinct interior times are full rank at the representation level, motivating multi-time supervision. Appearance-derived directional cues help infer the latent derivatives that endpoint displacement alone cannot identify. On X4K1000FPS, SNU-FILM, and Vimeo90K-Septuplet, the largest gains occur for difficult non-uniform motion and high interpolation ratios. The amortization applies to trajectory-coefficient prediction; downstream reversal, refinement, and synthesis remain query dependent.

</details>


### [165] [OS-Omni: Benchmarking Generalist Computer-Using Agents on Long-Horizon Workflows Across Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34232)
*Hui Shen,Yunta Hsieh,Jianing Ma,Zhuoran Yin,Zhuhan Bao,Ziyuan Liu,Qi Han,Xiuqi Xu,Xianbang Wang,Wenhao Chai,Sihan Xu,Chenyang Zhao,Yanheng Shang,Dongfu Jiang,Rui Sun,Xingzhi Li,Chenqi Yin,Zesen Zhao,Boyuan Zheng,Jisen Li,Xiaoxia Wu,Ruoyan Zhang,Ruiyao Liu,Jing Xiong,He Xiao,Nan Zheng,Hangxiang Chen,Shengyang Tao,Deyang Luorong,Yifeng Liu,Jingxuan Zhang,Chaofan Tao,Yuren Wang,Jiaqi Mo,Rui Hu,Xing Wei,Xiaofeng Liu,Xin Xu,Han Wu,Mengyao Wen,Guobin Zhang,Shatong Zhu,Hei Ting Una Chan,Sicheng Chen,Kai Zhang,Mengkang Hu,Zhongwei Wan,Xin Wang,Chuanyang Zheng,Ziheng Zhang,Hengyuan Zhang,Zunhai Su,Kanzhi Cheng,Dehan Kong,Yian Wang,Hanrong Zhang,Jiawei Lu,Yifan Zhang,Junnan Li,Changyi Yang,Jintao Qu,Wenyao Gao,Jiaxuan Luo,Jiaxin Deng,Yuhao Chen,Xuesong Ye,Jingwen Gu,Yichi Zhang,Qun Lou,Shen Yan,Ben Athiwaratkun,Qiushi Sun,Mi Zhang,Ping Luo,Wenhu Chen,Ngai Wong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 OS-Omni，一个覆盖 Windows、Ubuntu、macOS、iOS、Android 与 Web 的实时跨平台计算机使用智能体基准与执行环境。v1 版本含每平台 48 个任务和 12 个桌面-安卓跨端工作流，平均需 67.54 步交互。最强智能体 Claude Opus 5.5 平均成功率 68.5%，落后人类参考 77.3% 达 8.8 个百分点，且在 iOS、macOS 上差距超 25 点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准将 Web、移动端、桌面环境割裂评估，无法判断单一通用智能体能否完成跨操作系统、带持久应用状态的长时程工作流；因此需要一个统一、可复现、可执行终态验证的跨平台评测环境。

Method: 构建 OS-Omni 实时跨平台基准与执行环境，按版本波浪式发布（每波 300 任务），v1 含每平台 48 任务及 12 个桌面+Android 跨端工作流；提供可复现初始化、平台专用适配器、轨迹日志与可执行终态评估器，并内置受控服务端应用（账户、购物车、预算、日历、消息等）以支撑持久状态工作流；通过人类研究获取步数与成功率参考基线。

Result: 任务平均需 67.54 个交互步骤，显著高于 OSWorld 的 15.0 步；v1 上最强智能体 Claude Opus 5.5 平均成功率 68.5%，低于人类参考 77.3% 共 8.8 点；平台间差距不均，前沿模型在 Linux 上超过人类，但在 iOS 与 macOS 上落后逾 25 点。

Conclusion: 当前智能体距离平台鲁棒的长时程计算机使用仍有较大差距，尤其在需要持续状态跟踪与持久终态变更的场景中；OS-Omni 为跨平台通用智能体的系统评估提供了可复现、可扩展的基准基础设施。

Abstract: Computer-using agents are increasingly expected to complete realistic user goals rather than isolated GUI actions. However, existing benchmarks typically isolate web, mobile, or desktop environments, leaving it unclear whether a single generalist agent can execute long-horizon workflows that span multiple operating systems and persistent application states. We introduce OS-Omni, a live cross-platform benchmark and execution environment for evaluating computer-using agents across Windows, Ubuntu, macOS, iOS, Android, and Web environments. OS-Omni is released in versioned waves of 300 tasks each; this paper focuses on the first release, v1, which contains 48 tasks per platform and 12 cross-platform workflows that pair a desktop with an Android phone. OS-Omni provides reproducible initialization, platform-specific adapters, trajectory logging, and executable final-state evaluators, and includes controlled service-backed applications for workflows involving account, cart, budget, calendar, message, and other persistent state. Human studies show that OS-Omni tasks require 67.54 interaction steps, substantially exceeding OSWorld references of 15.0 steps. On v1, the strongest evaluated agent, Claude Opus 5.5, reaches 68.5% average success, trailing the 77.3% human reference by 8.8 points; the gap is uneven across platforms, with frontier models exceeding humans on Linux but trailing them by more than 25 points on iOS and macOS. These results indicate that current agents remain far from platform-robust, long-horizon computer use, especially when success requires sustained state tracking and durable final-state changes.

</details>


### [166] [Admoab-DETR: Resolution-Tiered Fusion for Infrared-Visible Object Detection with Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34163)
*Wenchao Li,Haoran Jia,Shuyuan Wen,Keyan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一实时检测器 Admoab-DETR，用一套权重支持红外单模、可见光单模与配对输入，通过分辨率分层融合与模式匹配蒸馏实现精度-效率最优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外与可见光图像互补，可支撑全天候目标检测，但真实场景中传感器失效会导致某一路缺失，使依赖双输入的检测器性能退化；同时现有模态自适应方法在各尺度上统一施加沉重的跨模态注意力，在高分辨率特征图上产生难以承受的 token 开销。

Method: 提出 Admoab-DETR：一是分辨率分层融合（RTF），按空间 token 密度与特征粒度分配融合容量——最高分辨率尺度用轻量空间模态门控做精细局部融合并支持零成本单视图直通，较粗语义尺度用通道压缩的可变形 Admoab neck 捕获跨模态对应关系；二是模式匹配特征蒸馏（MMFD），以与可用性同步的全融合教师对齐多尺度编码器特征，避免病态的跨模态幻觉。并构建 Admoab 严格配准的 2 万对红外-可见光基准。

Result: 参数量减少 18.1%，配对输入延迟降低 39.6%，且推理零额外开销；在缺失模态与配对基准上取得 SOTA 的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: Admoab-DETR 以单一权重的统一框架同时覆盖红外单模、可见光单模与配对三种输入模式，兼顾实时性与精度，为传感器缺失下的鲁棒全天候检测提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Infrared and visible images offer complementary cues for all-weather object detection, yet real-world sensor dropouts can leave one stream unavailable and degrade paired-input detectors. Meanwhile, existing modality-adaptive methods apply heavy cross-modal attention uniformly across scales, incurring prohibitive token overhead on high-resolution maps. We propose Admoab-DETR, a unified real-time detector supporting infrared-only, visible-only, and paired inputs with a single set of weights. Specifically, \emph{Resolution-Tiered Fusion} (RTF) allocates fusion capacity by spatial token density and feature granularity: a lightweight Spatial Modality Gate governs fine local fusion at the highest-resolution scale with zero-cost single-view pass-through, while channel-compressed deformable Admoab necks capture cross-modal correspondence at coarser semantic scales. Furthermore, \emph{Mode-Matched Feature Distillation} (MMFD) aligns multi-scale encoder features with an availability-synchronized full-fusion teacher, avoiding ill-posed cross-modal hallucination while cutting parameters by 18.1\% and paired latency by 39.6\% at zero inference overhead. We also construct Admoab, a strictly registered 20,000-pair infrared--visible benchmark across diverse scenes. Experiments show Admoab-DETR achieves state-of-the-art accuracy--efficiency trade-offs across missing-modality and paired benchmarks. Our code will be available at https://github.com/liwenchao0615/Admoab-DETR.

</details>


### [167] [Spe3DUp: Faithful Trajectory Distillation for Fast Image-to-3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34141)
*Liangchao Xu,Jiaye Fu,Youtian Lin,Feihu Zhang,Yao Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Spe3DUp：基于忠实轨迹蒸馏（FTD）的由粗到细加速框架，对粗几何生成与几何精化两个DiT分别做少步蒸馏，并配合高效几何解码与并行重网格化，实现端到端7.7倍加速且保持与参考输入的对应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量3D生成模型依赖长多步采样、反复调用大网络，推理耗时严重；而游戏资产重建、产品数字化等参考驱动任务要求生成几何忠实对应输入图像，因此加速必须在削减采样计算的同时保留对特定参考对象的重建能力。

Method: 提出由粗到细框架Spe3DUp，核心为忠实轨迹蒸馏（FTD）：直接以教师模型在输入条件下的去噪轨迹监督少步学生模型，以保持其生成行为；FTD分别应用于粗几何生成与几何精化两个阶段，压缩两处采样过程；并以高效几何解码与并行重网格化加速完整网格生成流水线。

Result: 几何质量与输入对应性接近教师模型；粗几何与精化DiT分别加速10.0倍与19.9倍；端到端相比原始教师流水线加速7.7倍。

Conclusion: 轨迹级监督式蒸馏可在少步采样下有效保留条件生成行为，配合解码与重网格化的工程优化，可在参考驱动的3D网格生成中实现显著加速而不牺牲保真度。

Abstract: Recent 3D generative models can produce high-quality meshes with complex structures and fine geometric details, with broad applications in games, film, and digital design. However, high-quality generation typically relies on long multi-step sampling processes that repeatedly evaluate large networks, leading to long inference times. At the same time, reference-driven tasks such as game-asset reconstruction and product digitization demand close correspondence to the input image: generated geometry must faithfully reconstruct the specific reference object. Acceleration must therefore reduce sampling computation while preserving the model's ability to reconstruct that object. To enable fast and faithful 3D generation, we introduce Spe3DUp, a coarse-to-fine framework based on Faithful Trajectory Distillation (FTD). FTD directly supervises few-step students with the teacher's input-conditioned denoising trajectories to preserve its generation behavior. We apply FTD separately to coarse geometry generation and geometric refinement, compressing both sampling processes while maintaining correspondence to the reference input. We further accelerate the complete mesh-generation pipeline with efficient geometry decoding and parallel remeshing. Experiments show that Spe3DUp maintains geometric quality and input correspondence close to those of the teacher, accelerates the coarse and refinement DiTs by 10.0x and 19.9x, respectively, and achieves a 7.7x end-to-end speedup over the original teacher pipeline.

</details>


### [168] [Benefit of Structured Representations: When Equivariance Helps in Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34140)
*Florian A. Hölzl,Daniel Rueckert,Georgios Kaissis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ConvNeXt-C4（C4 四分之一转旋转等变 ConvNeXt），在全部任务上超越基线，参数少 6 倍、FLOPs 少 1.6 倍，且样本效率优势随训练集缩小而增大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等变神经网络理论上能通过结构保证改善表征学习，但主流视觉编码器很少引入除平移外的大多数几何对称等变性。作者希望在可控条件下实证回答：视觉编码器何时能从几何归纳偏置中获益，并厘清等变性带来的收益来源（结构性保证 vs. 容量/效率）。

Method: 选择离散四分之一转旋转群 C4：它在离散像素网格上无插值、无边界伪影地无损作用，配合图像分类这一受控的不变目标构成干净的实验平台。构建 ConvNeXt-C4：维度规模等价但表征被显式赋予方向（orientation）结构，从零训练，与基础 ConvNeXt 对照；并用分类头分析检验方向信息是否被实际利用。

Result: C4 变体在所有任务上均优于基线，同时参数量减少约 6 倍、FLOPs 减少约 1.6 倍；性能优势随训练集规模下降呈对数线性增长；预训练对两类模型的增益相近；分类头分析显示利用表征结构中的方向信息可带来 4.90% 的整体提升。

Conclusion: C4 等变归纳偏置带来显著的样本效率与参数效率收益，且保留方向信息的结构化表征具有独立价值；作者建议将该研究推广至图像分类之外的任务。

Abstract: Equivariant neural networks promise improved representation learning through structural guarantees. However, mainstream vision encoders rarely incorporate equivariance for most geometric symmetries. Images are a particularly interesting modality with many such symmetries and extensive datasets from which to learn this structure. In this work, we investigate when vision encoders benefit from geometric inductive biases. We study the discrete rotation group of quarter-turns (C4) which acts losslessly without interpolation or boundary artifacts. Combined with image classification as a controlled invariant objective, this allows for a principled empirical investigation into the role of structural guarantees. We introduce ConvNeXt-C4 as a rotation equivariant ConvNeXt with dimension-equivalent yet orientation-structured representations. Training from scratch, our experiments show, that the C4 variant outperforms its base model across all tasks while operating with 6x fewer parameters and 1.6x fewer FLOPs. Its performance advantage increases log-linearly with decreasing training set size, with pre-training providing a similar improvement across both model variants. Analyzing the classifier head reveals that orientation information from the representations' structure is actively used, improving performance by 4.90% across all datasets. Our results highlight the sample-efficiency benefits of equivariance and the value of retained orientation information, motivating further investigation beyond image classification. Code is available at https://github.com/hlzl/equivariant-convnext.

</details>


### [169] [An Image Detectability Field as a Viewpoint Utility for 2D-3D Localization in Sparse Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34139)
*Han Sun,Jun Li,Christy Jie Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出并验证"图像可检测性场"作为视角选择效用：无需微调即可查询任意表面点附近3D位置，预测2D检测器在该点投影处响应的概率，并在合成与真实场景中优于计数/几何启发式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手持或机器人搭载相机可在拍摄前选择视角，2D-3D定位成功与否依赖图像检测器能否在所选视角下检测到地图地标；作者追问：仅读取地图几何的"图像可检测性场"能否作为有效的视角效用函数。

Method: 构建可查询的可检测性场：输入表面附近任意3D点的局部几何，输出2D图像检测器在该点投影处响应的概率；直接使用已训练场、不为视角选择微调。固定SuperPoint + EPnP-RANSAC定位器评估所选视角。两设置预注册测试：(1) ShapeNet合成对象+稀疏点图，对比求和场值、计地标数、求和Harris-3D秩；(2) SceneFun3D真实室内场景，将场加入学习式逐地标规划器，与原版及以Harris-3D/曲率替代者对比。

Result: 合成稀疏点图：对可见地标求和场值比计数高+2.0个定位成功率百分点（对象聚类95%区间[+0.7,+3.2]），比求和Harris-3D秩高+2.2[+0.8,+3.5]。真实室内：自适应的学习式视角评分器LWL在测试房间上低于计数；将场加入逐地标规划器后，在稀疏且地标亦由场选取的测试房间上比无场版高+1.8点，比用Harris-3D或曲率替代高+1.0与+0.8点，三个房间聚类区间均高于零。

Conclusion: 可检测性场是有效的视角效用，在稀疏地图中提升定位成功率，在稠密地图中与Harris-3D相当；但学习式视角评分器在该真实场景上未能超越计数，说明须与逐地标规划器结合使用。

Abstract: In 2D-3D localization, a camera estimates its pose by matching an image to a 3D map. A camera held in a hand or mounted on a robot can often choose its viewpoint before it takes the image. In this work, we ask whether an image detectability field is a useful viewpoint utility when the planner reads only the geometry of the map. The field can be queried at any 3D point near a surface. From the local geometry around the point, it predicts how likely a 2D image detector is to fire at the projection of the point. We use the trained field as it is, without fine-tuning it for view selection. A fixed SuperPoint and EPnP-RANSAC localizer then estimates the pose from the chosen view. We answer in two settings, each with a test registered before the runs. On synthetic ShapeNet objects with sparse point maps, summing the field over the visible landmarks beats counting them by +2.0 points of localization success rate (object-clustered 95% interval [+0.7, +3.2]). It also beats summing Harris-3D ranks by +2.2 [+0.8, +3.5]. On real indoor scenes from SceneFun3D, our adaptation of the learned view scorer LWL falls below counting on the test rooms. We therefore add the field to a learned per-landmark planner. On the test rooms with sparse maps whose landmarks the field also selects, the planner with the field beats the same planner without it by +1.8 points. It beats the planner with Harris-3D or curvature in its place by +1.0 and +0.8 points. All three room-clustered intervals lie above zero. In both settings, the field helps in sparse maps and is on par with Harris-3D in dense maps.

</details>


### [170] [Exploring the Design Factors of Pixel-space Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34093)
*Mang Ning,Le Zhang,Albert Ali Salah,Itir Onal Ertugrul,Jintao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出像素空间扩散模型EiT，通过通道瓶颈的U形Transformer与加深网络实现更早去噪和更长基提升阶段，在ImageNet 256/512上FID优于JiT，并可无缝迁移到视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统探究像素空间扩散的关键设计因素（模型架构、表征、频域），以缩小像素扩散与潜空间扩散的差距，并提升生成质量。

Method: 受去噪自编码器瓶颈思想启发，构建沿通道维度显式压缩的U形像素扩散Transformer；分析各层与各时间步的三阶段表征动态（去噪/局部特征扩展、基提升、后验投影）；从频域论证网络保持像素空间时像素空间扩散与频域扩散的等价性。

Result: ImageNet 256×256 FID 1.61（JiT 1.85），512×512 FID 1.73（JiT 1.99）；将patch扩展至时间维即可用于视频像素扩散，在Kinetics-600（256分辨率）上FVD优于视频潜扩散（总参数可比）。

Conclusion: 通道瓶颈加深网络天然带来更早去噪与更长基提升阶段，是提升像素扩散质量的关键；EiT在图像与视频生成上均具竞争力。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce EiT by exploring the key design factors of pixel-space diffusion from three perspectives: model architecture, representation, and frequency domain. Inspired by Denoising Autoencoders (DAE) which perform denoising through a bottleneck, we construct a U-shaped pixel diffusion transformer that performs explicit compression along the channel dimension. In terms of representation, we find that both pixel and latent diffusion transformers exhibit a three-stage representation dynamics across layers and timesteps: denoising/local feature expansion, basis lift-up, and posterior projection. Moreover, earlier denoising and a longer basis lift-up stage are associated with better generation, which EiT naturally achieves through its channel bottleneck and increased network depth. From the frequency perspective, we establish the equivalence between pixel-space and frequency-space diffusion when keeping the network operating in pixel space. On ImageNet $256\times256$ and $512\times512$, EiT consistently outperforms JiT (Just image Transformers) in generation quality. Specifically, our EiT achieves better FID than JiT: 1.61 vs. 1.85 at $256\times256$, and 1.73 vs. 1.99 at $512\times512$. Remarkably, EiT can be seamlessly applied to video pixel diffusion by simply extending the patch size to temporal dimension and outperforms video latent diffusion in FVD evaluated on Kinetics-600 (resolution 256), under comparable total network parameters. Code is available at https://github.com/forever208/EiT

</details>


### [171] [When to Respond, Where to Ground: Streaming 3D Affordance Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34067)
*Wenting Luan,Weilong Yan,Siqi Lu,Ming Li,Beibei Lin,Yongbin Zheng,Robby T. Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 StreamAff，依据接地模型自身视觉证据同时决定何时响应与何处定位，并发布 StreamFun3D 基准与 StreamIoU 指标，在15秒流上取得30.14% StreamIoU，相对最强基线提升40.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D可供性接地方法假设观测完整，而流式视觉语言模型缺乏细粒度3D功能性接地能力，需要在连续观测中联合解决响应时机与功能部件定位问题。

Method: StreamAff：对已读帧的视觉patch打分，将累积支持度与“等待”选项比较以决定响应时机，最高分patch选择接地帧；引入条件回看策略（在请求时刻基于早前观测作答或等待新证据）与zoom-and-refine接地，在所选帧的3D点上分割答案所指部件。

Result: 构建真实场景衍生的 StreamFun3D 基准（Current/History/Future/Unobserved 四种证据设定）与 StreamIoU 指标（仅在响应及时且源帧有效时计分）；StreamAff 在15秒流上 StreamIoU 达30.14%，相对最强离线适配/外部决策器基线提升40.6%。

Conclusion: 以模型自身证据驱动的“何时答+答何处”统一框架在流式3D可供性接地上显著优于离线方法与VLM外部决策基线，表明响应时机应与接地点位联合建模。

Abstract: Given a continuous RGB-D stream and a language request, streaming 3D affordance grounding requires an agent to decide when to respond and where to locate the requested functional part using only observations available so far. Existing 3D affordance grounding methods typically assume complete scene observations, while streaming vision-language models lack fine-grained 3D functional grounding. Our goal is to jointly determine response timing and functional-part localization during continuous scene observation. We propose StreamAff, an evidence-guided response strategy that makes both decisions from the grounding model's own visual evidence. The model scores the visual patches of the frames it reads: their combined support, weighed against a wait option, decides when to respond, and the highest-scoring patch selects the frame to ground in. A conditional look-back policy lets the model answer from an earlier observation at the moment of the request or wait for new evidence, and zoom-and-refine grounding segments the part named in the answer on the 3D points of the selected frame. We further introduce StreamFun3D, a benchmark derived from real-world 3D scenes that evaluates response timing and grounding across four evidence settings: Current, History, Future, and Unobserved, together with StreamIoU, a metric that credits a mask only when the response is timely and grounded in a valid source frame. We adapt offline 3D affordance grounding methods and build external-decider baselines that pair vision-language models with our mask decoder. StreamAff achieves 30.14% StreamIoU on 15-second streams, a 40.6% relative improvement over the strongest of these baselines.

</details>


### [172] [ShowNow: Fine-Grained Speculative Streaming for Responsive World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34693)
*Yiren Song,Guian Fang,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ShowNow 提出细粒度投机式流式框架，利用播放期间的空闲算力预先准备动作条件化未来，将输入到显示的时延从 620ms 降至 208ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
