Motivation: 交互式世界模型需在连续生成视频的同时快速响应用户动作。仅提升实时吞吐无法解决输入时延：粗粒度生成块会延迟动作更新，且输入后再生成会把计算加入响应路径。

Method: 1) 单潜变量提交（single-latent commitment）支持高频决策；2) 可配置的预准备覆盖 1–8 个动作；3) 构建匹配的多未来数据集，训练同一状态下的多种后续分支；4) 共享历史树执行与融合提交提升多分支准备效率。

Result: 单张 H100 上，5B 模型在准备 8 个未来分支时以 16fps 生成 480p 视频。ViZDoom 中完整部署将中位输入到显示时延从 620ms 降至 208ms；匹配策略实验将重启空帧率从 1.56% 降至 0.09%（约 94%），并提升对动作变更的准时响应率。

Conclusion: 细粒度提交与提前准备具有互补作用，并在视觉保真度与长时一致性上存在可测权衡；该工作将播放期计算转化为响应式视觉交互。

Abstract: Interactive world models must generate video continuously while responding promptly to user actions. Real-time throughput alone leaves input latency unresolved: coarse generation blocks delay action updates, and generation after input adds work to the response path. We introduce ShowNow, a fine-grained speculative streaming framework that uses computation available during playback to prepare action-conditioned futures before input arrives. Single-latent commitment enables frequent decisions, while configurable preparation covers one to eight actions. We construct a matched multi-future dataset to train alternative continuations from a common state; shared-history tree execution and fused commitment make their preparation efficient. On a single H100, our 5B model sustains 480p generation at 16 fps with eight prepared futures. In ViZDoom, the complete ShowNow deployment reduces median input-to-display latency from 620 to 208 ms relative to the native-block SolarWM-FT deployment. Matched-policy experiments reduce restart empty ticks from 1.56% to 0.09% (94%) and improve on-time responses to action changes. These results establish complementary roles for fine-grained commitment and advance preparation, with measured trade-offs in visual fidelity and long-horizon consistency. ShowNow turns playback-time computation into responsive visual interaction. Project: https://github.com/showlab/ShowNow.

</details>


### [173] [DuCo: Exploiting Dual Cross-Layer Consistency for Sparse Geometric Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34608)
*Liangyu Xiang,Junyu Gao,Xiaofeng Wang,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DuCo 是一种免训练稀疏注意力框架，利用跨层注意力分布一致性与 value 冗余结构局部变化两种跨层一致性，通过历史注意力估计当前层 dense 注意力概率、并用跨层局部更新的 value 聚类刻画冗余，以概率加权 value 失真联合优化四种聚类表示状态，在注意力预算下构建稀疏掩码，在 3 个视觉几何基础模型、5 个基准上取得更优的精度—效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉几何基础模型依赖 dense global attention 建模跨视角几何，但二次复杂度使长图像序列推理效率受限。现有稀疏注意力方法主要依据注意力概率选择全局注意力矩阵元素，且逐层独立构建掩码，忽略了 value 冗余与跨层信息复用。

Method: 作者分析跨层注意力分布并聚类 value 表示，发现两类跨层一致性：注意力概率分布高度相似、value 冗余结构仅发生局部变化。据此提出 DuCo：1) 复用历史注意力信息估计当前层 dense 注意力概率；2) 引入 value 聚类刻画冗余，并通过跨层局部更新高效精化簇；3) 以概率加权 value 失真为统一准则，在给定注意力预算下联合优化四种簇表示状态，用于构造稀疏掩码。方法免训练。

Result: 在三种视觉几何基础模型与五个基准上实验，DuCo 相较基线展现出更优的精度—效率权衡。

Conclusion: 跨层一致性与 value 冗余可作为稀疏注意力的有效先验，在不训练的前提下显著提升长序列几何推理效率，同时保持精度。

Abstract: Dense global attention is crucial for capturing cross-view geometry in visual geometry foundation models, yet its quadratic complexity limits efficient inference on long image sequences. Existing sparse attention methods primarily select entries of the global attention matrix based on attention probabilities and construct masks independently across layers, overlooking value redundancy and cross-layer information reuse. By analyzing attention distributions and clustering value representations across layers, we reveal two forms of \emph{cross-layer consistency}, characterized by highly similar attention probability distributions and localized changes in value redundancy structures. Building on these observations, we propose \textbf{DuCo}, a training-free sparse attention framework that exploits historical attention information and value redundancy for efficient geometric inference. Specifically, DuCo leverages historical attention information to estimate current-layer dense attention probabilities and introduces value clustering to exploit redundancy, with clusters efficiently refined through local updates across layers. Both factors are integrated through probability-weighted value distortion to jointly optimize four cluster representation states under an attention budget for sparse mask construction. Experiments across three visual geometry foundation models and five benchmarks demonstrate favorable accuracy--efficiency trade-offs.

</details>


### [174] [Physics-Guided Generative Modelling of Oxy-Combustion Flame Images: From Conditional LSGANs to Denoising Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34568)
*Imane Er-rabhi,Gorkem Oztarlik,Jean Caudal,Renaud Gaudron*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建深度生成代理模型，将15维燃烧工况参数直接映射为512×64热释放火焰图像，比较cLSGAN与DDPM两种范式的精度与效率，验证其作为燃烧实验视觉代理的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统含氧燃烧器设计验证依赖昂贵实验台架与OH*化学发光成像，逐工况迭代设计极慢，亟需快速可视化的数值代理。

Method: 基于OXYTEC双旋流非预混含氧燃烧数据库的1,609个实验样本，训练两类条件生成模型：空间锚定条件最小二乘GAN（cLSGAN-L1-CBN，单次前向生成）与物理引导去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM，迭代采样）；在分层留出测试集上做消融验证，并进行最近邻误差溯源与参数敏感性分析。

Result: 优化后cLSGAN达RMSE 0.022、FID 20.68，移动工作站GPU上约20 ms/图，但延长训练纹理真实度不再提升；DDPM长训练持续改善，40,000轮达RMSE 0.019、FID 20.29，但单图约30 s，慢约三个数量级。最大误差集中于参数空间稀疏采样区，扰动输入响应平滑且物理一致。

Conclusion: 经恰当引导的生成模型可作为可信的燃烧实验视觉代理；GAN适合实时推理，扩散模型适合追求精度的离线场景。

Abstract: Validating industrial oxy-combustion burner designs traditionally requires costly experimental campaigns in which each operating condition is physically reproduced on an instrumented rig and imaged via OH* chemiluminescence, rendering iterative design exploration prohibitively slow. This work develops deep generative surrogate models that map a 15-dimensional vector of continuous operating parameters (thermal power, swirl numbers, momentum ratios, mixture fractions, etc.) directly to a $512\times64$ heat-release flame image, trained on 1,609 experimental samples from the OXYTEC dual-swirl non-premixed oxy-combustion database. Two complementary generative paradigms are investigated, as they offer opposite strengths for surrogate modelling: a custom spatially-anchored conditional least-squares GAN (cLSGAN-L1-CBN), whose single-pass generation enables near-instantaneous inference, and a custom physics-guided denoising diffusion probabilistic model (DDPM), whose iterative sampling favours the smooth, continuous heat-release gradients of fluid flows. Both are validated through ablation on a stratified held-out test set. The optimized cLSGAN reaches RMSE 0.022 and FID 20.68 and generates an image in about 20 ms on a mobile workstation GPU, though extended training leaves its texture realism unchanged; the DDPM improves steadily over long training horizons, reaching RMSE 0.019 and FID 20.29 at 40,000 epochs, at the cost of roughly thirty seconds per image on the same hardware --- three orders of magnitude slower than the adversarial surrogate. A nearest-neighbour analysis traces the persistent worst-case errors to sparsely sampled regions of the parameter space, and a parameter-sensitivity study confirms smooth, physically consistent responses to perturbed inputs. These results demonstrate that carefully guided generative models can act as reliable visual surrogates for combustion experiments.

</details>


### [175] [SceneDesigner++: A Unified 9-DoF Pose Control Framework for T2I and T2V Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34461)
*Xincheng Shuai,Zhenyuan Qin,Henghui Ding,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneDesigner++ 是一个统一支持 T2I 与 T2V 生成的 9-DoF 位姿控制框架，通过绝对 CNOCS 图编码与相对 VPRoPE 编码解耦帧级空间定位与帧间运动建模，并配套数据集 ObjectPose9D++ 与评测基准 T2IVPoseEval。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确的 9-DoF 位姿控制对视觉内容生成中指定物体位置、尺寸与朝向至关重要，但现有方法多局限于 T2I 或 T2V 单一任务，且位姿可控性与时序一致性不足。

Method: 构建带精确位姿标注的图像/视频数据集 ObjectPose9D++；采用绝对+相对双路位姿编码：绝对位姿以 CNOCS map 序列提供逐帧显式 9-DoF 引导，相对位姿以 VPRoPE 将相对位姿注入 RoPE 建模帧间位姿转移；为每个注意力头引入可学习正交变换以捕获互补几何关系并保持特征范数稳定；采用两阶段训练分别学习两类编码；可与独立训练相机控制模块结合，实现物体与相机运动的分离或联合控制；提出 T2IVPoseEval 基准。

Result: 在 T2I 与 T2V 任务上，SceneDesigner++ 在同骨干基线比较中于位姿保真度与运动质量方面均持续领先。

Conclusion: 轻量化、解耦的统一 9-DoF 位姿控制框架可同时提升位姿可控性、时序一致性与模块可组合性，并为 T2I/T2V 位姿控制提供标准化数据与评测基础。

Abstract: Precise 9-DoF pose control is essential for visual content generation, enabling users to specify object location, size, and orientation in 3D space. However, existing methods typically focus on either text-to-image (T2I) or text-to-video (T2V) generation, with limitations in pose controllability and temporal coherence. We present SceneDesigner++, a unified framework for 9-DoF pose control across both T2I and T2V generation. We construct ObjectPose9D++, a dataset containing high-quality images and videos with accurate pose annotations. Trained on ObjectPose9D++, SceneDesigner++ combines absolute and relative pose encoding strategies to decouple frame-wise spatial grounding from inter-frame motion modeling. Specifically, absolute pose encoding leverages CNOCS map sequences to provide explicit, frame-wise 9-DoF pose guidance. Meanwhile, our relative pose encoding, termed VPRoPE, incorporates relative pose information into rotary position embeddings to explicitly model inter-frame pose transitions, thereby promoting temporally coherent object motion. A learnable orthogonal transformation is further introduced for each attention head, allowing different heads to capture complementary geometric relationships while preserving input feature norms to promote training stability. We further adopt a two-stage training strategy to learn the absolute and relative pose encoding mechanisms separately. Benefiting from this lightweight and decoupled design, SceneDesigner++ also supports integration with an independently trained camera-control module, enabling separate or joint control over object and camera motion. Finally, we introduce T2IVPoseEval, a benchmark for evaluating diverse object poses and motion patterns in both T2I and T2V tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SceneDesigner++ consistently outperforms same-backbone baselines in pose fidelity and motion quality.

</details>


### [176] [Tracking Any Point with Agentic Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34943)
*Xuweiyi Chen,Jin Yao,Linzhan Mou,Lei Gong,Di Liu,Cheng Zhang,Ziqiao Ma,Sihan Xu,Adam W. Harley,Shengyi Qian,Zezhou Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于VLM的智能体AgenTAP，主动推理视频并直接预测点坐标与可见性，无需对应专用微调，在点定位与可见性预测上超越专家视觉模型；并构建EgoTAP-4D三维点轨迹数据集，提升D4RT的三维跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用VLM已在目标检测、视频识别等领域超越专家模型，但点对应超出目标级识别范畴：需在跨观测中实现同一物理表面点的像素级定位，即使外观变化或点消失后重现，这对VLM仍具挑战。

Method: 构建VLM-based智能体AgenTAP，直接预测点坐标与可见性并主动推理视频：从自身估计出发，自主选择检查区域，利用所得证据验证点的身份并迭代精化预测；无需针对对应任务的微调。随后用AgenTAP在点消失后重识别物理点、重连碎片化轨迹，构建长时真实第一视角视频的3D点轨迹数据集EgoTAP-4D。

Result: 搭配GPT-6 Astra时，在点定位与可见性预测上均超越专家视觉模型；在采样的重现点上预测接近人类一致性水平；将EgoTAP-4D加入D4RT训练可提升平均3D跟踪性能。

Conclusion: VLM智能体可通过主动推理实现高质量点对应与可见性判断，并能用于长时3D点轨迹数据构建，反哺三维跟踪模型训练。

Abstract: A general-purpose Vision-Language Model (VLM) has surpassed expert vision models in many domains such as object detection and video recognition. However, point correspondence is known to be challenging for VLMs since it goes beyond object-level recognition: it requires pixel-level localization of the same physical surface point across observations, even when appearance changes or the point disappears and later reappears. In this paper, we introduce AgenTAP, a VLM-based agent that directly predicts point coordinates and visibility and actively reasons over videos. Starting from its own estimate, the agent autonomously chooses what to inspect and uses the resulting evidence to verify point identity and refine its predictions. With GPT-6 Astra, it surpasses expert vision models in both point localization and visibility prediction, without correspondence-specific fine-tuning. We further use AgenTAP to re-identify physical points after disappearance and reconnect fragmented tracks, constructing EgoTAP-4D, a dataset of 3D point trajectories in long real-world egocentric videos. On sampled reappearing points, its predictions approach human agreement, and adding EgoTAP-4D to D4RT training improves average 3D tracking performance.

</details>


### [177] [Agentic LongLive: Asynchronous Agentic Memory for Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34907)
*Shaoteng Liu,Shuai Yang,Wei Huang,Luozhou Wang,Song Han,Yukang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Agentic LongLive：异步智能体记忆实现实时长视频生成中的长程一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时自回归视频生成器可流式生成长视频并在线接受新提示，但在长时间交互叙事中难以保持人物、物体与场景的一致性；现有离线多镜头智能体方案需预读完整剧本、逐镜头规划并重生成失败镜头，不适合直播式流式场景。

Method: 提出异步智能体记忆框架：记忆智能体运行于独立GPU，仅读取目前已揭示的提示（不读取生成视频），为每个人物、物体、场景维护文本状态与演化图像；由实体记忆合成全局图像与逐镜头记忆图像，经辅助GPU在下一镜头开始前预填充进生成器的attention sink；为命名实体的提示写入实体卡片，使视频生成无需等待记忆且视频误差不回写。采用强化学习训练的小语言模型，配合以下游视频结果校准的图像奖励，在未知下一提示前决定记忆内容。

Result: 在LongStory-Online（25个故事、857个镜头的逐镜头揭示基准）上，持久状态准确率提升26.3点、主角身份一致性提升44.9点、重访地点一致性提升40.4点，额外生成时间约1%（H100）；单台8xB200节点、生成器BF16下以34.3 FPS超实时流式生成，14分钟故事图像质量稳定；在两个MiniMax-H3生成器上提升身份一致性，其中LongLive-H3亦实现实时运行。

Conclusion: 首个由LLM智能体维护文本与图像记忆的实时视频生成器，在不阻塞生成的前提下显著提升长程实体一致性。

Abstract: Real-time autoregressive video generators stream minutes of video and accept new prompts on the fly, but struggle to keep people, objects, and places consistent over a long interactive story. Agent systems provide such memory for offline multi-shot generation, yet they read the whole script in advance, plan each shot before generating it, and regenerate failed shots, which a live stream does not allow. We present Agentic LongLive, an asynchronous agentic memory for long video generation. A memory agent on separate GPUs reads only the prompts revealed so far, never the generated video, and keeps a text state and evolving images for every person, object, and scene. From this entity memory it composes a global image and a per-shot memory image, which an auxiliary GPU prefills into the generator's attention sink before the next shot starts, and writes entity cards for the prompts that name them, so the video never waits for memory and video errors are not written back. A small language model, trained with reinforcement learning and an image reward calibrated using downstream video outcomes, decides what to remember before the next prompt is known. On LongStory-Online, a new benchmark of 25 stories (857 shots) revealed one shot at a time, Agentic LongLive improves persistent-state accuracy of LongLive-2.0, its base generator, by 26.3 points, protagonist identity by 44.9, and revisited-place consistency by 40.4, at about 1% extra generation time on H100. The full system streams faster than real time on one 8xB200 node with the generator in BF16 (34.3 FPS), keeps image quality stable over 14-minute stories, and improves identity on two MiniMax-H3 generators, one of which, LongLive-H3, also runs in real time with it. To our knowledge, it is the first real-time video generator with text and image memory kept by an LLM agent.

</details>


### [178] [Guidance as Variance Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34801)
*Ali Backour,Artem Lukoianov,Frank Permenter,Tommi Jaakkola,Justin Solomon,Vincent Sitzmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文发现扩散模型引导（guidance）并不改善后验均值预测、反而使其变差，真正作用是恢复被压缩的预测方差；据此提出"方差匹配引导"这一闭式规则，无需调参即可复现11个主流模型的手调引导系数，并给出"引导将误差差异重缩放为具有后验协方差的样本"的机理解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引导显著提升扩散模型采样质量，但其作用机制长期缺乏清晰解释；本文旨在厘清引导到底改变了预测的什么性质，并用一个可验证的定量解释取代经验性调参。

Method: 1）实证检验：在实际使用的引导尺度下，比较引导前后对后验均值（模型训练目标）的预测精度，并比较预测方差与数据方差；2）提出 variance-matched guidance：以"使引导后预测方差匹配数据方差"为准则，闭式求解引导尺度，并可逐噪声水平得到无超参的引导调度；3）机制解释：将引导视为对两个模型后验均值预测中随机误差之差的重新缩放，使其匹配后验协方差；由此推导并检验两个可证伪假设（负引导尺度可行、用自身checkpoint引导需更大尺度）。

Result: 在实践尺度下，引导使后验均值预测变差而样本质量提升；后验均值预测方差低于数据方差，引导可将其恢复。方差匹配规则在无需调参的情况下，于图像、视频、音频、运动与世界生成共11个广泛使用模型中复现了人工挑选的引导尺度（此前无任何引导解释能做到这一点）；逐噪声水平应用得到的调度在 SD 3.5 Medium 上优于手工设计的调度。实验同时支持两个新假设：引导在负尺度下仍可生效；用模型自身checkpoint引导所需尺度大于用独立训练模型引导。

Conclusion: 引导并非提升预测准确度，而是通过校正扩散模型后验均值预测的方差不足、并将误差差异重缩放至后验协方差来改善采样。方差匹配为引导尺度提供了有预测力的原理性解释与免调参方案，也拓展了对负尺度引导与自引导/交叉引导差异的理解。

Abstract: Guidance repels the prediction of a diffusion model away from a weaker reference prediction. It substantially improves sample quality, but its mechanism remains poorly understood. We first find that, at the scales used in practice, guidance does not improve the prediction of the posterior mean (the model's training target); it makes the prediction worse, while samples improve. Second, posterior-mean predictions have less variance than the data, and we find that guidance restores it. We introduce variance-matched guidance: a closed-form rule that picks the guidance scale by matching the variance of the guided predictions to that of the data. Without tuning, it recovers the hand-picked scales of eleven widely used models across image, video, audio, motion, and world generation; to our knowledge, no previous explanation of guidance predicts them. Applied per noise level, it yields a schedule without tuned hyperparameters that users prefer to hand-crafted ones on SD 3.5 Medium. Third, we offer an interpretation of the mechanism behind guidance: each model makes a random error in predicting the posterior mean, and guidance rescales the difference of these errors into a sample with the posterior's covariance, which improves sampling. This view also suggests two new hypotheses that our experiments support: guidance can work with negative scales, and guiding a model with its own checkpoint needs a larger scale than guiding it with an independently trained model.

</details>


### [179] [LiDAR Intensity Reconstruction with Limited Real Data: An Architecture Benchmark under Fixed-Epoch Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34724)
*Christopher M. Coovrey,Aniruddha Bora*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文在固定20轮训练协议下基准测试9种神经网络架构，用于从深度、语义、入射角和材质反射率重建LiDAR强度，并在含0%–100%真实帧的数据混合下评估UNet-V2与DDPM等架构的重建精度、图像质量、推理时间与频谱一致性。UNet-V2在纯真实数据下指标最优，DDPM在1%–10%真实数据混合时表现更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理仿真可补充稀缺的真实LiDAR强度测量，但其有效性依赖学习架构与可用真实数据量，因此需要系统比较不同架构在不同真实数据比例下的表现。

Method: 使用真实SemanticKITTI与合成VoxelScape距离图像数据，以深度、语义标签、入射角和材质反射率为输入，对9种神经架构进行强度重建基准测试；统一采用20轮训练协议（仅控制训练轮次而非跨架构计算量）。评估MAE、MSE、PSNR、SSIM、推理耗时及频谱能量一致性；并对4种选定架构在固定规模、真实帧占比0%、1%、5%、10%、20%、50%、100%的混合数据下评测。

Result: 纯真实数据训练时UNet-V2重建指标最佳（MAE 0.234、MSE 0.140、PSNR 8.64 dB、SSIM 0.647），去噪扩散模型（DDPM）次之；在含1%–10%真实数据的多种混合配置中，DDPM误差更低、图像质量指标优于所评估的确定性模型；频谱分析显示不同频段存在与架构相关的差异。

Conclusion: 研究刻画了所述数据混合与固定轮次协议下的重建权衡，但未建立计算量匹配下的优越性，也未将扩散目标与骨干网络差异相分离。

Abstract: Physics-derived simulation can supplement scarce real-world LiDAR intensity measurements, but its effectiveness depends on the learning architecture and the amount of real training data available. We benchmark nine neural architectures for LiDAR intensity reconstruction from depth, semantic labels, incidence angle, and material reflectance, using real SemanticKITTI and synthetic VoxelScape range-image data. All models follow a fixed 20-epoch protocol; this controls the number of training passes, not the computational cost across architectures. We report mean absolute error (MAE), mean squared error (MSE), peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR), structural similarity (SSIM), inference timing, and spectral-energy agreement. We also evaluate four selected architectures with fixed-size training mixtures containing 0\%, 1\%, 5\%, 10\%, 20\%, 50\%, or 100\% real frames. With real-only training, UNet-V2 achieves the best reported reconstruction metrics (MAE 0.234, MSE 0.140, PSNR 8.64 dB, and SSIM 0.647), followed by a denoising diffusion model (DDPM). In several mixtures containing 1--10\% real data, DDPM achieves lower error and higher image-quality metrics than the evaluated deterministic alternatives, whereas UNet-V2 leads with 100\% real data. Spectral analysis additionally reveals architecture-dependent discrepancies across frequency bands. These findings characterize reconstruction trade-offs under the stated data mixtures and fixed-epoch protocol, without establishing compute-matched superiority or isolating the diffusion objective from backbone differences.

</details>


### [180] [Seeing the Right Context: Grounded On-Policy Distillation for Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34700)
*Yichang Luo,Hourun Zhu,Shixiong Liu,Kan Wei,Shi Liang,Yidan Zhang,Lei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GeoContext-5K数据集与GeOPD方法，通过显式建模遥感场景中的空间上下文关系，以on-policy蒸馏提升MLLM遥感VQA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感场景理解要求MLLM结合邻近目标与周边结构等空间参照来解读被查询目标，但标准答案与边界框往往使这些上下文关系保持隐式，模型难以学到有效的空间推理。

Method: 构建GeoContext-5K（5,394条VQA训练对，含空间线索、视觉事实与支撑区域）。GeOPD在此基础上做on-policy蒸馏：冻结教师模型在带区域标记的全图+视觉事实下提供指导，并保留周边空间参照；优先采纳与学生"证据恢复后下一token分布偏移"相一致的教师纠正；辅以对比目标，按掩码使教师对同一采样token对数概率下降的幅度，对学生两视图得分差加权。

Result: 在五个遥感VQA基准上，GeOPD优于所评估的开源MLLM及同等后训练基线，相较4B与9B基座模型平均分别提升5.81与2.97个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式化的空间上下文标注配合证据驱动的on-policy蒸馏，可有效增强MLLM的遥感空间推理能力，数据集与代码将开源。

Abstract: Remote sensing scene understanding requires multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to interpret queried targets in relation to nearby objects and surrounding structures, which serve as spatial references or define the scope of an answer. However, standard answers and bounding boxes often leave these contextual relationships implicit. Thus, we construct GeoContext-5K to make them explicit in 5,394 training pairs for visual question answering (VQA), with spatial cues, visual facts, and supporting regions. GeOPD uses these annotations for on-policy distillation, guiding a frozen teacher with region marks on the full image and a visual fact while preserving surrounding spatial references. It prioritizes teacher corrections that agree with the shift in the student's next token distribution when evidence is restored. A complementary objective weights the contrast between the student's scores on the two views by how much masking lowers the teacher's log probability for the same sampled token. Experiments on five remote sensing VQA benchmarks show that GeOPD outperforms the evaluated open-source MLLMs and matched post-training baselines, improving over the 4B and 9B base models by 5.81 and 2.97 percentage points on average. The dataset and code will be publicly released at https://github.com/YeeCharm/GeOPD.

</details>


### [181] [Can Visual World Models See Force?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34422)
*Felix Tristram,Benjamin D. Killeen,Nassir Navab*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出“力感知”这一诊断性概念，质疑视觉基础模型表征是否编码交互力，并用轻量读出层在同步视频-力数据上探测冻结的图像、视频、世界模型与策略模型表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型与VLA策略模型被视为机器人操作的基础，但其成功依赖不可观测的交互力这一隐变量；神经生理学表明人类具有对力的视觉先验，因此有必要检验视觉基础模型表征是否也捕获了交互力。

Method: 将力感知定义为三项能力：(1) 判断接触力何时施加、(2) 跟踪交互过程中力的变化、(3) 反映力的大小。在两套操作数据集的同步视频与力测量上，训练轻量读出层探测冻结的图像、视频、世界模型及策略模型表征。

Result: 多数模型能够捕获接触何时发生，但无法估计施加力的大小；机器人后训练（post-training）也未带来改善。

Conclusion: 现有视觉基础模型表征存在重要的力感知缺口，接触检测与力表征能力之间存在显著不对称，揭示了当前模型能力上的关键不足。

Abstract: World models and vision-language-action policy models are increasingly proposed as foundations for robot manipulation. They predict how scenes evolve and which actions to take, yet successful manipulation depends not solely on following observable trajectories but also on the hidden variable of interaction force. Neurophysiology has shown that humans possess a visual prior for this hidden variable, motivating us to ask the question: Do the representations of visual foundation models also capture the forces involved in an interaction? We define force awareness as the ability of representations to (1) indicate when contact force is applied, (2) track how it changes during an interaction, and (3) reflect how large it is. We probe frozen image, video, world- and policy model representations with a lightweight readout trained on synchronized video and force measurements from two manipulation datasets. Our experiments show that most models capture when contact occurs but not how much force is being applied. Surprisingly, robot post-training does not improve this either, highlighting an important gap in current models capabilities.

</details>


### [182] [MoCA4D: Monocular 3D Bounding Box Lifting and Tracking with Camera and Object Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34370)
*Changwoo Jeon,Jongyoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MoCA4D框架，用双分支显式分离相机运动与物体刚性运动，并通过Star Optimization迭代精化深度与朝向，实现单目视频的3D框提升与跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目3D框跟踪方法存在缺陷：逐帧框提升配合时序滤波将物体运动与相机视角变化隐式混合；静态场景融合方法则假设物体静止，无法处理动态目标。

Method: 输入单目视频、关联2D框提示与相机内参。观察分支估计逐帧几何；动态分支预测所有物体共享的相机变换及每个物体独立的刚性变换。提出Star Optimization，利用观察结果与经上述变换传播的历史框精化当前深度与朝向，精化后的框再指导后续传播。

Result: 在Argoverse 2与ADT上，Star Optimization相较对同样观测做Kalman滤波，中心运动误差分别降低38.1%与38.7%；MOVi-F上八次转移运动窗口内取得最低角点误差与旋转误差及最高IoU；真实数据纯相机对照实验表明预测物体运动可改善动态目标轨迹的定位。

Conclusion: 显式解耦相机与物体运动并结合历史框迭代精化，可有效提升单目动态场景下的3D框跟踪精度。

Abstract: Monocular 3D bounding box tracking must account for both object motion and changes in camera viewpoint. Framewise box lifting with temporal filtering leaves these motion components implicit, while static-scene fusion assumes stationary objects. We introduce MoCA4D, a framework for 3D box lifting and tracking from monocular video, associated 2D box prompts, and camera intrinsics. An observation branch estimates per-frame geometry, while a dynamic branch predicts a camera transformation shared across objects and a separate rigid transformation for each object. We propose Star Optimization to refine current depth and orientation using the observation and historical boxes propagated through these transformations. The refined box then guides subsequent propagation. Across all evaluated objects in Argoverse 2 and ADT, Star Optimization reduces center motion error by 38.1% and 38.7%, respectively, relative to Kalman filtering of the same observations. On MOVi-F, the dynamic branch achieves the lowest corner and rotation errors and the highest IoU among the evaluated methods over eight-transition motion windows. Real-data camera-only controls further link predicted object motion to improved localization on dynamic-object tracks.

</details>


### [183] [AIRIS: Benchmarking Social Embodied Intelligence in Egocentric Streaming Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34347)
*Yuchen Zhou,Hongsheng Wu,Jiayu Tang,Yunxuan Mao,Hanjing Ye,Shenghao Zhang,Jing Tang,Chun-Kai Fan,Yufei Huang,Yonggen Ling,He Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出因果流式基准AIRIS，将社交具身智能建模为时间锚定的A-I-R-I链（Attention-Intention-Reaction-Interaction），含8,000个专家问题、357段自我中心视频，揭示45个模型能力碎片化且时间校准失当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准将社交线索识别、干预决策与响应执行割裂评估，或依赖已完成的完整视频，无法定位在线社交决策的具体失败环节。

Method: 构建因果流式基准AIRIS：每次查询仅提供该时刻之前可得的观测前缀，采用决策感知指标，奖励校准的等待与完整反应、惩罚过早承诺；覆盖11类情境、36个能力维度、8,000个专家标注问题。

Result: 评估45个专有与开源模型（通用、具身、流式视频、全模态）显示能力碎片化与时间失校准。通用VLM擅长被动线索定位与意图识别，具身专用模型在孤立反应子决策上领先；但所有模型族常在证据不足时过早猜测意图，且难以在同一回合内联合对齐干预时机、动作域与具体执行。

Conclusion: AIRIS为弥合被动社交感知与可靠具身交互之间的差距提供诊断基础。

Abstract: Embodied agents operating around people must not only recognize social cues, but also decide when the available evidence is sufficient, whether to intervene, and how to respond without violating social norms. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate these abilities in isolation or with access to completed videos, obscuring where an online social decision fails. We introduce AIRIS, a causal streaming benchmark that formulates social embodied intelligence as a time-anchored chain of Attention, Intention, Reaction, and Interaction (A-I-R-I). AIRIS contains 8,000 expert-curated questions grounded in 357 egocentric clips across 11 context categories and 36 capability dimensions. At each query, models receive only the observation prefix available at that time; decision-aware metrics reward calibrated waiting and complete reactions while penalizing premature commitment. Evaluating 45 proprietary and open-source general-purpose, embodied, streaming-video, and omni-modal models reveals fragmented and temporally miscalibrated competence. General-purpose VLMs excel at passive cue grounding and intent recognition, whereas embodied specialists lead on isolated reaction sub-decisions; yet across all families, models frequently guess intents before sufficient evidence emerges and fail to jointly align intervention timing, action domain, and concrete execution within the same episode. AIRIS thus provides a diagnostic foundation for bridging the gap between passive social perception and reliable embodied interaction.

</details>


### [184] [Semantic State Reading for Joint Image-Semantic Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34279)
*Guanxi Lai,Zixuan Li,Zhiwei Li,Qi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SSR（Semantic State Reading）在扩散Transformer中联合去噪图像latent、编码图像级上下文的全局CLS token和保持空间对应的小局部patch状态，并以不同读取路径注入，显著加速训练并取得更优FID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉表征可改善扩散Transformer训练，但如何同时利用全局上下文与局部空间结构，需要决定建模哪些语义状态以及如何访问它们。

Method: 联合去噪图像latent、全局CLS token与紧凑patch状态；局部状态在输入端与对应图像token融合，全局状态经独立投影注入每个transformer块，使两类语义在去噪全过程可访问；并通过消融与语义轨迹干预分析各部分贡献。

Result: ImageNet 256×256上，SiT-XL/2+SSR以分别少100×、40×、2×的训练迭代达到或超过SiT、REPA、REG的参考FID；2M迭代无引导FID 1.78，仅用CFG达1.34；CFG+autoguidance在1M迭代达FID 1.31，为主对比表中最佳。

Conclusion: 局部状态建模与重复全局读取具有互补收益，语义轨迹可改变固定类别标签下的图像布局；SSR在训练效率与生成质量上均优于若干强基线。

Abstract: Pretrained visual representations improve diffusion-transformer training, yet exploiting both global context and local spatial structure requires deciding which semantic states to model and how to access them. We introduce Semantic State Reading (SSR), which jointly denoises image latents, a global CLS token encoding image-level context, and a compact patch state preserving spatial correspondence. SSR couples this global-local modeling with distinct reading paths: local states are fused with corresponding image tokens at the input, while independent projections inject the current global state into every transformer block. Both semantic scopes thus remain accessible throughout denoising. Ablations show complementary benefits from local-state modeling and repeated global reading, and semantic-trajectory interventions alter image layout under a fixed class label. On ImageNet $256 \times 256$, SiT-XL/2 with SSR reaches or surpasses the reference FIDs of SiT, REPA, and Representation Entanglement for Generation (REG) with $100\times$, $40\times$, and $2\times$ fewer training iterations, respectively. SSR achieves unguided FID 1.78 at 2M iterations and FID 1.34 with classifier-free guidance alone. Combining classifier-free guidance with autoguidance yields FID 1.31 at 1M iterations, achieving the best FID in our main comparison table.

</details>


### [185] [Detector Operating Behavior Inference via Data-Space Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34252)
*Stefan Becker,Simon Weiss,Wolfgang Hübner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DOBI任务：从单张无标签图像的内部证据推断固定检测器阈值相关的分布级运行行为（PR曲线），并用轻量monitor实现，在受控基准上显著优于图像无关先验，可支撑多种工作点选择策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测器在给定置信阈值下的召回与误报依赖输入分布，因此固定的标定/阈值在实际数据条件下未必可靠；作者希望仅凭一张无标签图像就预测检测器在该数据条件下的分布级阈值响应，从而支持动态的工作点选择。

Method: 形式化"检测器运行行为推断"（DOBI）二阶段任务，将阈值响应（各阈值下的recall、FP、precision）视为当前数据条件下的潜在行为状态；离线通过受控数据空间采样探测该状态空间，再训练轻量monitor学习"单图→行为状态"的逆映射，其单一功能输出可派生多种运行策略。实验用固定YOLOv10-Nano行人检测器与COCO派生的退化数据做受控概念验证，并设置连续/组合探测、模板查找、区域监督查找等对照与oracle基线。

Result: 预测PR行为的平均绝对偏差由图像无关均值先验的0.128降至0.054；预测功能距离与经验区域距离的Spearman相关为0.96。单张无标签图像推断的工作点选择效果与已知真实区域的不可部署查找相当（macro-regime F1均为0.6604，原始标定为0.6407，oracle 0.6620）。在未见组合上，功能解码器的平均曲线误差低于模板查找，且随偏离训练条件程度增加优势扩大；但查找在插值情形更准确。

Conclusion: 在受控探测条件下，单张无标签图像推断分布级检测器行为是可行的；功能化推断在分布外组合上具优势，而基于区域的查找在插值与离散基准上仍是强基线，界定了该方法适用边界。

Abstract: A detector scores candidate detections, but the recall and false alarms at a given confidence threshold depend on the input distribution. We introduce Detector Operating Behavior Inference (DOBI), a second-level inference task: estimating a fixed detector's threshold-dependent, distribution-level operating response from the internal evidence of a single unlabeled image. DOBI treats the detector's threshold response, i.e. recall, false positives, and precision across thresholds, as a latent behavioral state under the current data condition. Controlled data-space sampling probes this state space offline, and a lightweight monitor learns the inverse map from one image to this state, whose single functional output supports multiple operating policies. In a controlled proof of concept with a fixed YOLOv10-Nano person detector and COCO-derived degradations, predicted PR behavior reduces mean absolute deviation from 0.128 for an image-independent mean-behavior prior to 0.054, and predicted functional distances track empirical regime distances (Spearman 0.96). From one unlabeled image, the inferred behavior selects operating points as well as a non-deployable lookup that knows the true regime (macro-regime $F_1$ 0.6604 for both, versus 0.6407 under pristine calibration; oracle 0.6620). On this discrete benchmark, regime-supervised curve lookups are strong controls. Pre-specified continuous and compositional probing shows where functional inference matters: on unseen combinations, the functional decoder has a lower mean curve error than a template lookup, increasingly with distance from the training conditions, whereas the lookup is more accurate for interpolation. These results establish the feasibility of single-image inference of distribution-level detector behavior over the probed conditions. Source code is available at https://github.com/beckerio/dobi

</details>


### [186] [Analemma $\infty$: A Temporal Benchmark for Geospatial Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34240)
*Alex López-Cifuentes,Laura Martínez-Ferrer,Juan Ignacio Bravo Pérez-Villar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Analemma——一个包含六个数据集的时间序列遥感基准，为像素级时序基础模型提供完整生长季的 Sentinel-2 全部 10/20 m 波段观测，并配套冻结编码器评测协议。结果显示 TESSERA 模型总体排名第一，但时间输入本身解释了大部分性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开基准缺乏时间轴，或仅将时间下采样至少数几个日期，无法真实评估像素级时序基础模型读取整年卫星观测的能力；同时原始数据集发布常缺失几何信息、日期或云掩膜。

Method: 构建 Analemma 基准：六个数据集，包含全部 10 m 与 20 m Sentinel-2 波段，时间序列跨越完整生长季，并补全原文缺失的几何、日期与云掩膜。采用冻结编码器协议，在不同标签比例与三个随机种子下评测所有模型，并设置两个无预训练基线。

Result: TESSERA 模型总体排名第一。基于月度合成数据的从零训练模型除 OlmoEarth v1.3-Base 外优于所有基础模型，说明时间输入解释大部分性能。源文献未说明的三项评测选择各自使 TESSERA v1 的 mIoU 变化 7.6–18.6，而前二名差距仅 0.6–2.8。

Conclusion: 时间轴输入是时序基础模型性能的主导因素，且评测协议细节对结论影响远大于模型间差距，凸显标准化时间基准与评测规范的必要性。数据、评测框架与排行榜将公开。

Abstract: Satellite time series capture how land surfaces change over time. Pixel-wise temporal foundation models read a year of satellite observations per pixel, yet public benchmarks lack a temporal axis or subsample it to a few dates. We introduce Analemma, a temporal benchmark of six datasets that carry every 10 and 20 m Sentinel-2 band and a time series spanning a growing season, completed where their releases omit geometry, dates, or cloud masks. One frozen-encoder protocol evaluates all models across label ratios and three seeds, with two baselines without pretraining. The TESSERA model ranks first overall. A from-scratch model on monthly composites outranks every foundation model except OlmoEarth v1.3-Base, suggesting that temporal input explains most of the performance. Three evaluation choices left unstated by the source publications each shift TESSERA v1 by 7.6 to 18.6 mIoU, against top-two gaps of 0.6 to 2.8. Data, harness and leaderboard will be publicly released soon.

</details>


### [187] [Variation-aware Temporal Correspondence for Self-Supervised Video Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34220)
*Yang Liu,Qianqian Xu,Peisong Wen,Siran Dai,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VT-CoRe框架，通过三明治采样与Patch配准模块建立可靠的时序对应，并结合自蒸馏语义对齐与时间变化建模，实现兼顾语义一致性与时序变化的视频自监督表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MVM方法将MIM扩展到视频，但视频存在固有时间变化（目标位置、外观、运动状态持续改变），因此MVM在恢复掩码内容时需在捕捉区域时序变化的同时保持语义一致性，由此引出两大挑战：1）如何建立跨帧可靠的时序对应；2）如何构建兼顾语义一致性与时序变化的表示学习目标。

Method: 针对挑战1，引入sandwich sampling策略构造双侧时序参考，并设计Patch Registration Module (PRM)建立patch级可靠对应；针对挑战2，通过自蒸馏架构对配准后的表示施加隐空间语义对齐，并提出temporal variation modeling约束表示随时间的轨迹。

Result: 在16个benchmark的8项下游任务上验证有效性，覆盖像素级、帧级、视频级及视觉机器人学习场景；补充表示分析表明VT-CoRe学习到更具结构化的隐空间。

Conclusion: VT-CoRe通过变化感知的时序对应学习，统一建模语义一致性与时序变化，在多种下游场景中展现出有效性与适用性。

Abstract: Self-supervised video representation learning has achieved remarkable progress by leveraging large-scale unlabeled video data. Recent methods commonly extend Masked Image Modeling (MIM) to Masked Video Modeling (MVM), where video representations are learned by capturing semantic information from temporal contexts during masked content recovery. Unlike static images, videos exhibit inherent temporal variation, as visual objects continuously change their positions, appearances, and motion states across frames. Therefore, MVM needs to preserve semantic consistency while capturing temporal changes of corresponding regions, which raises two central challenges: 1) how to establish reliable temporal correspondence across frames, and 2) how to formulate an appropriate representation learning objective for modeling both semantic consistency and temporal variation. To address these challenges, we propose Variation-aware Temporal Correspondence for video Representation learning (VT-CoRe) framework. For challenge 1), VT-CoRe introduces a sandwich sampling strategy to construct two-sided temporal references and a Patch Registration Module (PRM) to establish reliable patch-level correspondence. For challenge 2), VT-CoRe imposes latent-space semantic alignment on registered representations through a self-distillation architecture and further proposes temporal variation modeling to constrain representation trajectories over time. Experiments on 8 downstream tasks across 16 benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness and applicability of VT-CoRe across pixel-level, frame-level, video-level, and vision-based robotic learning scenarios. Complementary representation analysis shows that VT-CoRe learns a more structured latent space for variation-aware temporal correspondence learning.

</details>


### [188] [Loop4D: Embodied World Modeling with 4D VAEs and Looped Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34199)
*Gen Li,Fan Luo,Mengjun Lin,Shu Han,Bohan Li,Hao Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Loop4D通过保运动的4D表示与循环生成式细化，联合预测机器人操作中的RGB外观与3D几何演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测机器人操作结果需同时建模视觉外观与演化中的3D几何，但存在两大挑战：视频压缩会模糊细粒度机器人运动；联合预测RGB与几何需要足够的生成容量。

Method: 提出4D VAE，将RGB与点图序列编码为紧凑时空隐变量；RGB VAE结合几何引导监督与预测性重建以减少压缩伪影；几何VAE用轻量适配器适配预训练RGB表示并保留其先验。在其上构建双流循环扩散Transformer，外观流与几何流分离、共享Transformer权重并具跨模态连接，通过跨循环复用Transformer块增加有效深度而不复制主干参数。

Result: 实验实现高质量4D生成，并在仿真机器人操作任务中验证了Loop4D的有效性。

Conclusion: Loop4D以保运动4D表示结合循环共享参数的扩散Transformer，使较少参数模型通过增加计算量提升RGB-几何联合预测性能。

Abstract: Predicting the outcomes of robotic manipulation requires modeling both visual appearance and evolving 3D geometry. This poses two challenges: video compression can obscure fine-grained robot motion, while jointly predicting RGB and geometry demands sufficient generative capacity. We introduce Loop4D, a framework that addresses these challenges through motion-preserving 4D representations and recurrent generative refinement. Our 4D VAE encodes RGB and pointmap sequences into compact spatiotemporal latents while preserving motion details and geometric structure. Our RGB VAE combines geometry-guided supervision with predictive reconstruction to reduce compression-induced motion artifacts. Our geometry VAE uses lightweight adapters to adapt pretrained RGB representations while preserving their priors. Building on these representations, our Dual-Stream Looped Diffusion Transformer maintains separate appearance and geometry streams with shared Transformer weights and cross-modal connections. By reusing Transformer blocks across loops, we increase effective depth without replicating backbone parameters, enabling models with fewer parameters to improve joint RGB-geometry prediction through additional computation. Experiments demonstrate high-quality 4D generation and validate Loop4D in simulated robotic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [189] [HSTTA: Hierarchical Selective Test-Time Adaptation for Label-Free Cross-Subject EEG-to-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34153)
*Hongzhou Chen,Yuhao Wei,Lianghua He,Yihang Liu,Zengqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出分层选择性测试时适应框架HSTTA，为每个EEG查询按其预测置信度分配匹配的监督粒度，并结合跨会话一致性校准与互惠分层回退，显著缓解跨被试EEG到图像检索中的主体偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨被试EEG到图像检索在遇到新被试时因主体偏移（subject shift）导致性能大幅下降。测试时适应（TTA）可用无标签的目标被试EEG微调预训练模型，但现有TTA方法难以让监督粒度与每个查询的预测置信度相匹配，导致大量查询无法获得可靠伪标签。

Method: 1) 在gallery图像嵌入上构建层次结构，采用分层选择性预测为每个EEG查询分配与其当前预测置信度匹配的监督粒度，并据此构造适应目标；2) 提出跨会话一致性校准（CSCC），一种无监督方法，用于校准全局置信度阈值，使分层预测达到预设的准确率水平；3) 提出互惠分层回退（RHB），将缺乏反向匹配支持的查询移至更粗粒度节点，以缓解错误强化。

Result: 在两个公开基准及消融实验中验证了HSTTA及关键组件的有效性。在THINGS-EEG2上，余弦排序（CSLS排序）下Top-1/Top-5准确率分别比最强EEG专用TTA基线提升17.9（13.4）与8.9（3.7）个百分点。进一步分析表明，HSTTA在适应过程中产生的伪标签更准确，从而支持更可靠的模型更新。

Conclusion: 按置信度自适应调整监督粒度的分层选择性TTA，配合阈值校准与反向匹配回退机制，可有效提升跨被试EEG到图像检索的测试时适应性能与伪标签可靠性。

Abstract: Cross-subject EEG-to-image retrieval suffers from substantial performance degradation on new subjects due to subject shift. Test-time adaptation (TTA) can mitigate this shift by adapting pretrained models with unlabeled target-subject EEG. However, most existing TTA methods struggle to match supervision granularity to each query's prediction confidence, leaving many queries without reliable pseudo-labels. To address this, we propose a novel framework named Hierarchical Selective Test-Time Adaptation (HSTTA). HSTTA builds a hierarchy over gallery image embeddings, uses hierarchical selective prediction to assign each EEG query a supervision granularity matching its current prediction confidence, and constructs adaptation objectives accordingly. We further propose Cross-Session Consistency Calibration (CSCC), an unsupervised method that calibrates the global confidence threshold to target a predefined accuracy level for hierarchical predictions. Moreover, we introduce Reciprocal Hierarchical Backoff (RHB), which moves queries lacking reverse-matching support to coarser nodes to mitigate error reinforcement. Experiments on two public benchmarks and ablation studies validate the effectiveness of HSTTA and its key components. On THINGS-EEG2, HSTTA outperforms the strongest EEG-specific TTA baseline by 17.9 (13.4) and 8.9 (3.7) percentage points in Top-1/Top-5 accuracy, respectively, under cosine (CSLS) ranking. Further analyses show that HSTTA yields more accurate pseudo-labels during adaptation, supporting more reliable model updates.

</details>


### [190] [When Position Favors the Wrong Part: Evaluating Left-Right Discrimination in Semantic Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34100)
*Pingan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出用"位置冲突"条件来暴露被总体判别准确率掩盖的失败：在SPair-71k上以边界框侧向分数定义"一致/冲突"条件，将稠密描述子按水平反射分解为偶/奇分量，检验冲突下的部位判别能力，并用奇分量分数指导坐标选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 总体部位判别准确率可能掩盖严重失败——当图像位置（如边界框侧向）偏向错误的双侧部位时，描述子是否仍能区分正确部位不得而知。同时，判别分数能否真正用于匹配坐标选择也缺乏严格检验。

Method: 在同一目标图像与标注部位对上，用边界框侧向分数定义"位置一致"与"位置冲突"两种条件，度量位置一致性与冲突；在水平反射下将描述子分解为偶分量与奇分量并比较；固定骨干与适配器，考察特征上采样；基于奇分量分数测试候选选择规则；并分别报告纠正数与引入错误数。

Result: 冻结DINOv2的奇分量判别准确率从一致条件86.66%骤降至冲突条件19.63%，而MARCO为95.27%→86.16%。固定MARCO输入几何时，框侧冲突下奇准确率在原网格为34.12%，经学习上采样并池化回原网格后为88.84%。奇分数规则在DINOv2与DINOv3上整体优于原始硬峰值匹配器，在MARCO的冲突查询上亦然。固定MARCO软解码器时，对余弦峰值重排序使每对平均PCK提升+0.47 pp。对监督式GeoAware-SC，用奇分数选择定位窗口中心仅净增15个正确匹配（330次纠正、315次引入错误）。

Conclusion: 在位置冲突下评估判别力可揭示总体准确率所掩盖的失败；分别报告纠正数与引入错误数，才能判断判别分数能否产生有效的匹配决策。

Abstract: Overall part-discrimination accuracy can conceal failures when image position favors the wrong bilateral part. We examine whether dense descriptors distinguish the correct part under this conflict and whether their discrimination scores help select matching coordinates. We evaluate position agreement and conflict within the same target images and annotated part pairs, using a bounding-box-side score to define the conditions. We compare the original descriptors with their even and odd components under horizontal reflection, examine feature upsampling with the backbone and adapters fixed, and test candidate-selection rules based on odd scores. On SPair-71k, odd discrimination accuracy falls from 86.66% under agreement to 19.63% under conflict for frozen DINOv2, compared with 95.27% to 86.16% for MARCO. With MARCO's input geometry fixed, odd accuracy in box-side conflict was 34.12% on the native grid and 88.84% after learned upsampling and pooling back to that grid. Odd-score rules improve on the original hard-peak matchers overall for DINOv2 and DINOv3 and on conflict queries for MARCO. With MARCO's soft decoder fixed, reranking the cosine peaks changes mean per-pair PCK by +0.47 pp. For supervised GeoAware-SC, using the odd score to select the localization window center yields only 15 net additional correct matches, with 330 corrections and 315 introduced errors. Evaluating discrimination under position conflict reveals failures hidden by aggregate accuracy. Reporting corrections and introduced errors separately also shows whether a discriminative score produces useful matching decisions.

</details>


### [191] [FoMNet: Adaptive Kernels Meet Contextual Matching for Cross-Modal Homography Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34096)
*Zhuoran Yang,Zhenyu Yi,Runmin Zhang,Linshan Li,Yiming Li,Xiaokai Bai,Xiaohan Zhang,Hui-liang Shen,Si-Yuan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FoMNet，将视觉基础模型(VFM)特征的学习式空间自适应与上下文对应估计结合，用于跨模态单应性估计，在五个跨模态数据集上取得最低平均角点误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨模态单应性估计在感知模态外观差异下仍需可靠对应。多尺度细化虽能逐步缩小搜索范围，但精度受限于匹配特征区分近邻候选的能力；VFM提供互补表征，然而通过固定插值融合会使空间聚合与图像内容及对应目标无关。

Method: FoMNet包含两个模块：(1) Patch Feature Upsampling (PFU) 逐步预测位置特异的卷积核，在上采样网格上聚合邻域特征，为VFM表征与CNN特征在不同匹配尺度上的融合做准备；(2) Contextual Correlation Refinement (CCR) 联合处理源描述子与局部相关性及空间上下文，在每次单应性更新前细化候选得分。为降低上采样开销，FoMNet-D用双三次插值替代PFU，并以多尺度特征关系蒸馏恢复精度。

Result: 在五个跨模态数据集上，FoMNet在所评估方法中取得最低MACE，相对MCNet降低10.6%–72.7%；FoMNet-D保持有竞争力的精度，同时相对FoMNet将推理延迟降低34.0%。

Conclusion: 内容感知的VFM特征空间自适应与上下文相关性细化可有效提升跨模态单应性估计精度；通过插值替代加蒸馏的效率版本可大幅降低延迟而精度损失有限。

Abstract: Cross-modal homography estimation requires reliable correspondences despite appearance differences between sensing modalities. Multi-scale refinement progressively reduces the search range, but its accuracy remains limited by the ability of matching features to distinguish nearby candidates. The vision foundation model (VFM) provides complementary representations, yet integrating these features through fixed interpolation leaves their spatial aggregation independent of image content and the correspondence objective. We propose the Foundation Model-Enhanced Matching Network (FoMNet), which combines learned spatial adaptation of VFM features with contextual correspondence estimation. Patch Feature Upsampling (PFU) progressively predicts location-specific kernels to aggregate neighboring features on upsampled grids, preparing VFM representations for fusion with CNN features at different matching scales. Contextual Correlation Refinement (CCR) then jointly processes source descriptors and local correlations with spatial context to refine candidate scores before each homography update. To reduce upsampling overhead, we develop FoMNet-D, which replaces PFU with bicubic interpolation and uses multi-scale feature-relation distillation to recover accuracy. Experiments on five cross-modal datasets show that FoMNet achieves the lowest mean average corner error (MACE) among the evaluated methods, reducing MACE by 10.6%-72.7% relative to MCNet. FoMNet-D retains competitive accuracy while reducing inference latency by 34.0% relative to FoMNet.

</details>


### [192] [Projector Subspace Optimization for Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34078)
*Hao Yu,Quan Cheng,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PROUD仅对视觉-语言投影器最后一层做受漂移预算约束的度量正交子空间更新，零推理/路由开销地在UCIT与CoIN上取得更高持续准确率并用极少参数抑制遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免回放的多模态持续指令微调方法在多层级解码器上扩展prompt或路由式LoRA专家，参数与路由开销大且会改变内部特征分布；作者识别出视觉-语言投影器的最终线性层是高容量、坐标稳定的"桥梁"，顺序更新易通过置换历史视觉token造成接口漂移。同时低秩子空间适配中，仅依赖历史统计或任务梯度选择方向会牺牲可塑性或稳定性，无约束多步更新仍累积大漂移。

Method: 提出PROUD（Projector Refinement through Optimized Update Directions）持续子空间优化框架，仅作用于投影器终端层。任务开始时，结合保留的输入统计与初始梯度，解析地提取度量正交子空间，在漂移预算下最大化一阶任务增益；训练中通过精确Frobenius惩罚将该子空间内的更新正则化，并在训练后无损合并，推理与路由零额外开销。

Result: 在UCIT与CoIN数据集、多个MLLM骨干上，PROUD在持续准确率上优于近期免回放基线，同时以极少可训练参数抑制遗忘。

Conclusion: 将持续学习聚焦于投影器终端线性层并结合漂移预算约束的子空间优化，可在零推理开销下有效平衡稳定性与可塑性，是一种轻量高效的MCIT方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance through instruction tuning, yet adapting them to sequential tasks requires Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning (MCIT). Existing replay-free methods expand prompts or routed LoRA experts across multi-layer decoders, incurring heavy parameter and routing overhead while shifting internal feature distributions. Instead, we identify the final linear layer of the vision-language projector as a high-capacity, coordinate-stationary bridge, where sequential updates easily induce interface drift by displacing historical visual tokens. While low-rank subspace adaptation offers a compact remedy, selecting directions solely from historical statistics or task gradients sacrifices plasticity or stability, and unconstrained multi-step updates still accumulate large drift. To resolve both issues, we propose Projector Refinement through Optimized Update Directions (PROUD), a continual subspace optimization framework for the terminal projector layer. At task onset, PROUD combines retained input statistics with initial gradients to analytically extract a metric-orthonormal subspace maximizing first-order task gain under a drift budget. During training, it regularizes updates within this fixed subspace via an exact Frobenius penalty before losslessly merging them with zero inference or routing overhead. Experiments on UCIT and CoIN across MLLM backbones show that PROUD outperforms recent replay-free baselines in continual accuracy while curbing forgetting with a fraction of trainable parameters.

</details>


### [193] [SPARC: Geometry-Guided Attention Pruning for Long-Horizon Camera-Controlled Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34064)
*Zesong Yang,Weikai Chen,Liyuan Cui,Yuan Li,Jiaxin Huang,Lutao Jiang,Runze Zhang,Yingda Yin,Xiaoyang Huang,Kai Yan,Keyang Luo,Xin Wang,Hujun Bao,Zhaopeng Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPARC 提出一种几何引导的注意力剪枝框架，将预训练视频扩散Transformer的原生自注意力同时作为相机控制的几何条件与持久场景记忆的空间寻址机制，在无需额外可学习控制/记忆模块的前提下实现精确相机跟随与场景重访，注意力计算大幅减少，结合四步蒸馏推理加速超过50倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在视频扩散Transformer中实现相机控制与长时场景记忆通常需引入额外可学习的控制或记忆模块；作者观察到注意力连通性本身即可同时承载几何条件与历史记忆检索功能，因此希望挖掘原生注意力的双重用途，并利用重建已观测内容与合成新区域对上下文需求的不对称性来降低注意力开销。

Method: 提出SPARC几何引导注意力剪枝框架：对已观测token，仅限制其关注几何对应的历史token与局部时空上下文；对未观测区域则保留稠密注意力。配合对应感知的注意力偏置（correspondence-aware attention biasing）与顺序无关的历史定位（order-invariant historical positioning），实现跨长时间间隔的稳定检索；不引入额外可学习控制或记忆模块。

Result: 大量实验表明激进的注意力剪枝有效提升相机跟随精度、重访一致性与视觉保真度；结合四步蒸馏后推理加速超过50倍。

Conclusion: 注意力连通性可作为统一的几何条件与空间记忆寻址机制；基于不对称上下文需求的注意力剪枝在保持甚至提升生成质量的同时显著降低计算，为视频扩散Transformer的相机控制与场景记忆提供了一种无需额外模块的高效方案。

Abstract: We present SPARC, a geometry-guided attention pruning framework that turns the native self-attention of pretrained video diffusion transformers into a unified mechanism for camera control and persistent scene memory. Our key insight is that attention connectivity can simultaneously serve as geometric conditioning for camera control and spatial addressing for historical memory retrieval. Motivated by the asymmetric contextual requirements of reconstructing observed content and synthesizing novel regions, SPARC restricts observed tokens to geometrically corresponding historical tokens and local spatiotemporal context, while preserving dense attention for unobserved regions. Correspondence-aware attention biasing and order-invariant historical positioning further enable stable retrieval across long temporal gaps. Without introducing additional learnable control or memory modules, SPARC achieves precise camera adherence and faithful scene revisitation while substantially reducing attention computation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of aggressive attention pruning in improving camera adherence, revisit consistency, and visual fidelity, with an inference speedup of over 50x when combined with four-step distillation. Project page is available at https://zju3dv.github.io/SPARC.

</details>


### [194] [One Patch, Many Policies: Object-Associated Physical Patch Attacks on Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34360)
*Yiqian Liu,Yuqi Lin,Hui Lu,Jiaming He,Qi Zhu,Deyi Ji,Bing Li,Kaizheng Wang,Zhiyuan Zha,Yi Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出物体关联补丁攻击（OAPA）框架，一种通用且可迁移的物理补丁攻击方法，攻击者仅通过改变补丁放置位置即可选择破坏哪个任务物体的操作，无需重新优化补丁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型与世界动作模型（WAMs）易受对抗性视觉干扰，且现有研究缺乏通用、可迁移的物理补丁攻击手段，尤其缺乏通过放置位置控制攻击目标的能力。

Method: OAPA 由三部分组成：物体关联放置策略、可微物理合成（建模多相机视角下的透视与遮挡）、以及最终动作专家表征散度引导（采用相对 RMS 或余弦距离作为优化目标）。

Result: 实验表明攻击在不同任务套件与策略间具有强迁移性，并能通过补丁放置选择性地破坏攻击者指定任务物体的操作。

Conclusion: OAPA 实现了通用、可迁移且目标可选的物理补丁攻击，揭示了 VLA/WAM 系统在物理世界中的对抗脆弱性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) and World Action Models (WAMs) are vulnerable to adversarial visual interference, motivating the study of universal and transferable physical patch attacks. We propose Object-Associated Patch Attack (OAPA), a universal and transferable physical patch framework that enables an attacker to select which task object's manipulation to disrupt through patch placement, without re-optimizing the patch. Our framework combines object-associated placement, differentiable physical compositing, and final action-expert representation divergence. Physical compositing accounts for perspective and occlusion across camera views, while representation divergence guides patch optimization using either relative RMS or cosine distance. Experimental results demonstrate strong attack transferability across task suites and policies, together with the ability to selectively disrupt manipulation of an attacker-chosen task object through patch placement.

</details>


### [195] [SPARC: Semantic Calibration with Latent Multimodal Knowledge for Robust Image-Text Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34344)
*Zhongkuan Mao,Wenzhuo Zhao,Haidong Chen,Shangze Li,Yidong Wang,Jinhe Bi,Yunpu Ma,Junfeng Fang,Fei Shen,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SPARC方法，通过从冻结编码器中挖掘潜在多模态知识，提升不完整查询下的候选区分度与排序稳定性，实现鲁棒的图文检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒图文检索方法主要针对分布偏移进行适配，未能充分解决不完整查询下的候选区分问题与排序不稳定问题。

Method: 包含三个模块：(1) 基于球面乘积空间的语义校准模块，通过切空间更新精炼不完整视觉与文本特征，并保持余弦相似度所需的单位球结构；(2) 鲁棒语义引导模块，结合完整图文特征中的潜在多模态知识与跨模态语义锚点，促进校准特征对齐；(3) 匹配关系约束模块，融合原始与校准特征，施加软排序约束以惩罚初始正匹配边距的缩减，限制排序扰动。

Result: 在Flickr30K与COCO上，于多种图像遮挡和单词删除设置下均优于对比方法，并在不同预训练骨干网络上取得一致增益。

Conclusion: 利用冻结编码器的潜在多模态知识并结合球面校准与排序约束，可有效提升不完整查询下的图文检索鲁棒性，且具备跨骨干泛化性。

Abstract: Robust image-text retrieval aims to maintain accurate cross-modal matching when image or text queries are incomplete, supporting reliable information access in real-world applications. However, existing methods typically focus on adapting to distribution shifts, without adequately addressing candidate discrimination for incomplete queries and ranking instability. To this end, we propose semantic projection and alignment with relational constraints (SPARC), which elicits latent multimodal knowledge from frozen encoders to improve candidate discrimination and ranking stability. Specifically, we first devise a semantic calibration module on a product of spheres that refines incomplete visual and textual features through tangent-space updates, maintaining the unit-sphere structure used for cosine similarity. Second, we introduce a robust semantic guidance module that combines latent multimodal knowledge encoded in complete image-text features with cross-modal semantic anchors to promote semantic alignment of the calibrated features. Finally, we introduce a matching relation constraint module that fuses original query features with their calibrated counterparts and applies a soft ranking constraint to penalize reductions in initially positive matching margins, thereby limiting ranking perturbations. Extensive experiments on Flickr30K and COCO demonstrate that SPARC outperforms competing methods under various image occlusion and word deletion settings, while achieving consistent gains across different pretrained backbones.

</details>


### [196] [PRAM: Activating Latent Multimodal Knowledge through Partwise Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34640)
*Haidong Chen,Ying Guo,Shangze Li,Zhongkuan Mao,Linrui Xu,Canran Xiao,Fei Shen,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PRAM（部件级跨模态推理），通过共享枢轴模态组合部件级统计关系，激活冻结编码器中的潜在多模态知识，实现跨模态分类与检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有涌现式跨模态分类与检索方法偏重样本级对齐，忽略了局部判别信息与部件间统计关系。

Method: PRAM包含三个模块：(1) 多粒度协方差正则化：在两族模态-枢轴对的全局与局部向量上估计协方差，通过与跨部件均值自适应混合稳定局部估计；(2) 基于枢轴的谱关系推理：通过正则化协方差合成推断目标模态间的全局与部件级统计关系，并借助白化与谱分解提取配对投影方向及关联强度；(3) 多粒度得分融合：标准化并融合全局与局部关系的匹配分数，引入枢轴关联与类语义等任务特定信息用于类别预测与候选排序。

Result: 在十个数据集上，PRAM在16种分类设置与19种检索设置中达到最先进性能，并在三种传感场景中表现极具竞争力。

Conclusion: 以枢轴模态为介导的部件级统计关系推理可有效利用冻结编码器中的多模态知识，在多粒度层面提升跨模态分类与检索性能。

Abstract: Emergent cross-modal classification and retrieval enable transfer between modalities without direct paired supervision. However, existing methods often prioritize sample-level alignment, overlooking local discriminative information and partwise statistical relations. To this end, we propose partwise reasoning across modalities (PRAM), which activates latent multimodal knowledge in frozen encoders by composing partwise statistical relations through a shared pivot modality. Specifically, we first present a multi-granularity covariance regularization module that estimates covariances from global and local vectors in two collections of modality-pivot pairs, stabilizing local estimates through adaptive blending with their cross-part means. Second, we devise a pivot-based spectral relational reasoning module that infers global and partwise statistical relations between target modalities through regularized covariance composition and extracts paired projection directions and their correlation strengths via whitening and spectral decomposition. Finally, we introduce a multi-granularity score fusion module that standardizes and fuses matching scores from global and local relations, incorporating task-specific information on pivot associations and class semantics for class prediction and candidate ranking. Extensive experiments across ten datasets demonstrate that PRAM achieves state-of-the-art performance in 16 classification settings and 19 retrieval settings, while exhibiting highly competitive performance in three sensing scenarios.

</details>


### [197] [Beyond Pose Control: Anatomically Consistent Ultrasound Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34622)
*Yue Zhou,Fangxu Han,Tony Danjun Wang,Nassir Navab,Yuan Bi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出为超声视频生成器引入"解剖先验+3D潜记忆"两类知识，替代单纯位姿控制，实现未扫描区域的合理生成与重访切片的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机可控视频生成依赖大规模数据隐式学到的先验，但超声场景恰好相反：解剖提供显式先验，而带位姿追踪的扫描数据极少，无法隐式学习。因此仅靠位姿条件会导致未扫描区域解剖不合理、重访时解剖不一致。

Method: 赋予生成器类似超声医师心智模型的两种知识：(1) 基于人群受试者构建的解剖先验，指导未扫描区域生成，并由每个生成的chunk在线细化以逼近当前受试者；(2) 依据超声断面物理设计的3D潜记忆，将生成的切片累积为患者体数据并按新位姿重切片。细粒度记忆在输入端注入生成器，粗粒度解剖先验在Transformer各层间指导布局。

Result: 在甲状腺、肾脏和腰椎数据上，相较位姿条件基线与记忆增强基线，视觉保真度、位姿跟随性与解剖一致性均有提升。

Conclusion: 在超声视频生成中，显式解剖先验与3D潜记忆应取代单纯的位姿条件，作为实现可控且解剖一致生成的核心机制。

Abstract: Camera-controlled video generation anticipates how a scene appears from new viewpoints by relying on a prior learned implicitly from large-scale data. Ultrasound poses the same problem, with the probe pose as the viewpoint, but under opposite conditions: anatomy offers an explicit prior that every sonographer relies on, whereas tracked scans are too scarce to learn it implicitly. As a result, pose control alone is not enough, and pose-conditioned ultrasound generators produce implausible anatomy in unscanned regions and inconsistent anatomy on revisits. We go beyond pose control and give the generator the two kinds of knowledge that make up a sonographer's mental model. An anatomical prior, built from a population of subjects, guides generation where nothing has been scanned, and is refined online by every generated chunk towards the scanned subject. A 3D latent memory, designed around the cross-sectional physics of ultrasound, compounds the generated slices into a volume of the patient and reslices it at new poses, keeping revisited anatomy consistent. The fine-grained memory enters the generator at the input, while the coarse anatomy prior guides the layout across transformer blocks. Experiments on thyroid, kidney, and lumbar spine show improved visual fidelity, pose following, and anatomical consistency over pose-conditioned and memory-augmented baselines.

</details>


### [198] [SEAM: A Self-Evolving and Adaptive Probing Framework for Automatic MLLM Hallucination Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34598)
*Shiyu Zhang,Zengyi Wo,Yuming Liao,Ran He,Caifeng Shan,Yanbo Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SEAM：面向MLLM幻觉评估的自演化自适应探测框架，通过可组合探测技能库与双环（自适应探测+技能演化）实现自动、可持续的测试项生成与评估，平均有效幻觉发现率相对提升89.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM幻觉评测多依赖预定义静态测试项，构建成本高、随模型演进迅速过时，且难以探测模型特有的弱点，缺乏可持续、自适应的自动评测范式。

Method: 提出SEAM框架：构建可组合的幻觉探测构造库（10种幻觉探测技能、3种诱导包装器、5种问题格式）；内环为自适应探测，协调技能引导的视觉分析、问题组合与分层质量验证，结合验证器与目标模型响应反馈迭代优化测试项；外环为技能演化，由技能更新器从历史探测轨迹中蒸馏局部化可复用规则更新，经留出验证后冻结再用于测试。

Result: 在4个目标MLLM与10类幻觉类型上实验，SEAM在12组匹配对比中平均有效幻觉发现率相对提升89.2%，显著优于代表性静态基准。

Conclusion: SEAM建立了自动化、自演化的幻觉评测范式，克服静态基准的高成本与快速失效问题，能够有效揭示模型特异性幻觉弱点。

Abstract: The severe susceptibility of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to hallucinations poses a critical threat to their reliability, making a rigorous evaluation framework indispensable. However, existing evaluation pipelines largely rely on predefined test items, suffering from high construction costs, rapid obsolescence as MLLMs evolve, and ineffectiveness in probing model-specific weaknesses. To address these limitations, we propose SEAM, a self-evolving and adaptive probing framework for automatic MLLM hallucination evaluation. Specifically, SEAM introduces a composable Hallucination Probe Construction Library comprising ten hallucination-probing skills, three elicitation wrappers, and five question formats. Building on this library, SEAM establishes a dual-loop framework. In the inner loop (adaptive probing), skill-guided visual analysis, question composition, and hierarchical quality verification are coordinated with verifier and target-response feedback to refine test items and uncover model-specific hallucinations. In the outer loop (skill evolution), a skill updater distills historical probing trajectories into localized, reusable rule updates, which undergo held-out validation and are frozen before testing. Driven by these innovative structural designs, SEAM establishes an automated and self-evolving evaluation paradigm. Experiments across four target MLLMs and ten hallucination types demonstrate that SEAM substantially outperforms representative static benchmarks, achieving an 89.2\% relative improvement in mean valid hallucination discovery rate across 12 matched comparisons.

</details>


### [199] [Feed-Forward Adaptive Gaussian Splatting for Interactive Medical Volume Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34478)
*Zhongpai Gao,Benjamin Planche,Meng Zheng,Anwesa Choudhuri,Chaoyi Zhou,Terrence Chen,Ziyan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RenderFM-Next 是一个前馈高斯泼溅模型，单网络支持 CT/MR，每体数据仅预测一次高斯、支持精确裁剪与按需细化，在约2000例基准上较 RenderFM 提升 2.8/2.4 dB，1600×1600 视图渲染仅 4–6 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路径追踪体渲染虽逼真但每次改变视角、裁剪面或传递函数（TF）都需重新计算，帧率受限；RenderFM 等前馈高斯泼溅模型虽快，但仅支持 CT、每次 TF 编辑需重跑网络、无裁剪算子，且高斯固定在均匀 3 mm 网格上导致器官边界与切面模糊。

Method: 单一网络同时处理 CT 与 MR；高斯每体数据仅预测一次，TF 编辑无需再前馈，裁剪面借助已有算子精确切割高斯。模型先预测全容积 3 mm 粗高斯，仅在能提升图像处细化至 1.5 mm；一个小型打分头按"细化后渲染误差降低幅度"对粗高斯排序，渲染时用户决定细化数量，裁剪面附近高斯强制细化以锐化切面。

Result: 构建约 2000 例 CT/MR 扫描基准（临床预设、TF 编辑、裁剪条件下的路径追踪参考）。RenderFM-Next 较在同基准重训的 RenderFM 提升 CT 2.8 dB、MR 2.4 dB，1600×1600 视图渲染 4–6 ms。细化模块相较无细化同模型提升 0.30/0.69 dB（切面处 0.51/1.17 dB）；细化学习选出的 10% 粗高斯即可匹敌随机 50%，高斯数减少 2.4 倍。

Conclusion: 通过单网络跨模态、一次性高斯预测、精确裁剪与学习式自适应细化，RenderFM-Next 在保持毫秒级帧率的同时显著提升 CT/MR 路径追踪体渲染质量与交互效率。

Abstract: Path-traced rendering shows CT and MR volumes with realistic lighting, but it restarts after every change of view, clipping plane or transfer function (TF), the map from voxel intensity to colour and opacity, which limits the frame rate. Feed-forward Gaussian splatting models such as RenderFM predict the Gaussians directly from the volume instead of fitting them to each scan, and are fast. RenderFM, however, handles only CT, runs its network again after every TF edit, has no clipping operator, and places its Gaussians on a uniform 3-mm grid that blurs organ boundaries and cut faces. We present RenderFM-Next, a feed-forward model that addresses these limits. One network handles both CT and MR. Its Gaussians are predicted once per volume: a new TF needs no pass of this network, and a clipping plane cuts them exactly, using operators from prior work. The model predicts coarse 3-mm Gaussians for the whole volume and refines them to 1.5 mm only where refinement improves the image. A small score head ranks the coarse Gaussians by how much refining them would reduce the rendering error; at render time the user chooses how many are refined, and those next to a clipping plane are always refined, which sharpens cut faces. We build a benchmark of about 2,000 CT and MR scans with path-traced references under clinical presets, TF edits and clipping. Our RenderFM-Next improves over RenderFM retrained on this benchmark by 2.8 dB on CT and 2.4 dB on MR and renders a 1600x1600 view in 4-6 ms. Refinement adds 0.30 / 0.69 dB over the same model trained without it (0.51 / 1.17 dB on cut faces), and refining a learned 10% of the coarse Gaussians matches a random 50% with 2.4x fewer Gaussians. Project page: https://gaozhongpai.github.io/renderfmnext/

</details>


### [200] [AnyCam: Anything Can Represent Camera for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34944)
*Weijie Lyu,Zhixin Shu,Yuntian Ke,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AnyCam，用场景中的锚定物体（anchor object）而非相机参数或显式几何来表示相机，实现尺度感知、视角完整、易于指定的环绕式相机可控视频生成，并蒸馏出少步流式生成器 AnyCam-AR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机可控视频生成中，绕物体移动相机是最自然的视角变化方式，但现有方法存在缺陷：以相机参数为条件在单目视频中尺度歧义；以显式几何（warped point cloud、depth）为条件在大视角变化下失效。需要一种既尺度明确又在大视角变化下鲁棒的相机表示。

Method: 将相机表示解耦为“锚定物体”：先把锚定物体重建为3D，并沿目标轨迹渲染，得到尺度感知、视角完整、可相对物体轻松指定的相机信号；再用视频扩散模型把源视频的内容与动态迁移到新视角。模型采用 H-Concat 布局在时间上对齐源帧与目标帧，从而可蒸馏为 AnyCam-AR——一种用于实时相机控制的流式少步生成器。训练数据为多相机视频与静态场景采集生成像素对齐锚定渲染的成对视频。

Result: 在多种数据集与轨迹上的实验表明，AnyCam 在相机控制精度与视觉质量上优于相机可控视频生成方法与3D重建方法。

Conclusion: 以锚定物体作为相机代理表示，可同时解决尺度歧义与大视角变化的几何失效问题，并支持向流式少步生成蒸馏，为物体中心的环绕式相机控制提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Camera-controlled video generation re-renders a video along a target camera trajectory, yet moving the camera around an object, the most natural way to change the viewpoint of an object-centric scene, remains difficult. Existing methods condition either on camera parameters, which are scale-ambiguous in monocular video, or on explicit geometry such as warped point clouds or depth, which breaks down under large viewpoint changes. We present AnyCam, a method that represents the camera with an anchor object in the scene: we reconstruct it in 3D and render it along the target trajectory, a camera signal that is scale-aware, view-complete and easy to specify relative to the object. A video diffusion model transfers the content and dynamics of the source video to the new viewpoint. Its H-Concat layout aligns source and target frames in time, so the model can be distilled into AnyCam-AR, a streaming few-step generator for on-the-fly camera control. Both models are trained on paired videos with pixel-aligned anchor renderings from multi-camera videos and static-scene captures. Experiments on diverse datasets and trajectories show that AnyCam performs favorably against camera-controlled video generation and 3D reconstruction methods in camera control and visual quality.

</details>


### [201] [ProbFaS: Probabilistic Video Frame Selection for MLLMs via Multi-Fidelity Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34903)
*Tianru Zhang,Mayank Nautiyal,Hugo Eidmann,Prashant Singh,Ekta Vats*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 ProbFaS，一种无需训练与标签的概率式关键帧选择流程，将稠密的图文对齐分数与稀疏的 MLLM 内部判断通过多保真高斯过程（co-kriging）融合，再用时间行列式点过程选帧，在 13/15 个设置上超越 14 种免训练选择器与均匀采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频 MLLM 处理成本高，需高效选帧。均匀采样易漏关键帧；现有免训练的图文对齐（ITA）选择器虽逐帧打分，但其排序与下游准确率相关性弱，甚至在超过 40% 的问题上呈负相关，说明 ITA 信号与 MLLM 真实需求不匹配。

Method: 先验证 MLLM 内部 yes/no 判断是更强信号；将稠密 ITA 分数视为低成本、有偏的测量，稀疏 MLLM 判断视为高成本、准确的测量，用 co-kriging（多保真高斯过程）在全部帧上校准相关性；再由时间行列式点过程（temporal DPP）选帧，兼顾相关性峰值与时间覆盖。

Result: 在 Video-MME、MLVU、LongVideoBench 上，8–128 帧共 15 个设置中 13 个最优，最高超过最强基线 3.1 个百分点；在 MLVU 与 LongVideoBench 上，32 帧 ProbFaS 即可匹配或超越 128 帧最优基线及最多 768 帧的均匀采样。

Conclusion: ITA 排序并非可靠的相关性代理；以多保真高斯过程融合廉价稠密信号与昂贵稀疏的 MLLM 判断，并配合兼顾覆盖性的 DPP 选帧，可在完全免训练、免标签前提下显著提升长视频理解效率与精度。

Abstract: Processing long videos with multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is prohibitively expensive, making informative frame selection essential for accurate video understanding. The default uniform sampling often misses task-relevant keyframes, while recent training-free selectors instead pick frames by how well each one matches the question under an image-text alignment (ITA) score. However, we show that ITA signals correspond poorly with the MLLM's true needs: their rankings correlate weakly with downstream accuracy and are even negatively correlated on over 40% of questions, whereas the MLLM's internal yes/no judgment yields a much stronger signal. We present ProbFaS, a training- and label-free probabilistic pipeline that fuses both signals for key frame selection. ProbFaS treats dense ITA scores as cheap, biased measurements and sparse MLLM judgments as accurate, costly ones, unifying them via co-kriging, a multi-fidelity Gaussian process, to calibrate relevance across all frames. A temporal determinantal point process then selects frames capturing relevance peaks while preserving temporal coverage. Across Video-MME, MLVU and LongVideoBench, ProbFaS is the most accurate among fourteen state-of-the-art training-free selectors and uniform sampling in 13 of 15 settings (8-128 frames), outperforming the strongest baseline by up to 3.1 percentage points. On MLVU and LongVideoBench, 32 ProbFaS frames match or surpass the top baseline at 128 frames and uniform sampling with up to 768 frames.

</details>


### [202] [DocDUR-Net: Degradation-Aware Unified Document Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34860)
*Haowei Xu,Peirong Zhang,Xuhan Zheng,Lianwen Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DocDUR-Net，一种从输入图像直接推导复原引导的退化感知统一文档图像复原网络，无需外部任务选择，在统一方法中取得最优平均PSNR 29.405 dB、SSIM 0.960。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档图像受不受控成像条件影响存在多种退化，单退化复原方法需按退化类型分别训练与部署，须先做模型选择；现有统一复原方法虽共享单一网络，但仍依赖外部任务选择，限制了自动复原。

Method: 设计退化感知模块（DPM）提取退化表征并预测退化权重；提出退化先验引导的自适应特征融合（DP-AFF），将可学习退化先验与输入提取的退化特征结合；将所得引导注入共享复原主干，实现无需外部任务选择的自适应复原，同时保留显式任务选择的手动模式。

Result: 在文档图像复原基准上，统一方法中平均PSNR 29.405 dB、SSIM 0.960为最优，并与单退化方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过从输入图像自动感知退化并生成引导，DocDUR-Net 消除统一文档复原对外部任务选择的依赖，实现自动、自适应的统一文档图像复原。

Abstract: Captured document images often suffer from diverse degradations caused by uncontrolled imaging conditions, which reduce readability and obscure fine text structures. Single-degradation restoration methods are usually trained and deployed for individual degradation types, and therefore require model selection before restoration. Unified restoration models alleviate the need to maintain multiple restoration networks by sharing one network across tasks, but representative unified document image restoration methods still depend on external task selection. This dependency limits automatic document image restoration. We propose DocDUR-Net, a degradation-aware unified restoration network that derives restoration guidance directly from the input image. It introduces a Degradation Perception Module (DPM) to extract degradation representations and predict degradation weights, and Degradation Prior-guided Adaptive Feature Fusion (DP-AFF) to combine learnable degradation priors with degradation features extracted from the input. The resulting guidance is injected into a shared restoration backbone, enabling adaptive restoration without external task selection. DocDUR-Net also supports a manual mode with explicit task selection. Experiments on document image restoration benchmarks show that DocDUR-Net achieves the best average PSNR of 29.405 dB and SSIM of 0.960 among unified methods while remaining competitive with single-degradation methods.

</details>


### [203] [TIDE-Flow: Fine-Grained Temporal Evidence and Structured Displacement Matching for Spike Optical Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34854)
*Hanwen Liang,Wenxuan Liu,Wenxin Huang,Jingling Yuan,Zhaofei Yu,Xian Zhong,Tiejun Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 TIDE-Flow 脉冲相机光流估计框架，联合利用时间积分证据（TIE）与位移证据编码（DEE），在差分相关循环细化框架中实现时序运动建模与对应关系估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲相机通过积分-放电机制捕捉高速运动，无曝光模糊，但其二值流编码的是累积光照而非瞬时强度，使可靠运动估计困难。现有方法依赖时间聚合构造运动表征，可能削弱细粒度放电模式；同时基于相关性的估计器多依赖学习到的匹配特征，未显式刻画候选位移的空间分布与歧义性。

Method: TIDE-Flow 包含两部分：(1) TIE 结合原始脉冲模式、多尺度放电统计、时间矩与边界感知区间线索，在空间特征压缩前保留互补时序信息；(2) DEE 将结构化候选网格特征与显式位移统计相结合，刻画匹配几何与竞争性对应关系。二者统一嵌入差分相关循环细化框架。

Result: 大量实验验证了 TIDE-Flow 的有效性，受控消融实验证实了时序证据建模与位移证据建模各自的贡献。

Conclusion: 通过在空间压缩前保留多源时序证据并显式编码位移分布特征，可有效提升脉冲相机光流的运动建模与对应估计性能。

Abstract: Spike cameras capture high-speed motion through an integrate-and-fire mechanism, providing fine-grained temporal observations without exposure blur. However, their binary streams encode accumulated illumination rather than instantaneous intensity, making reliable motion estimation challenging. Existing spike-based methods exploit temporal aggregation to construct motion representations, but this process may weaken fine-grained firing patterns. Meanwhile, correlation-based estimators often rely on learned matching features without explicitly characterizing the spatial distribution and ambiguity of candidate displacements. To address these challenges, we propose TIDE-Flow, a spike-based optical flow estimation framework that jointly exploits Temporal Integration Evidence (TIE) and Displacement Evidence Encoding (DEE). TIE combines raw spike patterns, multi-scale firing statistics, temporal moments, and boundary-aware interval cues, preserving complementary temporal information before spatial feature compression. DEE further integrates structured candidate-grid features with explicit displacement statistics to characterize matching geometry and competing correspondences. Both components are incorporated into a difference-correlation recurrent refinement framework, enabling effective temporal motion modeling and correspondence estimation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of TIDE-Flow, with controlled ablations validating the contributions of temporal and displacement evidence modeling.

</details>


### [204] [Was the Model Given the Right Action? Auditing and Repairing Action Interfaces in Navigation World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34739)
*Ajmain Istiak Apon,Md Mehedi Hasan,Sejuti Rahman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文审计了动作条件世界模型评估中"动作预处理一致"这一隐含假设：在Navigation World Models中发现训练与推理代码存在三处相互作用的差异，仅修正输入即可在不重训练的情况下将视觉预测误差降低42%，并提供工具ActRosetta用于检测、诊断与修复动作编码不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型以"由动作预测未来观测"作为评估方式，但这类评估默认rollout时的动作处理与训练时完全一致；一旦存在未被察觉的失配，模型会被误判为精度更低，从而影响模型对比与基准排名。作者因此系统审计这一假设在NWM中是否成立。

Method: 审计NWM的训练代码与rollout代码，定位三处相互作用的差异；在RECON导航数据集上以DreamSim度量视觉预测误差，按输入修正前后对比（不重训练），并做checkpoint排名与两个替代模型的探索性分析；进一步对两个替代模型人工注入编码错误以检验敏感性；最后提出ActRosetta工具，用于检查动作编码是否与训练一致、识别支持的不一致成因并在信息充分时修复输入。

Result: 修正输入后平均视觉预测误差（DreamSim，RECON）下降42%；该修正改变了NWC checkpoint在探索性分析中的排名，并使两个替代模型测得的优势被消除或削弱；仅修正归一化偏移一项反而使误差上升，说明相互作用的失配必须联合检查；人为注入编码错误后两个替代模型精度均下降，表明对输入准备方式的敏感性不限于NWM。

Conclusion: 对任何动作条件世界模型，报告rollout误差前都应确认评估阶段的动作编码与训练完全一致。ActRosetta虽有帮助，但存在局限：当预测对动作几乎不敏感时修复效果有限、诊断覆盖范围有限、控制性能改善尚未得到验证。

Abstract: World models are evaluated by predicting future observations from actions. These evaluations assume that actions are prepared the same way as during training, but an unchecked mismatch can make a model appear less accurate than it is. We audit this assumption in Navigation World Models (NWM) and find three interacting differences between its training and rollout code. Correcting the inputs, without retraining, reduces mean visual prediction error by 42%, measured with DreamSim on the RECON navigation dataset. The correction also changes the ranking of NWM checkpoints in an exploratory analysis and removes or reduces two alternative models' measured advantages. Correcting only the normalization offset instead increases error, showing why interacting mismatches must be checked together. To test whether sensitivity to input preparation extends beyond NWM, we deliberately introduce encoding errors into two alternative models; both become less accurate. We introduce ActRosetta, a tool that checks whether action encoding matches training, identifies supported causes of disagreement, and repairs inputs when sufficient information is available. Repair helps little where predictions barely respond to actions, the tool's diagnosis has limited coverage, and improved control remains unproven. For any action-conditioned world model, the lesson is the same: before reporting rollout error, verify that evaluation encodes actions exactly as training did.

</details>


### [205] [WorldInteract: Controllable Streaming Video Generation for Multi-Material Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34730)
*Ahmed Hatem,Caoliwen Wang,Robert Hoang,Siyuan Chen,Spencer Szabados,Zike Wu,Xiyang Tan,Shenyifan Lu,Rafael Bischof,Bernd Bickel,Renjie Liao,Peter Yichen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldInteract：将跨材料交互分解为"材料相关行为生成"与"接触响应计算"的流式视频生成框架，无需监督式接触视频即可实现可控跨材料交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控视频生成对材料相关动力学（material-dependent dynamics）探索不足，在包含多种材料的场景中难以实现可靠控制；且跨材料接触视频（覆盖多种材料配对与接触构型）的标注成本高昂。

Method: 提出WorldInteract流式生成框架：1) 将外力推动与接触冲量统一表示为共享的速度增量接口（velocity-increment interface），使从无交互视频中学到的材料行为可复用于交互场景；2) 物理控制器估计质量与恢复系数，预测接触并计算速度增量；3) 因果生成器利用学到的材料行为执行这些增量；4) 在22类材料、30k条模拟无交互视频上训练生成器，并引入Material-Aware RL，以可验证的物理奖励精炼材料行为与控制执行。

Result: 在Physics-IQ基准上显著超越现有SOTA方法，并在真实世界图像上提供更可靠的推动控制（push control）。

Conclusion: 通过解耦材料行为与接触响应的设计，可在缺乏跨材料接触监督数据的条件下实现可靠的跨材料交互生成；速度增量接口与物理奖励强化学习是提升材料动力学可控性的有效手段。

Abstract: Video world models aim to create interactive physical worlds that capture how diverse materials move and interact. Yet material-dependent dynamics remain underexplored in controllable video generation, limiting reliable control in scenes with different materials. We present WorldInteract, a streaming video generation framework that decomposes cross-material interaction into material-dependent behavior generation and contact response computation. External pushes and contact impulses are expressed through a shared velocity-increment interface, allowing material behaviors learned from interaction-free videos to be reused in interacting scenes. A physics controller predicts contacts and computes velocity increments from estimated masses and restitution coefficients, while a causal generator executes these increments using learned material behaviors. This separation enables cross-material interaction without supervised contact videos spanning diverse material pairings and contact configurations. We train the generator on 30k simulated interaction-free videos spanning 22 material classes and introduce Material-Aware RL, using verifiable physical rewards to refine material behavior and control execution. WorldInteract substantially outperforms state-of-the-art methods on Physics-IQ and delivers more reliable push control on real-world images.

</details>


### [206] [AnyCam4D: Ray-Based 4D Gaussian Rendering for Arbitrary Camera Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34223)
*Sascha Kirch,Rafael Pagés,Jan Ondřej,Clara Pérez-Molina,Sergio Martin*

Main category: cs.CV
