TL;DR: 提出 AnyCam4D，用基于光线的光栅化在原始（未去畸变）素材上训练原生 4D 高斯，通过数值稳定的 4D 协方差平方根条件化恢复高斯朝向与尺寸，保留视场与分辨率，并以与标准投影相当的每步开销支持鱼眼、卷帘快门、景深等相机同时渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实相机并非针孔模型，但现有动态高斯泼溅普遍假设针孔，需先去畸变，导致宽角/鱼眼素材损失视场或细节。静态高斯泼溅已用基于光线的渲染解决该问题，但原生 4D 高斯在时空中建模运动，无法直接套用：其朝向与尺寸不能从协方差中可靠恢复。

Method: 对 4D 协方差做平方根分解并加以条件化（condition a square root of the 4D covariance），以数值稳定的方式提取各高斯的朝向与尺寸；据此在原始影像上直接进行基于光线（ray-based）的光栅化训练原生 4D 高斯，无需相机特定推导即可支持其他相机模型（卷帘快门、景深、色差）。

Result: 合成 170° 鱼眼相机下，用原始图像训练使 PSNR 提升 3.0–8.1 dB；真实素材上，在相近 PSNR 下感知误差低于逐相机适配的标准投影，且无需相机特定推导；合成相机相对采集装置视角旋转 60–80° 时，相对真值领先 6.2 dB。

Conclusion: 基于光线的光栅化结合稳定的 4D 协方差分解，可使原生 4D 高斯直接适配任意相机，避免去畸变带来的视场与细节损失，并在宽角/大视角偏差场景显著优于标准投影，代价仅为与标准投影相当的每步开销。

Abstract: Real cameras are not pinholes, yet most dynamic Gaussian splatting assumes they are. Footage is therefore undistorted first, losing field of view or detail, especially with wide-angle and fisheye cameras. Static Gaussian splatting has largely solved this with ray-based rendering, which suits any camera. Native 4D Gaussians, which model motion in space-time, cannot use it directly: each Gaussian's orientation and size cannot be recovered reliably from its covariance. We present AnyCam4D, which trains native 4D Gaussians on original footage by ray-based rasterization, preserving field of view and resolution at about the per-step cost of standard projection. Our key contribution is a numerically stable way to obtain orientation and size: we condition a square root of the 4D covariance. Trained models render through other cameras, with rolling shutter, depth of field or chromatic aberration. With a synthetic 170-degree fisheye camera, training on original images improves peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) by 3.0-8.1 dB. On real footage, AnyCam4D has lower perceptual error than the standard projection adapted to each camera, at comparable PSNR and without camera-specific derivations. With the synthetic camera rotated 60-80 degrees away from the capture rig's view, it leads by 6.2 dB against ground truth.

</details>


### [207] [TORE: Token-Wise Taylor Order Selection via Decomposed Residual Error Estimation for Diffusion Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34261)
*Lei Tang,Baoquan Zhang,Yakun Zhang,Yunming Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TORE，用残差预测误差估计来选择token级Taylor阶数，解决代理特征与全模型残差之间的 mismatch，在FLUX.1-dev和Wan2.1上分别实现4.31×与4.18×加速且保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型迭代去噪推理延迟高。现有token自适应加速方法用浅层代理分数为全模型残差选择Taylor预测阶数，候选阶数在代理特征上评估却用于预测全模型残差，存在代理—目标不匹配，代理偏好的阶数未必最小化残差预测误差。

Method: TORE基于全模型残差的估计预测误差进行token级Taylor阶数选择。由于阶数选择时无未来真值残差，TORE通过分析Taylor外推误差来源来估计预测误差：将预测误差分解为截断误差与有限差分导数误差，并利用已缓存残差估计二者贡献，从而为后续skip步预选token级阶数。

Result: 在FLUX.1-dev上实现4.31×加速，在Wan2.1上实现4.18×加速，同时保持较高的生成质量与保真度。

Conclusion: 以残差预测误差（而非代理分数）驱动token级阶数选择可有效消除代理—目标不匹配，在图像与视频扩散模型上均取得显著加速且质量损失小。

Abstract: Diffusion models excel at image and video generation, but iterative denoising incurs substantial inference latency. Recent token-adaptive acceleration methods select Taylor prediction orders for the full-model residual using shallow-layer proxy scores. However, candidate orders are evaluated on these proxy features but used to predict the full-model residual, creating a proxy--target mismatch: the proxy-preferred order may not minimize residual prediction error. To address this mismatch, we propose \textbf{TORE} (\textbf{T}aylor \textbf{O}rder selection via \textbf{R}esidual \textbf{E}rror estimation), which selects token-wise Taylor orders based on estimated prediction errors for the full-model residual. Yet direct error evaluation requires future ground-truth residuals, which are unavailable during order selection. TORE therefore estimates prediction error by analyzing how errors arise in Taylor extrapolation. Specifically, it decomposes prediction error into truncation error and finite-difference derivative error, then estimates both contributions from cached residuals to preselect token-wise orders for upcoming skip steps. Extensive experiments show that TORE achieves $4.31\times$ speedup on FLUX.1-dev and $4.18\times$ on Wan2.1 while maintaining high generation quality and fidelity.

</details>


### [208] [OceanFix: Reference-Guided Diffusion for Sparse-View Underwater 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34155)
*Gen Li,Maoxin Chen,Yao Xiao,Xinyang Li,Yu Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OceanFix：一种参考引导的稀疏视角水下3D重建框架，结合扩散式视角修复与高斯场景细化，迭代渲染-修复-蒸馏以减轻稀疏观测下的伪影并恢复细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯泼溅虽提升了水下重建效率，但稀疏观测、光衰减与后向散射使部分场景区域欠约束，导致新视角渲染出现伪影，需要额外监督来修复这些区域。

Method: 提出两阶段训练的扩散修复模型：第一阶段从参考观测与估计的介质特征学习水下外观适配；第二阶段用相机位姿与参考深度对齐特征，并以相对深度监督引导几何修复（推理时无需目标视角深度）。同时提出介质感知蒸馏，依据几何修复需求与通道相关的光学可观测性将修复视角迁移入3D场景；通过迭代渲染—修复—蒸馏并保留观测视角监督。

Result: 在水下数据集上，该方法提升了稀疏视角3D重建的渲染质量，减少了可见伪影并恢复了更细的场景细节。

Conclusion: 将扩散视角修复与高斯场景细化闭环结合的参考引导范式，可有效缓解水下稀疏视角重建的欠约束问题，是可行的外观—几何解耦+蒸馏增强思路。

Abstract: Recent advances in Gaussian splatting have enabled efficient underwater reconstruction, but sparse observations, light attenuation, and backscatter still leave scene regions underconstrained and cause novel-view artifacts. We present OceanFix, a reference-guided framework for sparse-view underwater 3D reconstruction that combines diffusion-based view repair with Gaussian scene refinement. Our repair model follows a two-stage training strategy that separates underwater appearance adaptation from geometric refinement. The first stage learns underwater appearance from reference observations and estimated medium features. The second uses camera poses and reference depth to align reference features, with relative-depth supervision guiding structural repair without requiring target-view depth at inference. We further propose medium-aware distillation that transfers repaired views into the 3D scene according to geometric repair demand and channel-specific optical observability. Through iterative rendering, repair, and distillation, OceanFix refines underconstrained regions while retaining supervision from the observed views. Experiments on underwater datasets demonstrate that OceanFix improves rendering quality in sparse-view 3D reconstruction, reducing visible artifacts and recovering finer scene details.

</details>


### [209] [From Diagnosing Alignment to Joint Supervision in Multimodal Draft Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34091)
*Haozhe Du,Runze Liu,Jiaming Fan,Yitong Tao,Shuxia Lin,Chonghan Liu,Daming Cao,Xu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文通过配对预测诊断多模态草稿模型的对齐情况，将分布误差分解为文本条件与视觉响应两部分，并提出MNRS-Joint按组件风险配置多模态/纯文本联合监督混合比例，在投机解码中提升接受长度与加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态草稿模型可同时接受多模态(MM)与纯文本(TXT)监督，但两类监督对草稿-目标对齐的影响及如何组合仍不清楚，缺乏细粒度的诊断与配置方法。

Method: 通过有/无视觉输入的配对预测诊断对齐，将分布误差分解为文本条件与视觉响应两个组件；据此分析不同草稿的视觉响应风险与接受率差异；开展受控续训实验对比高风险、低风险与随机分组；提出MNRS-Joint，基于各草稿的组件风险分布，用模态特定平均风险确定MM:TXT混合比。

Result: 受控续训中高风险组优于随机组与低风险组，视觉响应风险比聚合风险更能指导MM训练；三次warm-up中MNRS-Joint的混合比均落在比例扫描的高性能区间；在LLaVA-1.5-7B上，贪心树解码下平均接受长度由3.35提升至3.53，平均加速比由2.04×提升至2.16×（相对组成匹配的随机训练），随机链式解码下增益同样成立。

Conclusion: 组件级风险分解可有效刻画多模态对齐，视觉响应风险是引导MM监督的关键信号；基于该诊断的MNRS-Joint能自动配置联合监督比例并在投机解码中稳定提升接受长度与加速效果。

Abstract: Multimodal drafts learn from multimodal (MM) and text-only (TXT) supervision, yet their effects on alignment and how to combine them remain unclear. We diagnose draft-target alignment through paired predictions with and without visual input, decomposing distributional error into text-conditioned and visual-response components. Drafts with similar text-conditioned alignment can still differ markedly in visual-response risk and acceptance. Controlled continuation training shows that high-risk groups outperform random and low-risk groups, and visual-response risk guides MM training more effectively than aggregate risk. Building on these diagnostics, we propose MNRS-Joint, which configures joint supervision from a given draft's component-risk distributions, using modality-specific mean risks to determine the MM:TXT mixture. Across three warm-ups, its distinct mixtures fall in high-performing regions of the ratio sweeps. On LLaVA-1.5-7B, MNRS-Joint improves mean accepted length from 3.35 to 3.53 and mean speedup from $2.04\times$ to $2.16\times$ over composition-matched random training under greedy tree decoding. The gains also hold under stochastic chain decoding.

</details>


### [210] [TaCo: Rethinking Correlation for Visual Tracking on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34084)
*Qingmao Wei,Fagui Liu,Quan Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TaCo 通过"目标对齐相关"替代注意力：用模板一次性生成的描述符与对齐卷积核在 backbone 内完成条件化与匹配，在边缘 NPU/CPU 上以极低开销实现优于注意力类跟踪器的速度—精度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量跟踪器用注意力替代互相关后，在低功耗边缘硬件上受限于 softmax、归一化、reshape 等访存密集型算子，实际耗时远超其算术量。实测（统一数值验证协议、edge NPU + Arm CPU）表明：相关类跟踪器速度占优，但精度落后，除非改用 ViT backbone——而这又把注意力带回。作者因此重新审视"相关到底缺什么"。

Method: 指出模板—搜索交互有两重角色：一是以目标信息条件化搜索特征（conditioning），二是空间匹配（matching）；注意力两者皆做，而相关只做匹配，且匹配所用特征在提取时并不知晓目标。TaCo 的做法是让模板仅以"每段视频只计算一次的操作数"形式进入逐帧网络：(1) 目标描述符在 backbone 内部对搜索特征做逐通道的缩放与平移（条件化）；(2) 用与目标框对齐、且权重由模板预测的卷积核，以一次矩阵乘积与其相关（匹配）。backbone 保持冻结。

Result: TaCo-T：LaSOT AUC 62.3%，RV1126B NPU 上 184 FPS、Raspberry Pi 5 CPU 上 96 FPS，NPU 速度为 HiT-Tiny 与 AsymTrack-T 的 3.0x、3.5x，且精度更高。TaCo-B：AUC 65.6%，NPU 上 50 FPS，速度超过 HiT-Base、AsymTrack-B、LightFC-vit 两倍以上，精度同样更高。

Conclusion: 在 backbone 固定的前提下，所提出的相关交互依然不可或缺，而 NPU 额外开销可忽略，速度—精度权衡优于所测试的注意力交互方案；说明补上"条件化"这一缺失角色即可让相关类跟踪器在不引入注意力的条件下兼顾精度与边缘效率。

Abstract: Lightweight trackers have replaced cross-correlation with attention, and on low-power edge hardware they pay for it, since memory-bound softmax, normalization and reshaping operators of attention cost far more time than their arithmetic suggests. Benchmarking efficient trackers on an edge Neural Processing Unit (NPU) and an Arm CPU under one numerically validated protocol, we find that correlation trackers are the fast ones, but they lag in accuracy unless they adopt a ViT backbone, which brings attention back. We revisit what correlation lacks. Template--search interaction serves two roles: conditioning the search features on the target and matching them against it spatially. Attention does both, whereas correlation only matches, and does so on features extracted without knowledge of the target. We present TaCo (Target-aligned Correlation), which supplies the missing conditioning without attention by letting the template enter the per-frame network only as operands computed once per video. A descriptor of the target scales and shifts the search features channel by channel inside the backbone, and kernels aligned with the target box, each with a weight predicted from the template, correlate with them in one matrix product. With the backbone fixed, our correlation remains essential while incurring negligible NPU overhead, offering a better speed-accuracy trade-off than the attention-based interactions we tested. TaCo-T achieves 62.3% LaSOT AUC at 184 FPS on the Rockchip RV1126B NPU and 96 FPS on a Raspberry Pi 5 CPU, 3.0x and 3.5x the NPU speed of HiT-Tiny and AsymTrack-T at higher accuracy. TaCo-B reaches 65.6% at 50 FPS on the NPU, more than twice the speed of HiT-Base, AsymTrack-B and LightFC-vit, again at higher accuracy. Code and models are available at https://github.com/TsingWei/TaCo-Tracker.

</details>


### [211] [Understanding Diffusion Acceleration: Methods, Trade-Offs, and Open Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34056)
*Xiaofeng Mao,Fu-yun Wang,Le Zhuo,Zhengkai Jiang,Shaohao Rui,Weijie Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理扩散与流式生成模型推理加速的四类方法（蒸馏、一致性训练、采样方法、去噪器设计），用统一记号比较学习目标、采样轨迹与单次去噪计算量的变化，并总结图像/视频基准上的质量—成本权衡及开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散/流模型采样需数十至数百次去噪评估，随分辨率、视频长度与模型规模增大成本急剧上升，影响交互与边缘应用的时延与能耗，亟需系统比较各类加速推理方案。

Method: 综述性研究：将训练目标、转移映射与数值更新统一表述为共同记号，从三个维度比较方法——学习目标的改变、采样轨迹的改变、单次去噪前向计算的改变；并结合已发表的图像与视频基准进行对比分析。

Result: 基准结果显示：一步/少步生成存在明显的质量—成本权衡；求解器与时间步调度存在交互作用；特征缓存可降低计算；首帧时延与持续流式吞吐是不同的性能指标。

Conclusion: 提出未来开放问题：面向视觉原生架构与压缩后蒸馏、求解器感知的少步训练，以及基于因子化分布匹配的语义轨迹对齐。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-based generative models support high-quality image, audio, and video synthesis. Sampling can require dozens to hundreds of denoiser evaluations, with each pass becoming more expensive as resolution, video length, or model size increases. This cost affects latency and energy use in interactive and edge applications. We examine four approaches to accelerating inference: distillation, consistency training, sampling methods, and denoiser design. We express training objectives, transition maps, and numerical updates in a common notation to compare changes to the learning target, the sampling trajectory, and the work performed within each denoiser pass. Published image and video benchmarks illustrate the quality--cost trade-offs of one- and few-step generation, the interaction between solvers and timestep schedules, the effect of feature caching, and the difference between first-frame latency and sustained streaming throughput. We close with open problems in visual-native architectures and post-compression distillation, solver-aware few-step training, and semantic trajectory alignment with factorized distribution matching.

</details>


### [212] [Counting Beyond Frames: Codec Motion Tells Frozen Diffusion VLMs How Many](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34657)
*Zhuoqin Li,Jiayu Ding,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架 CoDiCount，利用视频编解码器记录的运动（块位移）作为计数的运动假设证据，转化为"计数势"，在扩散视觉语言模型（dVLM）去噪过程中重加权答案概率，在 MotionBench、TOMATO、SVCBench 上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频中的计数任务对视频-语言模型（自回归与扩散模型均有）极其困难。困难根源于"帧间"信息：模型只采样少量帧，而每次重复动作都发生在帧与帧之间。作者希望利用已有但被忽视的信号——视频编解码器稠密记录的块位移运动信息——来为计数提供直接证据。

Method: 核心洞见：候选计数可视为一个可检验的"运动假设"，将其蕴含的周期/循环展开（unwinding），即可度量观测到的运动方向对该计数的支持强度。据此构建统一、免训练框架 CoDiCount：把该检验转化为计数势（count potential）；在冻结的扩散视觉语言模型（dVLM）去噪每一步，通过结构化计数消息，将每个选项字母或每个语法合法的开放整数编辑映射为其所表达的计数值，在该集合内对模型自身概率进行重加权。

Result: 在 MotionBench、TOMATO、SVCBench 上相较官方解码器均获得一致提升，体现跨扩散模型、跨数据集、跨多种计数任务的泛化能力。7B dVLM 在 MotionBench 上超过 GPT-5.1 与 Gemini-2.5-Pro，在 TOMATO 上超过 GPT-4o 及更大的开源模型，在 SVCBench 上与 GPT-5.4 相当。

Conclusion: 运动（编解码器块位移）是视频计数被忽视的强证据来源；将其形式化为运动假设检验并作为去噪期概率重加权的计数势，可在无需训练的前提下显著提升扩散视频语言模型的多任务计数性能，并使中小规模模型媲美甚至超越大型专有模型。

Abstract: Counting how many times something happens is among the simplest questions about a video and among the hardest for video-language models, autoregressive and diffusion alike. The difficulty lies between the frames: a model samples only a few of them, while each repetition unfolds as movement in between. Video codecs already record this movement densely as block displacements, and codec-based video models use it to shape their visual input. We are the first to show that this motion provides direct evidence for the count: a candidate count is a testable motion hypothesis, and unwinding the cycles it implies measures how strongly the observed directions support it. We present CoDiCount, a unified training-free framework that turns this test into a count potential and applies it while a frozen diffusion vision-language model (dVLM) denoises its answer. At every denoising step, structured count messages map each option letter, or each grammar-valid edit of an open integer, to the count it expresses and reweight the model's own probabilities within that set. Consistent gains over the official decoders on MotionBench, TOMATO, and SVCBench demonstrate the broad generalization of CoDiCount across diffusion models, datasets, and diverse counting tasks. With CoDiCount, a 7B dVLM outperforms GPT-5.1 and Gemini-2.5-Pro on MotionBench, surpasses GPT-4o and much larger open models on TOMATO, and matches GPT-5.4 on SVCBench.

</details>


### [213] [A Hard Background Pairing Transfers across Classifiers and Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34645)
*Karl Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出并验证一种“最难配对”（hardest pairing）复合基准的构建方法：用一半分类器在一组物体/背景标签配对中挑出最难配对，再在另一半留出分类器和新照片上检验其难度可迁移性。结果显示该配对显著降低留出分类器准确率（50.3%→43.7%），说明可用于比较新模型的固定基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合图像（把照片中的物体粘贴到其它照片背景上）能暴露分类器对上下文/背景的依赖，但“最难配对”可能只对该组分类器或仅对用于选配对的照片困难，缺乏泛化性证据，故需检验其可迁移性。

Method: 将40个ImageNet分类器按报告准确率均衡分为两半；一半在COCO的8个物体标签×8个背景标签配对中选择最难配对（每个物体标签配不同背景标签，如truck on sea）；另一半作为留出集，在新照片上将该配对与同物体、同背景的随机配对进行比较。

Result: 对14个“物体贴回原背景后净准确率变化很小”的留出分类器，新照片复合图像上的平均准确率由随机配对的50.3%降至最难配对的43.7%，下降6.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 最难配对跨分类器、跨照片保持困难，可作为固定配对基准来比较该分类器池中的留出模型；同时强调需控制净准确率变化以排除干扰。

Abstract: An image classifier names the object in an image, and its accuracy is the share of images named correctly. A classifier that names a car on a road may miss the same car pasted onto clouds. Composite benchmarks measure such failures by pasting objects cut from photographs onto backgrounds from other photographs. A pairing puts every object label on a different background label, such as truck on sea. The pairing on which some classifiers do worst, their hardest pairing, makes a strict test, but may be hard only for them or only on the photographs used to choose it. We show that the hardest pairing of some classifiers stays hard for others and on new photographs. We split 40 ImageNet classifiers into halves balanced by reported ImageNet accuracy. One half picks its hardest pairing of 8 object and 8 background labels from Common Objects in Context (COCO). The other half, held out of the choice, compares that pairing with random pairings of the same objects and backgrounds. For the 14 held-out classifiers with little net accuracy change after pasting objects back onto their own backgrounds, mean accuracy on composites from new photographs falls from 50.3% over random pairings to 43.7% on the hardest pairing, 6.6 percentage points lower. A benchmark using these composites can compare this pool's held-out classifiers on the fixed and random pairings.

</details>


### [214] [ADClaw: A Programmable Visual Agent for Industrial Anomaly Detection and Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34591)
*Xinyi Wu,Rongxin Gao,YuHui Lin,Yonglin Hong,Chu Li,Wei Li,Dingzhao Li,Shijia Zhao,Chaopeng Li,Haixin Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ADClaw 是一个无需任务特定微调的工业异常检测智能体，通过写并执行代码来验证异常假设，在 2D/3D 基准上取得有竞争力的检测与定位结果，并在 MMAD 上完成异常理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 IAD 视觉语言模型多依赖大规模领域数据微调且单次前向预测，无法复查歧义区域；虽有工具型智能体可回看，但预定义工具接口限制了对中间结果的组合式二次检验，导致漏检细微缺陷或误判缺陷类型。

Method: 提出 ADClaw 检查智能体：对每个假设，结构化正常性先验（Structured Normality Prior）确定适用规则并检索正常视觉参考；智能体在持久化 Inspection Workbench 中编写并执行代码，将前序步骤的掩码、异常图与测量组合成新的缺陷检验；每次检验后，由假设引导的证据推理（Hypothesis-Guided Evidence Reasoning）评估证据是否支持该缺陷及其位置，并以证据缺口或不一致指导下一步代码动作。

Result: 在 2D 与 3D 工业异常检测基准上，检测与定位结果具有竞争力；在 MMAD 上的异常理解任务亦取得良好表现。

Conclusion: 以可执行代码替代固定工具接口的多轮假设验证范式，可在免微调条件下提升工业异常检测的复查能力与缺陷类型判定的可靠性。

Abstract: Industrial anomaly detection (IAD), the task of identifying and localizing deviations from normal product conditions, is essential to automated quality inspection in manufacturing. Many vision-language models for IAD rely on large domain-specific datasets for fine-tuning and make predictions in a single pass, leaving no opportunity to re-examine ambiguous regions. Although tool-using agents can revisit these regions, predefined tool interfaces limit how they can combine intermediate results in a follow-up check. When such checks are insufficient, models may miss subtle defects or misidentify the defect type. To address this limitation, we introduce ADClaw, an inspection agent that uses executable code to test anomaly hypotheses without task-specific fine-tuning. For each hypothesis, a Structured Normality Prior identifies the applicable rules and retrieves normal visual references for the inspection target. Guided by this prior, the agent writes and executes code in a persistent Inspection Workbench to combine masks, anomaly maps, and measurements from earlier steps into a new test of the suspected defect. After each check, Hypothesis-Guided Evidence Reasoning assesses whether the evidence supports the suspected defect and its location, using gaps or inconsistencies in that evidence to guide the next code action. ADClaw achieves competitive results in detection and localization across 2D and 3D industrial anomaly detection benchmarks and in anomaly understanding on MMAD.

</details>


### [215] [Score-based Black-box Attack via Adversarial Condition Guided Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34564)
*Yuhang Chen,Chih-Hong Cheng,Kurt Debattista,Siddartha Khastgir,Xingyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出将基于分数的黑盒攻击适配到反向扩散过程，并引入自适应目标引导与目标搜索引导，以受害者模型的输出概率引导潜变量更新，在黑盒设定下兼顾攻击效果与图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的黑盒攻击主要依赖替代模型的迁移性，效果对替代模型与受害者模型的差异敏感；提升迁移性又会扭曲语义内容、降低图像质量；而沿用原始提示词虽保持保真度，却浪费了扩散模型条件先验在对抗引导上的潜力。

Method: 将基于分数的黑盒攻击适配到反向扩散：在不访问受害者梯度的前提下，利用其输出概率引导潜变量更新。进一步提出自适应目标引导，以受害者当前次优类作为对抗条件，将去噪导向决策边界；为缓解其带来的语义变化，又提出目标搜索引导，用基于分数的攻击在原始条件嵌入附近搜索小幅扰动。

Result: 在 ImageNet-Compatible 数据集上的实验表明，该框架在攻击效果和图像质量两方面均优于基于迁移的基线方法；目标搜索引导在取得与次优类引导相当的攻击效果的同时，图像质量更优。

Conclusion: 将黑盒分数攻击与反向扩散结合、并以受害者反馈驱动条件引导，可在无梯度访问条件下实现高质量的无限制对抗样本，为生成式黑盒攻击提供了一条不依赖迁移性的有效路径。

Abstract: Generative models enable unrestricted adversarial attacks that aim to fool classifiers while preserving the natural appearance and semantics of the original image. However, existing diffusion-based black-box attacks largely rely on transferability from surrogates, making their effectiveness sensitive to differences between surrogate and victim. Improving transferability can also distort semantic content and degrade image quality. Moreover, retaining the original prompt to preserve fidelity leaves the diffusion model's conditional prior underused for adversarial guidance. We address these limitations by adapting score-based black-box attacks to reverse diffusion, using the victim's output probabilities to guide latent updates without accessing its gradients. We further introduce adaptive-target guidance, which uses the victim's current runner-up class as an adversarial condition to steer denoising towards the decision boundary. Because such guidance can introduce unwanted semantic changes, we also propose target-search guidance, which searches for a small perturbation of the original condition embedding using a score-based attack. This achieves comparable attack effectiveness with better image quality than runner-up guidance. Experiments on the ImageNet-Compatible dataset show that our framework outperforms transfer-based baselines in both attack effectiveness and image quality.

</details>


### [216] [Decoder-as-a-Probe: Verbalizing and Steering Visual Representations in LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34515)
*Adhemar de Senneville,Xavier Bou,Jérémy Anger,Rafael Grompone,Gabriele Facciolo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Decoder-as-a-Probe (DaP)，将 LVLM 的 LLM Decoder 视为以候选词为类别的分类器，用其一阶泰勒展开作为线性分类器，从而在视觉表示中找到可解释方向，性能优于经典探针与引导基线，尤其在标注数据稀疏时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM 中高层概念常被认为在表示空间线性可分，找到视觉表示中的可解释方向对可解释性很重要。经典做法是在表示上训练线性分类器，但需要大量标签，且只能反映"可从表示中读出"的信息，而非模型实际使用的信息。

Method: 观察到 LVLM 的 LLM Decoder 本身即为以可能的下一个 token 为类别的分类器，因此将其一阶泰勒展开作为线性分类器，据此构建 Decoder-as-a-Probe (DaP)，用于探测与操控视觉表示中的方向。

Result: DaP 显著优于经典探针与引导（steering）基线，尤其在训练数据稀疏时优势明显；能够(1)用语言描述 LVLM 在给定图像中所感知的内容；(2)精准改变 LVLM 对特定属性（如颜色、穿着、动作）的感知。

Conclusion: DaP 简单且数据高效，可作为即插即用工具服务于 LVLM 研究，如可解释性、幻觉缓解与指令微调。

Abstract: It is commonly accepted that high-level concepts are represented linearly in the representation space of large neural networks. For Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs), finding those interpretable directions in visual representations is important for interpretability. To find them, the classical approach is to train a linear classifier on the representations, but it needs a large amount of labels and only recovers what can be read from the representations, not what the model actually uses. In this paper we start from the following insight: The LLM Decoder of an LVLM is also a classifier whose classes are the possible next tokens. So we use its first-order Taylor expansion as a linear classifier. Hence, we introduce Decoder-as-a-Probe (DaP), which significantly outperforms classical probing and steering baselines especially when training data is sparse. (1) It verbalizes what an LVLM perceives in a given image. (2) It can surgically change the perception of the LVLM about specific attributes (e.g. change only the color, the clothes worn or the action done). Due to its simplicity and data efficiency, we believe DaP can serve as a plug-and-play tool for LVLM research, such as interpretability, hallucination mitigation or instruction tuning.

</details>


### [217] [PreserveReward: Reliable Consistency Reward Modeling for Image Editing via Dual-Teacher On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34446)
*Xueheng Li,Yong Liu,Wen Xue,Shi-Yu Tian,Zhuofan Xia,Chengjun Xie,Yipeng Sun,Simiu Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PreserveReward：结合视觉 On-Policy 自蒸馏（OPSD）的图像编辑一致性奖励模型，通过局部/联合双教师视图与校准蒸馏监督，提升细粒度视觉感知与内容保持评估的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令引导图像编辑中，模型常误改本应保持不变的内容，因此可靠的一致性评估对衡量内容保持和为强化学习提供奖励信号至关重要。现有奖励模型存在两类缺陷：忽略细微视觉变化，或无法区分必要修改与非预期改动。

Method: 以回归式打分损失从参考保持分数中学习；引入 On-Policy Self-Distillation，采用两个互补教师视图：局部教师用匹配裁剪块捕捉细微变化，联合教师融合局部细节与全局上下文判断变化是否符合编辑意图。为提升蒸馏监督可靠性，在参考分数误差约束下用最小 KL 投影校准教师分数分布，并依据与打分目标的梯度对齐度自适应选择教师过程监督，缓解打分偏差与优化冲突。

Result: 在多个基准上实验表明，PreserveReward 在非编辑内容保持方面与人类偏好高度对齐，并在多样编辑场景中具备良好泛化性。

Conclusion: 通过自蒸馏增强细粒度视觉感知并结合校准的教师监督，可显著提升图像编辑一致性奖励模型的可靠性，为 RL 奖励信号提供更可信的评估基础。

Abstract: Instruction-guided image editing has a wide range of applications, yet existing models may inadvertently alter content that should remain unchanged. Reliable consistency evaluation is therefore essential for assessing content preservation and providing reward signals for Reinforcement Learning (RL). However, existing reward models may overlook subtle visual changes or fail to distinguish necessary modifications from unintended alterations, limiting the reliability of consistency assessment. To address this challenge, we propose PreserveReward, a consistency reward model for image editing that integrates visual On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) to enhance fine-grained visual perception and consistency assessment. PreserveReward learns from reference preservation scores through a regression-based scoring loss and employs two complementary teacher views for self-distillation. The local teacher uses matched crops to identify subtle visual changes, while the joint teacher combines local details with global context to determine whether these changes align with the editing intent. To improve the reliability of distillation supervision, we calibrate teacher score distributions via minimum-KL projection under reference-score error constraints and adaptively select teacher process supervision based on gradient alignment with the scoring objective, mitigating scoring bias and optimization conflicts. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that PreserveReward achieves strong alignment with human preference on non-edited content preservation and generalizes well across diverse editing scenarios.

</details>


### [218] [Improving and Expanding Video Editing Capabilities Without Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34941)
*Ji Xie,Yanhao Jia,Xiangpeng Yang,Martin Q. Ma,Sihan Xu,Erik Cambria,Louis-Philippe Morency*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Image-to-Video Transfer (I2VT)，无需成对视频、无需改动架构，仅用单张图像让预训练视频编辑器"扮演"图像编辑器，把图像编辑能力迁移并扩展到视频编辑；并推出含四项新任务的OpenVE++基准，三个视频编辑器上的实验取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像编辑模型能遵循广泛指令，但视频编辑模型受限于难以收集成对的源视频与编辑后视频，能力落后。作者希望在不采集新成对视频的前提下，将先进图像编辑能力迁移到视频编辑器。

Method: I2VT：让视频编辑器以单帧视频方式编辑图像，在每步去噪中由图像编辑器引导视频编辑器的预测，实现无架构改动、无需新视频的高效后训练；推理时直接作用于完整视频片段并保持时序一致性。另构建OpenVE++，在OpenVE-Bench基础上新增动作变化、表情变化、肢体运动、场景重打光四项任务。

Result: 在三种不同视频编辑器上，I2VT均提升既有编辑能力并扩展到新任务；模型在OpenVE-Bench与OpenVE++上达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 借助图像编辑器作为监督信号的单图后训练，可高效迁移并扩展视频编辑能力，时序一致性得以保持，为视频编辑能力提升提供了低成本可扩展范式。

Abstract: Image editing models can follow a wide range of instructions, but video editing models still lag behind, limited in part by the difficulty of collecting paired source and edited videos. Is it possible to transfer advanced image editing capabilities to video editors without new paired videos? We introduce Image-to-Video Transfer (I2VT), which improves pretrained video editors and expands them to new editing tasks using an image editor. It enables efficient post-training with no architectural changes and no new videos, using only individual images. Our key idea is to let the video editor role-play as an image editor: it edits an image as a one-frame video, with the image editor guiding the video editor's predictions at each denoising step. Although the video editor sees only single images during training, at test time it applies the improved and expanded capabilities to whole video clips while preserving temporal consistency. To push the boundaries of video editing, we introduce OpenVE++, an extension of OpenVE-Bench that adds four new editing tasks useful for video production, including action changes, expression changes, body movements, and scene relighting. Our experiments across three different video editors show that I2VT improves existing editing capabilities and expands them to new tasks, and our models achieve state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance on both OpenVE-Bench and OpenVE++.

</details>


### [219] [LuxAvatar: Real-Time Relightable Head Avatars via Causal Neural Shading of 3D Intrinsic Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34940)
*Yipengjing Sun,Chenyang Wang,Boyao Zhou,Jiapeng Zhu,Ka Leong Cheng,Shengping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LuxAvatar：将头像合成分解为可动画的3D本征高斯代理与因果自回归神经着色器，通过异构蒸馏实现单图驱动的实时三维可控头像动画与重光照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单张肖像需同时实现三维可控驱动与复杂面部光传输，二者在实时性约束下难以兼顾：显式PBR计算代价高，纯生成模型缺乏三维可控性。

Method: (1) 本征高斯代理：解耦规范几何与反射率属性，将形变高斯快速光栅化为视角与运动一致的PBR缓冲区；(2) 因果自回归神经着色器：以PBR缓冲区为条件，把前向渲染建模为条件视频扩散过程，跳过显式PBR求解；(3) 异构蒸馏：将预训练多步双向重光照教师蒸馏为两步因果自回归学生，降低条件与解码延迟；(4) 构建混合重光照数据集进行领域微调。

Result: 实现3D可控的实时肖像动画与重光照，在视觉保真度、运动控制与光照准确性上优于SOTA。

Conclusion: 本征高斯代理与扩散着色器解耦的混合范式，配合蒸馏与领域微调，可有效兼顾三维可控性、光照真实性与实时流式性能。

Abstract: We present LuxAvatar, a feed-forward framework for real-time head avatar animation and relighting from a single portrait. To achieve both 3D controllability and complex facial light transport, we factorize avatar synthesis into an animatable 3D intrinsic Gaussian proxy and a causal autoregressive neural shader. This proxy disentangles canonical geometry and reflectance attributes, rapidly rasterizing deformed Gaussians into view- and motion-consistent PBR buffers. Conditioned on these buffers, our neural shader formulates forward rendering as a conditional video diffusion process, bypassing explicit PBR computation to synthesize realistic shading effects under target illumination. We achieve real-time streaming through heterogeneous distillation from a pretrained multi-step bidirectional relighting teacher to a two-step causal autoregressive neural shader, inheriting broad synthetic lighting priors while minimizing conditioning and decoding latency. To further specialize these priors to human heads, we construct a hybrid relighting dataset for domain-specific fine-tuning, capturing authentic facial dynamics and physically grounded light transport. Extensive experiments show that our framework enables 3D-controllable, real-time portrait animation and relighting, outperforming state-of-the-art methods in visual fidelity, motion control, and lighting accuracy.

</details>


### [220] [Diffusion Steering via Adaptive Reward Estimation and Resampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34839)
*Abinitha Gourabathina,Sana Tonekaboni,Marzyeh Ghassemi,Akbar Rafiey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练的 TrailMix 方法：在去噪过程中用并行"轨迹"生成低成本前瞻图，借外部评判器打分估计引导强度，通过重加权与重采样把概率质量向更对齐文本的结果再分配，从而提升文本-图像对齐与可控编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像扩散模型虽能响应复杂提示，但常遗漏所请求的物体、属性或关系。作者希望利用模型自身"可能结果"的分布来更可靠地生成与文本对齐的图像。

Method: 免训练框架：(1) 去噪时从并行轨迹（trails）生成低成本 lookahead 图像；(2) 用外部 judge 对图像-提示对齐打分；(3) 由奖励分数分布估计应对生成施加多强的引导以达目标对齐水平；(4) 据此对轨迹重加权与重采样，偏向更有希望的结果。并扩展至定向图像编辑，同时提出 SEAM-Bench 基准。

Result: 在三个文本到图像基准上优于现有基线，在罕见多物体组合上提升尤为显著；在 SEAM-Bench 与 PIE-Bench 上验证了其在受控图像编辑中的有效性（语义编辑可保持其余图像内容）。

Conclusion: 模型自身的可能结果分布可为可靠、可控的生成提供信号；通过概率质量再分配实现对齐提升，无需额外训练即可兼顾生成与编辑任务。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models can generate images that satisfy complex prompts, yet often produce images that omit requested objects, attributes, or relationships. We ask if models can more reliably generate text-aligned images by redistributing probability toward their more successful outcomes. We propose TrailMix, a training-free method that uses the base model's distribution of possible outcomes to adaptively guide generation toward better text alignment. During denoising, TrailMix generates inexpensive lookahead images from parallel trajectories (trails) and scores their alignment with the prompt using an external judge. The resulting distribution of reward scores allows us to estimate how strongly to steer generation toward a desired alignment level. We use these estimates to reweight and resample trajectories, favoring more promising outcomes. Across three text-to-image benchmarks, TrailMix outperforms existing baselines, with particularly strong gains on rare multi-object compositions. We further extend TrailMix to targeted image editing and introduce SEAM-Bench (Semantic Editing and Attribute Maintenance), a systematic benchmark evaluating whether semantic edits preserve other image content. Experiments on SEAM-Bench and existing benchmark PIE-Bench demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach for controlled image editing. Our results show how a model's own possible outcomes can inform reliable and controllable generation.

</details>


### [221] [Reading, Not Adapting: Unlocking Frozen Visual Knowledge for AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34719)
*Yuncheng Guo,Ying Dong,Xiaodong Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReMoT框架，在完全冻结的视觉基础模型中，从空间对齐的RGB与wavelet视图读取互补知识，仅3,074个可训练参数即在八组基准上取得91.2%平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型已编码可迁移的检测知识，而进一步的主干适配反而可能损害泛化能力，因此需要从"主干适配"转向"知识读出"。然而传统线性探针仅限最终层RGB特征；多线索检测器虽扩展证据来源，却存在适配破坏预训练可迁移性、异构编码器难以融合、后期融合缺乏中间层跨视图交互等问题。

Method: ReMoT受Mixture-of-Transformers启发，在完全冻结的视觉基础模型内，从空间对齐的RGB与wavelet两个视图读取互补知识；采用分阶段联合注意力实现受控的局部与全局跨视图交互；可学习的融合查询在Transformer各深度上渐进式读取互补证据，且不回写（no writeback）。

Result: 在八个基准组上平均准确率达91.2%，可训练参数仅3,074个，在所评估方法中取得最高总体平均表现。

Conclusion: 结构化知识读出可作为主干适配的有力替代方案，验证了冻结基础模型下多视图知识读出的有效性。

Abstract: Generalization to unseen image generators has driven efforts to adapt pretrained visual representations for detection. Yet recent findings show that vision foundation models already encode transferable detection knowledge, while further adaptation can impair generalization. This motivates a shift from backbone adaptation to knowledge readout. Conventional linear probing offers a direct way to read this knowledge but is limited to final-layer RGB features. Multi-cue detectors broaden the evidence base, but their designs introduce other tradeoffs: adaptation may compromise pretrained transferability, heterogeneous encoders complicate feature integration, and late fusion precludes intermediate cross-view interaction. To overcome these limitations, we propose ReMoT, a Mixture-of-Transformers-inspired framework that reads complementary knowledge from spatially aligned RGB and wavelet views within a fully frozen visual foundation. ReMoT employs staged joint attention to enable controlled local and global cross-view interaction, while learnable fusion queries progressively read complementary evidence across Transformer depth without writeback. ReMoT achieves 91.2% average accuracy across eight benchmark groups with 3,074 trainable parameters, attaining the highest overall average among the evaluated methods. These results support structured knowledge readout as a promising alternative to backbone adaptation. Code is available at https://github.com/yunncheng/ReMoT.

</details>


### [222] [SPGSR: A Small Pixel-Space Generator for One-Step Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34418)
*Yun Yue,Luming Liang,Yiqi Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPGSR是一个仅视觉的扩散/引导式超分框架，通过在LR图像上学习RGB残差校正，并用冻结DINOv2引导、轻量模块局部精修，以31.8M参数（VOSR-0.5B的6.5%）实现更优的PSNR/SSIM与LPIPS/DISTS，且推理显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有一步式超分依赖大规模视觉模型（至1.4B参数）从LR图像提取语义线索。作者质疑：LR图像本身已提供大量结构信息，是否可用远小的生成器达到相当质量。

Method: SPGSR在LR图像上叠加可学习的RGB校正量；冻结的DINOv2提供预测引导（语义先验），再由一个轻量块在局部范围内精修该校正量，整体为仅视觉、无文本/无T2I先验的单步框架。

Result: 在三个基准上PSNR/SSIM高于VOSR、LPIPS/DISTS更低；生成器31.8M，为VOSR-0.5B的6.5%；与T2I类SR方法质量相当；生成器训练+蒸馏仅用VOSR的0.60% HR像素；512×512输出下相对VOSR-0.5B/1.4B分别获2.80×/3.35× GPU流水线加速。

Conclusion: 超分生成器的规模可远小于当前趋势，将任务重新定义为对LR的残差式RGB校正并借助冻结语义特征引导，即可在质量、参数效率与速度上同时获益，挑战“大生成器即高保真”的假设。

Abstract: In one-step super-resolution (SR), vision-only models extract semantic cues directly from the low-resolution (LR) image, keeping those cues spatially aligned with the structures they help restore. Recent models achieve high restoration quality with generators of up to 1.4 billion parameters. Given how much structure the LR image already provides, could a much smaller generator achieve comparable quality? We introduce SPGSR, a vision-only framework that adds a learned RGB correction to the LR image. A frozen DINOv2 guides prediction, and a lightweight block refines the correction locally. SPGSR achieves higher PSNR/SSIM and lower LPIPS/DISTS than VOSR, a leading vision-only SR method, across three benchmarks. Its $31.8\mathrm{M}$-parameter generator is just $6.5\%$ the size of VOSR-0.5B's. SPGSR also achieves competitive quality against T2I-based SR methods. SPGSR's generator uses only $0.60\%$ of the HR pixels used to train and distill VOSR's generator. For $512\times512$ outputs, SPGSR achieves $2.80\times$ and $3.35\times$ GPU-pipeline speedups over VOSR-0.5B and VOSR-1.4B, respectively.

</details>


### [223] [PostMesh: Self-Rewarded Preference Post-Training for Flow-Based Artist Mesh Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34319)
*Gihyun Kwon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PostMesh是首个针对扩散/流式艺术网格生成器的后训练方法，无需昂贵的人工标注，通过自采样与基于稠密输入的奖励实现LoRA后训练，使快速生成器的网格质量媲美缓慢的自回归方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有艺术网格生成器存在速度与质量的矛盾：自回归方法逐token解码，每网格耗时数十秒至数百秒；扩散/流式方法虽快，但生成的网格质量达不到艺术家水准。同时，高质量艺术家标注数据获取成本高昂。

Method: 提出PostMesh后训练框架。设计"艺术网格奖励"（artistic mesh reward），从稠密输入与生成输出计算奖励，既保证输出与输入稠密几何的一致性，又将网格结构调整为艺术化风格；并采用多负样本流偏好优化（multi-negative flow preference optimisation）将该奖励迁移至生成器。整个流程依赖生成器自采样，无需人工真值，仅需短暂的LoRA后训练。

Result: 经短时LoRA后训练后，PostMesh在多数艺术网格质量指标及盲测VLM偏好测试中，与缓慢的自回归生成器相当或更优，而耗时仅为其一小部分。

Conclusion: PostMesh证明了后训练可有效提升快速扩散/流式艺术网格生成器的质量，在无需昂贵标注的前提下兼顾速度与艺术级质量，为艺术网格生成提供了一条高效可扩展的技术路线。

Abstract: Artistic mesh generators turn a dense 3D object into an artist mesh: a compact, quad-dominant mesh that is easy to edit. Most of current models are auto-regressive methods which decode a mesh token by token, take tens to hundreds of seconds per mesh. Diffusion- and flow-based generators are much faster, but their meshes still fall short of artist quality. To our knowledge, PostMesh is the first post-training method for a diffusion- or flow-based artist mesh generator, and it needs no expensive artist-made ground truth: the generator is trained on its own samples, scored by a reward computed from the dense input and the output. We propose an artistic mesh reward that keeps the output consistent with the dense input while making the mesh structure into artistic. We also propose a multi-negative flow preference optimisation that transfers the reward to the generator. After a short LoRA post-training, PostMesh is comparable to or better than slow autoregressive generators on most artist-mesh quality metrics and in a blind VLM preference test, at a fraction of their time.

</details>


### [224] [Focus Only on What's Important: Training-Free Focus Maps for Frozen Vision Transformers in Long-Video Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34863)
*Harsha Karipineni,Rajesh Reddy Chintha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出训练无关的"聚焦图"策略，在冻结DINOv3 ViT+Cutie头上按目标位置自适应分配分辨率，在LVOS V2长视频上以更少编码器FLOPs超过ALGM，但实际推理延迟未明显降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT编码器用于视频目标分割代价高；现有training-free token merging均匀压缩全帧，未区分跟踪目标与背景，导致目标区域也被粗化。仅保护上一帧mask在长视频中因快速运动失效，目标移出边界后进入背景合并，误差累积锁定。

Method: 在训练的Cutie头上挂接冻结DINOv3 ViT，构造免训练focus map：目标处全分辨率、其余区域粗粒度合并。策略取速度平移mask与块匹配mask的并集，执行相机补偿的"surprise"异常检测，将不确定区域交给对比的merger，全部目标丢失时回退到该merger。在LVOS V2、DAVIS 2017上以三个ViT-S种子做预注册协议评估，并做对照与计时分析。

Result: LVOS V2 valid：较ALGM 0.98提升+2.76 J&F（95% object CI [+1.22,+4.44]；video-cluster [+0.99,+4.52]），编码器FLOPs少16%（0.385 vs 0.459），距dense encoder基线差1.0 J&F；ViT-B上较ALGM 0.95提升+3.89 [+2.03,+5.87]。DAVIS 2017 val在ViT-S下差距-0.19 J&F（ViT-B -0.12）。对照显示仅保护上一帧mask在更高FLOPs（0.486）下精度即可持平或更优，说明expectation与surprise主要降低的是计数的编码器计算量。

Conclusion: 方法在精度与计数FLOPs上优于ALGM，但实际效率有限：12段LVOS样本中startup-inclusive平均Cutie步时未下降（稳态仅低3%–8%），同等工程化的ALGM在ViT-S/ViT-B上更快；Section 5.2另讨论Cutie永久记忆调度的延迟与内存权衡。

Abstract: Vision transformers are strong but costly image encoders for video object segmentation. Training-free token merging cuts compute uniformly across the frame, including on tracked objects. We give a frozen DINOv3 ViT inside a trained Cutie head a training-free focus map: full resolution where the segmenter expects its objects and coarser merging elsewhere. Protecting only the previous frame's mask works on short clips but fails on long videos, where fast motion pushes targets outside the margin and into background merging, locking in loss. Our policy instead takes the union of velocity-shifted and block-matched masks, runs a camera-compensated surprise check, delegates uncertain regions to the compared merger, and falls back to it when all objects are lost. On long videos (LVOS V2 valid, pre-specified protocol, three ViT-S seeds), it beats ALGM 0.98 by +2.76 J&F (95% object CI [+1.22,+4.44]; video-cluster [+0.99,+4.52]) at 16% fewer counted encoder FLOPs (0.385 vs. 0.459), sitting 1.0 J&F point below the dense-encoder baseline; at ViT-B the gain over ALGM 0.95 is +3.89 [+2.03,+5.87]. On DAVIS 2017 val it stays within -0.19 J&F of the dense encoder at ViT-S (-0.12 at ViT-B). Controls qualify the result: protecting only the previous mask under ALGM matches or exceeds accuracy at higher FLOPs (0.486), so expectation and surprise mainly reduce counted encoder compute. Practically, lower counted encoder compute did not reduce startup-inclusive mean Cutie step time on a 12-video LVOS sample (3%-8% lower in steady state; an equally engineered ALGM is faster at ViT-S and ViT-B). Section 5.2 separately examines a Cutie permanent-memory schedule and its latency and memory trade-offs. Code: https://github.com/boletoms/focused-cv

</details>


### [225] [Plausible Is Not Proven: Testing Failure Explanations in Monocular Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34393)
*Zeeshan Ali,Md Rayyan,Anshuman Chakravorty,Omer Faraz,Anshi Bajaj,Amit Saini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Vision Error Observatory (VEO) 审计流程，对单目深度模型的失败区域打上"疑似原因"标签，并用四项检验（标注一致性、盲评人类一致性、定向编辑响应、训练信号价值）验证标签可靠性；结论是绝大多数失败原因标签经不起检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目深度模型失败分析普遍为每个错误直接命名原因（遮挡边界、细薄物体、反射面等），但这些标签几乎从不被验证，其可信度未知。

Method: VEO 在传感器实测像素上定位未通过 δ1 判据的区域，为每块区域给出可能原因标签或弃权，然后施加四项检验：(1) 在标签模型未见过的数据上与物体标注比对；(2) 与盲评人类评分者比对；(3) 对图像做定向编辑观察响应；(4) 作为微调训练信号的价值。在 NYU Depth V2、DIODE、iBims-1 上评估 8 个模型，标签另在 Hypersim 上核查，多数判定规则在见结果前固定。

Result: 遮挡边界标签与标注一致（AUROC：NYU 0.85、Hypersim 0.81、iBims-1 0.69），但单一深度边缘线索效果相当，且最强模型多数边界失败在 2 像素容差下消失；反射标签无法迁移到合成数据；无纹理标签接近随机；四项编辑检验中仅一项探索性检验支持其标签；与 5 名盲评者对比，标签与人类共识仅达随机水平（Cohen's κ=0.05）；在诊断区域加权微调使目标像素误差在每个测试权重下均上升；先前版本声称的架构特异性失败指纹也未能跨数据集复现。

Conclusion: 看似合理的失败解释在被信任前必须经过实证检验；作者公开协议、代码、评分与全部结果文件以促进可复核的误差分析。

Abstract: Failure analyses of monocular depth models often name a cause for each error, such as an occluding boundary, a thin object or a reflective surface, but such labels are rarely tested. We present the Vision Error Observatory (VEO), an audit that finds regions failing the $δ_1$ criterion on sensor-measured pixels, labels each with a likely cause or abstains, and then subjects the labels to four tests: agreement with object annotations on data the label model never saw, agreement with blinded human raters, response to targeted image edits, and value as a training signal. We apply it to eight models on NYU Depth V2, DIODE and iBims-1, with labels also checked on Hypersim, and fix most decision rules before their results. The occluding-boundary label agrees with annotations (AUROC 0.85 on NYU, 0.81 on Hypersim, 0.69 on iBims-1), but a single depth-edge cue does as well, and most boundary failures of the strongest model vanish under a 2-pixel tolerance. The reflection label does not transfer to synthetic data, the textureless label is near chance against annotations, and only one of four edit tests, an exploratory one, supports its label. Evaluated against five blinded raters, VEO's labels matched the human consensus only at chance level (Cohen's $κ$ = 0.05). In addition, an increase in the weight on the diagnosed regions during fine-tuning led to an increase in the error on targeted pixels at every weight tested. The architecture-specific failure fingerprint claimed by an earlier version of this project also failed to replicate across the datasets. Plausible failure explanations, in short, need to be tested before they are trusted; we release the protocol, code, ratings and every result file.

</details>


### [226] [Commit2Drive: Perception-Grounded Language Decisions as Commitments for Trajectory Planning in Vision-Language-Action Driving Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34614)
*Zikai Zhang,Hubert P. Shum,Toby Breckon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Commit2Drive，将语言决策作为轨迹规划的一致性约束，用可微的"承诺"（机动类别+连续参数）统一决策与轨迹，在 Bench2Drive 上以 0.5B 轻量模型取得 DS 78.50、SR 55.91%，并显著降低语言-动作不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 自动驾驶模型中，语言决策或为独立输出、或仅作为相似候选轨迹的索引，且标注未与轨迹校验，导致闭环驾驶中语言决策与轨迹常常不一致，削弱了可解释性带来的可信度。

Method: 将决策定义为"承诺"：机动类别加连续参数，由确定性规则从任意轨迹读出，并基于模型自身感知事实接地；Maneuver Bank 将承诺映射为具运动学含义的码；一致性损失训练规划器执行场景中所有可采纳决策；自动数据管线用专家未来运动标注决策、以反事实执行联合训练决策与轨迹，并由规则函数配合 VLM 生成、审查与精炼语言标注；决策码为查表形式，不依赖语言模型隐状态。

Result: Bench2Drive 上 DS 78.50、SR 55.91%，与最先进方法具竞争力；语言-动作不一致大幅降低；0.5B 语言模型即可在单张消费级显卡上在线闭环推理；同一规划器无需重训即可执行规则式决策。

Conclusion: 以一致性约束而非松散耦合的方式建模语言决策，可在轻量模型下兼顾可解释性、可扩展性与闭环性能，为 VLA 驾驶中语言与行为的对齐提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models introduce language reasoning into end-to-end autonomous driving. They output a language decision together with a trajectory, which makes the planner more interpretable. However, in existing methods the language decision is either an independent output or an index that selects among similar candidate trajectories, and its annotations are not verified against the trajectory. As a result, the language decision and the trajectory are often inconsistent in closed-loop driving. To address this, we propose Commit2Drive, a framework that imposes the language decision as a consistency constraint on trajectory planning. The decision is a commitment, a maneuver class with a continuous parameter that one deterministic rule reads from any trajectory, and it is grounded in facts reported by the perception of the model itself. A Maneuver Bank maps the commitment to a code with a kinematic meaning, and consistency losses train the planner to execute every admissible decision of a scene, so that the trajectory follows the decision. An automatic data pipeline supplies the supervision. Decisions are labeled from the future motion of the expert, counterfactual executions train the decision and the trajectory together, and rule-based functions with a Vision-Language Model (VLM) generate, review and refine the language annotations. The code is a lookup that involves no hidden state of the language model, so Commit2Drive is extensible to rule-based decisions, which the same planner executes without retraining. Commit2Drive uses a 0.5B language model and runs closed-loop inference online on a single consumer graphics card. On Bench2Drive, Commit2Drive reaches a Driving Score (DS) of 78.50 and a Success Rate (SR) of 55.91% with a lightweight model, which is competitive with the state of the art, and greatly reduces the language-action inconsistency. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [227] [You Only Look Multiple Times: YoloMT, a Real-Time Streaming Model on a T4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34601)
*Nessim Ben Abbes,Duc Han Le,Sabri Mtibaa,Van-Tam Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: YoloMT：449.8M 参数的单卡实时流式视频描述模型，每帧仅编码一次并缓存为 16 个 token，通过 SFT+GRPO 从 27B 教师蒸馏，在 OVO-Bench、MVBench、StreamingBench、Video-MME 上领先全部 1B 以下模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时流式视频描述需要处理大量帧，但重复视觉编码代价过高，现有生成式视觉模型在单 GPU 实时约束下难以兼顾效率与效果。作者希望以低成本读取更多帧，并让每帧表征信息密度更高。

Method: 提出 YoloMT 流式描述器：每帧只做一次视觉编码并压缩缓存为 16 个 token，使模型可在无需额外视觉前向的情况下利用与推断历史帧；训练上先以监督微调（SFT）再用 GRPO 从 27B 教师模型蒸馏，并给出分阶段训练流程、数据配方及各组件的消融实验。

Result: 在参数量同类的生成式视觉模型中达到 Pareto 前沿；在 OVO-Bench、MVBench、StreamingBench 与 Video-MME（基于其生成字幕作答）上优于所有 1B 以下模型，并超过 LFM2.5-VL-1.6B 与 SmolVLM2-2.2B。

Conclusion: 单帧单次编码 + 16-token 缓存是高效流式字幕生成的可行范式，配合蒸馏与强化学习可在小参数规模下取得领先性能；论文同时公开训练配方与消融，验证各组件贡献。

Abstract: We introduce a new model for real-time streaming captioning on a single GPU. YoloMT is a 449.8M-parameter streaming captioner that provides Pareto-frontier performance among generative vision models in its class. Distilled from a 27B teacher by SFT and GRPO, it leads every model under 1B parameters on OVO-Bench, MVBench, StreamingBench and Video-MME, answered from its captions, and beats LFM2.5-VL-1.6B and SmolVLM2-2.2B. YoloMT is designed to read more frames cheaply and make them informative, encoding each frame once and caching it as 16 tokens. This allows the model to incorporate and infer from past frames without additional vision passes, making it efficient and effective. We also provide a detailed recipe for training YoloMT, with its stages and data, as well as ablations that demonstrate the contribution of each component.

</details>


### [228] [Rank Before You Encode: Reading a Few Percent of a Whole-Slide Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34523)
*Haoyu He,Mahdi S. Hosseini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种免训练的“冻结全图模型+轻量选块”高效推理基线：用仅3.3K参数的轻量选择器匹配冻结模型的注意力来筛选tile，在匹配编码成本下优于随机采样与九种已训练高效方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全切片图像（WSI）分类需对数千个高分辨率tile编码，推理成本主要由编码器主导。现有高效方法通过少选tile降本，但通常需围绕其选择策略重新训练模型，成本高且缺乏与“冻结模型做同样降本”的直接对比基线。

Method: 保持现有full-bag MIL模型冻结，仅在固定编码成本下运行于被选中的tile上。选块器为3.3K参数轻量网络，用冻结模型的注意力作为监督（无需额外切片标签）：先从缩略图排序区域，按5x放大编码前K个区域，再在20x下从中选出b个送入模型。以随机采样作为匹配成本的对照，并在相同特征与划分下与九种高效方法比较；在七个冻结MIL聚合器、六个任务上评估。

Result: 预算(K,b)=(1000,10)时编码成本约为全图6.6%，推理快10–14倍（不含切块），36个聚合器-任务对中33个AUROC下降≤0.005；(500,10)时成本约4%、加速13–22倍，但CAMELYON17上AUROC最多下降0.035（小结节易漏检）。含局灶性病变任务上选块较随机采样提升约0.10 AUROC，扩散性任务随机采样表现良好。冻结模型在(500,10)下于60组配对比较中的55组领先九种高效方法。

Conclusion: “给现有WSI模型更少tile”应作为新高效方法在匹配编码成本下的必备基线；选块收益依赖病变类型（局灶 vs 扩散），极小病灶场景下激进降本存在风险。

Abstract: Classifying a whole-slide image requires encoding thousands of high-resolution tiles with a pathology foundation model, which accounts for most of the inference cost. Existing efficient methods reduce this cost by selecting fewer tiles, but typically train a new model around their selection strategy. A largely unexplored alternative is to keep an existing full-bag model frozen and run it on selected tiles at the same encoder cost. We introduce this comparison, alongside random tile sampling at matched cost. To select tiles, we fit a lightweight selector with 3.3K parameters to the attention of a frozen model, without additional slide labels. The selector ranks regions from a slide thumbnail, encodes the top K regions at 5x magnification, and selects b for processing at 20x. We evaluate seven frozen MIL aggregators on six tasks and compare them with nine efficient methods trained on the same features and splits. At a budget of (K,b)=(1000,10), encoding costs about 6.6% of the full bag and is 10-14x faster, excluding tiling. The AUROC drop is no more than 0.005 in 33 of 36 aggregator-task pairs. At (500,10), the cost falls to about 4% and inference is 13-22x faster, but AUROC drops by up to 0.035 on CAMELYON17, where small metastases are easily missed. Selection improves over random sampling by about 0.10 AUROC on tasks with focal lesions, while random sampling performs well on diffuse tasks. In our comparisons, the frozen model at (500,10) is ahead of nine efficient methods in 55 of 60 paired comparisons. Giving an existing WSI model fewer tiles is thus a baseline that a new efficient model should be compared with at matched encoder cost.

</details>


### [229] [TACC: Target-Aware Carrier Compression for Dense-Context Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34476)
*Yiming Fan,Yijia Guo,Zewen Guo,Liwen Hu,Yuheng Yuan,Yang Li,Zhiwei Li,Xitong Ling,Caihong Yuan,Shengbo Chen,Tiejun Huang,Lei Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TACC: a target-aware compression module for feed-forward latent NVS that uses a progressive carrier hierarchy and geometry-driven selective token retrieval to produce compact, target-conditioned source representations.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Feed-forward NVS eliminates per-scene optimization but relies on global scene-token context, causing high memory and latency as source views become dense; generic compression is target-agnostic and cannot adapt evidence allocation to each target view.

Method: Organize each source view into a progressive carrier hierarchy; use source-target geometric relevance to adaptively retrieve tokens and allocate exact computational quotas, yielding a compact target-conditioned representation.

Result: On RE10K with dense inputs, TACC achieves 9.23x (LagerNVS) and 64x (LVSM) higher Effective FPS than Full Context; Effective FPS excludes one-time source encoding and carrier construction.

Conclusion: Target-aware, geometry-guided token compression is an effective route to scale feed-forward latent NVS to dense source-view settings at much lower memory and latency cost.

Abstract: Recent feed-forward approaches mark a paradigm shift in novel view synthesis by eliminating the reliance on per-scene optimization, directly inferring 3D representations from input views. However, their reliance on global scene-token context incurs substantial memory and latency overhead, constraining their ability to handle increasingly dense source-view contexts. Compressing source representations offers an intuitive way to limit this token budget. Prior general-purpose compression methods are not tailored for novel view synthesis, as their fixed, target-agnostic nature fails to adapt evidence allocation to individual target views. To overcome this, we introduce TACC, a target-aware compression module tailored for feed-forward latent NVS. Specifically, TACC organizes each source view into a progressive carrier hierarchy. Driven by source-target geometric relevance, it adaptively retrieves tokens to dynamically allocate exact computational quotas. This selective retrieval directly yields a compact, target-conditioned representation. Extensive experiments on the RE10K demonstrate that under dense input settings, TACC achieves $9.23\times$ and $64\times$ higher Effective FPS than Full Context on LagerNVS and LVSM, respectively. Effective FPS excludes one-time source encoding and Carrier construction.

</details>


### [230] [Attacking Hard-Label Large Vision-Language Models with Model-Sensitive Adversarial Patch Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34467)
*Nian Ai,Guangke Chen,Xiaowen Cai,Zhongliang Guo,Daizong Liu,Pan Zhou,Ognjen Arandjelović*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HardPatch——一种仅在硬标签（查询）设定下针对大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）生成视觉对抗补丁的黑盒攻击方法，通过图像分块掩码评估敏感性、选最脆弱补丁并用随机噪声估计梯度迭代优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM对抗攻击依赖反向传播梯度理想化优化扰动，但真实LVLM应用不提供梯度或模型细节，导致可扩展性受限；本文针对这一"反实践"现象，研究硬标签设定下的可行攻击路径。

Method: 1) 将每张图像均匀划分为补丁，逐个掩码以单独评估各补丁对模型的敏感度；2) 按敏感度分数降序迭代选择最脆弱补丁，用噪声初始化并借助叠加随机噪声的有限差分方式估计梯度进行优化；3) 对多个补丁依次扰动，直至修改后图像满足对抗条件。

Result: 在多个LVLM模型与数据集上评估，验证了HardPatch的对抗有效性；经验观察表明合适的补丁替换与优化可在硬标签设定下构造有效对抗图像。

Conclusion: HardPatch表明LVLM在局部视觉区域存在可利用脆弱性，硬标签黑盒条件下亦可实施有效对抗补丁攻击，为LVLM鲁棒性评估提供了更贴近实际的范式。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities in handling multi-modal downstream tasks, gaining increasing popularity. However, recent studies show that LVLMs are susceptible to both intentional and inadvertent attacks. Existing attackers ideally optimize adversarial perturbations with backpropagated gradients from LVLMs, thus limiting their scalability in practical scenarios as real-world LVLM applications will not provide any LVLM's gradient or details. Motivated by this research gap and counter-practical phenomenon, we propose a novel hard-label attack method for LVLMs, named HardPatch, to generate visual adversarial patches by solely querying the model. Our method provides deeper insights into how to investigate the vulnerability of LVLMs in local visual regions and generate corresponding adversarial substitution under the practical yet challenging hard-label setting. Specifically, we first split each image into uniform patches and mask each of them to individually assess their sensitivity to the LVLM model. Then, according to the descending order of sensitive scores, we iteratively select the most vulnerable patch to initialize noise and estimate gradients with further additive random noises for optimization. In this manner, multiple patches are perturbed until the altered image satisfies the adversarial condition. Extensive LVLM models and datasets are evaluated to demonstrate the adversarial nature of HardPatch. Our empirical observations suggest that with appropriate patch substitution and optimization, HardPatch can craft effective adversarial images to attack hard-label LVLMs.

</details>


### [231] [WorldExpand: Building Coherent 3D Worlds via Sparse Joint Panoramic Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34810)
*Yuyang Yin,Chenjie Cao,Xuhui Zuo,Yuning Gong,Pengfei Wang,Junta Wu,Zhenwei Wang,Yao Zhao,Tengfei Wang,Yunchao Wei,Chunchao Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldExpand将单张全景图的世界扩展重构为"稀疏、互补、高分辨率全景观测"的生成问题，通过覆盖引导视角选择与联合全景扩散生成位姿全景，快速构建显式3D场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单张全景图存在大量未观测区域，难以构建完整3D世界。现有全景视频方法依赖密集序列生成扩展覆盖，但相邻帧冗余严重、长轨迹误差累积、独立生成的不同方向彼此不一致，且成本高。

Method: 1) 将世界扩展表述为稀疏、互补、高分辨率全景观测的生成，把生成预算集中于信息量高的区域；2) Coverage-Guided View Selection：用场景自适应象限与warp-based覆盖率得分定位信息量高的目标视角；3) Joint Panoramic Diffusion：基于高斯泼溅的几何warp联合合成四张位姿对齐、跨视角一致的全景图；4) 诊断引导的global–local注意力调度，仅在敏感层保留全局注意力，降低高分辨率联合生成训练与推理开销；5) 生成的带位姿全景图直接接入现有重建管线得到显式3D表示。

Result: 实验表明WorldExpand能恢复遮挡物后方与远处转角的内容，在多个数据集上提升新视角合成与下游3D重建指标，并对多样in-the-wild场景具有稳健泛化性；相比SOTA全景视频方法显著更快，同时输出更高分辨率、跨视角一致性更强的观测。

Conclusion: 以稀疏互补高分辨率全景观测替代冗余密集序列生成，是单全景世界扩展的有效范式：兼顾细节保真、跨视角一致性与效率，并可直接服务显式3D重建。

Abstract: Constructing complete 3D worlds from a single panorama is challenging because large portions of the scene remain unobserved. Existing panoramic-video methods expand spatial coverage through dense sequential generation, but neighboring frames contain substantial redundancy, long trajectories accumulate errors, and independently generated directions may be inconsistent. We present WorldExpand, which formulates single-panorama world expansion as the generation of sparse, complementary, and high-resolution panoramic observations. This representation concentrates the generation budget on informative scene regions and preserves the fine-grained details required for 3D construction. WorldExpand addresses two key challenges: where to observe and how to synthesize. Our Coverage-Guided View Selection identifies informative target locations using scene-adaptive quadrants and warp-based coverage scores, while our Joint Panoramic Diffusion uses Gaussian-splatting-based geometric warps to jointly synthesize four pose-grounded and cross-view-consistent panoramas. A diagnostic-guided global--local attention schedule retains global attention only in sensitive layers, reducing the training and inference costs of high-resolution joint generation. The resulting posed panoramas can be directly fed into an existing reconstruction pipeline to build an explicit 3D representation. Experiments show that WorldExpand recovers content behind occluders and around distant corners, improves both novel-view synthesis and downstream 3D reconstruction metrics across various datasets, and generalizes robustly to diverse in-the-wild scenes. WorldExpand runs significantly faster than the state-of-the-art panoramic-video method while producing higher-resolution observations with stronger cross-view consistency.

</details>


### [232] [Generation as Reverse Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34782)
*Cheng Shi,Sibei Yang,Hanzhong Guo,Yipeng Qin,Pietro Lio,Yizhou Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 RevFlow，将生成视为“逆向编码”：以冻结编码器的多层中间表示为逐级生成目标，并设计共享骨干的 HyC-DiT 统一生成模型完成各阶段转换，从而加速学习与采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉表示虽能加速生成学习，但抽象掉外观与空间细节后使“回到像素”的任务更依赖大型解码器；作者质疑：编码器已提供像素到语义的学习路径，表示到像素的路径为何要独立学习。

Method: 以冻结编码器的中间表示作为分层生成目标（先深层语义、再保留外观与空间细节的浅层，最后解码到像素）；为避免为每个阶段单独训练生成器，提出 HyC-DiT——共享标准 DiT 骨干、并通过阶段特定条件注入实现多阶段统一建模；并利用语义表示空间中的 native matching 实现少步生成。

Result: 在 ImageNet 256×256 上，仅训练 5 个 epoch 即达 690M 模型 1.96 FID、1.55B 模型 1.74 FID；以 89M 解码器取得 30.25 dB 重建 PSNR；RevFlow-XL 经短暂 native-matching 适配后，每阶段单步采样达到 2.85 FID。

Conclusion: 复用编码器路径的逆向生成范式可同时加速学习与采样，并以较小解码器实现高保真重建，验证了统一多阶段生成与语义空间匹配在少步生成上的有效性。

Abstract: Pretrained visual representations accelerate generative learning by abstracting away appearance and spatial detail. Yet the same abstraction makes the return to pixels far harder, shifting much of the burden onto a larger decoder. We ask why the representation-to-pixel path should be learned independently when the encoder already provides a learned pixel-to-semantics path (the semantic encoding path). We introduce RevFlow, based on the principle of generation as reverse encoding. RevFlow uses intermediate representations of a frozen encoder as successive generative targets, first generating deep semantic representations, then shallow representations that retain appearance and spatial detail, before decoding to pixels. To learn these transitions without training a separate generator for each stage, we develop HyC-DiT, a unified generative model with a standard DiT backbone shared across stages and stage-specific condition injection. We further show that native matching in semantic representation space enables few-step generation. RevFlow accelerates both learning and sampling. On ImageNet $256 \times 256$, RevFlow reaches 1.96 FID at 690M and 1.74 FID at 1.55B after only 5 training epochs. It also achieves 30.25 dB reconstruction PSNR with an 89M decoder. After a short native-matching adaptation phase, RevFlow-XL achieves 2.85 FID using a single sampling step in each stage. Our code repository is at https://github.com/ChengShiest/RevFlow

</details>


### [233] [What a Drop Reveals: Sim-to-Real Material Estimation for Deformable Objects from Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34894)
*Ziyue Peng,Yanxi Hu,Junyoung Kim,Andrew Chen,Jin Yang,Chandrajit L. Bajaj,Etienne Vouga,William T. Freeman,Qixing Huang,Brandon Y. Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PhysReader，用纯仿真训练的预测器从单个真实落体视频估计归一化刚度与屈服应力，发现2D光流轨迹迁移性优于3D轨迹，并发布PhysReader-Real基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实落体视频无材料标签，预测器只能在仿真中训练验证；然而仿真验证会错误地为几何输入排序（3D轨迹在仿真中信息量最高却迁移最差），因此需要研究何种输入在两个域中一致可测。

Method: 以仿真数据训练前馈预测器，比较2D光流tracklet与经深度提升的3D tracklet等输入；PhysReader读取视频与2D tracklet，并在仿真中通过匹配"可见精确3D形变的教师"学习向3D的提升；同时构建PhysReader-Real数据集（16个物体各下落5次，参考材料参数由多帧联合拟合并经拉伸测试校验）。

Result: 3D tracklet在仿真中最准但真实视频中最差，且提升深度质量（仿真深度/LiDAR/渲染精确深度）均无法修复；2D tracklet在仿真中无增益却迁移最佳。在PhysReader-Real上，PhysReader比所有其他输入和基线更接近参考材料（每视频12秒），仅逐场景优化器略优但需49分钟。

Conclusion: 材料属性应从两个域中被感知一致测量的量（2D测量 + 仿真学习的内在3D提升）中读取，而非仿真中最具信息量的量；该结论对未来仿真到现实迁移的物理感知设计具有普遍意义。

Abstract: How an object deforms when it hits the ground reveals what it is made of. From a single real video of a drop, we estimate a deformable object's density-normalized stiffness $E/ρ$, and its yield stress $σ_Y/ρ$ when the impact causes plastic deformation, with a feed-forward predictor trained only in simulation. Real videos carry no material labels, so such predictors are developed and validated on synthetic data alone. We show that synthetic validation ranks their geometric inputs in the wrong order. Material acts through 3D deformation, and tracklets lifted to 3D with estimated depth are indeed the most accurate input in simulation. However, on real drops they are the least accurate, and better depth does not repair them: the failure persists with simulator depth in training, LiDAR depth at test time, and exact depth on rendered replays of the real drops. Short 2D optical-flow tracklets do the opposite: they improve nothing in simulation, yet they transfer best. Material is therefore better read from what perception measures consistently in both domains than from what is most informative in simulation. Our predictor, PhysReader, reads the video and 2D tracklets and learns the lift to 3D in simulation by matching a teacher that sees exact 3D deformation. To measure transfer, we introduce PhysReader-Real: 16 everyday objects dropped five times each, with reference materials fit jointly to each object's drops and checked against tensile tests. On PhysReader-Real, PhysReader is closer to the reference materials than every other input and baseline. The one exception is a per-scene optimizer started from PhysReader's prediction, which ends slightly closer but takes 49 minutes per video instead of 12 seconds.

</details>


### [234] [Stranger Things: When Objects Appear Without Their Typical Neighbors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34881)
*Siddhartha Gairola,Jiahao Xie,Anna Kukleva,Francesco Locatello,Bernt Schiele*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统诊断现代视觉模型中普遍存在的"典型性偏差"(typicality bias)：模型依赖目标共现语境而非自身视觉特征进行识别。作者提出基于NPMI的度量与STRANGE-Bench基准，覆盖5类模型、3类任务，发现偏差普遍存在且不随规模缩小，并提出Anchor-Atypicality Sampling (AAS)进行缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语境虽有助于识别，但过度依赖会导致模型依据典型共现邻居而非物体本身特征做判断（typicality bias）。该问题在Transformer前有研究，但学界普遍假设架构与规模提升会自然解决，缺乏对现代视觉技术栈的系统性诊断。

Method: 1) 用归一化点互信息(NPMI)在物体对上量化典型性偏差；2) 构建STRANGE-Bench——写实、布局可控的合成基准，涵盖从典型到从未共现的17个子层级；3) 在5类模型族(专用检测器、开放词汇检测器、自监督、视觉语言模型、多模态大模型)与3类任务(监督检测、开放词汇检测、分类)上评估；4) 提出AAS，一种用于粘贴式增强的简单采样规则，利用NPMI信号将训练时共现分布偏向非典型搭配。

Result: 偏差普遍存在：物体出现在非典型语境时性能下降5–11个百分点，且不随模型规模一致缩小。AAS在自然图像COCO划分上最多缩小偏差1.6 p.p.，在合成STRANGE-Bench上最多缩小23.0 p.p.，覆盖三个模型族，且不损失整体性能。

Conclusion: 典型性偏差是现代视觉栈的结构性缺陷，规模提升不能自动消除；需通过针对共现捷径的数据增强干预。AAS以极简采样规则有效缓解该偏差，且不牺牲整体性能，为提升语境鲁棒性提供了可推广方向。

Abstract: Visual context is a powerful cue for object recognition: scenes inform what, where, and at what scale objects are likely to appear. Yet over-reliance on context becomes a failure mode when models predict objects based on their typical neighbors rather than the objects' own visual features, which we call typicality bias. While extensively studied in the pre-Transformer era, it has often been assumed that architectural and scale improvements would naturally solve it, and a systematic diagnostic across the modern vision stack remains absent. We provide such a diagnostic framework. We measure typicality bias via normalized pointwise mutual information (NPMI) over object pairs, and introduce STRANGE-Bench (Synthetic Typicality-RANked Grounded Evaluation Benchmark): a photorealistic, layout-controlled benchmark spanning 17 sub-tiers from typical to never-co-occurring object pairs. Across five model families (specialist detectors, open-vocabulary detectors, self-supervised, vision-language, and multimodal large language models) and three recognition tasks (supervised detection, open-vocabulary detection, and classification), the bias is prevalent: gaps of 5-11 p.p. persist when objects appear outside their typical company, and do not consistently shrink with scale. To mitigate it, we turn to data augmentation: existing augmentations are designed for visual diversity, not for breaking co-occurrence shortcuts, and sample from the very distribution that produces typicality bias. We propose Anchor-Atypicality Sampling (AAS), a simple sampling rule for paste-based augmentations that uses the NPMI signal to tilt the training-time co-occurrence distribution toward atypical partners. AAS reduces the typicality gap by up to 1.6 p.p. on natural-image COCO splits and 23.0 p.p. on synthetic STRANGE-Bench across three model families, without sacrificing overall performance.

</details>


### [235] [Towards Closing the Performance Gap in Cross-Modal Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34877)
*Ofer Idan,Dan Badur,Yoli Shavit*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CrossPlace两阶段跨模态地点识别框架，通过更高视觉token粒度、强文本编码器与局部跨模态匹配，显著提升文本到图像地点检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型在跨模态地点识别（CMPR）任务上远落后于视觉地点识别（VPR）基线，即便经过任务适配亦然。与通用文本-图像检索不同，地点识别依赖细微局部线索区分视觉相似地点，因此核心瓶颈在于图像区域与文本细节间的细粒度对应能力，这需要更高的视觉token粒度、强文本表征能力与局部跨模态匹配。

Method: 两阶段框架：Stage 1进行全局检索，采用联合对齐的DINOv2-B/14视觉主干与渐进式微调的BGE-Large文本编码器；Stage 2使用轻量级token级交叉注意力重排序器对Top候选进行精化。同时通过系统消融隔离检索精度驱动因素，并将评测扩展至城市尺度及恶劣天气与光照场景。

Result: 视觉token密度带来最大性能提升，文本编码器容量与任务微调提供互补增益；在所有现有及新增基准上，Recall@1相比先前跨模态方法实现两位数提升，缩小了与单模态文本检索的差距，并进一步逼近单模态VPR。

Conclusion: 细粒度局部跨模态对应是CMPR的关键瓶颈，提升视觉token粒度配合强文本编码器与token级重排序可大幅缩小跨模态与单模态地点识别的性能差距，且方法在城市尺度与恶劣环境条件下具备鲁棒性。

Abstract: Cross-modal place recognition (CMPR) matches natural language descriptions against databases of geo-tagged reference images, enabling localization without visual queries. Despite its potential for language-guided navigation, existing vision-language models lag far behind visual place recognition (VPR) baselines, even after task adaptation. Unlike general-purpose text-to-image retrieval, place recognition relies on subtle local cues to distinguish visually similar locations. We therefore hypothesize that a primary bottleneck in CMPR is capturing fine-grained correspondences between image regions and text details, which requires higher visual token granularity, strong text representation capacity, and local cross-modal matching. To address this challenge, we propose CrossPlace, a two-stage CMPR framework. Stage 1 performs global retrieval by pairing a jointly aligned DINOv2-B/14 visual backbone with a progressively fine-tuned BGE-Large text encoder, while Stage 2 refines top candidates using a lightweight token-level cross-attention reranker. Through systematic analysis, we isolate the drivers of retrieval accuracy and show that visual token density provides the largest performance improvement, while text encoder capacity and task fine-tuning offer complementary gains. To evaluate robustness under realistic conditions, we also extend CMPR evaluation to city-scale and severe weather and illumination settings. Across all existing and new benchmarks, CrossPlace achieves double-digit Recall@1 improvements over prior cross-modal methods, closes the gap to unimodal text retrieval, and narrows it to unimodal VPR. Code and benchmarks are available at https://github.com/oferidan1/CrossPlace

</details>


### [236] [ReMeDi: Single-Image PBR Material Estimation via Reparameterization between Metal and Dielectric Hypotheses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34284)
*Fan Fei,Xin Huang,Yixin Zhu,Jiawei Lu,Xin Yang,Jinglin Liang,Ruining Tang,Yifei Feng,Boxin Shi,Chunchao Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReMeDi，通过对基础色进行金属度加权重参数化并用双网络分别强制满足全金属/全介电假设，实现金属度与基础色的显式匹配，提升PBR材质估计精度与重光照保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PBR材质估计中，反照率（base color）的取值依赖于表面是金属还是介电体，因此生成任何反照率图都隐含一个"预期金属度"决策。若maps的预期金属度与实际不匹配，会渲染出物理上不合理或被误解释的材质。metallic–roughness工作流虽使金属度显式化并鼓励base color与之匹配，但并不强制该匹配，对逐图直接预测的自动化方法尤为严重。

Method: ReMeDi通过两个组件强制匹配：(1) 将base color重参数化为两个变体的金属度加权混合，两个变体分别在全金属与全介电假设下估计；(2) 由于现有监督无法让单一网络在每个像素上同时学习两种假设，改用两个独立网络各自限定于一种假设，确保每个变体遵循其对应假设。

Result: 实验表明ReMeDi在材质精度与重光照保真度两方面均优于现有最先进方法；同时，强制匹配的机制还改善了每个单独估计的材质图质量。

Conclusion: 将金属度的"预期"以加权混合形式显式编码进base color参数化，并配合假设分离的双网络训练，是保证PBR材质物理一致性的有效方案，且带来双重收益：既对齐金属度与基础色，又提升各材质图的估计质量。

Abstract: Physically based rendering (PBR) material estimation facilitates relighting, material editing, and 3D content creation. As the reflectance of a surface heavily depends on whether it is a metal or a dielectric, producing any map that determines albedo involves a decision on metalness, which we call the "intended metalness" of the map. Rendering maps with mismatched intended metalness yields physically implausible or misinterpreted materials. The metallic--roughness workflow makes metalness explicit and thus encourages base color to match it, but does not enforce this match, especially for automated methods directly predicting each map. Therefore, we propose ReMeDi, which enforces the match through two components. First, we reparameterize base color as a metalness-weighted blend of its two variants, estimated under the all-metal and all-dielectric hypothesis, respectively. Second, as available supervision cannot teach a single network both hypotheses at every pixel, we restrict each of two separate networks to one hypothesis, so that each variant is ensured to follow its hypothesis. Besides enforcing the match, this also improves each estimated material map. Experiments show that ReMeDi outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both material accuracy and relighting fidelity.

</details>


### [237] [VibeVideoAgent: Closing the Perceptual Loop for Code-Driven Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34237)
*Xuanyu Zheng,Luoyuan Zhang,Yancheng Long,Haoyang Xu,Muxi Diao,Yuzhe Chen,Kaiyu Jiang,Bin Wen,Tingting Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VibeVideoAgent 是一个免训练框架，让编码智能体在生产过程中通过隔离的短时多模态感知上下文和七个原语，对草稿进行细粒度检查，从而在代码驱动的视频生成中提升整体视听质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码驱动视频生成中，程序可正常退出却仍产生标签重叠、镜头偏离主体、交互断裂、旁白失同步等无运行时错误的缺陷。将渲染帧与音频回灌编码会话会膨胀长程上下文，而仅对最终全分辨率成品进行评审则使早期设计错误变为昂贵重写。

Method: 免训练框架。多模态感知运行于与编码器分离的短生命周期上下文，返回紧凑、有依据的 Findings。七个原语作用于统一的资产与草稿多时钟表示：由粗到细探索媒体、以各必需模态证据核验引用需求、在独立时钟上比较草稿与参考或早期版本的差异、局部度量流信号。配合轻量分阶段协议，先在最小预览上测试最高风险决策。

Result: 在全部 239 个 VibeVideoBench 任务上，匹配智能体运行时与固定感知模型下，L3 整体视听质量：Claude Opus 5.5 由 4.03 升至 4.43（L2 评分表合规率 98.87%→99.35%），GPT-6 Astra 由 4.03 升至 4.27，Claude Sonnet 5.5 由 3.97 升至 4.10；音画同步提升最大达 +0.56。消融实验表明各组件均有贡献，轨迹审计显示代码编辑粒度决定评分表遵循与感知质量间的权衡。

Conclusion: 在生产全程引入受控、紧凑的感知反馈可有效缓解代码驱动视频生成中的静默缺陷，且不依赖额外训练；编辑粒度是平衡规范遵循与感知质量的关键控制点。

Abstract: Code-driven video generation asks a coding agent to write and run rendering programs. This gives exact control over typography, data visualizations, synchronized audio, and multi-shot scenes, and it leaves an editable project behind. A program that exits cleanly can still render overlapping labels, a camera aimed away from its subject, broken object interactions, or narration that drifts out of sync, and none of these raise a runtime error. Feeding rendered frames and audio back into the coding session bloats its long-horizon context, while critiquing only the finished full-resolution render turns early design mistakes into costly rewrites. We introduce VibeVideoAgent, a training-free framework that lets coding agents inspect their drafts throughout production. Multimodal perception runs in short-lived contexts kept apart from the coder and returns compact, grounded Findings. Seven primitives operate on a unified multi-clock representation of assets and drafts: they explore media from coarse to fine, verify quoted requirements with evidence from every required modality, compare a draft with a reference or an earlier revision on separate clocks, and measure stream signals locally. A lightweight staged protocol has the agent test its riskiest decision on a minimal preview first. On all 239 VibeVideoBench tasks, with matched agent runtimes and a fixed perception model, VibeVideoAgent improves holistic audiovisual quality (L3) for Claude Opus 5.5 (4.03 -> 4.43, while L2 rubric compliance rises from 98.87% to 99.35%), GPT-6 Astra (4.03 -> 4.27), and Claude Sonnet 5.5 (3.97 -> 4.10), with the largest gains in audio-visual synchronization (up to +0.56). Controlled ablations show that each component contributes, and trajectory audits show that the granularity of code edits governs the trade-off between rubric adherence and perceptual quality.

</details>


### [238] [A Calibration-Referenced Protocol for Measuring Decision Stability under Reference Bank Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34134)
*Yiwen Chen,Shengming Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出四步评估协议，将工业图像异常检测中参考库压缩方法与校准参考的决策不一致性联系起来，发现在10%预算下PQ在多骨干/数据集上决策不一致最低，且AUROC损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参考库需压缩以适应部署预算，但压缩后的异常判定同时取决于压缩分数与运行阈值，现有比较未在匹配部署规模下、也未以校准参考衡量决策层面的影响。

Method: 四步协议：在匹配部署尺寸下比较压缩方法；用200次重校准建立参考分布；针对两个校准基准评估决策不一致；按与全库阈值的距离度量变化。骨干为WideResNet-50与ResNet-18，数据集VisA与MVTec AD，涵盖五个预算、两种运行水平，并对比int8标量量化与PQ等五种匹配方法。

Result: 10%预算下预算匹配PQ在五个方法中决策不一致最低（VisA、MVTec AD，两种运行水平均成立）；其余四个预算int8标量量化优于PQ。MVTec AD低运行水平下PQ在34/39单元达到校准中位数、39/39达到上分位；该预算下类别等权图像级AUROC损失为0.0003–0.0037。VisA 1%预算、1%运行水平时，近阈值异常不一致达28.12%（95% CI 23.25–33.58%），正常留出集为1.30%（0.79–2.14%）。

Conclusion: 10%预算匹配PQ在ResNet-18、干净参考库及基于正常目录探针估计阈值时仍保持最低不一致，协议将部署规模与校准参考的决策度量相连接，并识别出对检测最有信息量的评估人群。

Abstract: Reference banks used for industrial image inspection can be compressed to fit a deployment budget, but the resulting anomaly decisions depend on both the compressed scores and the operating threshold. We present a four-step protocol that compares compression methods at matched deployment sizes, establishes a reference distribution from 200 recalibrations, evaluates disagreement against two calibration benchmarks, and measures changes by distance from the full-bank threshold. With WideResNet-50, budget-matched product quantization (PQ) at the 10% budget has the lowest decision disagreement among five matched methods, including int8 scalar quantization, on VisA and MVTec AD at both operating levels. Int8 scalar quantization gives lower disagreement than PQ at the other four budgets. On MVTec AD at the lower operating level, PQ meets the calibration median in 34/39 cells and the upper quantile in 39/39 cells. Category-equally weighted image-level AUROC loss at this budget ranges from 0.0003 to 0.0037 across the evaluated configurations. On VisA at the 1% budget and a 1% operating level, disagreement reaches 28.12% (95% CI 23.25--33.58%) among near-threshold anomalies and 1.30% (0.79--2.14%) across the normal holdout. The 10% budget-matched PQ setting retains the lowest disagreement among four compared methods with ResNet-18, with clean reference banks, and when thresholds are estimated from normal-directory probes. The protocol connects deployment size to a calibration-referenced decision measure and identifies the evaluation populations most informative for inspection.

</details>


### [239] [Detectable by Design: Learning a Continuous 3D Saliency Field from 2D Detector Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34108)
*Han Sun,Jun Li,Christy Jie Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出以“图像可检测性”定义3D显著性，并构建连续隐式场DetField预测空间中任意点的可检测性，用于提升稀疏地图下的相机定位成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地图中保留的3D点决定相机能否被定位，地图越稀疏该选择越关键；但现有3D关键点检测器以语义、重复性、重建性或描述子可匹配性定义显著性，均未考虑该点是否真的会在图像中被检测到，导致所选点大多无法在图像中再次被检测。

Method: 将3D显著性定义为图像可检测性：某表面点上2D兴趣点检测器响应在其所有可见视图上的聚合。提出DetField——以点云为输入、可在其周围任意位置预测可检测性的连续3D场；监督信号为2D检测器响应从图像反投影到点云；在每个查询点将多尺度曲率不变量直接送入解码器，绕过编码器的体素池化。

Result: 在隔离3D点选择因素的定位基准上，DetField在物体与真实室内场景上优于Harris-3D及五种学习型3D关键点方法；在未见房间上相比最强基线Harris-3D定位成功率提升7.4个百分点；在物体上使用训练未涉及的2D检测器时仍领先；在稀疏地图上于物体与真实场景均保持对Harris-3D的优势。

Conclusion: 以“可被2D检测器检测到”作为3D显著性准则并建模为连续场，是提升3D点选择质量、进而改善稀疏地图定位的有效途径。

Abstract: The 3D points kept in a map decide whether a camera can be localized against it, and this choice matters more as the map becomes sparser. Existing 3D keypoint detectors usually define saliency by semantics, repeatability, reconstruction or descriptor matchability.
