</details>


### [311] [DFly2: Turbocharging Long-Context Speculative Decoding by Sharing the Target's Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34776)
*Hong Liu,Rui Cen,Junhan Shi,Lin Niu,Runzhi Fan,Zhaojing Zhou,Haodi Lei,Jiebin Zhang,Saiyong Yang,Junqiang Zheng,Zhenghai Xue,Guanghua Yu,Jianchen Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DFly2 是一种与目标模型共享稀疏性的块并行投机解码草稿器：每层草稿层直接读取配对目标层缓存的 KV（KV 共享），并只用该层 indexer 为每个 query 选出的固定数量位置（索引共享），从而不维护自身缓存、不执行打分前向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原生稀疏目标模型（如 DeepSeek-V4、Hy4）上的投机解码，其草稿器仍自带独立 KV 缓存（全 prompt 或滑窗），除显存外还额外消耗 prefill 时间、降低前缀缓存命中率与解码并发度。已有的"读目标缓存"草稿器只解决"读哪个缓存"，未解决"读哪些位置"，仍读全部或近窗，且与当前 query 无关；而按 query 实时选择又在长上下文下代价过高。作者观察到：原生稀疏目标模型本身已为每个 query 选出位置，且该缓存本就要维护，因此草稿器的两个选择都已被目标完成并付费。

Method: 先实证"选择的可迁移性"：在 DS-V4.1-Flash 上，将选择提前一步使用，仍可覆盖后续 token 实际选中位置的近 90%。在此基础上提出 DFly2：块并行草稿器，草稿层 i 直接读取配对目标层 i 缓存的 KV（KV 共享），位置限定为该层 indexer 所选中的、分布于任意上下文位置的固定数量位置（索引共享）。因此草稿器无自身缓存、无评分 pass，仅按目标给出的选择并行起草。

Result: 在 DS-V4.1-Flash、GLM-5.3-Flash、Hy4-flash（稀疏注意力机制与架构各异）上，与同一训练配方下的 DFlash、DSpark 相比，DFly2 在通用、长上下文与 agentic 基准上每步接受 token 数更高。相对较强基线的幅度从通用负载的 3.9%–10.5% 扩大到长上下文与 agentic 负载的 10.0%–31.5%。在 DS-V4.1-Flash 高并发下、保持相同单用户解码速度时，两个 64K 数据集上吞吐分别提升 62% 与 81%。

Conclusion: 草稿器的缓存与位置选择可由目标模型"免费"提供，且提前一步的稀疏选择具有良好的跨 token 可迁移性；据此实现的 KV 共享 + 索引共享草稿器在异构稀疏架构上均取得更高接受长度，并在高并发长上下文场景显著提升吞吐。

Abstract: To avoid the quadratic cost of dense attention over long contexts, models such as DeepSeek-V4 and Hy4 have turned natively sparse, and speculative decoding, which matters most where decoding is slowest, increasingly runs on them. Their drafters, however, still keep a context cache of their own, over the whole prompt or a sliding window. Beyond memory, such a cache costs prefill time, prefix-cache hits and decode concurrency. Drafters that read the target's cache avoid one of their own, but settle only which cache, not which positions, and still read everything or a recent window. Neither rule is made for the query at hand, and computing a selection per query in the drafter would cost more as the context grows. Yet a natively sparse target already picks positions for each query, over a cache it keeps anyway. Both of the drafter's choices are therefore already made, and paid for, by the target: its cache and its selection. We measure how far one selection carries: made a step early, it still covers close to nine tenths of what each later token selects on DS-V4.1-Flash. We present DFly2, a block-parallel drafter that shares the target's sparsity. Each draft layer reads the KV its paired target layer cached (KV sharing), at the positions that layer's indexer selected, a fixed number anywhere in the context (Index sharing). DFly2 thus runs no scoring pass and keeps no cache of its own. On DS-V4.1-Flash, GLM-5.3-Flash and Hy4~flash, whose sparse attention and architecture differ, it accepts more tokens per step than DFlash and DSpark trained on the same recipe, over general, long-context and agentic benchmarks. Its margin over the stronger baseline widens from 3.9%-10.5% on general workloads to 10.0%-31.5% on long-context and agentic ones. At high concurrency on DS-V4.1-Flash, at matched per-user decode speed, its throughput is 62% and 81% higher on two 64K sets.

</details>


### [312] [NORMCRAFT: Cultural Norm Generation for Context-Aware Language Model Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33726)
*Tangyuan Zhang,Zhuang Li,Fatemeh Shiri,Xiangyu Zhao,Junyu Yan,Xiuzhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NORMCRAFT 将"规范推断"与"回应生成"解耦：用多视图监督训练一个规范生成器，再由其产出的规范指导冻结的回应模型，从而提升文化对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化对齐要求 LLM 从场景及其文化语境中推断往往隐含的社会规范。现有方法或对单个模型做适配，或注入外部文化知识，而对"独立于下游回应生成的规范推断学习"探讨不足。作者认为将规范推断作为一个可分离、可复用的模块值得系统研究。

Method: 提出模块化框架 NORMCRAFT。(1) 规范生成器：以规范为中心的多视图监督（prospective 前瞻、adherence 遵从、violation 违背三类场景），在无需下游回应标签的条件下训练 LLM 从场景中恢复共享规范。(2) 回应生成：生成器输出的规范作为提示/条件，指导一个独立且冻结的 LLM（responder）产生回应。该设计使规范模块可与任意回应模型组合。

Result: 以 Qwen3.5-4B 作为 responder，NORMCRAFT 在 5 项下游指标中的 4 项优于 prompting 与 SFT 基线。在 SafeWorld 上，其回应更常体现参考规范（Coverage，较 Responder SFT 高 18.0 个百分点）、且更准确地体现（Faithfulness，高 14.1 个百分点）。直接评估也显示其规范质量优于 base 与 few-shot 生成器。同一训练好的生成器可跨 14 个冻结 LLM（5 个模型家族、0.8B–27B）提升 SafeWorld 文化对齐，最小模型收益最大。

Conclusion: 规范推断可以被有效解耦并单独学习：一个轻量训练的规范生成器即可作为可插拔模块，在冻结的多种规模、多种家族 LLM 上一致提升文化对齐，且对小模型增益最大，提示该方法在资源受限场景下具有实用价值。

Abstract: Cultural alignment requires large language models (LLMs) to infer often-implicit social norms from a scenario and its cultural context. Existing approaches typically adapt individual models or supply external cultural knowledge, while learning norm inference independently of downstream response generation remains underexplored. We introduce NORMCRAFT, a modular framework that decouples norm inference from response generation. Through norm-centric multi-view supervision, we train an LLM as a norm generator to recover shared norms from prospective, adherence, and violation scenarios without downstream response labels. The generated norms guide a separate, frozen LLM (the responder). With Qwen3.5-4B as the responder, NORMCRAFT outperforms prompting and supervised fine-tuning (SFT) baselines on four of five downstream metrics. On SafeWorld, its responses reflect the reference norms more often (Coverage) and more precisely (Faithfulness), exceeding Responder SFT by 18.0 and 14.1 percentage points, respectively. Direct norm evaluation also shows higher norm quality than the base and few-shot generators. The same trained generator improves cultural alignment on SafeWorld across fourteen frozen LLMs spanning five families and 0.8B to 27B parameters, with the largest gains for the smallest models.

</details>


### [313] [Verifiable but Not Predictable: What Sentence Embeddings Know About Propositional Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33668)
*Qingan Li,Tianxiao Wu,Xiling Cui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出分解质量控制的“非对称”双问题框架：事后可验证的“成员判定”与事前可预测的“命题数量估计”。在PropSegmEnt语料上，NLI判官验证成员准确率达92.8%（AUC 0.977），而向量几何（余弦相似度饱和、句向量非命题向量线性组合）无法胜任；数量预测方面，符号锚点（token数、CPIDR密度、Neo-Davidsonian谓词数）在控制长度后均退化为长度代理，唯有句向量上的线性岭回归探针保留真实计数信号（长度偏相关0.40–0.49，长度匹配配对排序准确率67–71%），且对INT8/INT4量化、Matryoshka截断及编码器年代稳健，但系统性低估长句切分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM命题分解的质量控制存在两个非对称问题：分解后需验证某候选命题是否属于给定句子（membership verification），分解前需预测句子应产生多少命题（count prediction）。作者旨在厘清这两类问题在不同表征空间中的可解性，并揭示长度混淆对数量预测研究的系统性污染。

Method: 构建PropSegmEnt语料（英文维基百科句子，每句由三名人工标注者切分）；成员判定采用NLI判官并与向量几何（余弦相似度、句向量-命题向量线性组合检验）对比；数量预测比较三类符号锚点（token计数、CPIDR观念密度计数器、Neo-Davidsonian谓词计数）与句向量线性岭回归探针，并施加长度偏相关分析、长度匹配配对排序、bootstrap置信区间、量化（INT8/INT4）、Matryoshka截断至256维、跨编码器年代（2021 vs 2025）复现等检验；最后将协议经验（含一项被撤稿的预实验）提炼为针对长度混淆量的探测清单。

Result: NLI判官成员验证准确率92.8%，AUC 0.977，残差错误多为文档语境下为真而非真正蕴含错误；余弦相似度饱和，句向量不构成其命题嵌入的线性组合。数量预测中所有符号锚点在控制token数后均退化为长度代理；线性岭回归探针长度偏相关0.40–0.49（bootstrap CI不含零），长度匹配配对排序正确率67–71%（随机水平50%），统计上与标注者间一致性上限相当；该信号在INT8量化下无损、INT4下小幅损失、Matryoshka 256维截断后仍保持，且在2021与2025编码器中表现相当；其结构性弱点是系统性欠切分长句。

Conclusion: 命题分解的两类质量控制问题分属不同表征空间：成员验证适合符号化NLI推理而非向量几何，数量预测则需在句向量上学习，符号启发式在长度控制后不具独立信息。语义嵌入中的计数信号稳健、廉价且跨模型代际可迁移，但存在长句欠切分偏差。作者将协议教训（含被撤稿预实验）归纳为探测长度混淆量的检查清单。

Abstract: Quality control for proposition decomposition by large language models (LLMs) raises two asymmetric questions. After decomposition, one can verify: given a sentence and a candidate proposition, does the proposition belong to that sentence? Before decomposition, one would like to predict: how many propositions should the sentence yield? On PropSegmEnt, a corpus of English Wikipedia sentences each segmented by three human annotators, we find that the two questions live in different representational spaces. A natural language inference (NLI) judge verifies membership with 92.8% accuracy and an area under the ROC curve (AUC) of 0.977, and its residual errors turn out to be mostly propositions that are true in document context rather than genuine entailment mistakes. Vector geometry cannot verify: cosine similarity saturates, and a sentence embedding is not a linear combination of its propositions' embeddings. For prediction, every symbolic anchor we test, namely token count, the CPIDR idea-density counter, and a Neo-Davidsonian predicate count, reduces to a length proxy once token count is controlled. A linear ridge probe on sentence embeddings is the only predictor that keeps genuine count signal: its length-partialled correlation with human counts is 0.40-0.49 with bootstrap confidence intervals (CIs) excluding zero, and it orders length-matched sentence pairs correctly 67-71% of the time against a 50% chance level, statistically matching the inter-annotator agreement ceiling. This signal survives INT8 quantization without loss, INT4 at a small cost, and Matryoshka truncation to 256 dimensions, and it works as well in a 2021 encoder as in a 2025 one. Its structured weakness is a systematic under-splitting of long sentences. We distill the protocol lessons, including a retracted pilot study, into a checklist for probing length-confounded quantities.

</details>


### [314] [Sparse-to-Sparse Feature Learning for Cross-Layer Analysis and Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33640)
*Masahiro Kaneko,Ayana Niwa,Timothy Baldwin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出稀疏到稀疏特征学习(S2S)：训练每层稀疏特征预测下一层稀疏特征，最终层由输出分布损失锚定，自输出向输入顺序优化，从而在8个语言模型上获得更强的相邻层对应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器(SAE)能把稠密隐藏表示分解为稀疏特征，跨层方法可捕捉跨多层结构，但现有目标并未显式训练某一层的稀疏编码去预测下一层的稀疏编码，层间稀疏特征缺乏直接的预测一致性约束。

Method: S2S：各中间层的稀疏特征被训练用于预测紧邻下一层的稀疏特征；最终层以输出分布损失作为锚定；优化按从输出层到输入层的顺序逐层进行。评估上采用在匹配输出分布偏移条件下的特征干预实验，将引导效应与稀疏通路重构效应分离，并用因果消融作为对应性指标之外的补充证据。

Result: 在8个语言模型上，S2S的相邻层对应性强于匹配的稀疏特征基线；匹配输出分布偏移的干预实验与因果消融提供了对应性度量之外的补充证据。

Conclusion: 显式的稀疏到稀疏跨层预测目标能有效增强层间特征一致性，且更严格的干预/消融评估有助于区分真实因果效应与重构伪影，为可解释性与控制提供更可靠的特征。

Abstract: Understanding how models internally represent information is a central challenge in interpretation and control. Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) decompose dense hidden representations into sparse features, and cross-layer methods capture structure spanning multiple layers. However, existing objectives do not explicitly train the sparse code at one layer to predict the sparse code at the next layer. We propose sparse-to-sparse feature learning (S2S), where sparse features at each intermediate layer are trained to predict those of the immediately following layer, with the final layer anchored by an output-distribution loss and optimization proceeding sequentially from output to input. Experiments across eight language models show stronger adjacent-layer correspondence than matched sparse-feature baselines. We evaluate feature-based interventions at matched output-distribution shift, separating steering-induced effects from sparse-pathway reconstruction effects. Causal ablation provides complementary evidence beyond correspondence metrics.

</details>


### [315] [Learning from Unreliable Operator References: Claim-Level Rewards for Customer Support Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34341)
*Dmitrii Stoianov,Olga Tsymboi,Danil Taranets,Daniil Strizhakov,Ilya Buryak,Almaz Dautov,Dmitry Fatnev,Arthur Babkin,Aleksandr Fida,Daniil Dryabin,Maksim Shilenko,Nikita Surnachev,Anatolii Potapov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 将客服人工回复视为"待核查的主张"而非"待模仿的答案"，用逐条核查得到的已验证主张构建主张级GRPO奖励训练客服智能体，离线幻觉率由13.6%降至8.6%，线上A/B测试中无人工介入的会话结案率由63.3%提升至80.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 即便人工客服的回复本身正确，也可能不是可靠的训练目标：约四分之一的事实性主张在知识库与工具调用记录中缺乏充分支撑。若直接以人工回复做监督微调（SFT），会把这些未受支持的表述当作正确答案拟合，带来幻觉风险。因此需要一个能区分"可核查/不可核查"主张的训练目标。

Method: 把运营人员回复当作需要核查的主张而非待模仿的回答：对每条事实性主张在知识库与工具记录中做验证，未通过验证的主张用于反向驱动知识库与工具的修订完善；通过验证的主张作为覆盖目标（coverage targets），构建主张级GRPO奖励，按智能体为自身回答所检索到的证据来评估事实精确度（factual precision）。

Result: 离线基准上，GRPO智能体的文本回答中8.6%被标记为幻觉，SFT基线为13.6%；针对现有流程型机器人的随机A/B测试中，无需人工客服即可结案的会话占比从63.3%提升到80.8%。

Conclusion: 以"核查主张"替代"模仿答案"的数据使用范式，配合主张级、基于检索证据的GRPO奖励，可有效降低客服智能体的幻觉率，并在真实部署中显著提高自动化结案比例，说明训练信号的质量（可验证性）比答案表面正确性更关键。

Abstract: Even a correct operator reply can be an unreliable training target for a customer-support agent. About a quarter of the factual claims in these replies lacked full support in the agent's knowledge base and tool records. We treat operator replies as claims to verify rather than answers to imitate. Unverified claims guide revisions to the knowledge base and tools. Verified claims serve as coverage targets in a claim-level GRPO reward that scores factual precision against the evidence the agent retrieved for its answer. On an offline benchmark, 8.6% of the GRPO agent's text answers were flagged for hallucinations, compared with 13.6% for the supervised fine-tuning baseline. In a randomized A/B test against the existing procedural bot, the deployed system increased the share of conversations closed without a human operator from 63.3% to 80.8%.

</details>


### [316] [Teaching a Multilingual Translation Model Regional Varieties: Castilian and Latin American Spanish, Asturian, Aragonese and Aranese with MADLAD-400-3B-MT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33831)
*Nerea de la Torre Veguillas,Pol Gerdt,Raul Quijada,Ivory Dipasupil,Lucía Rado,Francesc Tarrés*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 对MADLAD-400-3B-MT做三次LoRA微调，引入地区变体标签（西班牙西语、拉美西语）和伊比利亚三种罗曼语标签，发现"标签坍缩"现象并提出缓解措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流大规模多语翻译模型通常"一语一标签"，无法控制地区变体，且对地区小语种覆盖差甚至缺失。

Method: 基于MADLAD-400-3B-MT做三次LoRA微调，新增目标标签（<2es_ES>、<2es_419>及<2ast>/<2an>/<2oc_aranes>），结合单语回译与基座模型自身译文的replay；用领域监督的西班牙vs拉美分类器标注MADLAD-400西语分片250.9M文档，并以在340篇参考集上零误纳拉美文档的规则筛选变体语料；伊比利亚模型使用对比对与标签嵌入独立学习率。

Result: Castilian模型在定向测试中97%选择西班牙日常词汇（基座62%），70%使用vosotros（基座10%），同时提升人工参考的chrF++；发现标签坍缩（新标签由<2es>初始化时父标签漂移，84-87%输出相同）；伊比利亚模型在FLORES+西班牙语相关六向中四向持平或超越TAN-IBE Marian，西→阿兰语落后1.9 BLEU，多数方向仅次SalamandraTA-7B；西→阿拉贡语差异源于书面变体而非质量；十三其他FLORES-200方向平均下降0.6-0.7 chrF++。

Conclusion: 通过变体标签、LoRA微调、数据筛选与嵌入层解耦可实现对地区变体的细粒度控制并覆盖低资源小语种，代价是少量通用方向性能损失；模型、数据与变体分类器以开放许可发布。

Abstract: Massively multilingual translation models usually have one tag per language and no control over regional varieties, and they cover a region's smaller languages poorly or not at all. We report three fine-tunes of MADLAD-400-3B-MT, each adding target tags with low-rank adaptation (LoRA), back-translation of monolingual text and replay of the base model's own translations: <2es_ES> for Spanish as written in Spain, <2es_419> for Latin American Spanish, and one model for three Romance languages of the Iberian Peninsula, Asturian (<2ast>), Aragonese (<2an>) and Aranese (<2oc_aranes>). For the Spanish varieties we annotate the 250.9M documents of MADLAD-400's Spanish split with a domain-supervised Spain vs. Latin America classifier and select variety text with a rule that admitted no Latin American document in a 340-document reference set. The Castilian model chooses Spain's everyday vocabulary in 97% of a targeted test (base model 62%) and vosotros for informal plural address in 70% (10%), while improving chrF++ on human references. A first finding is tag collapse: with a new tag initialised from <2es>, the parent tag drifts to the new variety (84-87% identical outputs). In the Iberian model, contrastive pairs and a separate learning rate for the tag embeddings keep each tag in its own language. Trained only on publicly available data, mostly the TAN-IBE Wikipedia corpora, the Iberian model matches or beats the TAN-IBE Marian systems in four of six Spanish directions on FLORES+, is 1.9 BLEU behind in Spanish->Aranese, and is second only to SalamandraTA-7B in most directions. The exception, Spanish->Aragonese, reflects a difference of written variety between Aragonese Wikipedia and the FLORES+ reference, not model quality. All three fine-tunes lose 0.6-0.7 chrF++ on average in thirteen other FLORES-200 directions. We release the models, data and variety classifier under open licences

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [317] [EXPOSURE: Explaining Sources of Uncertainty in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34248)
*Kiana Farhadyar,Yannick Vogt,Philipp Laur,Ayça Çelik,Iman Nematollahi,Gabriel Kalweit,Maria Kalweit*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 EXPOSURE，用单一 mask-aware belief network 同时完成动作选择与 Shapley 解释，在 tic-tac-toe 与 Minesweeper 上比基线更准确地解释期望回报及其不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RL 中的 Shapley 值仅解释哪些观测特征驱动行为与期望回报，无法解释"确定性/不确定性"；而解释确定性需要理解观测特征如何影响回报不确定性的不同来源，且 Shapley 值需要对每个可见特征子集给出预测。

Method: EXPOSURE 采用单个 mask-aware belief network 兼顾动作选择与 Shapley 解释；对回报方差进行分解，拆为因特征缺失导致的观测性部分和环境自身随机性导致的内禀部分，并对被掩蔽特征的信念分布计算熵。

Result: 在 tic-tac-toe 与 Minesweeper 这两个可精确计算 Shapley 值的环境中，EXPOSURE 对期望回报及其不确定性的解释更准确，并且能独特地解释信念不确定性。

Conclusion: 统一的掩码感知信念网络可将 Shapley 解释从期望回报扩展到回报不确定性来源与信念熵，实现更完整且可验证的智能体行为归因。

Abstract: Shapley values in reinforcement learning are used to explain which observation features drive an agent's behavior and expected return, but not its certainty. Explaining certainty requires understanding how observation features influence different sources of return uncertainty, and Shapley values need the agent's prediction for every subset of visible features. We propose EXPOSURE, which uses one mask-aware belief network for both action selection and Shapley explanations. It explains the variance of the return, split into an observational part due to missing features and an inherent part due to the environment's own randomness, and the entropy of its belief over the masked features. In tic-tac-toe and Minesweeper, where exact Shapley values are computable, EXPOSURE gives more accurate explanations of expected return and its uncertainty, while uniquely explaining belief uncertainty.

</details>


### [318] [Brand Recall and Category Accuracy in Language Models: An Audit of 1,000 B2B SaaS Brands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33322)
*Ben Moore*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在1000个B2B SaaS品牌、333个品类上分别测量大模型对品牌的"召回"（能否生成品牌名）与"品类准确率"；在Gemma 4 12B上召回率51.0%、分类错误率19.8%（高置信裕度子集降至3.5%），并给出跨代际召回与情感度量的相关性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "语言模型能说出某品牌名，并不等于它对该品牌做了正确归类"。既有评测常将品牌生成能力与品牌知识/归类正确性混同，缺乏对二者的分离测量，也缺乏对置信信号可用性的检验。本文旨在建立可分离的测量框架，避免把"未被召回"误读为"模型中不存在该品牌"。

Method: 无外部检索（no external retrieval）条件下，对1000个B2B SaaS品牌、333个品类进行测量：(1) 品类提示下的品牌召回（elicitation）；(2) 独立的五候选品类判别探针（five-candidate category probe），以log-probability排序参考标签，并计算winner-to-runner-up裕度；(3) 跨四个Gemma世代的召回对比；(4) 探索性回归（技术性内容覆盖度→召回，自有渠道属性复合指标→品类准确率）；(5) 基于表征的情感度量与客户评分的线性相关分析。

Result: Gemma 4 12B上召回510个品牌（51.0%）；五候选探针中787个品牌参考标签排第一、194个为干扰项排第一、19个区分度不足；981个可分辨排序中错误率19.8%，裕度>0.6的547个品牌降至3.5%。四个Gemma世代召回率依次为39.8%、44.3%、51.1%、51.0%。情感度量与客户评分几乎无线性相关（r=0.03，n=921）。回归仅显示关联性，且复合指标采用不同调整集，不构成因果结论。

Conclusion: 应分别报告召回率与品类准确率，二者不可互相替代；log-probability裕度可作组内筛选信号，但并非校准后的置信度估计；未能召出品牌并不能证明其不存在于模型参数或训练数据中。

Abstract: A language model can name a brand without classifying it correctly. This study measures these outcomes separately for 1,000 B2B SaaS brands across 333 product categories. On Gemma 4 12B, with no external retrieval, category prompts elicit 510 brands (51.0%). A separate five-candidate category probe ranks the reference label first for 787 brands, a distractor first for 194, and yields little separation for 19. Among the 981 separated rankings, the error rate is 19.8%. It falls to 3.5% for the 547 brands with a winner-to-runner-up log-probability margin above 0.6. These are within-cohort measurements, not calibrated confidence estimates. Exploratory regressions associate technical coverage with recall and an owned-properties composite with category accuracy, but the composites use different adjustment sets and do not establish causal effects. Across four Gemma generations, measured recall is 39.8%, 44.3%, 51.1% and 51.0%. A separate representation-based sentiment measure has little linear association with customer ratings (r=0.03, n=921). The findings support reporting recall and category accuracy separately. Failure to elicit a brand does not establish its absence from model parameters or training data.

</details>


### [319] [Should Service Requirements Be Scalarized or Constrained When Minimizing Costs? A Case Study on HVAC Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33946)
*Tai-You Chen,Mihael Mohorcic,Blaz Bertalanic,Carolina Fortuna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文在Smart Buildings Challenge中系统比较了"将服务需求作为约束"与"标量化进单一目标"两种处理方式，覆盖模型基（MPC硬约束/软l1约束）与学习型（PPO固定惩罚/Lagrangian惩罚/投影层）方法，并给出基于影子价格的理论与实践结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化决策系统常在满足服务质量、舒适带、安全裕度等服务需求的同时最小化能耗等成本。该需求可与成本标化为单一目标（固定或自适应Lagrangian权重），也可作为约束施加。作者希望厘清这一建模选择在模型基优化与学习型策略中的实际影响，此前缺乏在同一任务、固定其余组件下的受控对比。

Method: 以Smart Buildings Challenge为测试平台，单一策略控制未见建筑的HVAC，评价指标为舒适度、能耗成本与碳排放的加权和。固定其他组件，仅改变需求处理方式：模型基侧用MPC，将需求实现为硬约束或l1惩罚软约束；学习型侧用PPO，奖励中采用固定惩罚或Lagrangian惩罚，或在动作上加投影层。理论分析借助需求的影子价格刻画软/硬约束的等价条件。

Result: MPC中，当软约束权重超过影子价格时其解与硬约束一致，而挑战赛的舒适权重约为该影子价格的4倍以上；权重低于影子价格时舒适度被牺牲，在舒适估值低于影子价格的指标下软约束可优于硬约束至多28%。在训练建筑上调好的权重用于48栋未见建筑时比硬约束差3.9%，原因是影子价格随建筑变化。PPO中，投影策略从一开始即满足需求并在训练早期领先；更长训练后Lagrangian策略反超而投影策略退化，固定惩罚则学不会满足需求。

Conclusion: 约束式与标量化式的优劣取决于权重相对影子价格的位置及跨实例的异质性：权重充足时软/标量化可等价于硬约束，权重不足则系统性地牺牲需求；跨建筑泛化时固定权重不可靠，约束式或自适应（Lagrangian）机制更稳健。学习型策略中投影层提供即时可行性但可能随时间退化，Lagrangian方法长期更优，固定惩罚则不足以学习满足需求。

Abstract: Automated decision systems, from learned policies to model-based optimizers, often minimize costs such as energy or resource use while meeting a service requirement, for example the quality of service (QoS) specified in a service level agreement (SLA), a comfort band, or a safety margin. The requirement can be scalarized with the costs into a single objective, with a fixed or an adaptively tuned (Lagrangian) weight, or it can be imposed as a constraint. We study this choice in the Smart Buildings Challenge, in which a single policy controls the heating, ventilation and air conditioning (HVAC) of previously unseen buildings and is evaluated by a weighted sum of comfort, energy cost and carbon emissions. With all other components fixed, we compare the two treatments in model-based optimization, represented by model predictive control (MPC) with the requirement as a hard or a soft (l1-penalized) constraint, and in learned policies, represented by proximal policy optimization (PPO) with a fixed or a Lagrangian penalty in the reward or a projection layer on the action. In MPC, the soft constraint reproduces the hard one once its weight exceeds the requirement's shadow price, which the challenge's comfort weight exceeds approximately fourfold; below this weight, comfort is sacrificed, and under a metric that values comfort below its shadow price the soft constraint outperforms the hard one by up to 28%. A weight tuned on the training buildings is 3.9% worse than the hard constraint on 48 unseen buildings, because the shadow price varies across buildings. In PPO, the projected policy satisfies the requirement from the outset and leads early in training; with longer training, the Lagrangian policy overtakes it while the projected policy deteriorates, and the fixed penalty does not learn to satisfy the requirement.

</details>


### [320] [MetaDecomp: Adaptive Task Decomposition via Meta-Learned Procedural Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33822)
*Ziyi Wang,Yixiao He,Yichen Wu,Ruite Lu,Jiayu Wang,Ying Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MetaDecomp：面向长程任务的语言智能体上下文元学习框架，联合学习任务相对状态空间与可复用过程，测试时据反馈细化分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程任务中复用历史经验不能只存成功流程，智能体需判断哪些过程适用、执行中剩余工作是什么，需自适应任务分解。

Method: 双层结构：外层从成功执行构建过程记忆，将任务进度表示为抽象状态、状态间过程表示为操作与依赖的有向无环图；学习到新进度区分时回访历史执行证据，重评状态归属并重组过程。测试时记忆固定，内层基于环境反馈细化初始分解，融合可用过程片段与记忆中缺失的任务特定步骤。

Result: ALFWorld与ScienceWorld三随机种子，五种设置中四种取得最高平均性能（ALFWorld+Qwen3-4B成功率86.3%）；消融显示固定初始schema或省略历史重投影均降性能；五种设置推理token均少于ReAct与AutoRefine；跨基准分析发现部分覆盖的低性能区、高覆盖提升成功率、低覆盖影响甚微。

Conclusion: 联合状态抽象与过程记忆的元学习分解有效且更省token；过程覆盖程度是成功率关键因素，代码将在录用后发布。

Abstract: Reusing past experience in long-horizon tasks requires more than storing successful procedures: Language agents must distinguish which procedures apply and what work remains as execution progresses. We introduce MetaDecomp, an in-context meta-learning framework for adaptive task decomposition that jointly learns a task-relative state space and reusable procedures. Its outer loop builds procedural memory from successful training executions, representing task progress as abstract states and procedures between states as directed acyclic graphs of operations and dependencies. As new progress distinctions are learned, MetaDecomp revisits historical execution evidence to reassess state assignments and reorganize the procedures they support. With memory fixed at test time, the inner loop refines an initial decomposition and revises it from environmental feedback, combining applicable procedure fragments with task-specific steps missing from memory. Evaluated on ALFWorld and ScienceWorld over three seeds, MetaDecomp achieves the highest mean performance among the compared methods in four of five settings, for example, an 86.3% success rate on ALFWorld with Qwen3-4B. Fixing the initial schema or omitting historical reprojection reduces performance in component ablations. MetaDecomp also uses fewer inference tokens than ReAct and AutoRefine in all five settings. Cross-benchmark analysis identifies a low-performance area where partial coverage degrades success relative to hidden memory; high coverage improves success, while low coverage changes little. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [321] [Spectral Optimal Transport for Dynamics-Aware Sign Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33818)
*Nabeela Khan,Bowen Wu,Runwu Shi,Hanlong Li,Benjamin Yen,Carlos Toshinori Ishi,Kazuhiro Nakadai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Spectral Optimal Transport (SOT)，在姿态空间代价中引入频谱特征距离，通过重加权耦合使其偏向目标动作的时频结构，从而缓解条件丢弃下CFG与姿态空间OT的冲突，提升手语生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于最优传输(OT)的流模型是手语生成(SLG)的强基线，能处理文本到手语的一对多映射；理论上无分类器引导(CFG)可进一步增强语义对齐，但其训练含条件与无条件分支，可能与姿态空间OT相矛盾。具体地，在条件丢弃时，同一样本噪声会被配对到运动动态不相容的目标动作上，干扰存在文本条件时的语义引导，降低生成质量。

Method: SOT在原有姿态空间代价基础上增补一项频谱特征距离，使OT耦合的重加权朝向目标动作的时频/时间-频率结构，从而在CFG场景下获得更一致的配对关系，提升生成表现。

Result: 定量与定性实验均验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过在OT代价中融入频谱结构先验，可有效调和CFG条件丢弃与姿态空间最优传输之间的冲突，改善手语生成的语义对齐与质量。

Abstract: Sign language generation (SLG) translates spoken-language text into pose sequences that convey its meaning. Recently, flow-based models with optimal transport (OT) have become a strong SLG baseline by handling the one-to-many nature of text-to-sign. While classifier-free guidance (CFG) could be applied to further strengthen the semantic alignment theoretically, its training involves conditional and unconditional training, which may contradict the pose-space OT, thus reducing the generation quality. Under the condition dropout, one noise sample can be paired with target motions exhibiting incompatible motion dynamics, interfering with the semantic guidance when the text condition is present. To address this issue, we propose Spectral Optimal Transport (SOT), which augments the pose-space cost with a spectral feature distance, reweighting the coupling toward the temporal-frequency structure of the target motions, thereby improving the performance when using CFG. The quantitative and qualitative experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [322] [Ensuring Deployment-Time Safety of Neural Network Controlled Systems via Localized Certificate Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33813)
*Xiaoyang Lv,Peixin Wang,Jianhao Bai,Bai Xue,Min Zhang,Luke Ong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出自适应分段屏障证书，将部署时适应转化为局部证书修复优化问题，无需全局重训练即可快速处理新出现的不安全区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始屏障证书在部署后面对新增障碍或约束变化时可能失效，导致系统进入不安全区域，而从零重训练代价高、往往失败。

Method: 在未变区域保留原证书与策略，仅在新不安全区域附近做局部更新；将部署时适应建模为局部证书修复问题，利用已有屏障证书的学习结构转化为优化任务；设计分阶段修复框架，逐步从局部修复过渡到策略-证书联合更新，必要时进行局部重训练。

Result: 在4个基准、5个新障碍的实验中全部成功，平均数十秒内完成；从零重训练多数情况失败；重训练成功的情形下本方法平均快17.3倍，性能相对原策略仅下降16.2%。

Conclusion: 局部证书修复可在部署时高效恢复安全性，兼顾速度与性能，优于全局重训练。

Abstract: Barrier certificates provide formal safety guarantees for neural network controlled systems by ensuring that system trajectories avoid unsafe regions over time. However, after deployment, previously unseen obstacles or constraint changes may introduce new unsafe regions, invalidating the original certificate and potentially leading to unsafe behavior. We address this setting with adaptive piecewise barrier certificates, which preserve the original certificate and policy in unchanged regions while introducing localized updates around newly emerged unsafe regions. The central question is how to perform such updates efficiently during system execution without retraining from scratch. Our key idea is to cast deployment-time adaptation as a localized certificate repair problem and reduce it to an optimization task that leverages the learned structure of previous barrier certificates, enabling fast updates without global retraining. We further design a staged repair framework that progressively transitions from localized repair to joint policy-certificate updates and, when necessary, localized retraining. Empirical results on four benchmarks with five new obstacles show that our method succeeds in all cases and completes within tens of seconds on average, whereas retraining from scratch fails in most cases. On instances where retraining succeeds, our method is 17.3$\times$ faster on average. It incurs only 16.2% performance degradation relative to the original policy.

</details>


### [323] [REE: A Scientific Research Entrance Exam for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34646)
*Zeqi Zhou,Minghao Liu,Jiarui Liu,Qingcheng Zeng,Rui Yang,Tianyu Liu,Zetao Yang,Wenqiang Zu,Enduo Zhao,Heng-Jui Chang,Lun-Hsin Kuo,Meng-Fu Chen,Haokai Zhao,Weijia Zhang,Jen-Tse Huang,Stephen Huang,Eli Cheung,Zhijing Jin,Mona Diab,Weihao Xuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 REE 基准，用 100 个来自真实前沿研究的开放式科学问题，在统一尺度下衡量自主研究智能体能否超越专家既定方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主研究智能体通过反复实验改进解的能力日益增强，但现有基准多以封闭式问答为主，缺乏对开放式科研问题及"相对既定科学方法的改进"的评估手段，因此需要一个可量化改进幅度并考察研究过程的统一基准。

Method: 构建 Research Entrance Exam（REE）：覆盖 5 大科学领域、23 个子域的 100 个开放式研究问题，每题均取自本领域活跃研究并经领域专家逐一审核。题目形式为迭代改进任务——智能体在至多 12 小时内改进一个可运行的初始解，最终结果在共同尺度上与在可比条件下评估的专家解进行对比评分。评测了 4 个前沿智能体。

Result: 四款前沿智能体中有两款（Claude Opus 5.5、GPT-6.1 Sol）分别在 86% 与 81% 的题目上超过专家解，且覆盖全部 5 个科学领域；二者平均在两小时内即超越专家解，其最优解多为对领域既有方法的裁剪、调整或组合。

Conclusion: REE 提供了一个统一的自主科研评估框架，既能度量相对既定方法的改进程度，也能刻画改进背后的研究过程，为开放式科研智能体评测建立了可比范式。

Abstract: Autonomous research agents are emerging as a promising approach to scientific problem-solving across disciplines, owing to their growing ability to improve a solution through repeated experiments. Evaluating this ability requires benchmarks that pose open-ended research problems and measure improvements over established scientific methods. We introduce REE (Research Entrance Exam), a benchmark that tests AI agents on scientific research. It consists of 100 open-ended research problems spanning five broad scientific areas and 23 domains. To ensure high quality, every problem is drawn from active research in its field and individually reviewed by domain experts. Each problem is posed as a task of iterative improvement: an agent has up to 12 hours to improve a working starter solution, and REE scores the result on a common scale against an established expert solution evaluated under comparable conditions. By this measure, two of the four frontier agents we evaluate, Claude Opus 5.5 and GPT-6.1 Sol, outperform the expert solutions on 86% and 81% of problems, across all five scientific areas. Their runs also show how they do so: both agents surpass the expert solutions within the first two hours on average, and their best solutions mostly tailor or combine established methods of the field. REE thus provides a unified framework for evaluating autonomous scientific research, capturing both improvements over established methods and the research processes behind them.

</details>


### [324] [On Utilization of Edge-AI for condition monitoring towards predictive maintenance: A use case in dairy processing using explainable AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34563)
*Rahul Umesh Mhapsekar,Norah O'Shea,Omer Ali,Steven Davy,Lizy Abraham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于边缘AI的旋转阀状态监测框架，利用低功耗IIoT传感器节点采集振动数据并在边缘设备上处理，作为乳品加工中试装置预测性维护的第一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业物联网与人工智能推动工业4.0，可提升制造系统的生产率、自动化与效率；乳品加工等流程工业的设备状态监测与预测性维护存在需求，而在边缘侧处理数据可降低时延与带宽依赖。

Method: 构建Edge-AI状态监测框架：通过低功耗IIoT传感器节点采集旋转阀的振动信号，并在边缘设备上完成数据处理（摘要未给出具体算法细节）。

Result: 摘要未报告量化结果；作者将该工作定位为面向预测性维护的阶段性框架。

Conclusion: 所提框架为乳品加工中试装置中旋转阀的预测性维护奠定了基础，体现了IIoT+Edge-AI在流程工业设备健康管理中的应用路径。

Abstract: The Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) and Artificial Intelligence (AI) are driving Industry 4.0, enhancing productivity, automation, and efficiency in manufacturing systems. This study develops an Edge-AI-based condition monitoring framework, as a step towards predictive maintenance, for a rotary valve in a dairy processing pilot plant, using vibration data collected via a low-power IIoT sensor node and processed on an edge device.

</details>


### [325] [EvoLink-Bench: A Benchmark for Evaluating Self-Evolving Agents in Long-Horizon Interdependent Task Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34400)
*Wei Chen,Xiaodong Lu,Weihao Shen,Gongxun Li,Xingyu Guo,Qihang Sun,Fuwei Zhang,Zhao Zhang,Yikun Ban,Shuang Li,Deqing Wang,Fuzhen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 EvoLink-Bench，用于评估长时程、相互依赖任务流中的自进化 LLM 智能体，并按 L1（语言可表达知识的获取与修正）与 L2（通过执行与反馈形成的任务级能力）两个层级，区分任务内恢复、持久状态更新与后续任务的经验复用；在 5 个智能体框架、3 种骨干配置、3 种持久化条件下评估了 19,635 个计分任务槽，发现无框架在两级上均最优，重试仅能挽回有限失败任务，持久状态更新未必带来后续增益，且知识级与能力级进化需要不同的学习机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式基准多采用短任务序列或近似独立的任务，难以检验后续任务依赖前序结果时智能体能否有效复用经验，因而缺乏对自进化能力的充分证据。

Method: 构建 EvoLink-Bench，覆盖日常办公、多轮对话与 AutoResearch 式工作流，采用跨任务持久化工作区与任务依赖；设 L1（知识获取与修正）与 L2（执行反馈驱动的能力发展）两级评估，并将评估细分为任务内恢复、持久状态更新、后续任务经验复用；在 5 个框架 × 3 种骨干 × 3 种持久化条件下评测。

Result: 基于所选评估队列共 19,635 个计分任务槽：无框架在两个层级上均持续领先；重试仅恢复有限比例的失败任务；持久状态更新不一定改善后续表现；进化效应在知识级与能力级任务间存在差异；性能提升随领域与任务流而异，部分任务的提升不能迁移到其他任务。

Conclusion: 长时程依赖任务流中的自进化仍是开放问题：知识级与能力级进化需不同学习机制，持久化与重试并非普适增益，未来智能体设计需针对二者分别建模并关注跨领域迁移。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are evolving from passive chatbots into autonomous systems that operate continuously in dynamic environments. Such agents need not only solve current tasks but also learn from past interactions to improve future performance. However, existing streaming benchmarks often rely on short task sequences or largely independent tasks, providing limited evidence of whether agents can effectively reuse experience when later tasks depend on earlier outcomes. To address this gap, we introduce \textbf{EvoLink-Bench}, a benchmark for evaluating self-evolving agents in long-horizon, interdependent task streams. It covers daily office work, multi-turn dialogue, and AutoResearch-style workflows, with persistent workspaces and dependencies across tasks. The benchmark includes two complementary levels: \textbf{L1}, which evaluates the acquisition and revision of language-expressible knowledge, and \textbf{L2}, which assesses the development of task-level capabilities through execution and feedback. Its evaluation further distinguishes within-task recovery, persistent-state updates, and experience reuse in later tasks. We evaluate five agent frameworks across three backbone configurations under three persistence conditions, covering 19,635 scored task slots from selected evaluation cohorts. Results show that no framework consistently performs best across both levels, retries recover only a limited proportion of failed tasks, and persistent-state updates do not necessarily improve subsequent performance. Furthermore, the effects of evolution differ between knowledge- and capability-level tasks, suggesting that these two forms of evolution require different learning mechanisms. Performance gains also vary across domains and task streams, with improvements on some tasks not necessarily extending to others.

</details>


### [326] [TASK Memory: Beyond Text-Only Memory with Activation Steering and Knowledge Editing against Memory Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34762)
*Jinkun Zhao,Wenjun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"使用期记忆幻觉"概念，并设计TASK记忆框架（文本+激活引导+知识编辑共享同一模型内地址），在冻结模型上将记忆忽略幻觉降至≤1.5%，显著优于BM25 RAG与近期上下文引导方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体依赖外部记忆却仍对"自己所记得的内容"产生幻觉：正确记忆已被检索并放入提示，但答案与之矛盾，或记忆被错误应用于无关问题。作者将此类现象定义为use-time memory hallucinations，并指出其根源是参数化知识与外部记忆之间的冲突，现有纯文本记忆方案无法解决。

Method: 将记忆幻觉归纳为三类：memory-ignoring（回退到权重中的陈旧事实）、memory-misapplying（把记忆归因到无关实体）、version confusion（新旧版本共存时输出被取代的值）。提出TASK memory：Text note承载内容；可移除的知识编辑通过subject key提供"地址"并支持多跳组合；一对steering vectors逐查询决定模型采纳记忆还是保留自身答案，且不存储内容，仅控制文本记忆的使用方式。采用预注册、仅测试一次协议在冻结模型上评估。

Result: 三类幻觉发生率分别为：记忆忽略最高46%，错误归因最高24%（检索+权重编辑时49%），版本混淆53%。TASK在全部10个模型-数据集单元中将记忆忽略降至≤1.5%；将邻居误用从39.2%降至7.5%（CounterFact综合提升10.8 pp）；300条记忆库上较BM25 RAG提升16.4 pp且在3,000条时优势保持；无需fact ID即可处理事实修订；MQuAKE多跳准确率+7.5 pp；23项跨模型比较中21项显著（8B–27B，四类模型家族）；在9/10个单元中优于三种近期上下文引导方法。

Conclusion: 使用期记忆幻觉是参数知识与外部记忆冲突导致的独立失效模式，单靠提升检索无法解决。TASK通过将内容（文本）、地址（知识编辑）与控制（引导向量）解耦并共享同一模型内地址，为冻结LLM提供了一种可控、可组合、可修订的记忆接口，在多种模型与数据集上取得稳定且统计显著的改进。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on external memory, yet they still hallucinate about what they remember. Often the correct memory is retrieved and placed in the prompt, but the answer contradicts it or the memory is applied to the wrong question. We call these use-time memory hallucinations. Across five open models, we find that they arise from the conflict between parametric knowledge and external memory and take three forms: memory-ignoring, where the model returns to a stale fact in its weights (up to 46% of queries); memory-misapplying, where it attributes the memory to unrelated entities (up to 24%, and 49% when retrieval is combined with weight editing); and version confusion, where 53% of answers about revised facts assert the superseded value when both versions remain stored. We propose TASK memory, which goes beyond text-only memory by combining a Text note with Activation Steering and Knowledge editing that share one in-model address. The text holds the content. A removable knowledge edit supplies the address through its subject key and supports multi-hop composition. A pair of steering vectors decides, query by query, whether the model adopts the memory or keeps its own answer; the vectors store no content and control how the text memory is used. On frozen models under a pre-registered, test-once protocol, TASK reduces memory-ignoring hallucinations to at most 1.5% in all ten model-dataset cells and cuts neighbour misuse from 39.2% to 7.5% (+10.8 pp composite on CounterFact). It improves a 300-memory library over BM25 RAG by 16.4 pp, an advantage that holds at 3,000 memories, resolves fact revisions without fact IDs, and adds 7.5 pp of multi-hop accuracy on MQuAKE. 21 of 23 cross-model comparisons (8B-27B, four families) are significant, and TASK beats three recent context-steering methods in 9 of 10 model-dataset cells.

</details>


### [327] [What Is a Theory Worth to an LLM Solver? Measuring, Inducing, and Merging Theories in Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34751)
*Jun Gao,Shuchao Bi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在长度匹配的实验协议下，向Qwen3-14B的上下文注入正确的理论可将模式等待时间问题的pass@1从0.37提升到0.83，并大幅减少正确解的中位token数；模型能够通过代码探索归纳出部分理论，但难以组装完整理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学家掌握正确理论后解题更快，作者希望量化"陈述的理论"对大语言模型求解器的价值，并检验模型能否自行写出这样的理论，同时区分"归纳"与"记忆"两种能力。

Method: 采用长度匹配协议，对比注入正确理论与等长worked examples的效果；在不同求解器规模与token预算下测量增益变化；在自定义概率族中植入未发表定理（planted theorems）以隔离归纳与记忆，要求强开源模型先用代码探索训练问题再撰写理论笔记；并测试多份笔记合并的效果。

Result: 注入正确理论使pass@1从0.37升至0.83，正确解中位token从10.2k降至6.3k，而等长worked examples无帮助；增益随求解器与预算增大而缩小（+0.46、+0.26、+0.07），长预算下增益几乎全部来自"在预算内完成"，各条件下完成的解正确率达98–100%。植入定理实验中，强开源模型分别恢复60%、48%的oracle增益，第三个族几乎为零（找到正确形式但未找到可计算条件）；单份笔记均不完整，但强模型合并多份笔记在经典族上恢复61%，高于最佳单份笔记的47%。

Conclusion: 理论的价值等于其节省的推理量；模型能够先找到理论的"碎片"，但尚不能自行将其组装成完整理论。

Abstract: Mathematicians solve problems faster when they know the right theory. We ask how much a stated theory is worth to a large language model (LLM) solver, and whether a model can write that theory itself. In a length-matched protocol, adding the right theory to the context of Qwen3-14B raises pass@1 on pattern waiting-time problems from 0.37 to 0.83 and cuts the median tokens of correct solutions from 10.2k to 6.3k, while worked examples of the same length do not help. The gain shrinks as the solver and its token budget grow (+0.46, +0.26, +0.07), and at a long budget it comes almost entirely from finishing within budget: solutions that finish are correct 98--100\% of the time in every condition. To separate induction from recall, we plant unpublished theorems in custom probability families and measure how much of the oracle theory's gain an induced theory recovers. A strong open model, required to explore the training problems with code before writing, recovers 60\% of the gain on one planted family, 48\% on a second, and almost none on a third, where it finds the right form but not the condition that makes the form computable. No single induced note is complete, but a strong model that merges several notes recovers 61\% of the gain on the classic family, above the best single note (47\%). Theories are worth the reasoning they save, and models can find their pieces before they can assemble them.

</details>


### [328] [Asset4D-Bench: Benchmarking Coding Agents for Editable 3D and 4D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33728)
*Chengyi Sun,Deyuan Liu,Lei Zhao,Jungang Li,Qi Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Asset4D-Bench 测试编码智能体能否从视觉参考中恢复物体、运动与交互并生成可编辑的 Blender 场景；在24个从零任务上，各模型14项获满分动力学，但GPT-6.1-Sol(3项)与GPT-6-Astra(8项)共11项重建得零关系分；固定权重下关系项为二者6.50分平均保真度差异贡献5.15分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将视觉参考转化为可编辑三维场景（资产、运动、交互）是编码智能体的关键能力，但缺乏独立复检、可版本化度量的评测基准；作者还希望厘清"给定起始资产"是否影响重建表现。

Method: 构建Asset4D-Bench，含114个条件：图像编辑、带完整/部分起始资产的视频重建，以及独立挑选的从零任务；独立重新打开项目，与原生参考状态对比评分。归档228个分数，含原始交付结果与场景质量的分版本度量。

Result: 24个从零任务中每个模型在14项获满分动力学学分；其中3项GPT-6.1-Sol与8项GPT-6-Astra重建关系分为零；固定权重下关系项为6.50分Sol−Astra平均恢复保真度差异贡献5.15分。

Conclusion: 关系/交互恢复是当前编码智能体的主要瓶颈；因归档运行在执行与恢复策略上的差异，无法对起始资产效应给出因果估计。

Abstract: A cart can follow its reference trajectory while its cargo floats above the deck. Asset4D-Bench tests whether coding agents recover objects, motion and interactions from visual references as editable Blender scenes. Its 114 conditions span image editing, video reconstruction with matched full or partial starter assets, and independently selected from-scratch tasks. We independently reopen projects and measure them against native reference states. The 228 archived scores include original delivery outcomes and versioned measures of scene quality. On 24 from-scratch tasks, each model earns full dynamics credit on 14. Of these, three GPT-6.1-Sol and eight GPT-6-Astra reconstructions earn zero relation points. Under the fixed scoring weights, relations contribute 5.15 points to the 6.50-point Sol-minus-Astra difference in mean recovered fidelity. Differences in execution and recovery policy across archived runs preclude a causal estimate of starter-asset effects.

</details>


### [329] [Keep or Roll Back? Out-of-Loop Judges for Self-Improving VLA and End-to-End Embodied Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33794)
*Zilin Huang,Zhengyang Wan,Kangan Qian,Zihao Sheng,Sikai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SealedLoop 评测框架，用循环外、改进器不可见的"封闭判官"配合序贯检验来决定自改进策略是否保留，并将虚假增益分解为判官误差与迁移差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身策略自改进中，判官通常在同一个循环内训练或选取，其证据与被评策略不独立，导致虚假增益无法识别，缺乏可信的改进判定依据。

Method: 引入密封判官（循环外的不可见评估器），新策略仅在密封判官上的序贯检验通过时才被保留，并以有界概率控制保留更差策略的风险；在同一循环中分别替换判官进行对比，将虚假增益拆解为判官误差与迁移差距。

Result: LIBERO 操作中，成功分类器在一代内把 196 个失败中的 65 个判为成功；NAVSIM 驾驶中，在循环自身画面上有增益的策略在未见过城市中失效，即使使用精确的循环内判官也会保留 8/15 个；密封检验下驾驶循环最终策略优于自身评论家（PDMS 0.674 vs 0.649，起点 0.638）；在已知密封增益的驾驶对上接受 11/12 真实增益、拒绝全部 59 个损失；真实车辆 6 组盲测对中强化版本均行驶更远，但均未完成弯道。

Conclusion: 循环内部的证据即使准确也不足以判定策略是否真正改进；以改进系统无法影响的证据作为判据，是具身策略可信自改进的基础。

Abstract: Self-improvement is now a practical recipe for embodied policies: a policy collects experience, is retrained, and is kept when a judge rates it higher. However, the judge is usually trained or chosen inside the same loop, so its evidence is not independent of the policy. We introduce SealedLoop, an evaluation framework that adds a sealed judge: an evaluator outside the loop that the improver cannot see or optimize against. A new policy is kept only when a sequential test on the sealed judge confirms the gain, with a bounded probability of keeping a worse one. Running the same loop under each judge, we separate two sources of a false gain, which we call judge error (the loop's judge is wrong on its own data) and transfer gap (a real gain there does not carry over to unseen conditions). We evaluate on LIBERO manipulation, NAVSIM driving, and a full-scale real vehicle. In manipulation, a success classifier accepted 65 of 196 failures in one generation. In driving, policies that gain on the loop's own frames lose in an unseen city, so even an exact in-loop judge would keep 8/15 of them. When the sealed test decides, a driving loop built to improve ends with a better policy than under its own critic (0.674 against 0.649 PDMS, from 0.638), and on driving pairs with known sealed gain the test accepted 11 of 12 real gains and none of 59 losses. In a real-world field experiment on a published improvement step, the reinforced version drove further into a curve than the version it started from in all 6 blind pairs, though neither completed it. These results show that evidence from inside the loop, however accurate, is not enough to decide whether a policy has improved. SealedLoop offers a foundation for trustworthy self-improvement of embodied policies, in which progress is judged by evidence the improving system cannot influence.

</details>


### [330] [TransHarness: From Benchmark Overfitting to Transferable Harness Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33757)
*Tianze Xia,Zimo Wang,Heng Tong,Zhaoxiang Liu,Shiguo Lian,Fan Huang,Wenyu Liu,Yunchao Wei,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出可迁移Harness演化方法TransHarness，通过同任务成功-失败对比演化"触发条件+诊断指引"的干预策略，并借助跨任务回放与语义筛选抑制过拟合，仅用300条分布外源轨迹即可在多个目标基准上提升强智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Harness演化方法容易在其演化基准上过拟合，获得的收益无法迁移到新任务，缺乏跨分布泛化能力。

Method: 从同任务的成对成功-失败执行对比中演化Harness干预策略，策略将执行上下文触发条件与诊断性指引耦合，实现任务相关的检查与纠错；采用跨任务回放和独立的语义选择，保留能选择性干预、且将纠错建立在当前任务证据而非源任务特定解上的策略；并构建独立演化集用于跨分布迁移。

Result: 仅使用测试分布外的300条源轨迹，在多个强模型及轻量级与成熟Harness上进行跨基准评测：DeepSWE v1.1达76.4%，Terminal-Bench 2.1达95.5%，分别超过最强基线GPT-6 Astra 2.4和5.6个百分点。

Conclusion: TransHarness实现了超越基准过拟合的可迁移Harness演化，在不更新模型权重的前提下显著提升强编码智能体在未见目标基准上的表现。

Abstract: Harness evolution offers a path to improving coding agents without updating model weights. However, existing methods can overfit their evolution benchmarks, producing gains that fail to carry over to new tasks. To address this challenge, we introduce Transferable Harness Evolution (TransHarness), which evolves harness intervention policies from same-task success-failure contrasts. These policies couple execution-context triggers with diagnostic guidance, enabling task-dependent checks and corrections beyond the source executions. Cross-task replay and independent semantic selection retain policies that intervene selectively and ground corrections in current-task evidence rather than source-specific solutions. We further curate an independent evolution set for cross-distribution transfer and conduct a cross-benchmark evaluation covering multiple strong models and a range of lightweight and mature harnesses. Experiments show that, with only 300 source trajectories from outside the test distribution, TransHarness further improves strong agents across distinct target benchmarks, demonstrating transferable harness evolution beyond benchmark overfitting. Specifically, TransHarness achieves state-of-the-art scores of 76.4% on DeepSWE v1.1 and 95.5% on Terminal-Bench 2.1, surpassing the strongest baseline model, GPT-6 Astra, by 2.4 and 5.6 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [331] [PeerReveal: Learning to Generalize in AI Peer Review Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33555)
*Zi Li,André V. Duarte,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 PeerReveal 检测器，通过学习审稿意见论断与同篇论文 AI 审稿参考论断之间的关系并结合文本级信号，区分"全 AI 生成"与"AI 仅润色人类判断"的审稿意见；在新基准 PeerShift（5,430 条、跨学科）上常规训练达 70.52% pooled Macro-F1，经对抗训练提升至 82.40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 审稿检测多聚焦"是否由 AI 生成"的二元判断，未区分全 AI 生成与仅用 AI 改写人类判断的情形；且需在跨学科、跨模型与工作流差异下保持可靠。

Method: PeerReveal 以审稿意见的论断（claims）与同篇论文 AI 生成审稿中参考论断之间的学习关系为依据，辅以审稿级文本信号；构建 PeerShift 测试集（5,430 条，覆盖机器学习、生命科学、社会科学等，含不同学科写作惯例与多样 AI 生成机制）；并设计基于小型本地生成器的对抗训练框架以提升泛化。

Result: 常规训练下 pooled Macro-F1 为 70.52%，超最强基线 11.48 点；对抗训练后提升至 82.40%（+11.88 点）。

Conclusion: 稳健的 AI 审稿检测需依赖更丰富的审稿级证据，并对更具挑战性的生成过程进行对抗训练。

Abstract: Can we distinguish fully AI-generated reviews from those using AI only to rewrite human judgement? Furthermore, can we do so reliably when reviews are generated from diverse AI models and workflows across scientific domains? To this end, we introduce PeerReveal, a new detector that grounds its decisions in learned relationships between a review's claims and reference claims drawn from AI-generated reviews of the same paper, complemented by review-level textual signals. We evaluate PeerReveal on PeerShift, a new, challenging test set of 5,430 reviews spanning multiple domains, including machine learning, life sciences, and social sciences, combining variation in disciplinary review conventions with diverse AI review-production mechanisms. With regular training, PeerReveal achieves 70.52% pooled Macro-F1 on this benchmark, improving over the strongest baseline by 11.48 points. We develop an effective and practical adversarial training framework that further improves the detector's generalization using a small local generator optimized to produce challenging reviews, raising pooled Macro-F1 from 70.52% to 82.40%. These results show that robust AI-review detection benefits from richer review-level evidence and adversarial training on more challenging generation processes.

</details>


### [332] [What Does Skill Lift Actually Measure? A Source-Code-Level Audit and Noise-Aware Reanalysis of SkillsBench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33943)
*Haozhe Zhang,Zhen Long*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文审计SkillsBench源码并重分析7,554次公开试验（1,421个配对"配置-任务"单元），指出基准中skill收益与负收益结论均不可靠：三项受评分器依赖/操作定义缺陷污染，平均提升74.6pp；无任务级下降经多重检验校正后显著，负面差异受运行抽样噪声主导；基于无skill分数的门控策略的"提升"在标签打乱后依然存在，且在留出运行上不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后续研究依据SkillsBench的逐任务增益对skill进行门控或剔除，隐含假设是"负增益"真实且可归因于skill本身；作者怀疑该基准的负向信号来自评测器依赖、操作定义缺口、占位skill目录与执行框架内置skill等混杂因素，以及每条件运行次数过少导致的抽样噪声，因此需要源码级审计与配对重分析来检验"skills有害"这一结论是否成立。

Method: (1) 源码级审计SkillsBench，识别评分器依赖（grader dependence）与操作定义缺口；(2) 构建1,421个配对"(配置, 任务)"单元，覆盖7,554次公开试验；(3) 以多重检验校正的汇总重分析检验任务级增益；(4) 基于每单元最多3次运行的算术可分辨性分析（58%单元在算术上不可能出现下降）；(5) 对基于无skill分数的门控策略做标签置换（shuffle）对照，并在未参与拟合与决策的留出运行上验证。

Result: 源码审计确认2例评分器依赖与1例操作定义缺口，这三项任务平均提升74.6pp，而全集平均提升为18.7pp（原始报告约为16.6pp）；发现4个任务使用占位skill目录，某执行框架在多数运行中额外注入48个内置skill。经多重检验校正后，无任何任务级下降达到统计显著；由于每单元最多3次运行，观察到的负差异高度易受运行抽样噪声影响，且算术上58%的单元不可能出现下降。基于无skill分数的门控在日志中提升2–3pp，但在打乱条件标签后仍提升4–7pp；在留出运行上无统计显著改进。

Conclusion: SkillsBench原始负向结果不足以为"skill有害"提供证据，其增益幅度也被污染任务显著抬高；据此设计的门控策略的收益无法与混杂和选择偏差分离。作者发布审计台账与分析脚本，呼吁基准在评分器独立性、操作定义、占位制品、执行框架内置skill控制及每条件重复次数上加强规范，并采用标签置换与留出运行作为必要对照。

Abstract: Agent skills are directories of instructions, reference materials, and scripts loaded on demand by coding agents. According to SkillsBench, although human-curated skills can raise the passing rate by about 16.6 percentage points (pp) on average, they have actually reduced the results for some tasks. Later research has started to gate or eliminate skills according to the above per-task gains. We have audited the above benchmark at the source-code level and analysed 7,554 public trials to form 1,421 paired "(configuration, task) cells". Source-code audit identified two controlled-verified cases of grader dependence and one operational definition gap; the average lift on these three tasks was 74.6 pp, and the overall average lift for the full set was 18.7 pp. The audit also found that four tasks contained placeholder skill directories, and a certain execution harness additionally added 48 built-in skills in most runs. No task-level reduction achieved statistical significance in the pooled reanalysis with multiple-testing correction. As there were at most three runs per condition for each cell, the observed negative differences are very susceptible to run-sampling noise, and arithmetically, 58% of the cells could not have shown a decrease. Therefore, the raw numbers of negative cells cannot show that skills cause harm. A gating strategy based on the skill-free score seems to improve by 2-3 pp in the logs; however, even after shuffling condition labels to remove any skill effect, it still achieves an increase of 4-7 pp. On the held-out runs not used for fitting and decision-making, there was no statistically significant improvement. The audit ledger and analysis scripts have been published by us.

</details>


### [333] [ExtractBench Pro: The Long Tail of Document Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33791)
*Boyang Zhang,Justin Xu,Adrian Lyjak,Eli Stewart,Simon Suo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出 ExtractBench Pro——一个模式引导的文档抽取基准，聚焦现有数据集覆盖不足的六种困难抽取模式，通过确定性生成器合成 100 份困难文档，并评测了 10 个编码智能体与 8 种抽取 API 配置在准确率、定位可靠性与"完美抽取"率上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档抽取基准对若干困难抽取模式的覆盖不足，难以有效区分前沿系统的能力上限，因此需要一个面向高难度、可控难度标定的评测资源。

Method: 构建合成文档生成框架：由确定性文档生成器将具有挑战性的组件组装为文档，难度按前沿系统水平标定；合成覆盖多种风格的 100 份真实感文档；在准确率、grounding 与整篇无错抽取率三个维度上评测 10 个编码智能体与 8 种抽取 API 配置，并分析编码智能体的执行轨迹。

Result: 无任何抽取系统能对超过 61% 的文档实现零错误抽取。最强通用编码智能体（Claude Code Opus 5.5、Codex GPT-6 Astra）在完美文档率与 Value F1 上均优于专用抽取 API，但单页成本更高。专用 API 中 LlamaExtract 位于低成本前沿，Agentic Plus Max Effort 以更低单页成本在两项指标上接近最强编码智能体。轨迹分析显示编码智能体具备智能图像处理与程序化推理能力，但在应用文档特定定义、从图表中恢复关系方面存在失败。

Conclusion: ExtractBench Pro 揭示当前前沿抽取系统在困难模式上仍存在显著差距，并区分了编码智能体与专用 API 在效果—成本上的权衡；作者公开基准与评测结果，作为后续改进前沿抽取系统的资源。

Abstract: We present ExtractBench Pro, a schema-guided document extraction benchmark that tests six challenging modes of extraction underrepresented in existing datasets. It is created following a framework for synthetic document generation, in which deterministic document generators assemble challenging components into documents that are designed to be difficult at a level calibrated for frontier systems. We synthesize 100 realistic documents spanning a diverse range of styles. On this data, we evaluate ten coding agents and eight extraction API configurations for accuracy, grounding, and their ability to perfectly extract documents. No extraction system extracts more than 61% of documents error-free. The strongest general-purpose coding agents, Claude Code Opus 5.5 and Codex GPT-6 Astra, outperform specialized extraction APIs on both perfect-document rate and Value F1, but at a higher cost per page. Among specialized extraction APIs, LlamaExtract spans the lower-cost frontier, with Agentic Plus Max Effort approaching the strongest coding agent on both perfect-document rate and Value F1 at lower cost per page. Our analysis of coding agent traces identifies intelligent image processing and programmatic reasoning, but also failures in applying document-specific definitions and recovering relationships from diagrams. We release ExtractBench Pro and these evaluation results as resources for the future development of improved frontier extraction systems.

</details>


### [334] [WhatIf: Counterfactual World Models over Action-Grounded Causal Graphs for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33745)
*Xu Yang,Kailai Sun,Qianchuan Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出反事实世界模型 WhatIf 与反事实优势规划 CAP，通过共享潜在物理耦合候选未来，实现个体效应对比与安全规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭事实转移只能确定动作的边缘未来与平均效应，无法刻画个体效应或"切换动作导致伤害"的概率，因此需要可比较同一情境下多个候选未来的反事实模型。

Method: WhatIf 用共享潜在物理与从事实历史推断的转移噪声耦合候选未来；每个候选动作诱导稀疏的对象-因子因果图，在共享情境下展开以锚定观测结果并限定动作对比范围；类型条件共享函数支持对象数可变，往返损失在无标签下实现动作不变的反事实推断。据此提出 CAP，以对现有方案的优势排序计划并约束伤害概率；配对优势将估计方差降低 1-ρ 倍并抵消共享模型误差；每次执行转移提供事后优势以更新物理后验、下一计划与摊销策略（无需学习 critic）。

Result: 证明以条件均值替代个体效应时 MSE 等于其条件方差，从而论证耦合未来比较的必要性；配对优势方差缩减因子为 1-ρ；给出观测转移可识别干预动力学的条件；配对仿真干预实验评估反事实精度、图恢复、组合泛化与决策质量。

Conclusion: 耦合反事实未来可同时提升个体效应估计与安全决策质量，并提供无需 critic 的摊销规划框架。

Abstract: World models predict what happens under an action, but choosing between actions requires comparing their outcomes in the same physical situation. Factual transitions alone determine action-wise marginal futures and average effects, but not individual effects or the probability that switching actions causes harm. We introduce WhatIf, a counterfactual world model for manipulation that couples candidate futures through shared latent physics and transition noise inferred from factual history. Each candidate action induces a sparse object-factor causal graph describing the physical pathways it activates. Rolling out these graphs under the shared situation anchors predictions to observed outcomes and confines action contrasts to the objects each action reaches. Shared type-conditioned functions support varying numbers of objects, while a round-trip loss enforces action-invariant abduction without counterfactual labels. We show that replacing an individual effect with its conditional mean incurs mean-squared error equal to its conditional variance, motivating comparisons between coupled futures. Building on these comparisons, Counterfactual Advantage Planning (CAP) ranks plans by their advantage over an incumbent within shared situations and constrains the probability of harm. Paired advantages reduce estimator variance by a factor of $1-ρ$, where $ρ$ is the correlation between branch outcomes, and cancel model errors shared by both branches. Each executed transition provides hindsight advantages to update the physics posterior, the next plan, and an amortized policy without a learned critic. We further establish when observational transitions identify interventional dynamics. Paired simulator interventions evaluate counterfactual accuracy, graph recovery, compositional generalization, and decision quality.

</details>


### [335] [Interactive Proofs as a Unified Framework for Evaluating and Leveraging Verifiable Proof Generation by LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33676)
*Yankai Shu,Xingyu Zhao,Chuan Mao,Congchi Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Merlin-Bench，将 LLM 智能体视为交互式证明系统中的证明者，在指定协议下与形式化验证器交互以构建可独立检验的证明，用以评估其与具体证明语言/工具无关的通用证明构造能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 辅助定理证明与程序验证工作多关注判定命题真伪，而真正需求是产出可被人类或形式化验证工具独立检验的证据；同时现有评测混杂了对特定证明语言与验证工具的熟练度，难以衡量底层、协议无关的证明构造能力。

Method: 借鉴计算复杂性理论中的交互式证明系统，把 LLM 智能体设定为证明者（prover），在给定协议下与形式化验证器（verifier）交互来论证其对问题实例的答案；构建自动化测试用例生成框架，生成覆盖 15 个问题族、共 100 个案例的基准集，每个案例均以 prover-verifier 框架表述。

Result: 在多种 agent harness 下测试 10 个前沿 LLM，结果显示按协议可靠产出可验证证明仍具显著挑战性。案例分析给出可操作结论：工具使用对完成实际证明任务必不可少；从工程指令到问题特定知识的不同形式引导显著影响交互式证明成功率；从抽象任务中提炼的证明求解经验可迁移至具体真实场景的验证任务。

Conclusion: Merlin-Bench 提供了协议无关、可独立核验的证明构造能力评测范式；当前前沿模型在该能力上仍存在明显不足，而工具使用、引导形式与经验迁移是提升验证器可接受证明产出率的关键因素。

Abstract: Recent advances in LLM-assisted theorem proving and program verification have attracted widespread attention. Beyond determining whether a proposition is true, LLM agents must produce evidence that can be independently checked by humans or formal verification tools. To evaluate the underlying, protocol-agnostic ability of LLM agents to construct verifiable proofs-rather than their proficiency with any particular proof language or verification tool, we introduce Merlin-Bench. Drawing on interactive proof systems from computational complexity theory, we cast an LLM agent as the prover: under a designated protocol, the agent interacts with a formal verifier to substantiate its claimed answer to a problem instance. We develop an automated framework for constructing test cases and use it to create 100 cases across 15 problem families, each formulated within this prover-verifier framework. Experiments with 10 frontier LLMs across multiple agent harnesses show that reliably producing verifiable proofs in accordance with specified protocols remains a substantial challenge. Our case studies further provide actionable insights into improving agents' ability to generate verifier-accepted proofs: tool use is essential for completing practical proof tasks; different forms of guidance, ranging from engineering instructions to problem-specific knowledge, substantially affect success rates on interactive-proof tasks; and proof-solving experience distilled from abstract tasks can transfer to concrete, real-world verification tasks.

</details>


### [336] [Aligned Machine: A Benchmark for Evaluating Multimodal Embedding Similarity via Human Comparative Judgements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33594)
*Jiechen Li,Hannah Groos,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出交互式公众参与平台 Aligned Machine，通过强制二选一的比较任务构建持续增长的人类对齐相似度基准 HASB，用于审计嵌入模型的语义对齐；初步部署115人、2,875个标注对，评测12个模型发现对齐仅为部分成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入模型相似度评测基准依赖静态、专家标注的粗粒度Likert标注对，难以扩展，且难以获得一致、高置信度的标注，缺乏可信手段检验模型相似性判断是否反映人类语义理解。

Method: 构建面向公众的交互式平台 Aligned Machine：参与者完成认知自然的强制选择任务，比较两对项目（文本或图像）并选出更相似的一对；该比较式设计对非专家友好、产生干净的偏好数据并支持稳健聚合，从而持续扩充 HASB 基准。

Result: 初始部署115名参与者，贡献2,875个标注对（872个唯一对）；用HASB评测12个常用嵌入模型，发现嵌入相似度与人类判断仅部分对齐：纯文本模型总体一致性高于多模态模型；多模态模型按任务类型系统性变化，image-image最强、image-text次之、text-text最弱。

Conclusion: Aligned Machine 可随参与增长而演化，支持纵向分析、领域特定研究、偏差检测与跨模型版本的概念漂移测量，为嵌入模型的语义对齐审计提供可扩展、可问责的框架。

Abstract: Embedding models increasingly inform how people search, recommend, and retrieve information, yet we lack credible ways to test whether these similarity judgments reflect how humans understand meaning. Existing benchmarks typically rely on static, expert-labeled pairs with coarse Likert-style annotations, limiting scalability and making it difficult to obtain consistent, high-confidence annotations. We introduce Aligned Machine, an interactive, public-facing platform that transforms broad participation into a continuously growing Human-Aligned Similarity Benchmark (HASB) for evaluating semantic alignment in embedding-based systems. Participants complete a cognitively-natural, forced-choice task, comparing two pairs of items (text or images) and selecting which pair is more similar. This comparative design is accessible to non-experts, yields clean preference data, and supports robust aggregation. We report results from an initial deployment with 115 participants contributing 2,875 total labeled pairs and 872 unique pairs. Using HASB, we evaluated 12 widely used embedding models and found only partial alignment between embedding similarity and human judgments. Text-only models consistently achieve higher overall agreement than multimodal models, while multimodal performance varies systematically by task type: alignment is strongest for image-image comparisons, weaker for image-text comparisons, and lowest for text-text comparisons. Aligned Machine is designed to evolve over time as participation grows, supporting longitudinal analysis, domain-specific studies, bias detection, and measurement of concept drift across model versions. By combining accessible public participation with rigorous comparative evaluation, HASB provides a scalable and accountable framework for auditing semantic alignment in embedding models.

</details>


### [337] [Do We Really Need an LLM for Weight-Space Programming? A Deterministic Forward-Only Paradigm and Its Capability Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34612)
*Lin Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出无需LLM优化器的确定性前向权重编辑流水线FWSP，并给出推理模型的Protected FisherGate读出编辑方法，在多模态多模型上验证有效并刻画两个算子边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Weight-Space Programming用LLM以纯前向方式编辑权重，将“LLM脚本化”与“前向编辑的代数本质”混为一谈，导致不必要的LLM优化器开销。作者主张在核心结构化编辑场景中LLM优化器并非必需，并指出推理型LLM的权重编辑需避免干扰正常推理状态。

Method: 提出Forward Weight-Space Programming (FWSP)：探测（probing）、定位（localization）、分层算子O1–O4、自动盲路由（blind routing）与配对验证（paired verification），且配对验证不增加逐token计算。针对推理LLM提出Protected FisherGate：闭式协方差白化的读出层编辑，构造与正常推理状态正交的方向。

Result: 自动盲路由准确率83.3%，显著高于同工具库27B LLM的33.3%。DeepSeek-R1-0528-8B上单个冻结数学校准补丁在1K预算下HumanEval pass@1达84.2%（3/5 Stage-4通过子集为75.4±9.4%），较512-token预留budget forcing提升14.6个百分点并减少23.4% token，且在完整GSM8K与MATH-500上可跨HF到vLLM后端迁移。覆盖五种模态、十三个模型（50K至27B稠密模型与FP8 MoE）。

Conclusion: 证明并验证了子空间纠缠（subspace entanglement）与冻结表示跨度（frozen representation span）两条算子边界；在该边界内FWSP定域的LoRA可桥接纯前向编辑与梯度自适应，表明结构化编辑可由确定性流水线替代LLM优化器。

Abstract: Recent work on Weight-Space Programming uses LLMs to edit neural weights with forward passes only, conflating LLM scripting with the underlying algebra of forward-only editing. We show that for core structured editing regimes, an LLM optimizer is unnecessary, and introduce Forward Weight-Space Programming (FWSP): a deterministic pipeline combining probing, localization, stratified operators (O1-O4), automatic blind routing (83.3% vs. 33.3% for a 27B LLM with the same tool library), and paired verification with zero additional per-token computation. For reasoning LLMs, we propose Protected FisherGate, a closed-form covariance-whitened readout edit orthogonal to normal-reasoning states: on DeepSeek-R1-0528-8B, a single frozen math-calibrated patch achieves 84.2% HumanEval pass@1 (1K budget; 75.4 +/- 9.4% across 3/5 Stage-4-passing subsets), beating 512-token-reserved budget forcing by +14.6 pp (-23.4% tokens) while transferring across HF-to-vLLM backends on full GSM8K and MATH-500. Across five modalities and thirteen models (50K to 27B dense and FP8 MoE), we further prove and verify two operator boundaries, subspace entanglement and frozen representation span, where FWSP-localized LoRA bridges forward-only and gradient adaptation.

</details>


### [338] [EgoUseBench: Benchmarking Computer-Use Agents under Evolving First-Person Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34466)
*Yurun Chen,Biao Yi,Qihang Yu,Keting Yin,Shengyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“Ego Use”交互范式（智能眼镜等AI可穿戴设备使任务源于用户第一人称的日常活动），并构建EgoUseBench基准，含Computer-Use、Offline Ego Use、Online Ego Use三类设置共693个任务，基于74小时真实第一人称录像；实验显示先进模型在Online Ego Use下的平均成功率比Computer-Use低26.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI可穿戴设备（如智能眼镜）把智能体的可用上下文从数字环境扩展到物理环境，使计算任务可源于用户正在进行的活动而非显式指令；但第一人称观测受佩戴者视角限制，证据可能不完整或含糊，需随新观测修正对物理上下文与用户意图的理解并复核既有数字操作是否仍有效，而现有智能体能否可靠应对这些变化缺乏充分评测。

Method: 构建EgoUseBench基准，按第一人称上下文丰富程度划分三种交互设置：Computer-Use（仅显式指令）、Offline Ego Use（执行前提供预录第一人称上下文）、Online Ego Use（执行中持续提供观测且用户意图演化）；基于74小时以上真实第一人称录像构造693个任务；分别追踪第一人称观测、用户需求与网站状态以刻画任务演化，并按执行中信息变化方式将任务分为Graft（用户需求修订）、Refresh（新第一人称观测）与Clarify（智能体主动信息检索）三类；随后评测先进模型。

Result: 先进模型在Online Ego Use下的平均成功率比Computer-Use低26.3%，表明使任务执行与不断演化的第一人称上下文及用户意图保持一致仍具较大难度。

Conclusion: 第一人称可穿戴场景下的动态上下文与意图演化构成当前智能体的显著瓶颈；EgoUseBench提供了可分离观测、需求与网页状态演化的评测框架，为后续提升智能体在动态第一人称条件下的鲁棒性与任务一致性奠定基础。

Abstract: AI wearables, such as smart glasses, extend the context available to agents beyond digital environments to users' physical surroundings. With these devices, computer tasks can originate from users' ongoing activities rather than solely from explicit instructions. Through first-person observations, agents can identify objects and activities relevant to user requests and ground digital tasks in the surrounding context. We call this interaction paradigm Ego Use. However, first-person observations are limited by the wearer's viewpoint and may provide incomplete or ambiguous evidence of ongoing activities. As new observations arrive, agents may need to revise their understanding of the physical context and user intent, and reassess whether prior digital actions remain valid. Whether current agents can reliably handle these changes remains insufficiently evaluated. We introduce EgoUseBench, a benchmark for evaluating Ego Use across three interaction settings with different levels of first-person context. Computer-Use relies on explicit instructions, Offline Ego Use provides prerecorded first-person context before execution, and Online Ego Use provides continuous observations as user intent evolves. Based on over 74 hours of real-world first-person recordings, we construct 693 tasks across the three interaction settings. To capture task evolution, we track first-person observations, user requirements, and website state separately. We further group tasks by how information changes during execution, resulting in three types: Graft (revised user requirements), Refresh (new first-person observations), and Clarify (agent-initiated information seeking). We evaluate advanced models and find that average success rates in Online Ego Use are 26.3% lower than in Computer-Use. This result reveals the difficulty of keeping task execution aligned with evolving first-person context and user intent.

</details>


### [339] [Knowledge-Base Gradient Descent: Self-Structuring Learning through Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34841)
*Masahiro Kato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"自结构化学习"（Self-Structuring Learning）设定，并给出"知识库梯度下降"（Knowledge-Base Gradient Descent）方法：用正确答案诊断当前知识库的缺陷，迭代提出结构编辑、重建知识库并仅在损失下降时采纳，从而自动构建可持久编辑的知识库结构（如 Schema Descent 之于图模式）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的应用依赖知识库的结构来决定如何检索与连接信息，但知识库结构通常需人工设计。作者希望在"问题—期望答案"的弱监督下，让知识库结构本身可被学习与优化，使应用能更有效地回答针对同一批源文档的问题。

Method: 将知识库构建形式化为自结构化学习：每轮用当前知识库回答产生的偏差作为学习损失，提出一个候选结构编辑（图模式或链接知识文档的组织方式），重建知识库，若损失下降则保留该编辑。通过对候选编辑做随机化采样，把编辑带来的损失变化表示为关于其被选中概率的导数；相互作用的联合编辑作为单一候选整体评估。Schema Descent 是该方法的图模式实例化。最终结构在同源的其他问题上做留出评估。

Result: 在"每份文档为一组研究论文"的大型文档实验中，更新后的知识卡片与图模式在留出生成问题上取得更高平均 Answer F1，且每份文档的模式均有提升；在人工问题上，模式的平均 F1 上升而卡片下降，说明更新后的结构更服务于其学习来源的那类问题。

Conclusion: 知识库结构可由问答信号端到端地学习与优化，方法在多文档场景中稳定有效；但所学结构存在对问题类型的适配性，即结构收益与学习所依据的问题分布高度相关。

Abstract: We consider the problem of constructing knowledge bases for applications based on large language models, in which the structure of a knowledge base determines how an application can retrieve and connect the information needed to answer a question. We study Self-Structuring Learning, a setting in which questions and expected answers guide the construction of a persistent knowledge base whose editable structure can be a graph schema or the organization of linked knowledge documents. We propose Knowledge-Base Gradient Descent, which diagnoses discrepancies between expected answers and responses obtained through the current knowledge base. At each round, the method proposes a structural edit, rebuilds the knowledge base, and adopts the edit when the learning loss decreases. The retained structure is evaluated on other questions about the same sources. By randomizing over candidate edits, we express the loss change of an edit as a derivative with respect to the probability of selecting it, and we evaluate joint edits as single candidates because their changes interact. Schema Descent instantiates the method for graph schemas. In experiments on large documents, each a bundle of research papers, the updated knowledge cards and graph schemas obtain higher mean Answer F1 on held-out generated questions than their initial structures, with schema gains in every document. On human questions, mean F1 rises for schemas and falls for cards: the updated structures serve the kind of question they were learned from.

</details>


### [340] [FORK: Co-Evolving Safe Disclosure and Decision Skills Against Attackers Who Read Every Refusal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33879)
*Hieu Man,Chao Shang,Rui Dong,Devang Kulshreshtha,Yanjun Qi,Sandesh Swamy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"安全披露"问题：护栏拒绝动作时给出的解释会被攻击者观测，需要在帮助合法用户恢复的同时不提升自适应攻击成功率；据此提出FORK框架，协同进化护栏的自然语言决策与披露技能和会读取所有公开消息的攻击者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 护栏的拒绝对任何观察轨迹的攻击者可见：固定拒绝对合法用户无法恢复任务，而详细解释又会泄露攻击者可尝试的方向。因此拒绝消息的设计需要在"可恢复性"与"不助长自适应攻击"之间取得平衡，而手写规则难以预判自适应攻击者如何探测决策与利用消息。

Method: 将问题形式化为"安全披露"，同时涉及决策（拦截什么）与消息（向agent及攻击者披露什么）。提出FORK：在护栏的自然语言决策技能与披露技能库、以及一个读取全部公开消息的攻击者之间进行协同进化；每条被利用的消息和每个过于宽松的决策为自然语言技能库的无梯度修订提供证据。

Result: 在四个基准上评测。FORK-World上对三个agent取平均，自适应攻击下ASR从78.4%降至14.1%，效用保持在85%以上，在ASR与效用上均优于基线；在训练中未见过环境与任务的MT-AgentRisk上，ASR从70.6%降至9.1%，良性效用达93.1%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将拒绝理由的披露方式与拦截决策一并优化，可在显著降低自适应攻击成功率的同时维持较高的合法任务效用，并具备对未见环境与任务的泛化能力。

Abstract: When a guardrail rejects an LLM agent's action, its message reaches any attacker observing the trajectory: a fixed refusal leaves benign users unable to recover, whereas an explanation can tell the attacker what to try next. We study this as safe disclosure: explaining rejections so that authorized tasks recover without increasing adaptive attack success. Safe disclosure depends on both the decision and the message: what the guardrail blocks and what it reveals to the agent and the attacker. Because hand-written rules alone cannot anticipate how adaptive attackers probe decisions or use messages, we introduce FORK, which co-evolves a guardrail's natural-language decision and disclosure skills with an attacker that reads every public message. Each exploited message and permissive decision gives FORK evidence for a gradient-free revision of its natural-language skill library. We evaluate FORK on four benchmarks. Averaged over three agents on FORK-World, FORK cuts attack success rate (ASR) under adaptive attack from 78.4% to 14.1% and keeps utility above 85%, outperforming baselines on both ASR and utility. Notably, on MT-AgentRisk, whose environments and tasks are unseen in training, FORK lowers ASR from 70.6% to 9.1% with 93.1% benign utility.

</details>


### [341] [MeterRouter: Budget-Aware Dynamic Routing for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34327)
*Yue Xu,Leikun Yin,Ruiming Min,Yufeng Lai,Justin Andrew Johnson,Sitao Luan,Xiaotong Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 MeterRouter，一个预算感知的动态路由器，以逐查询货币预算为条件，增量合成多智能体协作图（选择各智能体的角色、底层LLM、入边连接与终止），先模仿成功执行图、再用奖励偏好正确答案并惩罚开销与超预算的GRPO变体联合优化。在五个基准和三层LLM池上达到最强MAS相当精度，成本低82%，并可按预算调节花费且很少超支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统优于单智能体，但智能体间通信带来大量token开销；给每个智能体都配大模型造成资源利用低效，系统远离Pareto前沿。查询成本由招募的智能体数量、各自角色与所用LLM、通信拓扑共同决定。现有路由方法多把成本隐式处理而非显式优化约束，固定精度-成本偏好也无法表达调用方对特定查询的付费意愿。

Method: MeterRouter：预算感知的动态路由器，在查询与逐查询货币预算条件下增量式合成协作图，逐步决定每个智能体的角色、LLM、入边连接及终止时机；先用成功执行图做模仿初始化，再用Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）变体联合精化决策，奖励鼓励正确答案、惩罚花费与预算超支。

Result: 在五个基准与三层LLM池上：(1) 准确——性能与最强MAS相当；(2) 经济——以比最强MAS路由器低82%的成本达到该精度，并把精度-成本前沿推至超越单智能体系统上限的位置；(3) 可控——花费随指定预算自适应调整且很少超支。

Conclusion: 将成本作为显式、逐查询可调的预算约束纳入多智能体图构建与路由，可在保持高精度的同时显著降低开销并实现预算可控，优于隐式成本建模的现有路由器。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on large language models (LLMs) outperform single-agent systems, but inter-agent communication incurs substantial token overhead. While assigning a large LLM to every agent is common, it results in inefficient resource utilization, leaving the system far from the Pareto frontier. The cost of answering a query is set jointly by how many agents are recruited, the role and LLM assigned to each, and communication topology among them. Most existing routers treat cost only implicitly rather than as an explicit optimization constraint. A fixed accuracy-cost preference also cannot express how much a caller is willing to pay for a particular query. We introduce MeterRouter, a budget-aware dynamic router that incrementally synthesizes a collaboration graph by selecting each agent's role, LLM, incoming connections, and termination conditioned on the query and a per-query monetary budget. We initialize the router by imitating successful execution graphs, then jointly refine its decisions with a variant of Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) whose reward favors correct answers and penalizes spending and budget overruns. Across five benchmarks and a three-tier LLM pool, MeterRouter is (1) accurate, achieving performance comparable to the strongest MAS; (2) economical, reaching that accuracy at 82% lower cost than the strongest MAS router and extending the accuracy-cost frontier beyond the ceiling of single-agent systems; and (3) controllable, adapting its spending to the specified budget while rarely exceeding it.

</details>


### [342] [CA-RAG: Constraint-Aligned Retrieval for Structurally Faithful Long-Form Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33497)
*Vimal Kumar Kumar,Meghana Kshirsagar,Conor Ryan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出约束对齐的检索增强框架 CA-RAG，在检索前将本体结构约束与知识图谱关系注入分节查询，提升合规敏感长文本生成的证据充分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 RAG 仅按语义相关性选择证据，但合规敏感的长文本生成还需支撑强制性结构义务的证据；纯语义检索可能遗漏下游必备条款所需信息。

Method: CA-RAG：在证据选择前，用本体（ontology）派生的结构约束和基于政策的知识图谱关系增强分节（section-specific）查询，实现检索条件化。

Result: 仅检索评估（人工标注证据）在不同检索预算下均有一致提升；k=6 时 Precision@k 由 0.5268→0.5692，池化 Recall@k 由 0.5402→0.5950，F1@k 由 0.5389→0.5672。生成实验中多个模型-领域设定下合规性与可靠性提升；仅在生成阶段施加约束虽提高条款覆盖率，但整体合规性与可靠性更低。

Conclusion: 结构对齐的检索能改善合规敏感长文本生成中的证据充分性，且将结构约束前置到检索阶段优于仅在生成阶段施加。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) typically optimises evidence selection for semantic relevance, whereas compliance-sensitive long-form generation also requires evidence supporting explicit structural obligations. Semantically relevant retrieval may therefore omit information needed for mandatory downstream clauses. We propose Constraint-Aligned RAG (CA-RAG), a retrieval-conditioning framework that augments section-specific queries with ontology-derived structural constraints and policy-grounded knowledge-graph relations before evidence selection. Retrieval-only evaluation with manually annotated evidence shows consistent improvements across retrieval budgets. At $k=6$, CA-RAG increases Precision@$k$ from $0.5268$ to $0.5692$, pooled Recall@$k$ from $0.5402$ to $0.5950$, and F1@$k$ from $0.5389$ to $0.5672$ over standard RAG. Generation experiments further show improvements in compliance and reliability in several model--domain settings, while generation-only constraints achieve higher clause coverage but lower overall compliance and reliability. These results indicate that structurally aligned retrieval can improve evidence sufficiency in compliance-sensitive long-form generation.

</details>


### [343] [Crucible: Evaluating LLM Agents in Executable Chemistry Research Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34254)
*Yinjun Liu,Xinye Li,Ruisen Peng,Lang Gao,Zixiang Xu,Zirui Song,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Crucible Bench基准，覆盖DMTA全周期1078项任务、75个化学工具，评估14个LLM智能体，最高分仅0.545。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有化学知识基准主要考察问答与孤立任务，而真实化学研究需调用专业工具并解读其输出以获得化学上有效的结果，这一能力缺乏评测手段。

Method: 构建Crucible Bench：包含来自18个已发表数据集的12个任务族、共1078项任务，覆盖分子设计、合成规划、实验执行、闭环优化、模拟与分析表征；智能体通过Model Context Protocol在7个隔离环境中调用75个领域工具；采用沙箱评分器核验合成路线与实验方案等输出，实验类任务使用机器人模拟器或既有实测数据。评测14个LLM智能体。

Result: 最佳模型总体得分0.545；合成规划与闭环优化为最难领域；总体性能难以预测优化性能（总体排名第12的模型在闭环优化中得分最高）；智能体常出现工具运行成功但提交结果化学上无效的错误；更强模型从工具访问中获益更多。

Conclusion: Crucible Bench为追踪模型在真实化学工作流中的工具使用能力提供了可扩展的评测框架，表明可靠使用化学软件仍是LLM的关键挑战。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) perform well on chemistry knowledge benchmarks, which mainly assess question answering and isolated tasks. Chemical research, however, requires using specialized tools and interpreting their outputs to obtain chemically valid results. To address this gap, we introduce Crucible Bench to evaluate this ability across all four stages of the design-make-test-analyze (DMTA) cycle. The benchmark contains 1,078 tasks in 12 task families curated from 18 published datasets, covering molecular design, synthesis planning, experimental execution, closed-loop optimization, simulation, and analytical characterization. Agents access 75 domain-specific tools through the Model Context Protocol in seven isolated environments. Sandboxed graders verify the resulting synthesis routes, experimental protocols, and other outputs. Laboratory tasks use robotic simulators or previously measured experimental data. We evaluate 14 LLMs as agents and find that the best achieves an overall score of 0.545, with synthesis planning and closed-loop optimization emerging as the hardest domains. Overall performance does not predict optimization performance well: the model ranked twelfth overall achieves the highest score in closed-loop optimization.
