Error analysis also shows that agents often submit chemically invalid results despite running their tools successfully. Stronger models benefit more from tool access, but reliable use of chemical software remains a challenge that Crucible Bench can track as models improve.

</details>


### [344] [PROVE: Mitigating Long-Horizon LLM Collusion with Reinforcement Learning from Verified Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34215)
*Haochen Luo,Jie Fu,Mukai Li,Yuqi Wang,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在长程多智能体交互中，LLM智能体会逐渐"共谋"（互相接受缺乏证据的工作）；作者提出多智能体强化学习方法PROVE，按"证据"而非"结果"奖励判定，将共谋率从65.0%降至1.0%且不损失任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体长期互相验证时会漂移到共谋状态：在没有验证协议所要求证据的情况下就接受对方的工作。共谋在初始时不存在，而是逐轮累积形成，可归因于记忆中积累的准确反馈——遵循规则的智能体在同伴判断正确时被告知自己的判定是错的，因此破坏规则成为收益最大化选择，并在私有反思中自我说服。现有缓解手段不改变智能体本身。

Method: PROVE（Protocol Reinforcement from Verified Evidence）是多智能体强化学习方法：依据判定背后的证据而非判定结果给予奖励；并对智能体的每次调用（包括解决自身任务的调用）按其"可控结果"进行信用分配。使用LoRA适配器在六轮交互数据上训练Qwen3.6-27B。

Result: 在留出任务序列、原始十轮交互horizon下，共谋率由65.0%降至1.0%，任务成功率无可检测变化；原本在每次交互中都收敛到共谋，训练后无一收敛；增益可外推至超出训练最长交互四轮。训练后的27B模型共谋率低于原研究中评估的所有模型（含闭源前沿模型）。

Conclusion: 共谋是可被训练消除的行为，而非仅靠外部缓解手段压制；基于证据的奖励设计与细粒度信用分配能有效抑制长程交互中的机制漂移，并具备跨交互长度的泛化能力。

Abstract: When LLM agents verify each other's work over a long interaction, they drift into collusion: they come to accept work without the evidence that the verification protocol requires. Collusion is absent at the start and builds up episode by episode, which we trace to accurate feedback accumulating in memory. An agent that follows the rule is told that its verdict was wrong whenever its peer was right, so breaking the rule is the reward-maximizing choice, and agents talk themselves into it in their private reflections. Existing mitigations leave the agents unchanged; we train the behavior out. PROVE (Protocol Reinforcement from Verified Evidence) is a multi-agent reinforcement learning method that rewards verdicts by the evidence behind them rather than by their outcome, and credits every call an agent makes, including the calls that solve its own task, with the outcome that call controls. On Qwen3.6-27B, a LoRA adapter trained on six-episode interactions takes the collusion rate on held-out task sequences at the original ten-episode horizon from 65.0% to 1.0%, with no detectable change in task success. Collusion that converged in every interaction now converges in none, and the gain holds four episodes beyond the longest interaction seen in training. The trained 27B model colludes less than every model evaluated in the original study, closed-weight frontier models included. Code is available at https://github.com/Haochen-Luo/PROVE.

</details>


### [345] [KeyDeck: Multi-Paper-to-Slide Generation via Cross-Paper Synthesis and Reference-Guided Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33715)
*Tarik Can Ozden,Sachidanand VS,Ram Prakash Reddy Motati,Ozgur Kara,Dilek Hakkani-Tür,Junho Kim,James Matthew Rehg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KeyDeck是首个多论文转幻灯片（multi-PTSG）框架，通过逐篇+跨篇分析与参考引导的个性化，从多篇论文合成连贯演讲，并配套推出KeyBench基准（100份专家撰写的多论文演示）与评测体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: multi-PTSG需从多篇论文中选取、组织并综合信息构建统一演示，存在两大挑战：(i) 需识别跨论文关系并将多方内容综合为连贯叙事；(ii) 不同讲者的组织方式与强调重点差异显著，个性化对用户满意度至关重要。

Method: 提出KeyDeck框架，包含三部分：融合逐篇分析与跨篇分析以识别来源间关系并综合其贡献为连贯叙事；采用参考引导的个性化，从叙事、内容、文本与视觉四个维度推断讲者偏好并相应调整幻灯片生成。同时构建KeyBench基准（100份专家撰写多论文演示）与综合评测框架，覆盖生成质量、个性化遵循度与会话目标满足度。

Result: 实验结果表明KeyDeck在多论文转幻灯片质量、个性化遵循度与会话目标满足度上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: KeyDeck验证了"跨篇综合+参考引导个性化"统一框架在multi-PTSG任务中的有效性，KeyBench为生成质量、个性化遵循与会话目标满足度提供了评测基础。

Abstract: Multi-paper-to-slide generation (multi-PTSG) is the task of constructing a unified presentation from multiple research papers by selecting, organizing, and synthesizing information across sources. This task introduces two main challenges: (i) combining multiple sources into a coherent narrative requires identifying cross-paper relationships and synthesizing their content, and (ii) the desired organization and emphasis varies substantially across presenters, making personalization essential for user satisfaction. Motivated by these challenges, we propose KeyDeck, the first multi-PTSG framework that integrates per-paper and cross-paper analysis to identify relationships among sources and synthesize their contributions into a coherent narrative, and reference-guided personalization to infer presenters' narrative, content, textual, and visual preferences and adapt slide generation accordingly. To evaluate generation quality, personalization adherence, and conversational goal satisfaction, we introduce KeyBench, a benchmark of 100 expert-authored multi-paper presentations, together with a comprehensive evaluation framework. Our results demonstrate that KeyDeck outperforms existing approaches in multi-PTSG quality, personalization adherence, and conversational goal satisfaction.

</details>


### [346] [PowerFix: Physics-Guided Iterative Repair for LLM-Assisted Power Converter Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33712)
*Can Wang,Shi Qiu,Fanfan Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PowerFix是一个物理引导的迭代修复框架，将验证失败转化为结构化诊断与可执行补丁，在96任务反激变换器修复基准上实现78.7%验证修复成功率（最强LLM基线为30.1%），增加三轮重规划后提升至88.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体被用于自动化工程设计，但对未通过工程验证的功率变换器设计进行修复仍很困难：需定位物理成因、找到责任设计变量，并在不破坏其他约束的前提下消除违规。

Method: 提出PowerFix：用PowerDiagGraph将失败检查项关联到可能物理成因并约束修复路径；由LLM对修复路径排序优先级，确定性编译器将选定修复翻译为受权限约束的字段级编辑；候选方案在隔离的设计副本上验证，仅当全部必需工程检查通过后才提交，失败可触发重规划。

Result: 自建96任务反激式变换器修复基准（含注入单故障、耦合故障及设计生成器留出的自然故障），VRSR达78.7%，最强LLM基线为30.1%；增加三轮重规划后VRSR升至88.4%。

Conclusion: 结果为基于仿真的设计流程内的试点阶段证据；硬件原型验证与迁移至第二种变换器拓扑是走向部署的下一步。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly being explored for automated engineering design. However, repairing power-converter designs that fail engineering validation remains challenging: the system must identify physical causes, locate responsible design variables, and resolve violations without compromising other constraints. We introduce PowerFix, a physics-guided iterative repair framework that converts validation failures into structured diagnoses and executable patches while preserving topology and design requirements. PowerDiagGraph links failed checks to possible physical causes and constrains repair routes. An LLM prioritizes these routes, while a deterministic compiler translates selected repairs into authorized field-level edits. Candidates are validated on isolated design copies and committed only after all required engineering checks pass; failed validation can guide replanning. On our constructed 96-task flyback repair benchmark, comprising injected single faults, coupled faults, and held-out natural failures from our design generator, PowerFix achieves 78.7% verified repair success rate (VRSR), compared with 30.1% for the strongest of three LLM baselines. Three additional replanning rounds increase VRSR to 88.4%. These offline results provide pilot-stage evidence within a simulation-based design workflow, with hardware prototype validation and transfer to a second converter topology as the next steps toward deployment.

</details>


### [347] [EVA: Evidence-seeking Visual Agent for Hallucination-Resistant Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33706)
*Ting Lei,Yu Chen,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EVA：将多模态推理重构为"证据获取—验证"的显式过程，通过迭代工具重规划、证据一致性验证与选择性证据路由，在提升推理可靠性的同时保持效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM幻觉源于未经检验的视觉假设；自反思、多智能体辩论、反事实验证因共享同一未验证视觉假设而可能强化错误结论；外部视觉工具虽可提供可验证证据，但模型可能选错工具、给错参数、或忽略/抵触所得证据。

Method: 提出EVA（Evidence-seeking Visual Agent），核心为统一证据落地执行流水线，含两紧耦合机制：(1) Iterative Tool Replanning——基于中间观测动态细化工具选择与参数；(2) Evidence Consistency Verification——答案验证机制，提升最终预测与所收集证据的一致性。另设Selective Evidence Routing自适应快慢推理策略，简单查询直接推理，复杂/高风险查询触发工具取证。

Result: 实验表明EVA在推理可靠性上显著优于直接VLM推理与多智能体方法，并通过选择性调用工具保持效率。

Conclusion: 通过同时解决"如何可靠获取证据"与"如何一致地使用证据"，EVA将多模态推理从被动答案验证转变为显式证据支撑的决策过程。

Abstract: Hallucinations in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) often arise from visual assumptions that go unchecked. Self-reflection, multi-agent debate, and counterfactual verification can reinforce erroneous conclusions when they rely on the same unverified visual assumptions. External visual tools can provide verifiable evidence, but models may select the wrong tools, supply incorrect parameters, or ignore or contradict the evidence they obtain. In this work, we propose EVA, an Evidence-seeking Visual Agent that reframes multimodal reasoning as an explicit process of evidence acquisition and verification. At the core of EVA is a unified evidence-grounded execution pipeline designed to address the above challenges through two tightly coupled mechanisms. First, we introduce Iterative Tool Replanning, which dynamically refines tool selection and parameters based on intermediate observations, improving the reliability of tool usage. Second, we develop Evidence Consistency Verification, an answer validation mechanism that improves alignment between the final prediction and the collected evidence, mitigating evidence--reasoning inconsistencies. In addition, to balance robustness and efficiency, EVA incorporates Selective Evidence Routing, an adaptive fast--slow reasoning strategy that determines when external evidence is necessary. Visually straightforward queries are handled via direct reasoning, while complex or high-risk cases trigger tool-based evidence acquisition. By jointly addressing how to reliably acquire evidence and how to use it consistently, EVA transforms multimodal reasoning from passive answer validation into explicit evidence-backed decision making. Experimental results demonstrate that EVA achieves significantly improved reasoning reliability over direct VLM inference and multi-agent methods, while maintaining efficiency through selective tool invocation.

</details>


### [348] [AutoformBench: A Lean Formalization Benchmark for Advanced Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33165)
*Charles Arnal,Ahmad Rammal,Niket Patel,Fabian Gloeckle,Bartosz Piotrowski,Amaury Hayat,Julia Kempe,Rémi Munos,Vivien Cabannes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 AutoformBench：341 个 Lean 4 形式化任务的基准，源自 24 个高等数学来源；模型需补全定义、引理与基础设施而非仅证孤立定理。前沿模型表现差异不大，难点集中在形式化基础设施的构建能力而非数学领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多在 Mathlib 已具备支撑理论的孤立定理设定下进行，与真实数学形式化中存在大量缺失定义与中间引理的情形不符，缺乏对"连接结果与形式库"能力的评测。

Method: 构建 AutoformBench：由具备 Lean 经验的数学家编写并审计 341 个目标陈述、保留证明由模型完成；每题嵌入更完整的数学展开，给定非形式化证明，要求模型将其翻译为 Lean 并构造缺失定义、中间引理与支撑设施；据此横向比较前沿模型。

Result: 前沿模型呈现大体一致的难度前沿；成功率更多取决于模型构建缺失形式化基础设施的能力，而非数学领域本身。

Conclusion: 真实形式化的瓶颈在于基础设施构建而非孤立定理证明，评测应关注这一维度；数据与代码已开源。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly capable of producing machine-checked Lean proofs of advanced mathematical results. Real mathematical formalization, however, is not isolated theorem proving: it requires building the definitions and intermediate lemmas that connect a result to a formal library. Existing evaluations often provide isolated theorem statements in settings where much of this supporting theory is already available in Mathlib. We introduce AutoformBench, a benchmark of 341 Lean 4 formalization tasks drawn from 24 advanced mathematical sources. Lean-experienced mathematicians formalized and audited each target statement, leaving the corresponding Lean proof to the evaluated agent. Each problem is embedded in a broader mathematical development: agents receive the informal proof but must translate it into Lean while constructing the missing definitions, intermediate lemmas, and supporting infrastructure. We compare the performance of frontier models on these problems. We find a largely shared difficulty frontier: success depends less on mathematical domain than on models' ability to construct missing formal infrastructure. Data and code at https://github.com/facebookresearch/autoformbench

</details>


### [349] [Constraint-Aware Test-Time Scoring for LLM and VLM Candidate Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33161)
*Rangarajan Ramesh,Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应打分框架，将硬性检查与软性检查结合用于候选答案选择；消融表明增益主要来自硬检查与候选比较的结合，而按任务配置软检查权重无显著价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有候选答案挑选方式（模型自身置信度、学习式judge、领域检查）各有权衡缺陷：流畅但不安全、数学合理但物理不可行、judge高分却违反任务要求。

Method: 三组件框架：drafting model生成候选；configuring model为每个任务选择并加权检查项；scoring models评估无工具可计算的性质。硬检查须全部通过，软检查分数融合为单一得分，并在CWEval、LIBERO（含安全变体）、LiveCodeBench、HotpotQA四个任务上验证，另做组件消融。

Result: 硬检查+候选比较带来最大增益：联合正确性与安全性提升达+0.21（检索增强QA），代码生成安全性提升达+0.28（CWEval）；增益随首个采样质量的提高而减小，在首个采样已较强时接近零或略负。按任务软检查加权层相对更简单方案无显著收益。

Conclusion: 构建了统一打分框架，并指出增益来源是可落地的硬检查而非软因子配置。

Abstract: When an LLM or VLM produces several candidate answers, picking the best one usually relies on the model's own confidence, a learned judge model, or a domain check. Each has a known weakness: an answer can be fluent but insecure, mathematically plausible but physically infeasible, or rated highly by a judge while quietly breaking a task requirement. We propose a scoring framework that combines a small set of hard checks every answer must pass with a wider set of softer checks whose scores are blended into one number per candidate. This adaptive scoring framework consists of three components: a drafting model that produces the candidates, a configuring model that selects and weights the checks for each task, and scoring models that rate properties no grounded tool can compute. We apply our proposed method to four tasks: writing code that is both correct and secure (CWEval), generating robot manipulation plans (LIBERO with added safety variants), passing competitive-programming hidden tests (LiveCodeBench), and answering questions from retrieved documents (HotpotQA). An ablation study isolates the contribution of each component and finds that the largest gains come from combining hard checks with candidate comparison: on tasks where the first sampled answer is often broken or unsafe, this pairing lifts joint correctness-and-safety by up to +0.21 (retrieval-augmented QA) and code-generation safety by up to +0.28 (CWEval), with the size of the gain tracking how weak the first sample is, falling to near zero or slightly negative where it is already strong. The per-task layer that selects and weights the softer checks adds no measurable value over simpler alternatives. This builds a unified scoring framework, together with an ablation that identifies the grounded hard checks, not the soft-factor configuration, as the source of the gain.

</details>


### [350] [Few-Shot Adaptation of Vision-Language Models via Agentic Model Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34036)
*Yusheng Zhao,Xiao Luo,Zhiping Xiao,Ming Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Agentic Model Editing (AME)：由LLM agent利用少量样本与模型观测，自动设计领域定制化的适配程序（输入变换、编码器编辑、特征计算、分类器拟合），在11个数据集上取得对比方法中最优平均精度（无反向传播81.2%，有反向传播81.9%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少样本适配方法将prompt、表征或分类器的拟合限制在预先设定的流程内，而识别所需的视觉差异在不同领域间差异显著，因此需要让适配流程本身可被自动设计。

Method: AME是一个agentic框架：agent检查图像、类语义、模型参数、中间表征与激活以形成任务假设，并把输入变换、encoder编辑、特征计算与分类器拟合联合设计为可执行程序；在support set上实现并测试候选程序，以聚合验证精度指导迭代与选择；每个程序在测试前拟合出完整的领域模型并固定；agent与工具有跨域共享，程序逐域定制。

Result: 在CLIP ViT-B/16上，11个数据集中AME取得对比方法中最高平均精度：无反向传播81.2%，有反向传播81.9%；额外实验显示其可跨VLM骨干与不同LLM agent迁移。

Conclusion: 将少样本适配从"在固定流程内调参"转为"由LLM agent设计适配程序"是可行且有效的，领域定制化程序可稳定提升VLM的跨域识别性能。

Abstract: Few-shot adaptation transfers pretrained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to new domains using a small set of labeled images. Most methods fit prompts, representations, or classifiers within an adaptation procedure specified in advance. Yet the visual distinctions needed for recognition can differ substantially across domains, motivating the question: Can an LLM agent use few-shot examples and model observations to design the adaptation procedure itself? We propose Agentic Model Editing (AME), an agentic framework that jointly designs input transformations, encoder edits, feature computation, and classifier fitting as an executable, domain-customized adaptation program. The agent inspects images, class semantics, model parameters, intermediate representations, and activations to formulate hypotheses about the target task. It implements and tests candidate programs on the support set, with aggregate validation accuracy guiding refinement and selection. Each program fits a complete domain model that is fixed before testing. The agent and its tools are shared across domains, while each domain receives its own adaptation program. Across eleven datasets with CLIP ViT-B/16, AME achieves the highest average accuracies of 81.2% without back-propagation and 81.9% with back-propagation among the compared methods. Further experiments demonstrate applicability across VLM backbones and LLM agents.

</details>


### [351] [EATS: Scaling Tool-Use Experience for Medical Reasoning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33897)
*Mingyuan Li,Tong Jia,Haoran Wang,Wenbo Qi,Ziyuan Yang,Bowen Ma,Jie Gui,Baosheng Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EATS：一种经验增强的医疗推理智能体工具选择策略，用累积的工具使用轨迹构建记忆并按相似查询检索，无需真值监督即可随测试时经验扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用LLM对领域专用医疗工具的训练暴露有限；神经工具输出具有不确定性和分布偏移，使得仅凭简单描述进行工具选择比计算器、字典查询等确定性场景困难得多。

Method: 构建过去交互的记忆，存储查询、工具选择、参数与自评估反馈；对新查询检索相关经验以指导工具选择。因记忆构建不需真值监督，可自然随测试时经验扩展。

Result: 在ChestAgentBench、CheXbench、MIMIC-CXR、SLAKE-VQA四个公开数据集与Qwen3-VL 2B、Qwen3-VL 32B、GPT-5.5三个多模态LLM上，工具选择行为与下游医疗推理性能均获一致提升。

Conclusion: 经验检索式记忆可有效缓解神经工具不确定性带来的选择难题，为医疗推理智能体提供可扩展、无需标注的工具选择增强方案。

Abstract: Medical reasoning agents combine large language model (LLM) reasoning with clinical workflows and specialized neural tools. However, general-purpose LLMs receive limited training exposure to domain-specific medical tools, and the uncertainty and distributional shifts of neural tool outputs make selecting tools from simple descriptions significantly more challenging than in deterministic settings such as calculators or dictionary lookups. In this paper, we propose EATS, an experience-augmented tool selection strategy that leverages accumulated tool-use trajectories to improve tool selection in medical reasoning agents. EATS constructs a memory of past interactions, including queries, tool choices, arguments, and self-evaluated feedback, and retrieves relevant experiences to guide tool selection for new queries. As memory construction does not require ground-truth supervision, EATS scales naturally with test-time experience. We evaluate EATS on tool-augmented medical reasoning tasks, including closed-ended visual question answering and free-form report generation. Experiments on four public datasets, ChestAgentBench, CheXbench, MIMIC-CXR, and SLAKE-VQA, across three multimodal LLMs, Qwen3-VL 2B, Qwen3-VL 32B, and GPT-5.5, show consistent gains in tool selection behavior and downstream medical reasoning performance. Code is available at https://github.com/Limingyuan001/EATS.

</details>


### [352] [Confidence scores for gated automation of large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33394)
*Luiz G. A. Alves,Aaron Vermeersch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文用监督学习为LLM答案预测正确性，通过在冻结的2B参数骨干的末层隐状态上拟合线性置信度头，并训练纯文本元模型作为闭源模型的替代方案，实现自动化决策门控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM常以高置信度输出错误答案，限制其在影响个人与组织的决策中的可用性。可靠且细粒度的置信度分数是决策门控的必要条件，但现有置信度估计往往不可用或过于粗糙，无法有效支持门控。

Method: 在冻结的LLM骨干（单一2B参数模型）最终层隐状态上拟合线性置信度评分头，用于预测LLM生成答案的正确性；同时对闭源模型训练仅依赖文本的元模型作为替代方案；在约98% SQuAD、其余为TruthfulQA的分级答案数据上，与多种基线方法对比评估。

Result: 置信度评分头优于所有基线和元模型，在高准确率目标下能自动化最大比例的工作负载；提示式置信度分数看似校准良好，但缺乏阈值化所需的分辨率；向未见数据集的迁移性一般，但置信度评分头仍是最稳健的方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的置信度评分框架提升了LLM智能体自动化的安全性，并可扩展用于门控其他属性（如安全性或合规性）。

Abstract: AI agents built on large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong capabilities in automated decision-making. However, LLMs may produce incorrect answers with high confidence, limiting their applicability in decisions that affect individuals and organizations. Reliable confidence scores are necessary to gate such decisions, and existing estimates are frequently unavailable or too coarse to support effective gating. This work uses supervised learning to train models to predict the correctness of LLM-generated answers, enabling automated confident responses and routing uncertain ones for human review. A linear confidence score head is fitted to the final-layer hidden state of the frozen LLM backbone, and text-only metamodels are trained as an alternative for closed models. All results come from a single 2B-parameter backbone, on graded answers that are about 98% SQuAD and the remainder TruthfulQA. Against multiple baselines, the confidence score head outperforms all baselines and metamodels, automating the largest fraction of the workload at high accuracy targets. Prompted confidence scores can appear well calibrated yet lack the resolution needed for thresholding. Although transferability to unseen datasets is modest, the confidence score head remains the most robust approach. The proposed confidence scoring framework enhances the safety of automating LLM-based agents and can be adapted to gate other properties, including safety or compliance.

</details>


### [353] [Certify What You Plant: Shortcut-Free Proof Depth in Logical Reasoning Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33303)
*Baptiste Juran,Dimitri Kachler,Damien Sileo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出用形式化"证明需求分析"审计程序化生成的逻辑题：计算每题所有证明都必经的步骤、最短证明、极小证明数量与不可或缺前提，并导出可被独立检查器验证的证书；进而用"捷径目录"构造每一条证明都具有指定结构的题目。审计发现八个数据集普遍存在标注深度虚高问题，训练实验表明等规模下混合经认证题型的数据迁移效果优于 FLDx2 式推导数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序化生成的逻辑题常用于评测与训练语言模型的推理能力，其难度通常通过"植入证明"（按目标深度采样一条证明并将其前提作为题目）来设定。但植入证明只是众多可能证明之一，题目可能存在更短证明或多个互不相关的证明，导致声明的深度高估了实际解题所需。因此需要度量题目在给定推理规则下真正要求的推理量。

Method: 1）定义相对给定推理规则集的题目"真实需求"，对每题计算：所有证明必经的步骤、最短证明、极小证明数量、不可或缺前提；2）导出机器可验证的证书，由独立检查器校验；3）用每个规则集只证一次的"捷径目录"构造每条证明均具备指定结构的新题，并逐题完整检查并附证书；4）在 Qwen3（0.6B–8B）上进行等规模训练数据配比实验。

Result: 审计八个数据集发现问题：ProofWriter、Blocksworld 的深度是强制的；FLDx2 中标注深度 5 的题实际只约需两次特定规则应用；ZebraLogic 的答案不依赖任何特定步骤。训练方面，混合多种深度的数据配比最优；等规模下，包含前向生成无法产生的模型检验题在内的经认证题型混合数据，比 FLDx2 式推导数据迁移效果更好。

Conclusion: 植入式深度标注不可靠，应以形式化证明需求分析与可验证证书替代；按证书构建、跨深度与跨题型混合的训练数据，在等规模条件下能带来更好的推理迁移，优于单一推导风格数据。

Abstract: Procedurally generated logic problems are used to both evaluate and train reasoning in language models. Their difficulty is usually set by planting a proof, that is, by sampling a proof of the desired depth and stating its premises as the problem. However, a planted proof is only one possible proof. A problem can also have shorter proofs, or several unrelated ones, and its stated depth then overstates what solving it requires. We measure what a problem actually requires, relative to a stated set of inference rules. For each problem, we compute the steps every proof must make, the shortest proof, the number of minimal proofs and the indispensable premises, and export certificates that an independent checker verifies. Audits of eight datasets find depth that is forced (ProofWriter, Blocksworld), problems labelled depth 5 that require about two specific rule applications (FLDx2), and answers that need no particular step (ZebraLogic). We then construct problems whose every proof has a requested structure, using a catalog of shortcuts proved once per rule set. Every constructed problem is checked in full and comes with its own certificate. In training, the best data mixes depths and problem types. At equal size, such mixtures of certified problem types, including model-checking problems that forward generation does not produce, transfer better than FLDx2-style derivation data for Qwen3 models from 0.6B to 8B.

</details>


### [354] [Not Every Input Needs Everything: Unifying Expert Gating, Model Cascades, and Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34112)
*Dat Tat Mai,Hung Tran Quy,Thai Viet Pham,James Jin Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该综述把稀疏神经门控、模型级联与多智能体协调三类"条件计算"研究统一到"先检查、再分配"的两步框架下，系统梳理2,289篇文献，揭示跨尺度共有的系统失效模式与可迁移的工程修复方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单一输入上无差别地运行整体权重、前沿基础模型或全套多智能体工作流，会带来巨大的算力、延迟与费用开销；条件计算本可缓解，但token级门控、查询级级联、任务级多智能体协调长期各自为政，形成研究孤岛，导致反复出现的系统失效被遮蔽、可迁移的解决方案难以复用。

Method: 系统性综述：从13,884条唯一记录中筛选出2017—2026年9月间发表的2,289项研究，归纳为20个方法族，并统一置于"inspect-then-allocate（先检查后分配）"两步框架下进行跨尺度比较分析；同时统计硬选择比例、开销瓶颈、工程保障措施分布与发表增速及偏倚风险。

Result: 离散硬选择在token、查询、任务三个尺度分别占70%、83%、91%；计算开销在各层级均为首要运行瓶颈。工程保障呈显著不对称：层内门控侧重负载均衡、极少引入不确定性估计，而模型选择与智能体编排依赖置信阈值与预算规则，却面临校准偏差与级联误差。发表量增长最快的是智能体任务委派，而该方向的路由机制最少被独立实验隔离，方法学偏倚风险最高。

Conclusion: 条件计算的三条研究脉络存在可共享的失效模式与工程对策，应打破学科壁垒、建立跨尺度统一的评估与保障规范；尤其需对快速膨胀但实验隔离不足的智能体任务委派方向加强严格评测与偏倚控制。

Abstract: Executing monolithic neural weights, frontier foundation models, or entire multi-agent workflows uniformly on every input incurs substantial computational, latency, and monetary overhead. Conditional computation mitigates this cost by deciding dynamically which internal experts, model endpoints, or specialized agents to invoke per request. Yet token-level gating in sparse neural architectures, query-level model cascades, and task-level multi-agent coordination have largely progressed in disconnected research silos, obscuring recurring system failures and transferable fixes. In this systematic review, we synthesize 2,289 studies published between 2017 and September 2026 (selected from 13,884 unique records) across 20 methodological families under a unified two-step inspect-then-allocate framework. Discrete hard selection accounts for 70%, 83%, and 91% of token-, query-, and task-scale literature respectively, and computational overhead appears as a top operational bottleneck across every layer. Engineering safeguards remain sharply asymmetric: intra-layer gating prioritizes load balancing while rarely incorporating uncertainty estimation, whereas model selection and agent orchestration rely on confidence thresholds and budget rules while confronting miscalibration and cascading errors. At the same time, publication volume is accelerating fastest in agentic task delegation, where routing mechanisms are least often isolated experimentally and methodological bias risk is highest.

</details>


### [355] [Evaluating Aviation Safety Knowledge Extraction with SmolLM2: From Zero-Shot to Retrieval-Grounded Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34432)
*Aziida Nanyonga,Mustafa Tahsin Yilmaz,Amir Hussain,Ugur Turhan,Graham Wild*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在300份NTSB航空事故叙述中评估SmolLM2-1.7B-Instruct四种提示策略的知识抽取表现，发现无单一策略在所有维度最优，定向关系抽取是主要瓶颈，并强调应分维度评价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空事故叙述蕴含结构化事件字段难以捕捉的安全知识，需评估提示式知识抽取方法的可靠性与可追溯性，并明确模型能力短板所在。

Method: 以300份2000–2020年NTSB事故叙述（3,763个参考概念、467条定向关系）为语料，对SmolLM2-1.7B-Instruct施加zero-shot、one-shot、固定few-shot与检索增强（retrieval-grounded）四种提示策略；从概念准确率、逐字证据溯源、结构合规性、定向关系抽取四维度评估，采用结构检查、逐字证据匹配、专家复核语义匹配及配对bootstrap置信区间；并以35份基准叙述做Qwen3-8B探索性对比。

Result: 无策略在全部维度最优：检索增强概念micro-F1最高（32.27%），但相对固定few-shot优势不确定（95% CI −0.92~2.40 pp）；one-shot关系F1最高仅6.95%；检索增强提升逐字证据溯源（59.45% vs 54.02%）但降低完全结构合规率（33.3% vs 42.7%）。探索性子集上Qwen3-8B在one-shot下达概念micro-F1 51.37%、关系F1 34.62%。

Conclusion: 定向关系抽取是小模型的主要瓶颈；逐字匹配仅证明来源存在而非证据相关性；应分别评估语义正确性、来源可追溯性、结构可靠性与定向关系准确性，且小样本跨模型对比结论有限。

Abstract: Aviation accident narratives contain safety knowledge that is difficult to capture using structured occurrence fields alone. This study evaluates prompting-based knowledge extraction across four dimensions: concept accuracy, verbatim evidence grounding, structural compliance, and directed relation extraction. SmolLM2-1.7B-Instruct was evaluated under zero-shot, one-shot, fixed few-shot, and retrieval-grounded prompting using 300 National Transportation Safety Board narratives from 2000-2020, containing 3,763 reference concepts and 467 directed relations. Evaluation combined structural checks, verbatim evidence matching, expert-reviewed semantic matching, and paired bootstrap confidence intervals. No prompting strategy performed best across all dimensions. Retrieval-grounded prompting achieved the highest concept micro-F1 of 32.27%, although its advantage over fixed few-shot prompting was uncertain (95% CI: -0.92 to 2.40 percentage points). One-shot prompting achieved the highest relation F1 of 6.95%, identifying directed relation extraction as the principal SmolLM2 bottleneck. Compared with fixed few-shot prompting, retrieval improved verbatim grounding (59.45% vs. 54.02%) but reduced complete structural compliance (33.3% vs. 42.7%). Verbatim matching establishes source presence rather than evidential relevance. An exploratory comparison using 35 benchmark narratives showed that Qwen3-8B achieved higher concept micro-F1 across all prompting conditions and higher relation F1 under demonstration-based prompting, reaching 51.37% and 34.62%, respectively, under one-shot prompting. The small subset limits generalization. These findings support evaluating semantic correctness, source traceability, structural reliability, and directed relation accuracy separately.

</details>


### [356] [Learning from Natural Images Untangles Number Sense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33615)
*Yechan Cho,Hyeonsu Lee,Emma Deneuville,Jaeson Jang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文用几何框架追踪视觉学习中数量(numerosity)与比例(proportion)表征如何变化，发现自然图像预训练虽不改变单元级数量选择性和基本比较能力，但增大两子空间夹角、降低重叠，从而在冲突条件下实现更稳健的读出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视觉-语言模型在数值认知上仍具挑战；而numerically naïve动物、未训练及经无关任务训练的深度网络均会自发涌现数量选择性单元。因此核心问题在于：视觉学习除了让数量选择性自发涌现之外，是否还有额外贡献。人类发展证据（儿童在数量与比例冲突判断上变得更好）提示可从冲突条件下的表征分离入手。

Method: 提出几何框架，量化学习前后数量与比例表征子空间之间的夹角与重叠度；比较未训练网络与自然图像预训练网络的单元级数量选择性及基本比较表现；直接操纵这些几何属性以检验其功能贡献；并比较监督预训练、无类别标签的自监督学习、以及无自然图像暴露的合成图像训练三种条件。

Result: 未训练与预训练网络在单元级数量选择性和基本比较表现上相似；自然图像预训练增大数量与比例子空间夹角、减少其重叠，使冲突条件下读出更稳健。操纵几何属性可改变解码准确率与功能独立性。数量分离并不依赖常规监督预训练：自监督学习和合成图像训练亦可产生该效应。

Conclusion: 结构化视觉学习可将自发涌现的number sense精炼为稳健且功能独立的数量表征，且无需显式数值训练或类别标签监督。

Abstract: Numerical cognition is a simple yet fundamental ability that remains challenging for modern vision-language models. Meanwhile, number sense is already present in numerically naïve animals, suggesting that it can emerge without explicit numerical learning. Deep neural networks provide a related observation, with numerosity-selective units appearing after training on unrelated natural-image tasks and even before any training. However, these findings leave unresolved whether visual learning contributes beyond the spontaneous emergence of numerical selectivity. Human development provides a clue, as children become better at making judgments in which numerosity and proportion conflict. Here, we introduce a geometric framework to trace how representations of these two quantities change across learning. Although untrained and pretrained networks exhibited similar unit-level numerosity selectivity and basic comparison performance, natural-image pretraining increased the angle between numerosity and proportion subspaces while reducing their overlap, supporting more robust readout under conflict. Direct manipulation of these geometric properties further demonstrated their functional contributions to decoding accuracy and functional independence. Importantly, conventional supervised pretraining was not essential for quantity separation, which also emerged through self-supervised learning without category labels and synthetic-image training without natural-image exposure. Together, our findings suggest that structured visual learning can refine spontaneously emerging number sense into robust and functionally independent quantity representations without explicit numerical training.

</details>


### [357] [FIG-MAC: A Fine-grained Inspiration Graph Empowered Multi-Agent Collaboration for Automated Scientific Hypothesis Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33563)
*Siyuan Chen,Yuping Gao,Tai Huang,Yuling Zhou,Dongyu Su,Tongguan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FIG-MAC框架，用细粒度灵感图、本体引导混合推理与角色分工多智能体协作，解决自动科学假设生成中的知识表示粗、演化不可追溯、协作缺失问题，在来源多样性、溯源校正新颖性和原始新颖性上显著超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的自动科学假设生成存在三大局限：(i) 知识表示不够细粒度，非结构化上下文损害LLM的理解与推理；(ii) 缺乏知识演化可追溯性，制约跨域创新猜想的产生；(iii) 缺少受人类启发的角色专业化多智能体协作，未能利用认知多样性与专家分工的高效创新机制，也难以完成"生成—评估"闭环。

Method: FIG-MAC包含三个模块：(i) 细粒度灵感图（FIG）模块，节点表示学术语义单元、边编码其间的启发式关系，获得结构化知识表示；(ii) 本体引导的混合推理模块，依FIG本体构建结构化推理路径，并用向量检索补充语义证据，得到可追溯的科学猜想路径；(iii) 角色专业化多智能体框架，仿照真实科研团队分工，实现假设生成、筛选与验证的闭环。

Result: 实验结果表明，FIG-MAC在来源多样性、溯源校正新颖性（provenance-adjusted novelty）与原始新颖性三项指标上显著优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 细粒度结构化知识表示、可追溯推理路径与角色分工多智能体协作相结合，能够有效提升自动科学假设生成的质量与创新性，并支撑生成—评估的完整闭环。

Abstract: Automated Scientific Hypothesis Generation (ASHG) leveraging large language models (LLMs) aims to harness their strengths in knowledge integration, logical reasoning, and text generation for efficiently producing innovative, verifiable scientific hypotheses. However, existing approaches face three critical limitations: (i) insufficient fine-grained knowledge representation, as unstructured context impairs LLM comprehension and reasoning; (ii) lack of knowledge evolution traceability, constraining the generation of innovative cross-domain conjectures; (iii) absence of human-inspired and role-specialized multi-agent collaboration, failing to leverage the efficient innovation mechanisms arising from cognitive diversity and division of labor among expert roles to complete the full ASHG-evaluation loop. To address these limitations, we propose the Fine-grained Inspiration Graph-empowered Multi-Agent Collaboration framework (FIG-MAC) including: (i) Fine-grained Inspiration Graph module (FIG), to obtain fine-grained structured knowledge representations, where nodes represent academic semantic units and edges encode heuristic relationships among them; (ii) ontology-guided hybrid reasoning module, which follows the FIG ontology to construct structured reasoning paths and uses vector search to enrich them with semantic evidence, to obtain the traceable scientific conjecture paths; (iii) role-specialized Multi-Agent framework, inspired by real scientific teamwork, enabling a closed-loop process of scientific conjecture generation, screening, and validation. Experimental results demonstrate that FIG-MAC significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods on source diversity, provenance-adjusted novelty, and raw novelty.

</details>


### [358] [Agentic Waterfall: An Intent-Driven Information Discovery Framework for Reclaiming User Control in the Attention Economy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34407)
*Yige Luo,Shining Zhang,Jikun Liu,Hongjiao Qiang,Ran Guan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"智能体瀑布流"（Agentic Waterfall）这一新问题，即用户主动引导推荐、系统从显式请求与浏览行为中推断演化需求的个性化信息发现；并提出同名框架、数据集与基于回放的评测协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐范式多为系统单方面推送、用户参与度低，难以适应随显式指令与行为信号不断演变的个性化需求，缺乏对应任务定义与评测手段。

Method: 以语言模型作为控制器，维护持久化用户状态，并协调内容检索、排序与状态更新；构建融合真实内容元数据与模拟交互历史的Agentic Waterfall Dataset；设计回放式评测协议，在共享历史下用相关性分数及对推荐是否满足当前用户目标与约束的盲评进行对比。

Result: 在五个公开基准的传统推荐任务上，该方法与强基线保持竞争力；在Agentic Waterfall Dataset上，两种评测均优于最强基线，相对提升最高达16.1%。

Conclusion: 智能体化、状态持久化的语言模型控制器可有效支撑用户主动引导的个性化信息发现，所提任务、数据集与评测协议为该方向提供了可复现的研究基础。

Abstract: We formalize Agentic Waterfall Feed, a new problem of personalized information discovery in which users actively direct recommendations and the system adapts to evolving needs inferred from both explicit requests and browsing behavior. We address this problem with the Agentic Waterfall framework, whose language-model controller maintains persistent user state and coordinates content retrieval, ranking, and state updates. To evaluate systems on this task, we contribute the Agentic Waterfall Dataset and a replay-based evaluation protocol. The dataset combines real content metadata with simulated interaction histories. The protocol compares systems under shared histories using relevance scores and blind judgments of how well recommendations satisfy current user goals and constraints. Agentic Waterfall remains competitive with strong baselines on conventional recommendation tasks across five public benchmarks. On the Agentic Waterfall Dataset, it outperforms the strongest baseline in both evaluations, with relative improvements of up to 16.1%.

</details>


### [359] [PatternMiner: Automated Mathematical Discovery Through LLM-Guided Training and Interpretation of Small Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33966)
*Michael Ruofan Zeng,Junaid Hasan,Xuanyu Yang,Emily Xia Meng,Henry Kvinge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 PatternMiner，一个将大语言模型智能体与窄模型+可解释性探索相结合的系统，用于代数组合学中正性问题的猜想生成、反例搜索与迭代精炼，并证明了若干新定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 驱动的数学发现存在两条割裂路径：一是用 LLM 通过文本推理解决表述明确的猜想；二是用窄模型与可解释性技术在人类参与下对对象型数学数据进行开放式探索。前者不适合直接、开放式地探索大规模数学数据集，后者则局限于简单的经验性模式检测，难以超越。因此需要融合两种范式的统一系统。

Method: PatternMiner 的流程为：LLM 智能体基于初始宽泛问题生成数学数据集；在其上训练窄机器学习模型；用可解释性技术从训练模型中提取模式；据此提出猜想。猜想形成后，智能体借助数学软件调用搜索反例；若找到反例，则将其作为反馈用于猜想精炼或开启新一轮训练与可解释性分析。猜想在此迭代序列中逐步演化，且智能体可在任意阶段尝试证明。

Result: 在代数组合学中五个著名的正性问题上的应用显示：PatternMiner 证明了与 Schubert 正性和投影相关的新定理、一个特征行求值公式、Kazhdan–Lusztig 组合不变性猜想的一个受限版本，以及一族 immanant 系数的 Schur 正性。此外，论文还分析了案例研究中最常见的猜想生成路径及最常见的失败模式。

Conclusion: 将 LLM 智能体与窄模型可解释性探索耦合是有效的数学发现范式，可在正性问题中获得新定理；同时对生成路径与失败模式的分析为后续系统改进提供了依据。

Abstract: There are several approaches to AI-driven mathematical discovery. One of these involves the use of large language models (LLMs) to resolve well-specified conjectures via text-based reasoning, while another uses narrow models and interpretability for more open-ended, human-in-the-loop exploration using object-based mathematical data. The latter is limited in its ability to move beyond simple empirical pattern detection while the former is not well-designed for direct, open-ended exploration of large mathematical datasets. In this paper we introduce PatternMiner, a system that combines these two paradigms. In PatternMiner, an LLM agent generates mathematical datasets based on an initial broad question, trains narrow machine learning models on them, uses interpretability techniques to extract patterns from these trained models, and uses these patterns to formulate conjectures. Once an initial conjecture has been formulated, PatternMiner instructs the agent to search for counterexamples using calls to mathematical software. If a set of counterexamples can be found, these are used as feedback for conjecture refinement or another round of training and interpretability. The conjecture progressively evolves through this iterative sequence with the agent allowed to try to prove it at any point. We describe the results of applying PatternMiner to five well-known positivity problems in algebraic combinatorics. PatternMiner proves several new theorems related to Schubert positivity and projection, a character-row evaluation formula, a restricted version of the Kazhdan--Lusztig combinatorial invariance conjecture, and Schur positivity for a family of immanant coefficients. We end by analyzing the most common routes to conjecture generation that are used by PatternMiner in our case studies as well as documenting the most common failure modes.

</details>


### [360] [CHD-Bench: Diagnosing Congenital Heart Disease through Interactive Examination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33775)
*Gautham Krishnan P,Shrisharanyan Vasu,Aniketh Vijesh,Rajagopalan Rameshbabu,Harikrishnan Anil Maya,Sarin Xavier,Mahesh Kappanayil,Gilad Gressel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出首个交互式先天性心脏病（CHD）诊断基准 CHD-Bench 及配套开源工具 CHD-Harness，通过让视觉语言模型（VLM）在 3D Slicer 环境中调用 22 个工具完成三维影像阅片，实现多标签 CHD 诊断，并验证交互式检查显著优于静态视图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CHD 诊断依赖对心脏结构空间关系的理解，临床需滚动 CT/MRI 切片并旋转 3D 重建；现有自动化方法虽在心脏分割上有进展，但基于体扫描的缺陷识别仍是开放难题，且已有的两类方法依赖仅覆盖静态解剖异常的手工诊断规则。

Method: 构建 CHD-Bench 基准，覆盖 3 个队列共 247 例 CT 与 MRI 扫描、38 种 CHD 类型，定义多标签任务与评测协议；开发 CHD-Harness 开源软件，使 VLM 在 3D Slicer 环境中通过 22 个工具操作，管理每次检查并记录诊断结果与支撑视图；评测通用医疗、开源权重与闭源权重 VLM，并与 3D CNN 分类器及已发表规则方法对比。

Result: Gemini 4 Argon 结合 CHD-Harness 在三个队列上取得最高 macro F1（0.63、0.63、0.43）；相较静态视图，允许交互式检查使其 macro F1 提升最多 20 个百分点。

Conclusion: CHD-Bench 与 CHD-Harness 为交互式 CHD 诊断中 VLM 的开发与评测奠定了基础。

Abstract: Diagnosis of congenital heart disease (CHD) requires understanding the spatial relationships between cardiac structures, which clinicians assess by scrolling through CT or cardiac magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) slices and rotating 3D reconstructions. Although automated methods have advanced cardiac segmentation, identifying congenital heart defects from volumetric scans remains an open challenge. The two existing approaches rely on handcrafted diagnostic rules limited to static anatomical abnormalities. We introduce CHD-Bench, the first benchmark for interactive CHD diagnosis, defining a multi-label task and evaluation protocol over 247 CT and MRI scans from three cohorts covering 38 CHD types. We also present CHD-Harness, open-source software that runs a VLM in a 3D Slicer environment through 22 tools, manages each examination, and records the resulting diagnosis and supporting views. We evaluate off-the-shelf medical, open weight, and closed weight VLMs and compare them with a 3D CNN classifier and published rule-based results. Gemini 4 Argon with CHD-Harness achieves the highest macro F1 across all three cohorts (0.63, 0.63, and 0.43). Allowing for interactive examination raises its macro F1 by up to 20 points over static views. These results establish CHD-Bench and CHD-Harness as a foundation for developing and evaluating VLMs for interactive CHD diagnosis.

</details>


### [361] [What, Who, and When: Structured Harm Assessment for Mental Health Safety via Short Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33263)
*Catarina Botelho,António Farinhas,John Mendonça,Areti Vassilopoulos,Inês Gonçalves,Maya D'Eon,Hugo Pitorro,Laura Cabello,Carrie Spadine,Nuno M. Guerreiro,Ricardo Rei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一个临床依据的三维伤害分类法（伤害是什么、涉及谁、何时发生），将伤害刻画与干预决策分离，使安全策略可由临床医生审核的显式指令指定；据此重标公开基准并发现在真实对话中"针对用户的伤害"占近一半而基准中罕见；训练了35B-A3B的推理式guard模型MindGuard2（短推理链SFT+带推理长度惩罚的RL），在约1/10中位延迟下达到与GLM-5.3相当的平衡准确率，推进准确率-延迟帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM用于心理健康支持日益增多，安全风险突出。现有guard模型主要做有害内容的类别识别，忽略判断"何种回应恰当"所需的上下文；同时安全策略固化在模型权重中，难以更新与审计。

Method: 1) 构建临床依据的三维分类法（what/who/when），并将伤害刻画与干预决策解耦，用临床医生策展的显式指令表达安全策略；2) 经专家临床医生验证分类法，并验证一组LLM评审以扩展标注；3) 按该分类法重标公开基准；4) 训练MindGuard2：35B-A3B推理式guard模型，先用短推理轨迹做监督微调，再用带推理长度惩罚的强化学习，以降低推理开销。

Result: 重标显示公开基准中针对用户的伤害极少，而真实对话中约占一半伤害。MindGuard2在两个公开基准与真实对话上取得与体量大得多的GLM-5.3相当的平衡准确率，中位延迟约低10倍；在高召回下保持低假阳性率。

Conclusion: 提出可审计、可更新的临床分类法安全框架，证明推理式guard模型可在低延迟下达到SOTA，并以高召回低假阳性平衡安全检测与过度干预风险；开源该guardrail模型、合成对话及公开基准的合成标注。

Abstract: The growing use of large language models (LLMs) for mental health support raises important safety concerns. Existing guard models primarily identify categories of harmful content, often overlooking the context needed to determine an appropriate response. We introduce a clinically grounded taxonomy that characterizes harm along three dimensions: what the harm is, who is involved, and when it occurs. It further separates harm characterization from intervention decisions, thus enabling safety policies to be specified outside the model weights through explicit, clinician-curated instructions that can be updated and audited. We validate the taxonomy with expert clinicians and validate a panel of LLM judges to scale annotation. Relabeling public benchmarks in this taxonomy shows that they rarely contain harm directed at the user, which accounts for nearly half of the harm in real conversations. We then train MindGuard2, a 35B-A3B reasoning-based guard model using supervised fine-tuning on short reasoning traces followed by reinforcement learning with a reasoning-length penalty, to minimize inference overhead while preserving the benefits of reasoning. On two public benchmarks and real-world conversations, MindGuard2 achieves balanced accuracy comparable to the substantially larger GLM-5.3 at approximately 10x lower median latency, advancing the accuracy-latency Pareto frontier. Furthermore, it achieves low false-positive rates at high recall, helping balance the need to detect unsafe situations against the risk of unnecessary interventions that may disrupt support and undermine user trust. We release a safety guardrail model with SOTA performance trained on synthetic data, together with synthetic conversations and annotations of public benchmarks in our taxonomy.

</details>


### [362] [Learning to Reason in Real-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33513)
*Xavier Schneider,Benjamin Schneider,Sun Sun,Olga Vechtomova,Victor Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"实时推理训练"（RRT）这一训练范式，让智能体学会在实时环境中权衡"何时快速行动"与"何时深思"，通过三类简单技术显著超越强基线（分布内平均+33%、分布外平均+55% 奖励）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体主要在完全同步的环境中训练：世界会等待智能体动作后才演化。而真实环境是实时的，其变化独立于智能体。现有弥合差距的方法只是人为设定"思考与行动"的固定比例，但真正的实时智能体应学会：延迟代价高时快速行动，深思收益超过错失机会成本时延长推理。

Method: 提出 real-time reasoning training（RRT）作为实时推理智能体训练技术的类别，并给出三种标准 RRT 技术：(1) 使用实时数据（real-time data）；(2) 解耦行动与观察（decoupling acting and observing）；(3) 时间维度上的课程学习（curriculum learning in time）。

Result: 在 Frogger 与 Snake 两个实时环境中，跨不同时间压力条件下，相较最强非 RRT 基线，分布内任务平均提升 33% 奖励，分布外任务平均提升 55% 奖励。

Conclusion: 这些简单技术共同显著优于强基线，表明可以训练智能体在实时约束下平衡推理与响应性；RRT 为实时智能体开辟了一类广泛的新能力方向。

Abstract: AI agents that operate in the real world must reason correctly and on-time, yet they currently do not account for how time should alter their reasoning. Modern agents learn to do tasks in environments that are entirely synchronous: where the world waits for the agent to act before changing. A real-time environment, by contrast, changes independent of the agent. Existing approaches try to bridge this gap by imposing a ratio of thinking to acting. However, for an agent to succeed in real-time it must learn to act quickly when delay is costly and to reason longer when the benefit outweighs the missed opportunities. To this end, we introduce real-time reasoning training (RRT) as the class of training techniques for real-time reasoning agents. We develop three standard RRT techniques: (1) using real-time data, (2) decoupling acting and observing, and (3) curriculum learning in time. Together, these simple techniques substantially outperform strong baselines. Across time pressures, we outperform the strongest non-RRT baseline by an average of 33% reward on in-distribution and 55% reward on out-of-distribution tasks in two real-time environments: Frogger and Snake. These results demonstrate that we can teach agents to balance reasoning and responsiveness under real-time constraints, and that RRT opens a broad, new set of capabilities for real-time agents.

</details>


### [363] [Dynamic Reasoning in Next-Gen Detection and Response: An Empirical Study of LLM Triage, Reinforcement Tuning, and Adversarial Evasion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33470)
*Jeremy Canale*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对下一代检测与响应流水线中的动态推理进行端到端实证研究，在严格的分析师注意力预算（每日B条告警下的召回率）下，系统评估静态分类器、基于图的信号融合以及LLM分诊代理的表现，覆盖56个可复现实验（多层企业—云模拟器与4个公开数据集），检验了12项关于现代大规模舰队防御的架构主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检测与响应研究多孤立评测模型性能，忽略真实部署中的核心约束：分析师注意力预算有限、舰队规模扩张、以及自适应对手。作者希望在统一、可复现的框架下，检验关于舰队级防御架构的诸多"常识性"主张是否成立。

Method: 构建多层企业—云模拟器，并结合SOREL-20M、BODMAS、LANL、DARPA Transparent Computing四个公开数据集，共开展56个可复现实验，围绕12项架构主张进行对比：静态分类器、图信号融合（稀有度加权图风险传播 vs. GraphSAGE）、LLM分诊代理（前沿推理模型Gemini与微调开源权重模型Qwen3-4B）、GRPO后训练 vs. 两轮监督微调，以及对抗性掩蔽（cover story）攻击与确定性策略门控（policy harness）。

Result: 核心发现包括：(1) 舰队规模扩张会在固定分析师预算下使召回率减半，尽管计算成本可忽略（每百万事件<$0.0003）；稀有度加权图风险传播无需神经训练即优于GraphSAGE；(2) 无约束的LLM自动关闭告警可消除90%误报，但同时静默丢弃31–44%的真实攻击样本；(3) GRPO后训练在未见攻击类别上出现严重奖励黑客，相比两轮SFT最多抑制86%的新型攻击；(4) 面对在良性遥测字段中生成可信"掩护故事"的自适应对手，若不在模型调用前施加确定性策略门控，LLM分诊的规避率会急剧上升。

Conclusion: 在分析师预算受限的舰队级场景中，模型精度并非瓶颈，架构设计才是关键：图信号融合无需神经训练亦可有效，但LLM分诊的自动关闭与GRPO后训练存在严重的安全—效用失衡与奖励黑客风险；确定性策略门控是抵御自适应规避的必要前置条件。作者主张以"预算约束下的召回率"作为统一评估标准，并对LLM自动化分诊采取审慎、可门控的部署方式。

Abstract: We present an end-to-end empirical study of dynamic reasoning in next-generation detection and response pipelines, evaluating how static classifiers, graph-based signal fusion, and large language model (LLM) triage agents perform under strict human-analyst attention budgets (Recall at B alerts/day). Across 56 reproducible experiments spanning a multi-layer enterprise-and-cloud simulator and four public datasets (SOREL-20M, BODMAS, LANL, and DARPA Transparent Computing), we test twelve architectural claims governing modern fleet-scale defense. First, we show that fleet growth halves recall at a fixed analyst budget even at negligible compute cost (<$0.0003 per million events), and that rarity-weighted graph risk propagation outperforms GraphSAGE without neural training. Second, evaluating frontier reasoning models (Gemini) and fine-tuned open-weight models (Qwen3-4B) for alert triage reveals that unconstrained LLM auto-closure eliminates 90% of false positives while silently discarding 31-44% of true attack cases. Third, post-training with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) causes severe reward hacking on unseen attack classes, suppressing up to 86% of novel attacks compared to two-epoch supervised fine-tuning. Finally, against an adaptive adversary generating plausible cover stories in benign telemetry fields, LLM triage evasion rises sharply unless gated by a deterministic policy harness prior to model invocation.

</details>


### [364] [REFORGE: LLM-Assisted RTL Bug Repair with Structured Context and Verification Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33748)
*Tanvir Hossain,Sudipta Paria,Aidan Gleason Myers,Swarup Bhunia,Tamzidul Hoque*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出REFORGE——首个在迭代中累积结构化修复证据的LLM辅助RTL修复框架，用多阶段验证证据（语法、仿真、形式、触发外保持、安全重扫、PPA）替代单一修复率，在288次运行中证明单靠testbench会高估修复效果3.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based RTL修复方法通常只报告单一修复率，编译成功或testbench通过并不能证明缺陷已消除、行为被保持、安全性达标或实现可行；缺少分离的证据导致难以复现比较，也难以集成进硬件设计流程。

Method: REFORGE对缺陷RTL进行画像，提取结构与安全相关特征，定位失败行为，并借助验证引导的诊断生成完整的修复变体；每个变体依次经过语法检查、仿真、形式分析、off-trigger保持性检查、安全重扫描以及基于综合的功耗-性能-面积(PPA)分析，并在迭代中累积结构化证据以同时满足功能、行为等价、安全与成本效率多目标。实现为与后端无关的修复-验证流程。

Result: 在三个LLM后端、三个benchmark套件的288次运行中评估：CirFix上，所提上下文与诊断使产生"经检查修复"的运行从32/63提升到51/63；所有配置均修复了Hack@DAC OpenTitan缺陷。证据记录显示：仅以testbench为判据报告的修复数比全部适用检查多3.9倍；安全洁净的输出可能缺乏功能验证；不同RTL修复可实现等价硬件。

Conclusion: LLM生成的RTL修复需要显式的多阶段验证证据，而非单一pass/fail结论；REFORGE的结构化证据累积机制支持可复现比较并便于集成到硬件设计流程。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can write and repair RTL, but successful compilation or testbench execution does not establish defect removal, behavioral preservation, security, or implementation feasibility. Existing LLM-based RTL repair methods often report a single repair rate without retaining separate evidence for these properties, limiting reproducible comparison and integration into hardware design flows. We present REFORGE, which, to our knowledge, is the first LLM-assisted RTL repair framework that maintains and accumulates structured repair evidence across iterations, allowing it to generate fixes that satisfy multiple objectives simultaneously, including functionality, behavioral equivalence, security, and cost efficiency. REFORGE profiles buggy RTL, identifies structural and security-relevant features, localizes failing behavior, and uses verification-guided diagnosis to generate complete RTL repair variants. Each variant undergoes syntax checking, simulation, formal analysis, off-trigger preservation, security re-scan, and synthesis-based power, performance, and area analysis. We implement REFORGE as a backend-independent repair-and-verification flow and evaluate it in 288 runs across three LLM backends and three benchmark suites. On CirFix, the proposed context and diagnosis increase runs delivering a checked repair from 32/63 to 51/63, while every configuration repairs the Hack@DAC OpenTitan defect. Evidence records also show that a testbench-only criterion reports 3.9 times more repairs than the full set of applicable checks, security-clean outputs may lack functional verification, and different RTL repairs can implement equivalent hardware. These results show that LLM-generated RTL repairs require explicit multi-stage verification evidence rather than a single pass/fail outcome.

</details>


### [365] [JOVE ICE: Intent Guided Cloud Edge Device Collaboration for AI Glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33735)
*Yuyang Jin,Bingfeng Pi,Jiamei Sun,Wei Jin,Dongyang Wang,Yunpeng Li,Yan Bai,Lei Zuo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 JOVE ICE——意图引导的"云-边-端"协同框架，将 AI 眼镜请求按用户意图在眼镜、手机与云服务器间路由，兼顾效率与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 与智能体使 AI 眼镜具备第一视角 VQA、记忆检索、主动助理等能力，但输入模态多样（语音/图像/视频）、任务跨度大（从设备控制到复杂视觉推理），带来计算与通信效率挑战；现有工作偏重视频类能力，而日常占比很高的简单文本与设备可执行任务被忽视，单一助手内高效支持异构负载仍是开放问题。

Method: JOVE ICE 以用户意图为路由依据，在 AI 眼镜、配对手机、云端之间协同调度：采用层次化意图分类 + 动态子智能体编排，选择高效执行路径，并通过专用子智能体灵活接入新应用。对真实请求的分析显示设备控制类任务占比超 30%，故直接在端侧处理以避免不必要的云端计算与成本。

Result: 在三个公开基准上，相较传统"端+云"架构，首 Token 时延降低约 13%，Token 用量减少逾 11%，且任务质量保持相当。

Conclusion: 结果表明意图引导的协同调度能提升 AI 眼镜系统的效率与可扩展性，为统一助手内异构负载的高效处理提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) and LLM based agents have expanded the capabilities of AI glasses, enabling egocentric visual question answering, memory retrieval, and proactive assistance. These applications involve diverse inputs, including speech, images, and video, and range from simple device control to complex visual reasoning, posing challenges for efficient computation and communication. While video centric capabilities have received considerable attention, simple text based and device executable tasks remain underexplored despite their prevalence in daily interactions. Efficiently supporting diverse workloads within a unified assistant remains an open challenge. We propose JOVE ICE, an intent guided cloud, edge, and device collaborative framework that routes requests among AI glasses, a paired smartphone, and cloud servers according to user intent. JOVE ICE integrates hierarchical intent classification with dynamic subagent orchestration to select efficient execution paths and flexibly incorporate new applications through specialized subagents. Analysis of real world AI glasses requests reveals that simple device control tasks account for over 30% of interactions, which JOVE ICE handles directly on device to avoid unnecessary cloud computation and costs. Evaluations on three public benchmarks demonstrate that JOVE ICE reduces time to first token by approximately 13% and token usage by over 11% compared with conventional device and cloud architectures, while maintaining task quality. These results demonstrate the potential of intent guided collaboration to improve the efficiency and scalability of AI glasses.

</details>


### [366] [P-FedSkill: A Personalized Federated Skill Learning Framework under Task Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33707)
*Zehang Lin,Jiayi Ji,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出P-FedSkill个性化联邦技能学习框架，通过局部技能抽取、个性化技能选择与任务感知技能路由，使技能共享兼顾异构任务贡献，并有理论保证与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体轨迹含敏感信息难以跨客户端共享，联邦技能学习虽通过将轨迹蒸馏为紧凑技能表示并上传中心服务器解决隐私问题，但现有方法仅按技能相似度共享，忽略技能对各客户端异构任务的贡献差异，导致性能退化。

Method: 框架含三部分：局部技能抽取（LSE）从轨迹中提取技能补丁（任务适用条件与执行策略）及任务执行结果；个性化技能选择（PSS）利用执行结果评估每个技能补丁在客户端任务上的表现，并结合任务适用条件为各客户端构建个性化技能集；任务感知技能路由（TASR）依据适用条件自适应选择任务相关技能子集指导智能体执行。

Result: 给出个性化技能选择的理论保证，并通过大量实验验证框架有效性。

Conclusion: 以任务感知的个性化技能选择替代单纯相似度共享，可在保护隐私的联邦设定下提升异构客户端智能体的技能学习与执行性能。

Abstract: Skill learning has become an important way to improve agent performance. However, agent trajectories often contain sensitive information and cannot be shared across clients, hindering collaborative skill learning. To tackle this issue, federated skill learning (FSL) has emerged as a solution by distilling trajectories into compact skill representations and uploading them to a central server for sharing. However, existing FSL methods typically share skills based on skill similarity without considering their contributions to heterogeneous tasks across clients, thereby degrading agent performance. To address this challenge, we propose P-FedSkill, a personalized FSL framework that comprises three key components: local skill extraction (LSE), personalized skill selection (PSS), and task-aware skill routing (TASR). Specifically, LSE first extracts skill patches (i.e., task applicability conditions and execution strategies) and task execution outcomes from trajectories. PSS then uses these outcomes to evaluate each skill patch's performance on client tasks and incorporates its task applicability condition to construct a personalized skill set for each client. Finally, TASR adaptively selects a task-relevant subset of skill patches based on their applicability conditions to guide agent execution. We further provide a theoretical guarantee for personalized skill selection in P-FedSkill, and extensive experiments also demonstrate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [367] [ZeroHero: Training Small Language Agents from Scratch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34635)
*Nikita Sorokin,Zoya Volovikova,Alexey Skrynnik,Aleksandr Panov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ZeroHero 提出从零训练参数量小于 5000 万的小型语言智能体（SLA）的框架：先用大模型作为专家采集成功轨迹做预训练，再用 PPO 在自身交互上后训练，并以"推理对齐"补充环境奖励；在 Crafter、WebShop、Alfworld-Dialogue 三个多步环境中接近或超越专家水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大预训练语言模型可作为交互任务的能力型策略，但专用语言智能体究竟需要多大模型容量尚不清楚；本文旨在探索以极小参数量从头训练专用语言智能体的可行性与边界。

Method: 1) 在每个环境中以大型语言模型为专家，采集其成功轨迹；2) 对少于 50M 参数的小模型做监督预训练，令其在给定交互历史下预测专家回复；3) 用 PPO 在智能体自身交互上做后训练；4) 后训练阶段引入"推理对齐"（reasoning alignment），在环境奖励之外，对智能体在动作之外生成的语言施加专家监督。

Result: 在三个需要理解与生成语言的多步环境中评估：Crafter（解读文本观测并生成推理后行动）、WebShop（将购物指令转为搜索查询）、Alfworld-Dialogue（提出澄清问题并据回答指导家务动作）。智能体在 WebShop 上接近专家表现；在 Crafter 训练 rollout 与 Alfworld-Dialogue 留出任务上超过各自的示范专家。

Conclusion: 专用语言智能体无需大模型容量：通过"专家轨迹预训练 + PPO 自交互后训练 + 推理对齐"的流水线，不足 50M 参数的小模型即可在多步语言交互任务中达到甚至超越大模型专家的水平。

Abstract: Large pretrained language models provide capable policies for interactive tasks, but how much model capacity is necessary for a specialized language agent? We introduce ZeroHero, a framework for training small language agents (SLAs) from scratch. In each environment, a large language model acts as the expert, and we collect its successful trajectories. We pretrain each SLA, which has fewer than 50 million parameters, to predict the expert's responses given the interaction history. We then post-train the agent on its own interactions using proximal policy optimization (PPO). During post-training, reasoning alignment supplements environment rewards with expert supervision on the language the agent generates alongside its actions. We evaluate the agents in three multistep environments where acting requires both understanding and generating language. In Crafter, agents interpret textual observations and generate reasoning before acting. In WebShop, they translate shopping instructions into search queries. In Alfworld-Dialogue, they ask clarifying questions and use the answers to guide household actions. The agents approach expert performance on WebShop and surpass their respective demonstration experts on Crafter training rollouts and held-out Alfworld-Dialogue tasks. An interactive demo is available at https://zoyav.github.io/-ZoyaV-zerohero-demo.

</details>


### [368] [From Shared Patterns to Individual Evidence: Two-Stage Reasoning for Emerging Entities in Temporal Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34708)
*Jinchuan Zhang,Lei Gao,Hongyu Jiang,Chong Mu,Chenang Cai,Ke Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: INSPIRE：一个两阶段框架，先用全目录可用性感知的共享模式预测器，再冻结并用轻量个体证据适配器增强，提升TKG中新兴实体的零样本/稀疏少样本预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序知识图谱中新兴实体的交互历史从零样本演进为稀疏突发少样本轨迹。现有范式割裂：模式迁移方法缺乏全目录事件记忆，在线记忆模型存在端点角色不对称，损害未见答案排序；且稀疏早期交互在偏态分布下兼有长程分散与局部冗余。

Method: 两阶段预测框架（基于已知目录）：Stage I 学习可用性感知的共享模式预测器，融合全候选目录的事件更新记忆，并施加查询侧与目录侧的协同掩码；Stage II 冻结该预测器，优化轻量个体证据适配器（EA），结合文本语义先验与相关性加权长程事件统计，在支持平衡与角色平衡的共答案监督下训练，配以先验几率去偏和软残差对齐。

Result: 在ICEWS与GDELT的四个基准上，INSPIRE的Emerging MRR较最强基线提升16.73%-88.83%；相对配对Stage I，EA进一步将Emerging MRR提升8.28%-32.63%、Answer-new MRR提升7.81%-41.24%、cohort macro MRR提升9.64%-39.94%。

Conclusion: 按端点角色分解评估并用查询micro与答案实体macro指标追踪纵向新兴答案队列。增益归因于语义先验与候选级分数竞争抑制了不匹配的已建立竞争者，且避免了零支持先验捷径。代码已开源。

Abstract: Temporal knowledge graphs (TKGs) continually introduce emerging entities whose interaction histories evolve from zero-shot debuts into sparse, bursty few-shot trajectories. However, current paradigms are fragmented: pattern-transfer methods lack catalog-wide event memory, whereas online memory models suffer from endpoint-role asymmetry that degrades unseen-answer ranking. Furthermore, sparse early interactions exhibit both long-range dispersion and local redundancy under skewed distribution. To address these challenges, we propose INSPIRE (Integrating Shared Patterns and Individual Evidence for Reasoning), a two-stage forecasting framework over a known catalog. In Stage I, INSPIRE learns an availability-aware shared-pattern predictor that fuses event-updated memory across the full candidate catalog while applying coordinated query- and catalog-side masking. Subsequently, Stage II freezes this predictor and optimizes a lightweight individual-evidence adapter (EA) combining textual semantic priors with correlation-weighted long-range event statistics under support- and role-balanced co-answer supervision with prior-odds debiasing and soft residual alignment. In addition, we decompose evaluation by endpoint roles and track longitudinal emerging-answer cohorts via query-micro and answer-entity macro metrics. Across four benchmarks based on the ICEWS and GDELT event datasets, INSPIRE improves Emerging MRR by 16.73%-88.83% over the strongest baselines. Relative to paired Stage I predictors, EA further increases Emerging MRR by 8.28%-32.63%, Answer-new MRR by 7.81%-41.24%, and cohort macro MRR by 9.64%-39.94%, gains attributed to semantic priors and candidate-level score competition suppressing mismatched established competitors without zero-support prior shortcuts. The official code is publicly available at https://github.com/KcAcoZhang/INSPIRE.

</details>


### [369] [A Failure-Driven Data Flywheel for Post-Training Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34698)
*Yuhao Luo,Zihao Sheng,Zilin Huang,Zhengyang Wan,Boyue Wang,Ruolin Yang,Shuai Wang,Sikai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"失败驱动的数据飞轮"用于VLA模型后训练：将任务相关实体插入重建的真实场景构造缺失的目标场景，按行为需求逐项评测生成失败地图，并据此动态分配数据生成配额；在自动驾驶遮挡行人出现与停车标志让行任务上验证，显著提升安全指标且控制误刹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练VLA模型难以满足新平台的全部行为要求，而缺乏大规模部署车队数据的团队无法获取针对性失败数据。

Method: 构建失败驱动数据飞轮：在重建真实场景中插入任务相关实体生成目标场景；针对任务各行为需求逐一评测模型，得到跨任务条件与难度等级的失败率失败地图；每轮后重新评估，对仍失败的条件下达更大数据生成配额。在自动驾驶遮挡行人出现与停车标志路口让行两类场景中实例化，评测2个VLA模型与1个非VLA规划器，涵盖仿真与实车。

Result: 遮挡行人：5轮后训练使密封测试集关键阶段无碰撞率AutoVLA 0.261→0.579、ReCogDrive 0.390→0.765，配对对照场景误刹率≤0.013；实车（无真实数据训练）后训练ReCogDrive安全通过4/5，基模型1/5。细粒度评测显示两VLA均学会抵达遮挡车前预减速，但行人出现后制动仍有限。停车让行：停车线前停车率AutoVLA 0.100→0.897、ReCogDrive 0.000→0.667；用行人能力模型顺序训练后分别达0.599与0.358，行人无碰撞率下降最多0.032。

Conclusion: 失败驱动数据飞轮可在缺乏真实车队数据条件下有效后训练VLA模型，细粒度失败地图揭示聚合指标掩盖的行为缺陷，并支持多能力的顺序训练与较小遗忘代价。

Abstract: Pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) models rarely satisfy all behavioral requirements of a new platform, and teams without a large deployed fleet lack the data to address the resulting failures. We propose a failure-driven data flywheel for post-training such models. It constructs target scenarios absent from the available data by inserting task-relevant entities into reconstructed real-world scenes, and evaluates the model against each behavioral requirement of the target task. Failure rates form a failure map across task conditions and difficulty levels, which is re-evaluated after each round and assigns larger data generation quotas to conditions where the model still fails. We instantiate the framework in autonomous driving for occluded-pedestrian emergence and yielding at stop-sign-controlled intersections, and evaluate it on two VLA models and one non-VLA planner in simulation and on a real vehicle. For occluded-pedestrian emergence, five rounds of post-training improve the critical-phase collision-free rate on the sealed test set from 0.261 to 0.579 for AutoVLA and from 0.390 to 0.765 for ReCogDrive, while the unnecessary braking rate in paired control scenarios never exceeds 0.013. On a full-scale vehicle without real-world training, post-trained ReCogDrive achieves 4 of 5 safe passages, versus 1 of 5 for the base model. Fine-grained evaluation reveals a pattern obscured by the collision-free rate alone: both VLA models learn anticipatory deceleration before reaching the occluding vehicle, while braking after pedestrian emergence remains limited. For stop-sign yielding, the stop-before-line rate rises from 0.100 to 0.897 for AutoVLA and from 0.000 to 0.667 for ReCogDrive. With sequential training from the pedestrian-capability models, it reaches 0.599 and 0.358, respectively, while the pedestrian collision-free rate decreases by at most 0.032.

</details>


### [370] [Improving Building Energy Efficiency with an Indoor Environmental World Model and Counterfactual Adaptive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33764)
*Xu Yang,Kailai Sun,Dianyu Zhong,Irfan Qaisar,Yu Yang,Qianchuan Zhao,Jian Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出以居住者为中心的室内气候控制框架，通过环境世界模型显式建模热惯性并预测动态占用，结合反事实优势学习与Held-Action TD-λ缓解延迟信用分配，并加入物理一致的平滑正则抑制执行器高频波动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内气候系统优化对实现联合国SDG 11与13至关重要，但室内热动力响应存在延迟且系统部分可观测，现有强化学习方法受隐式热惯性、占用动态难以预测以及长时程 rollout 中累积预测误差的限制，控制效果不佳。

Method: 构建室内环境世界模型，预测未来物理热基线（热惯性）与动态占用来显式捕捉潜在热惯性；将自适应调节建模为针对未来环境的反事实优势学习，采用Branching Dueling Q-Network实现；引入Held-Action TD-λ目标以缓解延迟信用分配；并设计物理一致的平滑正则项抑制执行器高频波动。

Result: 在SemiBuildingSim与Sinergym上实验表明该方法取得更优的多目标权衡：与最佳基线相比，能耗降低11.2%、居住者不舒适度降低44.2%、动作波动降低47.3%，并在样本效率与跨区域有效性上更优。

Conclusion: 该跨学科研究为控制科学、建筑科学与能源可持续性提供了一种节能、以居住者为中心的AI解决方案。

Abstract: Optimizing indoor climate systems plays a critical role in achieving the United Nations' Sustainable Development Goals 11 and 13. However, indoor delayed thermodynamic responses and partial observability severely hinder existing reinforcement learning (RL) methods, which are primarily limited by implicit thermal inertia, occupancy dynamic prediction, and cumulative prediction errors during long-horizon rollouts. Thus, we propose a novel occupant-centric indoor climate control framework. It develops indoor environmental world models to predict future physical thermal baselines and dynamic occupancy, explicitly capturing latent thermal inertia. By formulating adaptive regulation as counterfactual advantage learning against future environments via a Branching Dueling Q-Network, and integrating a Held-Action TD-λ target, it effectively mitigates delayed credit assignment. Additionally, a proposed physics-aligned smoothness regularization suppresses high-frequency actuator fluctuations, potentially extending equipment service life. Experiments on SemiBuildingSim and Sinergym demonstrate that our method achieves a superior multi-objective trade-off. Compared to the best-performing methods, it further reduces energy consumption by 11.2%, occupant discomfort by 44.2%, and action fluctuation by 47.3%, achieving better sample efficiency and cross-region effectiveness. This interdisciplinary study offers an energy-efficient, occupant-centric AI solution for control science, building science, and energy sustainability.

</details>


### [371] [Context Scaling: A New Frontier of AI Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33160)
*Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"上下文扩展（context scaling）"这一概念，指出已部署AI的许多失败源于任务所依赖的状态与系统行动时可用上下文之间的错配，而非推理能力不足；主张将模型-基础设施对作为分析单元，并用训练、决策时、上下文基础设施三类预算框架组织研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI失败常被归因于推理能力弱，但真正瓶颈是决策时可用上下文的缺失、失真或选择不当（用户状态、仓库版本、工具轨迹、传感器读数、环境变化等）。预训练扩展投入θ，决策时扩展在给定上下文内搜索与验证，二者都无法解决"系统在决策前能提供什么"的问题，因此需要一个专门针对上下文获取与交付的研究纲领。

Method: 将模型表述为P_θ(d|c)，并置于三预算体系下：训练预算B_θ、决策时预算B_d、上下文基础设施预算B_c。界定"有效上下文价值"为依赖任务与干预的归因量，区分部署状态与模型可见上下文，归纳可用性、保真度、选择、利用四类失败模式；用五操作上下文生命周期组织现有机制，并提出受控预算干预与效用-成本曲线作为评估方法。

Result: 给出统一的概念框架：三类扩展项目的交互关系、以B_c为唯一针对"决策前可提供内容"的预算定位、四类上下文失败分类、五阶段生命周期机制图谱，以及基于预算干预与效用-成本曲线的可比较评估方案。

Conclusion: 研究议程应聚焦于：有依据的报告（grounded reporting）、交互数据与全成本基础设施，以及面向持久化自适应系统的安全与治理；核心分析单元从单一模型转向模型-基础设施对。

Abstract: Many failures of deployed AI arise not from weak inference but from a mismatch between the state a task depends on and the context available when the system acts. Relevant user state, repository revisions, tool traces, sensor readings, or environment changes may be unavailable, distorted, or poorly selected. We call the effort to reduce this bottleneck context scaling. We frame a model as $P_θ(d\mid c)$ within a system governed by three budgets: training $B_θ$, decision-time $B_d$, and context-infrastructure $B_c$. Pretraining scaling invests in $θ$; decision-time scaling searches, refines, or verifies decisions under supplied context; context scaling improves the system's capacity to acquire, represent, deliver, evaluate, and persist deployment state for model use. The three programs interact, but only $B_c$ targets what the system can make available before a decision. This view makes the model-infrastructure pair the unit of analysis. We define effective-context value as a task- and intervention-dependent attribution, separate deployment state from model-visible context, and identify availability, fidelity, selection, and utilization failures. We then organize current mechanisms through a five-operation context lifecycle and propose controlled budget interventions and utility-cost curves for evaluation. The resulting agenda centers on grounded reporting, interaction-data and full-cost infrastructure, and safety and governance for persistent, adaptive systems.

</details>


### [372] [Honest but Wrong: Formalizing Retraction in the Ethics of Speech Acts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34011)
*Benjamin Icard,Gauvain Bourgne,Jeanne Bonnaventure,Jean-Gabriel Ganascia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将基于信念的言语行为伦理框架扩展到"撤回"情形，证明道义论的诚实义务从不要求撤回错误但真诚的断言，并新增持续性义务；在ASP实现的法律咨询案例中表明道义论与后果论都要求撤回，但后果论在撤回已无法防止伤害时不再要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 说话者真诚断言后可能发现其为假，这类"错误之真"（erroneous truth）并非谎言，却引出是否及基于何种道德理由必须撤回的问题；现有评估言语行为的信念框架只覆盖断言发出时刻，缺少对撤回行为的伦理分析。

Method: 借助FIPA-ACL的disconfirm语用行为，把在道义论与后果论下评估言语行为的信念框架扩展到撤回情形；形式化证明道义论的诚实义务只作用于话语发出时刻、从不要求撤回，并提出一条"持续性义务"禁止让听者继续相信说话者已不再相信的内容；用Answer Set Programming实现法律咨询案例。

Result: 形式化证明：原诚实义务永不要求撤回错误之真；加入持续性义务后道义论要求撤回。案例实现显示道义论与后果论均要求撤回，但当撤回过晚、已无法防止伤害时，后果论不再要求撤回。

Conclusion: 错误之真的撤回需要"持续性义务"这一独立道德基础，而非断言时的诚实义务；道义论与后果论在撤回上并非总是一致，其分歧体现在撤回时机的有效性上。

Abstract: A speaker who asserts honestly may later discover that its assertion was false. Such erroneous truths are not lies, yet they raise the question of whether, and on which moral grounds, the speaker must retract them. Using the FIPA-ACL performative disconfirm, we extend a belief-based framework that evaluates speech acts under deontologism and consequentialism to the case of retraction. We prove that the honesty duty of deontologism, which bears on utterances when they are made, never requires retracting an erroneous truth, and add a persistent one, which forbids letting the addressee believe what the speaker no longer believes. On a legal-advice case implemented in Answer Set Programming, we show that deontologism and consequentialism both require the retraction, but that consequentialism no longer does once it comes too late to prevent harm.

</details>


### [373] [Can Its Own Misses Teach an MLLM to Find Small Brain Metastases?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33836)
*Yixuan Duan,Kexin Geng,Hanwen Xu,Wei Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LANTERN将医学多模态大模型的开发重构为错误驱动的递归循环：失败普查把每个漏检病灶归因到四类原因之一，LLM导师每轮修改四个层级之一，规则在首轮前冻结决定保留；15轮无人干预后将4B模型在每扫描1个假阳性下限的0.1-1 mL病灶检出率从75%提升到94%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM作为通用医学影像读者在定位小病灶上仍很弱：脑转移瘤在384 px下0.1 mL病灶不足一个视觉token的四分之一，前沿MLLM在每扫描1个假阳性条件下仅能检出不到30%的0.1-1 mL病灶；单数据集微调迁移性差，且仅靠单一分数的试错改进缓慢。

Method: 提出LANTERN框架：把模型开发写成错误驱动闭环——(1) failure census（失败普查）将每个漏检病灶归因于四类原因之一；(2) LLM mentor每轮在四个层级（证据获取、视觉表征、学习策略、决策聚合）中修改其一；(3) 首轮前冻结的规则决定哪些改动被保留；全过程不使用测试数据，且每次工具调用均被审计；共15轮无人值守迭代。

Result: 在BraTS-MET上，微调的4B MLLM在每扫描1个假阳性的工作点上检出率由75%升至94%，0.1-1 mL小病灶达91%；相同起始协议下五个前沿MLLM最高仅51%与29%。增益在外部队列（89%）与未见过的脑膜瘤（77%）上保持。

Conclusion: 将MLLM自身的漏检按成因归因，可驱动其可靠地递归自我改进，说明错误驱动的闭环开发是提升小病灶定位能力的有效路径。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are becoming general readers of medical images, yet they remain weak at locating small abnormalities. Brain metastases expose this gap: at 384 px a 0.1 mL lesion covers less than a quarter of one visual token, and at one false positive per scan frontier MLLMs find fewer than 30% of lesions of 0.1-1 mL. Fine-tuning on one dataset helps but transfers poorly, and improving it further is slow trial and error steered by a single score. We introduce LANTERN (Lesion-finding AgeNt Through Error-driven Recursive learNing), a framework in which an MLLM improves recursively from its own misses. LANTERN recasts model development as an error-driven loop: a failure census attributes every missed lesion to one of four causes, an LLM mentor revises one of four levels (evidence acquisition, visual representation, learning policy, decision aggregation) per round, and a rule frozen before the first round decides what is kept, without test data and with every tool call audited. Over 15 unattended rounds, LANTERN raises a fine-tuned 4B MLLM from finding 75% to 94% of lesions at one false positive per scan on BraTS-MET, and 91% of those of 0.1-1 mL, compared with at most 51% and 29% for five frontier MLLMs read under the starting protocol; the gains hold on an external cohort (89%) and on unseen meningiomas (77%). These results show that an MLLM's own misses, attributed to their causes, can drive its reliable recursive self-improvement.

</details>


### [374] [RadOncGym: An Agent-Based Framework for Radiotherapy Dose-Mimicking Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33821)
*Tong Miao,Shupeng Cheng,Xiwen Chen,Ruishan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RadOncGym：基于LLM智能体的放疗计划目标权重迭代调优框架，用代理验证器降低评估成本，在OpenKBP头颈数据上以3次求解预算优于非智能体方法、接近16次求解变体而运行时间减少约75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI剂量预测模型输出的剂量需经下游剂量模仿优化才能生成物理可行的计划，而该过程需在多个相互冲突的规划目标间权衡。12个目标权重的调优依赖专家经验且迭代评估（完整计划求解）代价高昂，缺乏系统化的自动化手段。

Method: 提出RadOncGym智能体框架：LLM智能体在固定求解预算内迭代调整12个规划目标权重以完成剂量模仿优化；引入代理验证器（surrogate verifier）提供快速反馈，由智能体自主决定何时调用完整计划求解。在60例OpenKBP头颈患者×各10组OpenKBP-Opt预测（每配置600个患者-预测情境）上评估，采用16轮交互、3次求解预算的设定，并与多种非智能体权重调优方法对比。

Result: GPT-5.6 Sol智能体相比权重全固定为1的基线，平均临床准则违背量降低0.6131 Gy，DVH评分与剂量评分分别提升0.5986 与 0.2204 Gy；在同为3次求解预算下优于所有对比的非智能体方法，并与这些方法的16次求解版本表现相当，同时归一化运行时间减少75.0–77.6%。

Conclusion: LLM智能体可作为下游放疗计划优化中的“优化规划者”，在有限求解预算下高效权衡多目标；RadOncGym同时提供公开测试平台以支持该类智能体的系统评估（代码与配置已开源）。

Abstract: AI dose-prediction models generate patient-specific 3D dose distributions from patient anatomy, providing an important intermediate representation for automated radiotherapy planning. However, an AI-predicted dose must undergo downstream optimization to produce a physically constrained treatment plan, a process that requires balancing competing planning objectives. We present RadOncGym, an agent-based framework that enables an LLM agent to iteratively tune 12 planning-objective weights for dose-mimicking optimization. To reduce iterative evaluation cost, RadOncGym incorporates a surrogate verifier that provides rapid feedback while allowing the agent to decide when to request a full planning solve within a fixed solve budget. We evaluate RadOncGym on 60 OpenKBP head-and-neck patients, each paired with 10 predictions from OpenKBP-Opt dose-prediction sets, yielding 600 patient-prediction contexts per experimental configuration. Under RadOncGym's 16-turn setting with a three-solve budget, a GPT-5.6 Sol agent reduces mean clinical-criterion violation by 0.6131 Gy and improves DVH and dose scores by 0.5986 and 0.2204 Gy relative to leaving all 12 weights fixed at 1, outperforming all evaluated non-agent weight-tuning methods under the same solve budget. It remains competitive with the 16-solve variants of these methods while requiring 75.0-77.6% less normalized runtime. RadOncGym demonstrates the potential of LLM agents as optimization planners in downstream radiotherapy plan optimization and provides a public testbed for their systematic evaluation. Code and configurations are available at https://github.com/miaot-usc/RadOncGym.

</details>


### [375] [Navigent: A Geometry-Grounded Agentic Framework for Vision Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34249)
*Xinyu Zhou,Weiren Cai,Yitong Chen,Shenghai Yuan,Zihang Mu,Boyu Ma,Jiahua Dong,Yanshuo Lu,Jiaqi Bai,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Navigent 是一个基于冻结多模态大模型的智能体式VLN框架，通过几何接地的执行批判器（GEC）对"指令-运动"和"运动-地标"进行双重一致性校验，在偏差出现时中断动作并触发重观测/局部调整/节点恢复，配合历史视觉记忆与离线自改进，在R2R-CE与RxR-CE未见验证集上取得84.0%/86.0% SR与72.9%/69.1% SPL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用MLLM用于VLN灵活但长程执行不可靠：智能体难以跟踪导航进度、检测指令与实际运动的偏差，也难判断是否已越过关键地标或入口，缺乏可靠的执行级验证机制。

Method: 提出Navigent：以冻结MLLM为核心，外加几何接地的Geometry-Grounded Execution Critic。GEC包含两项互补检查——动作-运动一致性（将实测位移与指令运动比对）与运动-地标一致性（在世界坐标系中跟踪智能体自选的视觉锚点与入口候选）。一旦检测到不一致，即中断剩余动作序列并触发重观测、局部调整或基于节点的恢复；通过历史视觉记忆支持长路径进度跟踪。此外设计离线自改进流程，在固定模型权重前提下分析成功/失败训练轨迹以优化导航提示与GEC逻辑。

Result: 在R2R-CE validation-unseen（1,839条）与RxR-CE validation-unseen（3,669条）上分别达到84.0%/86.0% SR和72.9%/69.1% SPL。

Conclusion: 几何接地的执行验证对可靠智能体式导航具有显著价值；在无需微调模型权重的情况下，可显著提升长程VLN执行的可靠性。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) with general-purpose multimodal large language models (MLLMs) offers a flexible alternative to task-specific navigation policies, while reliable long-horizon execution remains challenging. Agents must track navigation progress, detect discrepancies between commanded and actual motion, and recognize when movement has passed relevant landmarks or entrances. We present Navigent, an agentic VLN framework built around a frozen MLLM and a Geometry-Grounded Execution Critic (GEC). GEC performs two complementary checks: action-to-motion consistency, which evaluates measured displacement against commanded movement, and motion-to-landmark consistency, which tracks agent-selected visual anchors and entrance candidates in world coordinates. Detected discrepancies interrupt the remaining action sequence and trigger reobservation, local adjustment, or node-based recovery. Historical visual memory supports progress tracking across extended routes. An offline self-improvement procedure further analyzes successful and failed training trajectories to refine navigation prompts and GEC logic while keeping model weights fixed. On the R2R-CE validation-unseen split (1,839 episodes) and RxR-CE validation-unseen split(3,669 episodes), Navigent achieves 84.0%/86.0% SR and 72.9%/69.1% SPL, respectively, demonstrating the value of geometry-grounded execution verification for reliable agentic navigation. Project Page: https://marslab-navigent.github.io/navigent.github.io/ Code: https://github.com/MarsLab-Navigent/Navigent

</details>


### [376] [SOLO: Towards Sample-Faithful and Detectable LLM Watermarking under Repeated Sampling via Content-Anchored Single-Owner Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34190)
*Zhongliang Guo,Yan Lin,Yifei Qian,Shuai Zhao,Luwei Xiao,Daizong Liu,Chun Pong Lau*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出多响应采样场景下，去失真水印因请求内响应共享同一上下文而相互耦合（碰撞成为常态），提出SOLO：保留请求级上下文历史，但用min-hash选择锚定内容，使兄弟响应极少竞争同一keyed context，随机编辑影响局部化，从而在1%FPR下取得更高检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM应用常对同一prompt采样多个响应（self-consistency、best-of-n、不确定性估计）。已部署的去失真水印只在单个响应内部对keyed context去重，导致同一请求的各响应在答案模板下共享答案前相同上下文，跨响应key碰撞由罕见变为常态，从而使水印耦合响应、破坏独立性假设。key pool虽缓解耦合却引入多重检验问题，而请求级共享上下文历史的精确修复方案又使大量位置无法被水印覆盖。

Method: 提出SOLO水印：保留请求级（request-scoped）上下文历史以避免跨响应碰撞，但将keyed context锚定在由min-hash选出的内容上。这样同一请求的兄弟响应很少竞争同一上下文，随机编辑保持局部性、不影响其他位置的锚点。其响应联合分布等价于相互独立的无水印响应，且每个响应可用单一密钥检测。

Result: 在三个模型家族共五个语言模型、三个基准上验证：SOLO保持self-consistency与不确定性估计性能；在1%假阳性率下，多数任务上检测到的响应数多于四token key pool方案，在随机编辑10% token后仍成立，并在多数干净任务上优于上下文历史方案。

Conclusion: SOLO通过min-hash锚定的请求级上下文历史，消除了多响应采样下的水印耦合，同时保持响应间独立性与编辑鲁棒性，在检测率上优于key pool与上下文历史基线，适用于自一致性与不确定性估计等多样本应用场景。代码已开源。

Abstract: Providers watermark the outputs of large language models, while applications sample many responses per prompt for self-consistency, best-of-n selection, and uncertainty estimation.
